### [21] [MGRD: Compact morphology-gated residual diffusion for variance-aware cross-domain neurite forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23990)
*Tsung Yeh Hsieh,Cosmin Anitescu,Chunghwan Kim,Victoria A. Webster-Wood,Yongjie Jessica Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MGRD，一种紧凑的随机代理模型，可基于10帧观测联合预测20帧未来神经突形态，在保持极低参数量的同时优于gSTA并支持病例难度排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期延时成像追踪神经突形态资源消耗大、难以规模化，而现有数字孪生模型（如gSTA）仅给出单一确定性预测，无法刻画多种可能未来的变异性。

Method: MGRD（Morphology-Gated Residual Diffusion）：一种紧凑的随机扩散代理模型，以最新观测提取的形态特征为条件，从10帧观测联合预测未来20帧神经突形态；采用形态门控残差结构与DDIM采样，并通过重复采样得到病例级方差评分。

Result: 相场轨迹上轨迹平均MAE降低9.7%，更新参数量少4.46倍；人iPSC神经元显微数据上四项指标全面优于gSTA，MAE降低39.6%、骨架F1提升45.3%；无需小鼠域重训练即可在10–40分钟采样间隔及超过13小时预测时程上改善小鼠皮层神经球数据；保留约60%低方差病例使iPSC MAE降低17.6%、仿真降低16.8%；参数量仅为gSTA的1.01%，训练更新耗时不足其1/10，缓存形态特征后50步DDIM生成快7.9%。

Conclusion: MGRD是一种紧凑的随机代理模型，适用于仿真与显微数据上的神经突形态预测与病例优先级排序，兼具高精度、强泛化与低计算成本。

Abstract: Tracking neurite morphology over time helps characterize structural changes during neuronal development and deterioration, but long-term time-lapse imaging is resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Forecasting future morphology could reduce this burden. Existing neurite digital-twin models such as gated spatiotemporal attention (gSTA) produce a single deterministic forecast without representing variability among plausible futures. We introduce Morphology-Gated Residual Diffusion (MGRD), a compact stochastic surrogate that jointly forecasts twenty future neurite-morphology frames from ten observed frames while conditioning on morphology features derived from the latest observation. On controlled phase-field trajectories, MGRD reduces trajectory-wise mean MAE by 9.7% relative to a matched control while updating 4.46 times fewer parameters. On human iPSC-derived neuron microscopy, MGRD improves all four reported metrics over gSTA, including a 39.6% reduction in trajectory-wise mean MAE and a 45.3% increase in skeleton F1. Without mouse-domain retraining or fine-tuning, MGRD also improves MAE and skeleton F1 on mouse cortical-neurosphere microscopy across 10-40-min sampling intervals and forecast horizons beyond 13 hours. Repeated sampling provides a case-level variance score for ranking forecast difficulty. Retaining approximately 60% of the lowest-variance cases reduces mean MAE by 17.6% on iPSC microscopy and 16.8% on simulation data. MGRD uses 1.01% of gSTA's parameters, requires less than one tenth of its training-update time, and generates a 50-step DDIM trajectory 7.9% faster when morphology features are cached. These results establish MGRD as a compact stochastic surrogate for neurite-morphology forecasting and case prioritization across simulation and microscopy datasets.

</details>


### [22] [PRQuant: Permutation Residual Quantization for Low-Overhead Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22106)
*Peiran Wang,Anqi Wang,Jiaying Zhao,Huiwen Yang,Zhenyu Ming,Rongqian Wang,Yiwu Yao,Kun Tian,Xin Yao,Gong Zhang,Fan Yang,Zhongyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRQuant：一种免训练、低开销的低比特量化框架，通过通道重排与静态权重侧残差补偿，将散乱的残差补偿转化为规则的尾部增强GEMM，降低延迟并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性层低比特量化的精度瓶颈主要由少量离群值主导；现有的平滑、旋转或残差方法虽能缓解该问题，却常给权重引入新的精度瓶颈，且多为在线实现，执行开销大。

Method: PRQuant在AWQ式缩放后，识别对权重量化误差贡献最大的输入通道，将其重排为连续尾部块，并离线构建对应的残差权重子张量；推理时利用该连续结构使激活端无缝使用尾部块，免除昂贵的在线gather操作，将散乱的残差补偿转为规则的尾部增强GEMM。

Result: 实验表明PRQuant有效降低down-projection重建误差；消融显示平滑与残差补偿是数值提升的主要来源，重排带来一致的边际数值收益并实现硬件友好的连续布局、消除动态gather开销；在五个下游基准的平均精度上优于默认MXFP4及所评估的PTQ基线，在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507与Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507上分别较MXFP4提升1.24和0.55。

Conclusion: PRQuant以免训练、低开销的方式融合通道重排与静态权重侧残差补偿，在提升低比特量化精度的同时显著降低推理延迟，整体优于MXFP4与所评估的PTQ基线。

Abstract: Accuracy of Low-bit quantization of linear layers is often dominated by a small number of outliers. Although existing methods, such as smoothing, rotation, or residual-based approaches, may mitigate this problem, they often introduce new accuracy bottlenecks to weights. Besides, most of these techniques are implemented as online approaches, which can result in heavy execution overheads. To address the afore-mentioned issues, We propose PRQuant (Permutation Residual Quantization), a training-free and low-overhead framework that combines channel reorganization with static weight-side residual compensation. After AWQ-style scaling, PRQuant identifies the input channels that contribute most to weight quantization error, permutes them into contiguous tail blocks, and constructs their residual weight sub-tensors offline. During inference, this contiguous structure enables the activation side to use tail blocks seamlessly without the expensive online gathering operation, and turns scattered residual compensation into a regular tail-augmented GEMM, substantially reducing latency. Experiments demonstrate that PRQuant effectively reduces down-projection reconstruction error. Ablation studies confirm that smoothing and residual compensation are the primary drivers of numerical improvement, while permutation provides a consistent marginal numerical benefit and, more importantly, enables a hardware-friendly contiguous layout that eliminates dynamic gathering overhead. Overall, PRQuant outperforms default MXFP4 and the evaluated PTQ baselines in average accuracy across five downstream benchmarks, improving over MXFP4 by 1.24 and 0.55 on Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507, respectively.

</details>


### [23] [Generalized Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22107)
*Huizi Cui,Zongbo Han,Chenggong Ding,Naichuan Xiao,Jialong Yang,Jingdong Chen,Guangyu Wang,Qinghua Hu,Changqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于大规模合成多模态数据集训练的通用多模态基础模型，可在不依赖特定模态的前提下，通过上下文示例激活可迁移的多模态关联，实现对任意模态组合与任意预测任务的统一处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合模型部署后仅能处理预定义模态（如视觉、文本、音频）和单一任务，难以快速适应新的下游应用。因此作者提出一个更具挑战性的问题：是否存在一种通用多模态融合模型，能够适用于任意模态组合和任意预测任务。

Method: 作者认为统一的多模态融合模型不应依赖特定模态，而应编码可迁移的多模态关联模式。为此提出一种基于大规模合成多模态数据集生成训练的简单有效学习范式，合成数据包含多样化的因果结构，形式化刻画了现实世界多模态数据的生成过程。在此框架上提出通用多模态基础模型，统一处理广义多模态学习：训练时通过构建具有多样关联模式的大规模合成多模态数据集编码可迁移的多模态关联，推理时通过上下文示例激活恰当的关联。

Result: 在涵盖 12 种模态、11 类预测任务的 18 个真实世界数据集上进行大量实验，结果表明该模型无需任务特定适配即可达到与专用模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了通过合成数据学习可迁移多模态关联的可行性，为实现适用于任意模态组合与任意预测任务的通用多模态基础模型提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Making prediction with multimodal data is widely used in diverse scenarios. Existing multimodal fusion models, once deployed, can only handle predefined modalities (e.g., vision, text and audio) and single tasks, making it difficult to quickly adapt to new downstream applications. Therefore, a natural yet rather aggressive question arises, whether there exists a general multimodal fusion model that can be applied to arbitrary modality combinations and arbitrary prediction tasks. We argue that a unified multimodal fusion model should not depend on specific modalities and instead encode transferable patterns of multimodal correlation. To this end, we propose a simple and effective learning paradigm based on training over the generation of large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse causal structures that formally characterize the generative processes of multimodal data in real world. Building on this framework, we propose the generalized multimodal foundation model, a unified foundation model for generalized multimodal learning. By constructing large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse correlation patterns, our model encodes transferable multimodal correlations during training and activates appropriate associations through in-context examples during inference. Extensive experiments on 18 real-world datasets spanning 12 modalities and 11 prediction tasks demonstrate that our model achieves competitive performance with specialized models without task-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [24] [Correcting Learning-based Perception for Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22108)
*Yan Miao,Hussein Darir,Sayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两步式ML感知状态估计校正策略：离线用感知契约的原像刻画不确定性，运行时用风险启发式从不确定估计中选状态驱动控制，在45个存在安全违规的ACC场景中使73%恢复安全，平均完成时间仅增2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的感知在自动驾驶等自主系统中至关重要，但与传统传感器不同，ML感知失效边界难以刻画；感知错误会导致下游控制不安全或过度保守，因此需要一种既能保障安全又不过度保守的感知校正方法。

Method: 提出两步策略：第一步离线计算，利用感知契约（perception contracts）的原像来刻画ML模块状态估计所产生的不确定性；第二步在运行时使用风险启发式（risk heuristic），从不确定估计集合中选择特定状态来驱动控制决策。

Result: 在基于Yolo和LaneNet的多种视觉自适应巡航控制（ACC）模块上，跨不同天气与道路场景进行大量仿真评估：原感知控制系统出现安全违规的45个ACC场景中，运行时感知校正使73%保持安全，27%因感知契约原像构造不完全一致而无法恢复；同时校正策略不过度保守，被校正场景平均完成时间仅增加2.8%，干预轻微。

Conclusion: 运行时感知校正策略能有效恢复多数ML感知导致的安全违规，且代价小、不显著影响任务完成效率；其局限在于感知契约原像构造的一致性，未来需改进原像构建以覆盖剩余失败场景。

Abstract: Learning-enabled perception is important in many autonomous systems. Unlike traditional sensors, the boundary where ML perception does or does not work is poorly characterized. Incorrect perception can lead to unsafe or overtly conservative downstream control actions. In this paper, we propose a two-step strategy for correcting ML-based state estimation. First, an offline computation is used to characterize the uncertainties resulting from the ML module's state estimation, using preimages of perception contracts. Second, at runtime, a risk heuristic is used to choose particular states from the uncertain estimates to drive the control decisions. We perform extensive simulation-based evaluation of this runtime perception correction strategy on different vision-based adaptive cruise controllers (ACC modules), in different weather conditions, and road scenarios. Out of 45 ACC scenarios where the original perception-based control system using Yolo and LaneNet led to safety violations, in 73% of the scenarios, our runtime perception correction preserved safety; our method wouldn't be able to recover 27% of the scenarios where the construction of the preimages of perception contracts is not fully conformant. Further, our runtime perception correction strategy is not overly conservative---on the average only a 2.8% increase in completion time is experienced in the corrected scenarios, with mild interventions.

</details>


### [25] [A Shared Learning Rate Is Not a Neutral Control in Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22109)
*Chencheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有"选择性在线策略蒸馏"文献用统一学习率比较选择器是不合理的，二者存在"选择器-学习率纠缠"（selector-rate entanglement）现象，并建议报告"方法×学习率"矩阵而非单列共享学习率结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献在比较不同选择器（selector）时，采用单一共享学习率作为中性控制，但作者认为该控制并非中性，可能系统性影响选择器之间的比较结论，因此需要验证学习率是否会与选择性监督产生交互作用。

Method: 在GSM8K上以LoRA微调Qwen2.5-1.5B学生模型、7B教师，在8倍学习率网格上比较密集监督与多种选择性监督（随机5%子集、全变差选择器、可教性选择器）；并通过分析AdamW更新幅度与梯度范数、以及一项预注册的冻结评分消融实验（用初始学生打分，标准、预算与在线rollout不变，每格12个种子）来溯源；同时在MATH-500及全参数微调（1e-6至1e-5）下重复验证。

Result: 密集监督在学习率网格上统计上平稳（波动1.8pp，p=0.26），而各选择性方法随学习率显著波动（随机5%子集5.4pp、全变差选择器6.7pp、可教性选择器最高17.7pp）。密集与选择性的结论在学习率1e-4时为10.1pp，在5e-5时降至5.1pp（相差2.0倍）；选择器间6组显著性检验中有2组在相邻学习率间翻转。AdamW更新幅度与学习率的偏差在2.2%以内，尽管各方法梯度范数相差15.5倍。冻结评分消融显示实时评分额外增加3.79±1.69pp的学习率敏感性（p=0.035），冻结臂仍显著纠缠（p=0.015），说明反馈回路是加剧因素而非根源。全参数微调下该模式更强：密集监督波动19.8pp、选择性49.5pp，结论从已发表操作点的+3.6pp（不显著）到升高一档的+34pp（p=0.005）。MATH-500上LoRA下该学习率依赖不复现，但选择性训练约10pp的代价仍存在。

Conclusion: 选择性监督与学习率存在纠缠，其根源在于选择行为本身而非步长，实时评分反馈回路会加剧该现象。因此，比较选择器时应报告"方法×学习率"矩阵，而非单一共享学习率的列，以此作为选择器比较的前提条件。

Abstract: Selective on-policy distillation trains a student only at the token positions a selector scores highest, and the literature compares selectors under a single shared learning rate--a control chosen to be neutral. We show it is not. Under LoRA on GSM8K (Qwen2.5-1.5B student, 7B teacher), across an 8x learning-rate grid, dense supervision is statistically flat (swing 1.8 pp, p=0.26) while every selective arm moves with the rate: 5.4 pp for a random 5% subset, 6.7 pp for a total-variation selector, up to 17.7 pp for a teachability selector. Consequently the dense-versus-selective verdict reads 10.1 pp at lr=1e-4 but 5.1 pp at 5e-5--a 2.0x difference decided by a parameter the protocol treats as scenery--and two of six pairwise significance calls between selectors flip between adjacent rates without any rank inversion. We call this selector-rate entanglement and trace it to selection itself rather than step size: AdamW update magnitudes track the rate to within 2.2% despite 15.5x gradient-norm differences across arms. A preregistered frozen-scoring ablation (selection scored by the initial student; criterion, budget, and on-policy rollouts unchanged; 12 seeds per cell) shows live scoring adds 3.79+/-1.69 pp of rate sensitivity (p=0.035) while the frozen arm remains significantly entangled (p=0.015): the feedback loop aggravates the phenomenon rather than causing it. Under full fine-tuning at the rates this literature actually uses (1e-6 to 1e-5) the pattern grows: dense itself swings 19.8 pp, the selective arm 49.5 pp, and the verdict ranges from a non-significant +3.6 pp at the published operating point to +34 pp (p=0.005) one notch hotter. On MATH-500 the rate dependence does not reproduce under LoRA, scoping that result, while the ~10 pp cost of selective training does. We prescribe reporting the arm x rate matrix, not a shared-rate column, as a precondition for selector comparisons.

</details>


### [26] [Toward Fairness in Machine Learning Models for Predicting Treatment Retention and Premature Discontinuation in Medication for Opioid Use Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22113)
*Tongnian Wang,Carolina Vivas-Valencia,Cici Bauer,Yanmin Gong,Kim-Kwang Raymond Choo,Yuanxiong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了预测阿片类物质使用障碍药物治疗（MOUD）保留与提前中断的机器学习模型的算法公平性，发现即使整体性能可接受，模型仍存在亚组间性能差距，偏见缓解技术可缩小但无法完全消除差距且需权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOUD治疗中保留率与完成率持续偏低，促使研究者使用机器学习模型预测保留情况并识别提前中断风险患者；然而这些模型在不同患者群体间的公平性尚未被充分探究，引发其在治疗决策支持中应用的担忧。

Method: 使用横断面治疗事件数据集-出院记录（TEDS-D，涵盖2015—2019年美国出院个体的治疗事件），针对接受门诊MOUD的个体训练四个机器学习模型，预测提前中断治疗和保留超过180天。评估整体性能及按种族、族裔、年龄和性别划分的患者亚组错误率，辅以模型解释分析；进一步评估偏见缓解技术及其对公平性与预测性能的影响。

Result: ML模型在MOUD结局预测中即使整体预测性能看似可接受，仍可能表现出亚组层面的性能差距；偏见缓解技术可减少这些差距，但无法完全消除，且存在权衡。

Conclusion: 通过展示公平性感知评估与亚组性能透明报告的重要性，本研究为MOUD治疗场景中风险分层与护理优先级排序的ML模型的负责任、情境敏感使用提供了实践见解。

Abstract: Persistent low retention and completion rates in medications for opioid use disorder (MOUD) have driven the use of machine learning (ML) models to predict retention and identify patients at risk of premature discontinuation. However, the fairness of these models across patient populations remains largely unexplored, raising concerns about their application in treatment decision support. This study systematically assesses algorithmic fairness in ML models for predicting MOUD retention and premature discontinuation and investigates the effectiveness of bias mitigation techniques. Using the cross-sectional Treatment Episode Data Set-Discharges (TEDS-D), which includes treatment episodes for individuals in the U.S. discharged between 2015 and 2019, we trained four ML models to predict premature treatment discontinuation and retention beyond 180 days among individuals receiving outpatient MOUD. We evaluated overall performance and subgroup-level error rates across patient subgroups defined by race, ethnicity, age, and sex, complemented by model explanation analyses. We further assessed bias mitigation techniques and their effects on both fairness and predictive performance. Our findings demonstrate that ML models for MOUD outcome prediction can exhibit subgroup-level performance gaps even when overall predictive performance appears acceptable and that bias mitigation can reduce, but not fully eliminate, these gaps without trade-offs. By demonstrating the importance of fairness-aware evaluation and transparent reporting of subgroup performance, this study provides practical insights for the responsible and context-sensitive use of ML models for risk stratification and care prioritization in MOUD treatment settings.

</details>


### [27] [ZoAQ: Adaptive Zeroth-Order Querying via Query-Reuse Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22115)
*Yangyang Feng,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ZoAQ，一种基于查询复用的自适应零阶优化方法，可在不额外增加验证查询的前提下实现自适应查询分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化依赖函数评估估计更新，扰动查询是主要成本。固定预算每步使用相同查询数，效率低；自适应控制器虽可节省查询，但往往需额外调用oracle验证估计器可靠性，抵消了节省效果。

Method: ZoAQ围绕查询复用构建：不再在每步后丢弃历史评估，而是将其同时用于下一步更新和是否继续查询的决策，从而无需额外验证查询即可自适应分配查询预算。理论分析刻画了何种"一致性"信号能识别支持下降的更新，并引导控制器达到充分查询预算。

Result: 在合成目标上，相对固定基线在1.2M查询下减少43-48%查询；在黑盒攻击中，MNIST和CIFAR-10分别以平均320和625次查询达到100%成功率；在四个OPT微调设置中，相对固定K=4节省43-46%前向评估，任务内精度变化范围为-0.018至+0.010。

Conclusion: ZoAQ通过复用历史查询实现无额外验证开销的自适应查询分配，在合成优化、黑盒攻击和大模型微调任务中均显著降低查询/前向评估成本，同时保持效果。

Abstract: Zeroth-order optimization (ZOO) estimates updates from function evaluations, making perturbation queries a primary cost. Fixed budgets spend the same number of queries at every step, while adaptive controllers may offset their savings by using additional oracle calls to test estimator reliability. We introduce ZoAQ, an adaptive ZOO method built around query reuse. Rather than discarding past evaluations after each step, ZoAQ makes them useful for both the next update and the decision to query further. This enables adaptive query allocation without extra validation queries. Our analysis characterizes when this agreement identifies an update that supports descent and guides the controller to a sufficient query budget. On synthetic objectives, ZoAQ reduces queries by 43-48% relative to fixed baselines using 1.2M queries. In black-box attacks, it reaches 100% success with 320 and 625 average queries on MNIST and CIFAR-10, respectively. Across four OPT fine-tuning settings, ZoAQ saves 43-46% forward evaluations relative to fixed K=4, with accuracy changes within tasks ranging from -0.018 to +0.010.

</details>


### [28] [LE4Mob: Towards Inductive, Distance-Aware and General-Purpose Location Embedding for Human Mobility Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22117)
*Xinglei Wang,Stephen Law,Zichao Zeng,Junyuan Liu,Guangsheng Dong,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LE4Mob——一种归纳式、距离感知、地理衍生的位置嵌入框架，提升位置表征的可迁移性与空间关系保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置嵌入依赖移动性观测数据，无法表示未见过位置，且对地理距离的保持约束较弱，导致跨数据集、跨任务复用受限。

Method: LE4Mob扩展对比式语言-位置预训练，引入距离感知正则化目标，使嵌入空间保留空间关系；从地理上下文预训练，生成空间-语义信息，可归纳地为未见过位置生成嵌入，且不依赖下游任务监督。

Result: 在个体级下一位置预测与人口级通勤流生成任务上，跨多数据集和研究区域实验表明LE4Mob优于强基线，在归纳设置及下游模型直接依赖位置嵌入交互时优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 距离感知、地理衍生的位置表征可作为人类移动性建模的可复用基础。

Abstract: Location representations provide mobility models with fundamental information about the spatial position, functional characteristics, and relationships of places. However, existing embeddings are often dependent on mobility observations, unable to represent unseen locations, and weakly constrained to retain geographic distance. This limits their reuse across datasets and mobility tasks. To address these limitations, we propose LE4Mob, an inductive, distance-aware, and geography-derived location embedding framework for mobility modelling. LE4Mob extends contrastive language-location pre-training while introducing a distance-aware regularisation objective that encourages the embedding space to preserve spatial relationships. Pre-trained from geographic context, LE4Mob can encode rich spatial-semantic information and generate embeddings for unseen locations inductively. Its independence from downstream mobility task supervision also makes it transferable across different mobility tasks. We evaluate LE4Mob on individual-level next location prediction and population-level commuter flow generation. Experiments across multiple datasets and study areas show that LE4Mob outperforms strong baselines, with particular advantages in inductive settings and when downstream models rely directly on interactions between location embeddings. These findings demonstrate the potential of distance-aware, geography-derived location representations as reusable foundations for human mobility modelling.

</details>


### [29] [Success Leaves Detours: Learning Executable Walkthroughs for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22120)
*Kaijie Chen,Chenyu Fang,Liang Yan,Bo Li,Bo Zhang,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Trace 框架，从稀疏奖励轨迹中归纳出可执行的 Walkthrough Memory，将噪声轨迹编译为状态条件化、依赖一致、可验证的过程性知识，在多个基准上显著优于测试时学习与记忆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自进化智能体依赖复用历史经验，但稀疏奖励轨迹中充斥失败、循环与绕路，且常用摘要往往丢失执行所需的状态条件与动作依赖信息，导致经验难以直接复用。

Method: 将问题形式化为可执行 Walkthrough 归纳；先由奖励与持久状态变化检测进度锚点，传播信用以定位有价值的状态转移，并利用跨回合成功/失败证据估计动作前置条件；再通过反向依赖切片把所需事实回溯到其产生动作，抽取依赖一致的动作链，剔除无关循环与绕路，最终形成包含入口条件、有序状态—动作—效果步骤及完成/失败谓词的 Walkthrough Memory。

Result: 在 J-TTL、WebShop 与 ScienceWorld 上以三个开源 LLM 进行实验，Trace 持续优于八种测试时学习与记忆基线；相较最强基线，平均 AUC 与 Final-3 分别提升 30.0% 与 40.5%，同时使用更少的推理 token。

Conclusion: 长时程交互中，状态条件化的可执行过程比完整轨迹或抽象摘要更具复用价值，验证了依赖接地与信用引导相结合的经验归纳范式的有效性。

Abstract: Test-time self-evolving agents improve by reusing past experience, yet sparse-reward trajectories contain failures, loops, and detours, while summaries often omit the state conditions and action dependencies needed for execution. We study executable Walkthrough induction from sparse-reward trajectories: extracting compact, state-conditioned, and verifiable procedures. Our key observation is that delayed credit identifies actions associated with progress but cannot determine whether they produce facts required by later actions. We propose Trace, a credit-guided, dependency-grounded framework that compiles noisy trajectories into executable Walkthrough Memory. It detects progress anchors from rewards and persistent state changes, propagates credit to identify valuable transitions, and estimates action prerequisites from cross-episode success and failure evidence. Backward dependency slicing then traces required facts to their producers, extracting dependency-consistent action chains while removing irrelevant loops and detours. The resulting Walkthroughs encode entry conditions, ordered state--action--effect steps, and completion and failure predicates, supporting reuse, intermediate-state resumption, and programmatic verification. Experiments on J-TTL, WebShop, and ScienceWorld with three open-source LLMs show that Trace consistently outperforms eight test-time learning and memory baselines. Compared with the strongest baseline, it improves average AUC and Final-$3$ by $30.0%$ and $40.5%$, respectively, while using fewer inference tokens. These results show that long-horizon interaction benefits more from state-conditioned executable procedures than from complete trajectories or abstract summaries.

</details>


### [30] [Modelling daily activity patterns from mobile phone location data via deep representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22121)
*Xinglei Wang,Junyuan Liu,Guangsheng Dong,Zichao Zeng,Stephen Law,James Haworth,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自监督活动链编码器（ACE），将手机定位数据建模为「表示—聚类—解释」一体化流程，在无活动目的标签条件下学得日活动表示并聚类出伦敦六类工作日活动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动采集的手机定位数据虽能提供大规模、纵向的人类移动性观测，但无法直接揭示活动目的；访问地点的功能属性虽有情境价值，但在混合用途城市环境中其与活动目的的关系仍不确定。因此需要一种无需确定性活动目的标签、能融合时空与城市功能情境的方法来挖掘行为上有意义的活动模式。

Method: 作者将活动模式挖掘概念化为「表示—聚类—解释」三阶段一体化过程，并针对表示阶段提出活动链编码器（ACE）。ACE 为自监督模型：融合预训练城市嵌入（urban embeddings）与访问时序、停留时长，使用 Transformer 建模停留点的顺序组织；训练目标包括掩码活动建模（masked activity modelling）与身份引导对比学习（identity-guided contrastive learning），无需确定性活动目的标签。学习到的日表示被聚合成用户级画像，再行聚类，并通过时间—功能模式与基于普查（Census）的人口统计情境进行解释。

Result: 在伦敦的手机应用定位数据上应用该方法，并与三种代表性方法比较，ACE 支持识别出六类差异化的工作日活动模式群体，这些群体具有各自鲜明的日节律、城市功能情境与人口统计关联。

Conclusion: 时间—功能与人口统计等互补证据进一步支撑了基于实证的活动模式画像（personas）构建，为从无标签手机定位数据中提取行为上有意义的群体模式提供了完整路径。研究全流程源码已公开于 https://github.com/xlwang233/ACE。

Abstract: Passively collected mobile phone location data provide large-scale, longitudinal observations of human mobility but do not directly reveal activity purposes. The functional characteristics of visited locations offer useful contextual information, yet their relationship with activity purpose remains uncertain, particularly in mixed-use urban environments. We conceptualise activity pattern mining as an integrated process of representation, clustering, and interpretation, and propose the Activity Chain Encoder (ACE) for the representation stage. ACE is a self-supervised model that combines pre-trained urban embeddings with visit timing and duration and uses a Transformer to model the sequential organisation of stays. It is trained using masked activity modelling and identity-guided contrastive learning without requiring deterministic activity purpose labels. Learned daily representations are aggregated into user-level profiles, clustered, and interpreted through temporal-functional patterns and Census-derived demographic context. Applied to mobile phone app location data from London and compared with three representative methods, ACE supports the identification of six differentiated weekday activity-pattern groups characterised by distinct daily rhythms, urban functional contexts, and demographic associations. These complementary forms of evidence further support the development of empirically grounded activity-pattern personas, establishing a holistic route for deriving behaviourally meaningful population patterns from unlabelled mobile phone location data. The source code for the entire analytical pipeline developed in this study is publicly available at https://github.com/xlwang233/ACE.

</details>


### [31] [Rank Portability Does Not Imply Feasibility Portability: Target-Specific Evaluation of Joint Hardware Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22122)
*Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 跨设备硬件评估常假设架构排名可迁移，从而用代理设备支持目标侧模型选择。本文在 NAS-Bench-201 和 HW-GPT-Bench（10,000 个 GPT 架构、13 台设备）上检验该假设，发现排名可迁移性、可行性可迁移性与目标特定决策支持是三个不同的评估对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件评估中的代理设备假设（若架构排名跨设备一致，则代理设备可支持目标侧模型选择）在联合延迟-能耗可行性约束下缺乏严格检验。本文旨在压力测试该假设，区分排名可迁移性与可行性可迁移性。

Method: 在 NAS-Bench-201 上分析跨设备排名相关性与目标可比可行集重叠；使用忠实的 AdaProxy 诊断延迟排名改进；通过精确有限样本 split-conformal 分析阈值所需的校准观测数；在 HW-GPT-Bench 的 10,000 个 GPT 架构与 13 台设备上复现，以 RTX3080 为代理，测量目标延迟 SRCC 与代理复用违规风险。

Result: NAS-Bench-201 上跨设备排名相关性中等，可行集重叠不完整；AdaProxy 诊断显著改善延迟排名，表明边界失败并非仅由弱适配导致；单侧 90% 阈值至少需要九个校准观测。GPT 实验中，目标延迟 SRCC 为 0.951–0.996，但在匹配联合约束下代理复用违规风险高达 33.3%–100%。

Conclusion: 排名可迁移性、可行性可迁移性与目标特定决策支持是彼此不同的评估对象。跨设备评估应报告哪些目标环境真正支持所声称的工作点。

Abstract: Cross-device hardware evaluation often assumes that if architecture rankings transfer across devices, a proxy device can support target-side model selection. We stress-test this assumption for joint latency-energy feasibility across two public architecture families. On NAS-Bench-201, cross-device rank correlations are moderate, while target-comparable feasible-set overlap remains incomplete. A faithful AdaProxy diagnostic substantially improves latency ranking, showing that the observed boundary failures are not simply due to weak adaptation. Exact finite-sample split-conformal analysis also exposes an evidence bottleneck: a finite one-sided 90% threshold requires at least nine calibration observations. We then replicate the phenomenon on 10,000 GPT architectures across 13 HW-GPT-Bench devices. Relative to an RTX3080 proxy, target latency SRCC ranges from 0.951 to 0.996, yet proxy-reuse violation risk ranges from 33.3% to 100% under matched joint constraints. These results show that rank portability, feasibility portability, and target-specific decision support are distinct evaluation objects. Cross-device evaluations should therefore report which target environments actually support the operating point being claimed.

</details>


### [32] [StationPDE: Station-Oriented Surface PDE Learning for Multi-Station Multivariate Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22123)
*Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen,Rongwen Li,Hongwu Liu,Kun Fang,Zhuo Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StationPDE，一种面向气象站的表层PDE学习模型，用可解释的物理演化桥接离散站点数据与PDE天气建模的鸿沟，在多站多变量天气预报中平均降低约9.6%的MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多站预报模型（如数据驱动模型）仅学习离散站点间的统计依赖，缺乏显式的物理演化过程；而基于PDE的天气模型虽可提供可解释的物理动力学，却需要连续场和高空变量，而表层站点数据无法满足这些条件。因此需要一种能衔接二者的方法。

Method: StationPDE从离散站点观测中构建地形感知的连续表面场，并将其物理演化分解为地表风输送（显式演化可观测天气变量）与高空推断（用可学习水平扩散近似缺失高空变量的影响）；同时引入并行的数据驱动扩散分支捕捉补充运动模式，并通过自适应路由器融合两路预测，实现站级多变量预报。

Result: 在Weather2K和MeteoNet数据集上，StationPDE持续优于当前最先进的基线模型，相比最强基线平均降低约9.6%的MSE。

Conclusion: StationPDE通过向站点数据引入可解释的物理演化，有效桥接了离散站点观测与PDE建模之间的鸿沟，验证了物理与数据驱动融合在站级多变量天气预报中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Multi-station multivariate weather forecasting aims to forecast future weather variables at multiple weather stations from historical surface observations. Existing station forecasting models learn statistical dependencies among discrete stations, but lack explicit physical evolution. Meanwhile, PDE-based weather models provide interpretable physical dynamics, yet require continuous fields and upper-air variables unavailable in surface station data. To bridge this gap, we propose StationPDE, a station-oriented surface PDE learning model. StationPDE constructs a terrain-aware continuous surface field from discrete station observations and decomposes its physical evolution into surface wind transport and upper-air inference. Surface wind transport explicitly evolves observable weather variables, while upper-air inference uses learnable horizontal diffusion to approximate the missing influence of unavailable upper-air variables. A parallel data-driven diffusion branch captures complementary motion patterns, and an adaptive router integrates the two forecasts for station-level multivariate forecasting. Experiments on Weather2K and MeteoNet show that StationPDE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing MSE by about $9.6\%$ on average compared with the strongest baseline. Code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/hnu-vis/StationPDE.

</details>


### [33] [SolarFlowRefiner: Refinement-Aware Flow Matching for Surface Solar Radiation Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22126)
*Udbhav Srivastava,Antonita Racheal,Yiheng Chen,Runlong Yu,Xinyue Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SolarFlowRefiner，一种 refinement-aware 的流匹配框架，用于从粗分辨率 ERA5 辐射变量与卫星通道重建高分辨率地表太阳辐射（SSR），通过预测条件化精修与采样器反传实现生成与校正的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率 SSR 对光伏预测与电网调度至关重要，但物理一致的重分析产品（如 ERA5）分辨率过粗，难以刻画局地云致变率。单一 ERA5 格点内可能同时包含日照区与云影区，导致所需的高分辨率订正既空间锐利又本质歧义：单阶段预测器常过度平滑，而事后精修存在阶段错配——生成器独立优化，其输出却决定精修器的初始状态。

Method: 提出 SolarFlowRefiner：条件 FlowMatch 生成器先对上采样 ERA5 基线预测归一化订正；精修器在当前 FlowMatch 输出与目标残差之间的 prediction-conditioned 状态上训练，从而暴露于生成器的结构化误差；精修目标进一步通过 FlowMatch 采样器反传，使生成与校正针对最终重建结果联合优化。

Result: 在 day-blocked 的 ERA5–SolarCube 基准上，相较独立生成与事后精修两种基线均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: SolarFlowRefiner 为生成式预测器与迭代校正器的耦合提供了通用策略，可推广至更广泛的多源降尺度任务。

Abstract: High-resolution surface solar radiation (SSR) is important for solar forecasting and grid operation. However, physically consistent reanalysis products are too coarse to resolve localized cloud-driven variability. In this paper, we study a multisource downscaling task that reconstructs high-resolution SolarCube SSR fields from coarse ERA5 radiative variables and co-registered satellite channels. The task is challenging because a single ERA5 grid cell may contain both sunlit and cloud-shadowed regions. As a result, the missing high-resolution correction can be spatially sharp and inherently ambiguous. One-stage predictors often oversmooth these structures. Post-hoc refinement also introduces a stage-wise mismatch: the generator is optimized independently, even though its output determines the refiner's initial state. We introduce SolarFlowRefiner, a refinement-aware flow-matching framework for SSR downscaling. A conditional FlowMatch generator first predicts a normalized correction to an upsampled ERA5 baseline. The refiner is then trained on prediction-conditioned states between the current FlowMatch output and the target residual. This exposes the refiner to the structured errors produced by the generator. The refinement objective is also backpropagated through the FlowMatch sampler, allowing generation and correction to be jointly optimized for the final reconstruction. Experiments on a day-blocked ERA5--SolarCube benchmark show consistent improvements over standalone generation and post-hoc refinement. More broadly, SolarFlowRefiner provides a general strategy for coupling generative predictors with iterative correctors.

</details>


### [34] [Helix-FNO: Spectral-Domain Operator Learning Coupled with a High-Fidelity Mechanistic Model for Fast Surrogate Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22129)
*Jiabao Zhao,Chuwei Wang,Jinxi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Helix-FNO 的教师-学生架构：以32状态机理模型为教师、Fourier神经算子(FNO)为学生，学习解算子而非单一解，推理达毫秒级、比机理教师快三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全尺度处理过程的机理仿真模型是唯一可信且具外推性的物化动力学描述，但其运行时间过慢，无法支撑现代决策引擎在5分钟决策节奏下所需的数千次前向评估。常规的代理建模往往只学到单一配置下的单一解，难以泛化到新的进水剖面、控制设定或厂区布局。

Method: 提出 Helix-FNO 教师-学生架构：教师为32状态机理模型，提供输入场-解对的高保真数据集，并用拉丁超立方与基于不确定性的主动学习进行筛选，以覆盖边界与过载工况；学生为 Fourier 神经算子，在谱域中学习解算子本身而非某个具体解，从而由"学一个实例"转向"学一族方程"。文中给出算子形式化、谱卷积定义、加权蒸馏损失与主动学习准则，并分析了截断 Fourier 展开的近似误差与参数化解流形光滑性之间的关系。

Result: 算例研究在精度、数据集效率与推理时延上，将 Helix-FNO 与物理信息网络及数据驱动循环代理进行对比，并将各方法置于速度-精度 Pareto 前沿上。所得算子推理为毫秒级，比机理教师快三个数量级。

Conclusion: 该算子具备大规模候选筛选与在线决策支持所需的能力，是面向实时决策的可行代理方案。

Abstract: Mechanistic simulation models of full-scale treatment processes remain the only trustworthy, extrapolative description of the underlying physico-chemical dynamics, yet their runtime is far too slow to support the thousands of forward evaluations that a modern decision engine requires at a 5-minute decision cadence. The standard remedy-surrogate modelling-often produces a network that learns a single solution for a single configuration, so it generalises poorly to new influent profiles, control settings or plant layouts. This paper presents Helix-FNO, a teacher-student architecture that couples a thirty-two-state mechanistic teacher with a Fourier neural operator (FNO) student. The teacher supplies a high-fidelity dataset of input-field-to-solution pairs, curated by Latin-hypercube and uncertainty-based active learning to cover the boundary and overload regimes that matter in practice; the student learns, in the spectral domain, the solution operator itself rather than any single solution, thereby moving from learning one instance to learning an entire family of equations. We give the operator formulation, the spectral convolution definition, the weighted distillation loss and the active-learning criterion, and we analyse the approximation error of a truncated Fourier expansion with respect to the smoothness of the parametric solution manifold. An illustrative study compares Helix-FNO against a physics-informed network and a data-driven recurrent surrogate on accuracy, dataset efficiency and inference latency, and places the methods on a speed-accuracy Pareto front. The resulting operator is three orders of magnitude faster than the mechanistic teacher at millisecond inference, which is precisely the capability required for massive candidate screening and online decision support.

</details>


### [35] [Hierarchical Bayesian optimization of an aircraft-based multi-agent system-of-systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22130)
*Paul Saves,Thierry Lefebvre,Nathalie Bartoli,Jasper Bussemaker,Nikolaos Kalliatakis,Nabih Naeem,Prajwal Prakasha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层贝叶斯优化框架，利用高斯过程元建模处理SoS架构设计中的离散选择、条件依赖与异构变量，并在野火抑制多智能体航空系统案例中验证其搜索效率与鲁棒性优于传统代理优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂体系（SoS）架构设计依赖高保真多物理、多学科仿真，但其计算代价高且存在评估失败风险，直接优化困难；传统代理优化方法难以处理架构决策中的离散变量、条件依赖和异构设计变量。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯优化框架，采用高斯过程元建模（surrogate meta-modeling），对离散架构选择、条件依赖关系及异构设计变量进行分层表征与优化，以有限的仿真预算探索大规模、结构多样的设计空间。

Result: 与常规代理优化方法相比，分层表述提升了搜索效率与鲁棒性，能够在有限仿真预算下探索大规模且结构多样的设计空间；并在欧盟COLOSSUS项目中的野火抑制多智能体航空系统案例上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为SoS架构设计与模型探索提供了可扩展方法，是可迁移至航空、可持续出行与韧性系统设计的实践示范，也是分层贝叶斯优化应用于真实SoS问题的首批实际演示之一，兼具方法论与实践推进意义。

Abstract: Developing innovative system architectures increasingly relies on advanced modeling and optimization techniques to frame the architecting process and define the corresponding computational problems. For complex System-of-Systems (SoS), high-fidelity multiphysics and multidisciplinary simulations are essential for capturing detailed behaviors. However, their computational expense and the risk of evaluation failures make direct optimization challenging. To overcome these limitations, surrogate-based approaches, like Bayesian optimization, have emerged as effective tools for managing expensive, black-box simulation tasks. This work introduces a hierarchical Bayesian optimization framework that leverages Gaussian process meta-modeling to handle discrete architectural choices, conditional dependencies, and heterogeneous design variables inherent to SoS problems. Results show that the hierarchical formulation improves search efficiency and robustness compared to conventional surrogate-based methods, enabling the exploration of large and structurally diverse design spaces with limited simulation budgets.
  We apply the approach to an aircraft-based multi-agent system for wildfire suppression, a use case developed within the EU-funded COLOSSUS project that illustrates how SoS principles can coordinate heterogeneous aerial platforms with complementary roles, supporting both sustainable mobility and emergency response missions. Our framework provides a scalable methodology for SoS architecting and model exploration, offering transferable insights for applications in aviation, sustainable mobility, and resilience-oriented system design. By combining hierarchical representations with surrogate-based optimization, this work is among the first practical demonstrations of hierarchical Bayesian optimization applied to real-world SoS problems, advancing both methodology and practice.

</details>


### [36] [Weak Ties, Strong Signals: Efficient Training Data Detection in Diffusion LLMs via Independent Token Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22145)
*Hongyao Yu,Tianqu Zhuang,Ziyuan Xu,Hao Fang,Jiaxin Hong,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对扩散大语言模型（dLLM）在去噪过程中可能泄露训练数据的问题，提出独立令牌采样（ITS）框架，通过注意力导出的成对依赖代理近似CMI感知选择准则，并引入多样性促进策略，在有限查询预算下显著提升成员推断检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM在去噪时可能暴露敏感训练数据，但检测困难：dLLM缺乏因果架构高效的单次概率分解。现有方法依赖随机掩码获取可处理的令牌级检测信号，却无法控制掩码令牌间的依赖关系，导致非负结构性估计误差，掩盖微弱的记忆信号。

Method: 论文从理论上将令牌级近似的非负结构估计误差刻画为掩码令牌间累积条件互信息（CMI），据此提出可靠检测需选择内部依赖较弱的掩码令牌集。为避免直接估计令牌组合CMI的高昂代价，提出ITS框架：使用注意力导出的成对依赖代理近似CMI感知选择准则，并加入多样性促进策略提升采样轮次间的令牌覆盖率，从而聚合受依赖近似误差影响更小的令牌级信号。

Result: 在多个数据集上，ITS在不同模型和数据集上持续优于当前最优基线，在ArXiv数据集上AUC提升0.18，并在有限查询预算下保持强性能。

Conclusion: 通过控制掩码令牌间的依赖关系并提升令牌覆盖，ITS实现了查询高效且更可靠的dLLM训练数据使用检测，验证了弱内部依赖掩码选择对成员推断检测的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive models, yet they may expose sensitive training data during denoising. Detecting such usage is challenging because dLLMs lack the efficient one-pass probability decomposition of causal architectures. Existing methods rely on random masking to obtain tractable token-wise detection signals under limited query budgets, but fail to control dependencies among masked tokens. We demonstrate that this token-wise approximation introduces a non-negative structural estimation error, which is theoretically characterized by the cumulative conditional mutual information (CMI) among masked tokens and can obscure subtle memorization signals. This insight suggests that reliable detection requires masked token sets with weak internal dependency. To avoid the prohibitive cost of directly estimating CMI over token combinations, we propose \textit{Independent Token Sampling} (ITS), a query-efficient framework that uses an attention-derived pairwise dependency proxy to approximate the CMI-aware selection criterion. ITS further incorporates a diversity-promoting strategy to improve token coverage across sampling rounds, yielding aggregated token-wise signals that are less affected by dependency-induced approximation error. Experiments on multiple datasets show that ITS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across different models and datasets, achieving an AUC improvement of 0.18 on the ArXiv dataset while maintaining strong performance under limited query budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/Chrisqcwx/DLLM-MIA .

</details>


### [37] [GRRR: The Geometry of Reshaping, Rotation, and Routing in Decoder LLM post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22146)
*Jianing Qi,Hao Tang,Zhigang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究后训练如何改变大语言模型（LLM）的权重。作者在12条包含监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）的后训练链上，将每个权重更新表达在预训练权重的奇异值分解（SVD）框架中，并分解为对角、非对角和零空间三个几何分量。在数学评测套件上，移除对角分量通常仍能保留后训练的大部分收益，说明后训练增益主要由重构和扩展预训练路径承载，而非显著改变预训练模型的奇异值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解后训练相对于预训练究竟在权重层面改变了什么，是一个基础但尚未被充分刻画的问题。作者希望从几何角度解释后训练增益的来源：究竟是显著改变了预训练矩阵的奇异值，还是主要通过其他方式承载增益。

Method: 在12条后训练链（包含监督微调SFT和强化学习RL）中，将每个权重更新表示在预训练矩阵的SVD框架内，从而把更新分解为三个几何上不同的分量：对角分量（重塑奇异值）、非对角分量（旋转预训练输入方向与输出方向之间的耦合）以及零空间分量（在矩阵原始非零SVD核心之外进行路由）。随后在数学评测套件上评估各分量的作用。

Result: 在数学评测套件上，移除对角分量通常仍能保留后训练带来的大部分性能增益。

Conclusion: 后训练的增益主要由对预训练路径的重构与扩展所承载，而非显著改变预训练模型的奇异值。

Abstract: We study how post-training changes the weights of Large Language Models (LLMs) relative to their pretrained weights. Across 12 post-training chains with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), we express each weight update in the pretrained matrix's singular value decomposition (SVD) frame. This decomposition separates the changes of three geometrically distinct components: diagonal values, which reshapes singular values; off-diagonal values, which rotates the coupling between pretrained input and output directions; and null-space values, which routes outside the matrix's original nonzero SVD core. On a math evaluation suite, we find that removing the diagonal component usually preserves most of the gains from post-training. These results suggest that post-training gains are carried primarily by reconfiguring and extending pretrained pathways rather than by substantially changing singular values of pre-trained models.

</details>


### [38] [SafeTune: A Unified Faithful Library for Auditing and Repairing Safety Drift in Fine-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22153)
*Pratinav Seth,Saisab Sadhu,Anshul Kaushal,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeTune是一个开源的统一库，整合了四种缓解大语言模型微调后安全漂移的干预范式（事后权重恢复、安全约束微调、基于梯度的遗忘、推理时引导），并提供共享的可解释性、评估与部署工具，通过配置驱动的工作流和模块化注册机制支持方法扩展与受控比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对微调大语言模型（LLM）安全漂移（safety drift）的现有方法分散在互不兼容的实现、生命周期阶段和评估协议中，导致难以采用与横向比较，作者希望提供一个统一框架来解决这一碎片化问题。

Method: 提出SafeTune库，统一四种干预范式：事后权重恢复（post-hoc weight recovery）、安全约束微调（safety-constrained fine-tuning）、基于梯度的遗忘（gradient-based unlearning）、推理时引导（inference-time steering）；配套共享的可解释性、评估和部署工具；采用配置驱动的一致工作流，同时保留各范式所需的特有输入和干预点；通过模块化注册表（modular registry）支持新增方法、基准、评判器、模型和微调领域。

Result: 通过受控对比实验（controlled comparisons）以及金融和医疗领域的部署案例研究，展示了SafeTune能够刻画安全漂移、在常见拒答行为与能力评估上评价可行干预，并支持校准式或分层式缓解。

Conclusion: SafeTune为LLM微调后的安全漂移问题提供了一个统一、可扩展、可比较的开源解决方案，便于方法采纳、评估与在真实领域的部署。

Abstract: Methods for addressing safety drift in fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) are scattered across incompatible implementations, lifecycle stages, and evaluation protocols, making them difficult to adopt and compare. We introduce SafeTune, a source-available library that unifies four intervention paradigms: post-hoc weight recovery, safety-constrained fine-tuning, gradient-based unlearning, and inference-time steering, alongside shared interpretability, evaluation, and deployment utilities. SafeTune provides a consistent configuration-driven workflow while preserving the distinct inputs and intervention points each paradigm requires. Its modular registry supports new methods, benchmarks, judges, models, and fine-tuning domains without redesigning the surrounding pipeline. We demonstrate SafeTune through controlled comparisons and finance and medical deployment case studies, showing how it characterizes safety drift, evaluates feasible interventions on common refusal-behavior and capability evaluations, and supports calibrated or layered mitigation.

</details>


### [39] [A Comparative Framework for Evaluating Foundation Models on Tabular Data: A Case Study in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22154)
*Majid Lotfian Delouee,Sjors G. J. G. In 't Veld,Martijn C. Schut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个面向临床表格基础模型（TFM）的比较评估框架，从泛化性、隐私保护、数据需求、可扩展性、可解释性和公平性六个维度对模型进行打分与排序，并通过缺铁筛查和心衰预测两个案例展示不同临床场景下模型排名差异，同时给出45个TFM的架构分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中表格数据（实验室结果、用药记录、诊断编码、人口统计学信息）最为常见，而表格基础模型数量与种类迅速增长，导致临床医生和数据科学家难以判断针对特定任务应选择哪个模型、依据何在。现有综述多罗列模型能力，缺少将模型与实际应用需求对照的结构化比较方法。

Method: 提出名为\system{}的比较评估框架，在六个具有临床意义的维度上对TFM打分与排序：新数据集泛化能力、患者隐私保护、性能所需数据量、随数据集与特征空间增长的扩展性、对临床医生的可解释性、跨患者亚组的公平性。每个维度细分为可测量子项，子项可组合为附加复合分数（super-metrics，超级指标），以同时诊断模型在多个维度上的表现。框架应用于两个医疗用例：缺铁筛查与心衰预测。同时构建45个TFM按底层架构组织的分类体系。

Result: 在缺铁筛查与心衰预测两个用例中应用同一套指标得到的模型排名不同，表明排序结果取决于各临床场景中最被看重的维度；验证了框架的实用性，并提供了覆盖45个TFM的架构分类作为领域参考。

Conclusion: 该框架为表格基础模型提供了结构化、面向临床需求的比较评估手段，帮助研究者和实践者依据具体应用的价值取向选择模型，其架构分类体系亦有助于导航这一快速扩展的领域。

Abstract: Tabular data is the most common format in clinical practice, encompassing laboratory results, medication records, diagnostic codes, and patient demographics. As foundation models for tabular data have grown in number and variety, a practical question has become harder to answer: which model should a clinician or data scientist actually choose for a given task, and why? Existing surveys catalogue what these models can do, but they stop short of providing a structured way to compare them against the specific demands of a real application. We introduce \system{}, a comparative evaluation framework that scores and ranks tabular foundation models (TFMs) across six clinically meaningful dimensions: how well a model generalizes to new datasets, how effectively it protects patient privacy, how much data it needs to perform well, how it scales with growing datasets and feature spaces, how interpretable its predictions are to clinicians, and how fairly it performs across patient subgroups. Each dimension is broken down into measurable sub-components, and groups of sub-components can optionally be combined into supplementary compound scores, called super-metrics, that provide a diagnostic view of how a model performs across several dimensions simultaneously. To show how the framework works in practice, we apply it to two healthcare use cases, screening for iron deficiency and predicting heart failure, demonstrating how the same set of metrics leads to different model rankings depending on what matters most in each clinical context. We also provide a taxonomy of 45 TFMs organized by their underlying architecture, which serves as a reference for researchers and practitioners looking to navigate this rapidly expanding field.

</details>


### [40] [From Latent Biomarkers to Clinical Rules: Embedding-Guided Rule Mining and Attribution-Based Translation for Interpretable Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22155)
*Majid Lotfian Delouee,Hamed Ayoobi,Sjors G. J. G. In 't Veld,Martijn C. Schut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个四步流程，在FT-Transformer潜在空间中挖掘决策规则并翻译回可测量的临床特征，在六个临床/人群健康数据集上评估，翻译后的规则在五/六个数据集上优于原始特征规则，但存在翻译损失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持工具需要既准确又可被理解，基于规则模型可提供透明性，但直接从原始临床测量推导的规则可能遗漏多变量间交互产生的模式。

Method: 四步流程：将能持续区分患者群体的嵌入维度视为潜在生物标志物；用小决策树挖掘规则；通过梯度-输入显著性与CLS注意力归因翻译选定的规则；在六个公开临床和人群健康数据集、四种嵌入维度上评估。

Result: 翻译后规则在六个数据集中五个优于原始特征规则，平均AUROC提升0.04–0.23；心脏病数据集上嵌入空间规则达0.98 AUROC，但翻译后降至0.72，说明高性能潜在规则并不总能被简单原始特征条件表示。

Conclusion: 潜在空间规则发现能揭示预测性模式，同时将其翻译为临床可测量特征以供临床医生评估，但翻译过程中的可解释性-性能权衡仍需关注。

Abstract: Clinical decision support tools are most useful when accurate predictions are accompanied by understandable explanations. Rule-based models provide transparency, but rules derived directly from raw clinical measurements may miss patterns arising from interactions between multiple variables. We present a four-step pipeline that mines decision rules in the latent space of an FT-Transformer and translates them back into measurable clinical features. Embedding dimensions that consistently separate patient groups are treated as latent biomarkers, rules are mined using small decision trees, and selected rules are translated using gradient-input saliency and CLS attention attribution. We evaluate the framework on six public clinical and population health datasets at four embedding dimensions. Translated rules outperformed raw-feature rules in five of six datasets, with mean AUROC gains ranging from 0.04 to 0.23. On the heart disease dataset, embedding-space rules reached 0.98 AUROC, but translation reduced this to 0.72, showing that high-performing latent rules cannot always be represented by simple raw-feature conditions. These results show that latent-space rule discovery can uncover predictive patterns while translating them into clinically measurable features that can be evaluated by clinicians.

</details>


### [41] [The Limits of Speculation: Bounding Speculative Decoding in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22156)
*Aidar Amankulov,Denis Mamatin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将MoE模型中的推测解码预算选择建模为离线随机最短路径（SSP）问题，通过反事实模拟构建诊断性Oracle，发现被拒绝的候选在边际增量空间（Delta Space）中形成严格线性边界，表明全局优化在局部受"边际成本对期望进展"的必要条件支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型的推测解码存在由输入相关的专家加载导致的验证成本不稳定的问题，现有方法缺乏对这一机制的理论刻画与诊断手段。

Method: 将推测预算选择形式化为参考序列上的离线随机最短路径（SSP）问题；构建使用反事实模拟来刻画MoE验证成本的诊断性Oracle；在Qwen3-Coder与EAGLE-3配对上进行决策分析，并在边际增量空间（Delta Space）中考察拒绝候选的分布。

Result: 被拒绝的候选在Delta Space中形成严格的线性边界，说明复杂的全局优化在局部由一个平衡边际成本与期望进展（ΔE[Cost]/ΔE[a]）的必要条件所支配。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解MoE推测解码的验证成本物理机制提供了严格的数学参考点，并为未来设计自适应在线启发式方法奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Speculative decoding in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models faces the problem of unstable verification cost caused by input-dependent expert loading. To study the physics of this process, we formulate speculation-budget selection as an offline Stochastic Shortest Path (SSP) problem over reference sequences and build a diagnostic Oracle that uses counterfactual simulation to account for MoE verification cost. A detailed analysis of the Oracle's decisions on the Qwen3-Coder and EAGLE-3 pairing, in the space of marginal deltas (Delta Space), shows that rejected candidates form a strict linear boundary. This result demonstrates that a complex global optimization is locally governed by a necessary condition balancing marginal cost against expected progress ($\frac{Δ\mathbb{E}[Cost]}{Δ\mathbb{E}[a]}$), providing a rigorous mathematical reference point for designing future adaptive online heuristics.

</details>


### [42] [PAGE: Partition-Aware Gated KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22157)
*Pankaj Kumar,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将KV-cache驱逐重定义为逐输入的"准入决策"，提出无标签指标PAGE：用prefill注意力中成对top-k头一致性的early-to-late下降量预测输入是否属于"容量受限类"，仅在下降大时启用驱逐器，否则保留全缓存，从而把容量受限场景的危害率从0.75降到0.026。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-cache驱逐方法只决定"保留哪些token"，而不判断"是否应该驱逐"。这导致基准均值掩盖了一类输入：在这些输入上压缩会把准确率从99%打到0%。因此需要一种逐输入、无需标签的安全判断机制。

Method: 把驱逐重构为逐输入准入决策，并发现输入可分为两类：容量受限类（任何预算下驱逐都是灾难性的）和稀释倾向类（驱逐安全甚至有益）。提出单一无标签标量——prefill注意力中成对top-k头一致性的early-to-late下降量，可在解码前预测类别。PAGE对该下降量设阈值：下降大时启用任意基础驱逐器，否则保留完整缓存；无需训练、无需准确率标签。跨四种架构族该下降量对输入按驱逐安全性排序一致；约100条无标签输入的per-model pilot可为新架构族重新校准阈值。

Result: 作为安全阀，PAGE在容量受限区间把危害率从0.75降至0.026（29倍降幅），覆盖四种驱逐器、四个模型、两个基准，把99%到0%的崩塌变为稳定的89%，且无需重训驱逐器。在驱逐本已安全处，该门控不产生影响；其保护的容量受限类仅占输入中很小且可识别的少数，因此收益是定向安全增益而非平均增益。PAGE是逐输入安全阀而非压缩器：实际压缩率1.8–3.4×（均值2.9×），而标称预算为16×，静态分配下到batch 16时趋近1×；在同等内存下训练过的驱逐器更优。

Conclusion: 驱逐应按输入做准入决策而非默认执行；基于prefill注意力的无标签标量可提前识别驱逐不安全输入。PAGE以零训练、零标签方式实现高倍危害削减，但它是安全阀而非压缩方案，压缩率远低于标称预算，且在匹配内存条件下训练型驱逐器占优。

Abstract: KV-cache eviction methods decide which tokens to keep but not whether to evict at all, so a benchmark mean can hide a class of inputs on which compression drives accuracy from 99\% to 0\%. We reframe eviction as a per-input admission decision and show that inputs separate into a capacity-bound class, where eviction is catastrophic at every budget, and a dilution-prone class, where eviction is safe or beneficial. A single label-free scalar computed from prefill attention, the early-to-late drop in pairwise top-$k$ head agreement, predicts this class before any decoding. PAGE thresholds this drop: it applies any base evictor when the drop is large and retains the full cache otherwise, with no training and no accuracy labels. The drop orders inputs by eviction safety consistently across four architecture families, and a per-model unlabeled pilot of about 100 inputs recalibrates the threshold for a new family. Used as a safeguard, PAGE cuts the harm rate on the capacity-bound regime from 0.75 to 0.026, a 29 $\times$ reduction, across four evictors, four models, and two benchmarks, turning a 99\% to 0\% collapse into a flat 89\% without retraining the evictor. The gate is inert wherever eviction is already safe, and the capacity-bound class it protects is a small, identifiable minority of inputs, so the benefit is a targeted safety gain rather than an average one. PAGE is a per-input safeguard, not a compressor: realized compression is $1.8 - 3.4 \times$ (mean 2.9$\times$) against a nominal 16$\times$ budget and decays toward unity by batch 16 under static provisioning, and a trained evictor wins at matched memory.

</details>


### [43] [StepKV: Step-Aware KV Cache Compression for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22158)
*Boyu Feng,Jiahong Liu,Yifan Li,Wenhao Yu,Zexuan Qiu,Yuliang Sun,Ming Shen,Xiang Li,Quanyu Dai,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多步LLM智能体的KV缓存压缩，提出以推理步为保留单元的StepKV方法，联合步级效用与令牌级显著性进行全局排序剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存随上下文线性增长，增加存储与解码开销；多步智能体中查询扩展为推理、工具交互与检索观察的轨迹。现有剪枝方法将缓存视为扁平令牌流，按时间近因或注意力显著性排序，导致"压缩单元"与"推理单元"不匹配：令牌级剪枝删除单个条目，而多步智能体中的有用信息常以推理步为单位组织，重要性不均且延迟显现。早期观察或中间决策近期注意力低但对后续证据整合仍关键，作者称之为"推理连续性破坏"（Reasoning Continuity Disruption）。

Method: StepKV将推理步视为一等保留单元：将缓存条目与其生成步关联，从轨迹派生信号估计步级效用，并将该效用与令牌级显著性结合；据此对可剪枝令牌全局排序，在目标预算下保留得分最高的条目。

Result: 在多跳问答与长时程网页推理任务上，StepKV在低KV预算下能维持准确率，而令牌级基线方法准确率急剧下降。

Conclusion: StepKV为智能体KV缓存压缩提供了以步为中心的视角，在效率-准确率权衡上更稳健，适合多步智能体推理。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching is essential for efficient autoregressive large language model (LLM) inference, but the cache grows linearly with context length, increasing storage and decoding costs. KV cache compression mitigates this cost by retaining only a subset of cached tokens. This challenge is particularly important for multi-step LLM agents, where a query expands into trajectories of reasoning, tool interactions, and retrieved observations. Existing pruning methods typically treat the cache as a flat token stream and rank tokens by recency or attention saliency. This creates a mismatch between the unit of compression and the unit of reasoning: token-level pruning removes individual entries, whereas useful information in multi-step agents is often organized into reasoning steps with uneven and delayed importance. Consequently, an early observation or intermediate decision may receive little recent attention yet remain essential for later evidence synthesis. We term this failure mode Reasoning Continuity Disruption.These observations motivate KV cache compression that jointly considers token- and reasoning-step-level information. StepKV addresses this goal by treating reasoning steps as first-class retention units. It associates cache entries with their generating steps, estimates step utility from trajectory-derived signals, and combines this utility with token-level saliency. The resulting scores globally rank prunable tokens, from which StepKV retains the top-scoring entries under a target budget. StepKV thus provides a step-centric perspective for agent KV cache compression. Across multi-hop QA and long-horizon web reasoning tasks, StepKV sustains accuracy under low KV budgets where token-level baselines degrade sharply, offering a more robust efficiency-accuracy trade-off for multi-step agent inference.

</details>

*Davide Frizzo,Francesco Borsatti,Gian Antonio Susto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合深度学习序列模型与同时分位数回归（SQR）的预测性维护框架，用于不确定性感知的RUL估计与风险感知维护决策，并在PHM18离子铣削数据上验证，S4D表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造各组件高度互联，单一故障可能中断整条产线，因此需尽早识别高风险资产；同时维护规划必须权衡意外故障与过早中断设备寿命之间的矛盾。

Method: 构建结合深度学习序列模型与同时分位数回归（SQR）的预测性维护框架，实现不确定性感知的RUL估计；在2018 PHM数据挑战赛（PHM18）离子铣削数据上比较多种架构，包括基于状态空间模型（SSM）的架构，并采用预测指标与业务指标（意外故障UB、未利用寿命UL及成本加权目标）进行评价。

Result: 对角状态空间模型（S4D）在各分位数上给出最佳RUL估计；相较于预防性维护（PvM）基线，能避免系统性过早干预，显著降低业务成本。

Conclusion: 结果表明，不确定性感知、成本敏感的维护规划在半导体生产中具有可行性与实用价值。

Abstract: Semiconductor manufacturing relies on tightly interconnected components, so early identification of the assets most likely to fail is essential to prevent a single breakdown from disrupting the entire production pipeline. Maintenance planning must therefore balance unexpected failures against prematurely interrupted operating life. We present a Predictive Maintenance (PdM) framework combining Deep Learning (DL) sequence models and Simoultaneous Quantile Regression (SQR)
  for uncertainty-aware Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimation and risk-aware maintenance decisions. Several architectures are compared on ion-milling data from the 2018 PHM Data Challenge (PHM18), including architectures based on State Space Models (SSM), using prediction and business metrics: Unexpected Breaks (UB), Unexploited Lifetime (UL), and a cost-weighted objective. Diagonal State Spaces (S4D) delivers the best Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimates across quantiles and, relative to Preventive Maintenance (PvM) baselines, substantially lowers business cost by avoiding systematically early interventions. The results support uncertainty-aware, cost-sensitive maintenance planning in semiconductor production.

</details>


### [45] [TARGet: Topology-Aware Fusion-based Radio Frequency Circuit Functional Modeling using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22165)
*Soroosh Noorzad,Sebastian Bodero,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开源、拓扑感知的射频电路建模方法TARGet，通过子电路S参数表示与GNN融合架构实现跨拓扑建模，将训练数据需求降低最多35.5倍，预测误差低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法用于模拟与射频电路自动综合时存在三大局限：每更换一种电路拓扑（哪怕仅增删单个元件）都需重新训练数据；忽略电路拓扑信息，难以刻画复杂元件交互；依赖全连接网络与扁平特征表示，导致训练数据需求量大。因此需要一种拓扑感知、数据高效且可跨拓扑复用的建模方法。

Method: TARGet从两个层次建模电路：子电路层与整体拓扑层。子电路层采用S参数表示刻画子电路行为，而非依赖单个元件，从而为射频功能块提供可复用的行为抽象；同时显式引入电路拓扑信息以支持跨多种拓扑学习；并提出一种融合架构，将图神经网络（GNN）与子电路连接感知神经网络相结合，以提升数据效率。

Result: 在多种射频电路拓扑上的实验表明，TARGet预测误差低于1%，相比SOTA方法所需训练数据最多减少35.5倍；在严格1%误差阈值下，预测精度比SOTA高9.7倍。在留出的匹配网络评估中，TARGet展现出对未见子电路拓扑的零样本迁移能力，NMAE最多降低45%。

Conclusion: TARGet通过子电路级S参数抽象与拓扑感知的GNN融合架构，实现了高精度、数据高效且可跨拓扑及零样本迁移的射频电路建模，为模拟/RF电路自动综合提供了有效支撑。

Abstract: Automatic synthesis of analog and Radio Frequency (RF) circuits is an emerging area that requires an efficient circuit modeling method. In recent years, Machine Learning (ML) solutions have played a promising role in this regard. However, many existing ML approaches require separate training data for each circuit topology, even when a single circuit component is added or removed. In addition, they overlook circuit topology information, which limits their ability to capture complex component interactions. Furthermore, they rely on fully connected neural networks with flat feature representations, which require substantial amounts of training data. In this work, we propose an open-source topology-aware RF circuit modeling method, TARGet. Our model considers the circuit at two levels: sub-circuits and the overall circuit topology. At the sub-circuit level, TARGet leverages S-parameter representations to capture sub-circuit behavior rather than relying on individual circuit components, providing a reusable behavioral abstraction for RF building blocks. Moreover, TARGet explicitly incorporates circuit topology information into the model, enabling it to learn across multiple topologies. TARGet introduces a novel fusion-based architecture that integrates Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and sub-circuit connectivity-aware neural networks to improve data efficiency. Experimental evaluation across multiple RF circuit topologies demonstrates that TARGet achieves sub-1% prediction error while reducing the required training data by up to 35.5x compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches. Furthermore, TARGet achieves 9.7x higher prediction accuracy under a strict 1% error threshold relative to SOTA models. A held-out matching-network evaluation further demonstrates zero-shot transfer to an unseen sub-circuit topology, where TARGet reduces NMAE by up to 45%.

</details>


### [46] [Can Coding Agents Reproduce Official Statistics? Metadata, Retry Budget and the Limits of Execution Feedback in a Controlled Eurostat Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22222)
*Sabina-Cristiana Necula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在30个Eurostat统计编码任务上比较四种条件（纯任务、加元数据卡、加执行反馈修复循环、加同等重试预算但无诊断），共360次任务运行；结果显示元数据与重试预算提升可复现性，而执行诊断本身贡献有限，且评测契约设计的影响可能超过智能体误差本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能生成可执行的数据分析代码，但"代码成功运行"并不等于"得到有效的官方统计结果"。作者希望检验权威元数据与执行反馈能否提升编码智能体对Eurostat答案的可复现性，并厘清执行反馈究竟贡献了什么。

Method: 构建覆盖7个领域、7个Eurostat数据集、4个难度等级的30个自然语言任务基准，在四种条件下运行：A仅任务；B任务+冻结数据集元数据卡；C元数据+基于净化执行反馈的修复循环；D元数据+相同尝试预算但无任何诊断。由Claude Sonnet 5通过Anthropic Messages API生成Python代码，三次独立重复，共360次任务运行。正确性判定要求执行成功、数据集正确、过滤条件、输出形状、数值和单位均正确。另设一个输出契约欠明确的对照实验（未告知所需排序键与单位表示形式）。

Result: 执行诊断本身带来的增益有限，而元数据与重试预算对可复现性更为关键；在欠明确输出契约的对照实验中，条件C的表现被低估了23.4个百分点，说明评测器与契约设计对测得智能体误差的影响可能占主导地位。

Conclusion: 可靠的统计编码智能体需要针对冻结规范的语义校验、完全明确的输出契约以及重试预算，而非执行诊断。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable data-analysis code, but successful execution is not equivalent to a valid official-statistics result. This study asks whether authoritative metadata and execution feedback improve the reproducibility of Eurostat answers produced by a coding agent, and isolates what execution feedback actually contributes. A benchmark of 30 natural-language tasks covering seven domains, seven Eurostat datasets and four difficulty tiers was run under four conditions: task only (A), task plus a frozen dataset metadata card (B), metadata plus a repair loop driven by sanitized execution feedback (C), and metadata plus the same attempt budget with no diagnostics of any kind (D). Claude Sonnet 5 generated Python through the Anthropic Messages API in three independent replicates, yielding 360 task-runs. Exact correctness required successful execution, the correct dataset, filters, output shape, values and unit. A companion experiment run under an under-specified output contract, in which the required ranking key and unit representation were never stated to the model, understated condition C by 23.4 points, showing that evaluator and contract design can dominate measured agent error. Reliable statistical coding agents need semantic validation against frozen specifications, a fully specified output contract, and a retry budget - not execution diagnostics.

</details>


### [47] [Clustering-Based Collective Anomaly Detection in IoT Systems: A Graph Neural Network Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22166)
*Dalila Khettaf,Djamel Djenouri,Zeinab Rezaeifar,Youcef Djenouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督图集体异常检测框架UGCAD，基于变分图自编码器（VGAE）学习图表示并结合聚类算法，在无标签、无组别先验知识条件下检测IoT网络流量中的集体异常，在CICIoT2023和ToN-IoT数据集上验证了有效性并优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备大规模部署导致网络流量激增，攻击面扩大，对异常检测需求上升。然而现有异常检测研究主要聚焦于点异常（point anomaly），集体异常（collective anomaly）检测在文献中相对被忽视，且多数方法依赖组标签或成员先验知识，难以适用于无监督场景。

Method: 提出UGCAD框架：直接作用于图结构数据，无需组标签或成员关系先验；利用变分图自编码器（VGAE）学习图表示，并以此增强聚类算法实现节点有效分组；检测阶段先聚合与精炼被判定为正常的簇，再应用异常分数识别集体异常。

Result: 在CICIoT2023和ToN-IoT网络数据集上的大量实验表明，UGCAD在聚类与集体异常检测（CAD）两方面均有效；与多种传统及最先进的基于聚类的CAD方法对比，UGCAD在集体异常检测准确性上具有优越性。

Conclusion: UGCAD为IoT网络流量中的无监督集体异常检测提供了可行框架，弥补了集体异常研究不足的空白，并通过实验证明其相较现有聚类式CAD方法的性能优势。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Internet of Things (IoT) technology has led to the widespread deployment of smart, interconnected devices across a range of domains. However, this expansion has also resulted in a substantial increase in network traffic, creating more opportunities for malicious actors to launch cyberattacks and compromise sensitive information, thereby increasing the need for effective anomaly detection. The state-of-the-art in anomaly detection has predominantly focused on point anomalies. In contrast, the detection of collective anomalies remains relatively under-explored in the literature. In this paper, we introduce Unsupervised Graph Collective Anomaly Detection (UGCAD), a novel frame- work designed to identify collective anomalies in IoT network traffic. Unlike many existing methods, UGCAD operates on graph-structured data without any prior knowledge of group labels or membership. It leverages a variational graph autoencoder (VGAE) to learn the graph representation, which is subsequently used to enhance a clustering algorithm for effective grouping of nodes. To detect collective anomalies, clusters identified as normal are first aggregated and refined, after which anomaly scores are applied to detect collective anomalies. Extensive experiments conducted on the CICIoT2023 and ToN-IoT network datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of UGCAD in both clustering and collective anomaly detection (CAD). Furthermore, comparative evaluations against several traditional and state-of-the-art clustering-based CAD approaches confirm the superiority of UGCAD in accurately detecting collective anomalies.

</details>


### [48] [Role-Aware Morgan Fingerprints for Reaction Yield Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22167)
*Chinmay Mirji,Prashant Shekhar,Foram Madiyar,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MFP方法，通过角色感知的Morgan指纹将各反应组分聚合为固定长度描述符，配合前馈神经网络预测反应产率，在Suzuki-Miyaura和Buchwald-Hartwig基准上达到R²=0.878和0.969，训练速度比图神经网络和Transformer方法快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从分子结构和反应条件预测反应产率可减少实验试错、加速合成化学条件筛选。现有方法（图神经网络、Transformer编码器处理反应SMILES）存在预处理开销大、输入格式不一致时易失效的问题。

Method: MFP基于角色感知的Morgan指纹：对每个反应组分计算基于计数的环形指纹，按化学角色（反应物、试剂、产物）聚合，并结合对转化敏感的差异特征，构成固定长度反应描述符，输入前馈神经回归器。在两数据集上采用统一预处理与评估协议，与YieldBERT（含/不含数据增强）和GNAN对比，并进行指纹半径与折叠向量长度的消融实验及复杂度分析。

Result: MFP在Suzuki-Miyaura上R²=0.878，Buchwald-Hartwig上R²=0.969，训练速度比图/Transformer方法快一个数量级。复杂度分析表明MFP将全部表示开销折叠进一次性预处理，消除了图方法的逐epoch消息传递开销。消融显示半径2、nBits=2048在准确率、速度和跨划分稳定性间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: MFP是一个有效、可复现且高效的反应产率预测基线方法。

Abstract: Predicting reaction yield from molecular structure and reaction context can cut experimental trial-and-error and speed up condition screening in synthetic chemistry. Recent methods for this task use learned representations such as graph neural networks or Transformer encoders over reaction SMILES (Simplified Molecular Input Line Entry System), but these approaches carry heavy preprocessing overhead and can break when input formatting is inconsistent. We propose MFP, a reaction yield prediction method built on role-aware Morgan fingerprints where count-based circular fingerprints are computed for each reaction component, aggregated by chemical role (reactant, reagent, product), and combined with transformation-sensitive difference features into a fixed-length reaction descriptor fed to a feed-forward neural regressor. We test MFP against state of the art methods such as YieldBERT (with and without data augmentation) and GNAN (graph neural network) on the Suzuki-Miyaura and Buchwald-Hartwig benchmarks using a shared preprocessing and evaluation protocol. MFP reaches R2 = 0.878 on Suzuki-Miyaura and R2 = 0.969 on Buchwald-Hartwig while training an order of magnitude faster than graph- or Transformer-based alternatives. A formal complexity analysis confirms that MFP folds all representation cost into a one-time preprocessing step, removing the per-epoch message-passing overhead that graph methods carry. An ablation over fingerprint radius and folded vector length shows that radius-2 representations at nBits =2048 give the best balance of accuracy, speed, and cross-split stability on both datasets. These results establish MFP as an effective, reproducible, and efficient baseline for reaction yield prediction.

</details>


### [49] [Multiple latent orderings better predict language model preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22170)
*Aviral Chawla,William H. W. Thompson,Jean-Gabriel Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将LLM偏好中的非传递性解释为多个潜在一致的偏好序的聚合，提出噪声增强的混合Bradley-Terry模型从重复成对比较中推断潜在偏好成分，在七个模型四个任务上验证混合序比单一效用模型更好地解释结构性不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常被要求进行价值判断与选择，其观察到选择常呈现非传递性（A优于B、B优于C却C优于A）。现有工作将这类不一致视为单一潜在序周围的采样噪声，该假设掩盖了偏好结构本身可能的多重性，需要新的建模范式来解释观察到的结构不一致。

Method: 首先论证在任何单调链接函数下，单一序都无法解释观察到的非传递性；随后提出噪声增强的混合Bradley-Terry（MBT）模型，从重复成对比较中推断潜在偏好成分（多个内部一致的序）。在七个模型和四个任务上进行评估，并以Moral Machine困境为案例研究分析成分共享性。

Result: 在七个模型与四个任务上，序的混合比单一效用模型更能解释结构性不一致；聚合偏好常掩盖底层偏好异质性；Moral Machine案例显示在聚合序上不一致的模型仍可共享潜在成分。这表明LLM偏好呈现复数性（plural preferences）。

Conclusion: LLM的偏好应被视为多个连贯序的聚合而非单一函数。将LLM偏好当作单一函数的对齐与评估流程，可能在来自不同用户、本可分别认可的连贯偏好序之间进行平均，从而带来风险，需采用尊重偏好异质性的建模范式。

Abstract: Language models are frequently employed in settings where they are asked to make value judgments and choices. These observed choices often exhibit intransitivity: A model may prefer item $A$ to $B$ and $B$ to $C$, while also preferring $C$ to $A$. Existing work that models LLM preferences treats such inconsistencies as sampling noise around a single latent ordering. We instead propose that intransitivity reflects the aggregation of multiple latent, internally consistent orderings. We first show that observed inconsistencies cannot be explained by a single ordering under any monotone link function. We then introduce a noise-augmented mixture Bradley-Terry (MBT) model that infers latent preference components from repeated pairwise comparisons. Across seven models and four tasks, a mixture of orderings often explains structural inconsistencies better than single-utility models. We find that aggregate preferences often hide underlying preference heterogeneity. A case study on Moral Machine dilemmas shows that models which disagree on aggregate orderings can still share latent components. Together, these results suggest that LLMs reflect plural preferences. Alignment and evaluation pipelines that treat LLM preferences as a single function, therefore, risk averaging over coherent orderings that different users may endorse differently.

</details>


### [50] [Industrial Kinematic Trajectory Model (IKTM): Coordinate-Free Autoregressive Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22173)
*Max Amiri,David Eyers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IKTM——一种无坐标的工业车辆轨迹生成模型，以运动学序列（速度与航向变化）表示运动，在单站点训练、三站点零样本评估中取得低JS散度并显著优于先前马尔可夫模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹模型依赖绝对坐标、路网token或语义区域等站点相关表示，难以适配港口、矿区、机场等非结构化工业地形，缺乏可跨站点迁移的通用轨迹生成方法。

Method: IKTM采用自回归因果Transformer并结合概率混合头，在先前无坐标马尔可夫模型基础上引入深度序列建模与显式时长条件信号；以运动学序列（速度、航向变化）为表示，不含绝对空间参考，采用oracle-length与全零样本prior-length两种时长条件协议。

Result: 单站点训练、三站点零样本评估：各站点100个采样种子的平均JS散度在oracle-length协议下约0.035-0.050 bits，在零样本prior-length协议下约0.032-0.042 bits，两种协议均高于采样噪声下限（≤0.0049 bits）；按站点配对，oracle-length值比先前马尔可夫模型重实现低5.3-6.0倍；终止为时长条件而非空间条件，100%试验成功停止，长度跟踪误差+0.0±0.0 s（100/100精确命中）。

Conclusion: 无坐标运动学表示配合深度序列建模与时长条件，可跨未见站点零样本生成保真度高的工业车辆轨迹，优于既有马尔可夫模型，为异构工业场地提供可迁移的轨迹生成方案。

Abstract: Mobility simulation supports logistics, safety, and communications planning in industrial environments such as ports, mines, and airports. Existing trajectory models, however, rely on absolute coordinates, road-network tokens, or semantic zones: representations that are site-specific and not well suited to unstructured industrial terrain. We introduce the Industrial Kinematic Trajectory Model (IKTM), a coordinate-free trajectory generator that represents industrial vehicle motion through kinematic sequences (speed and heading change) with no absolute spatial reference. IKTM uses an autoregressive causal transformer with probabilistic mixture heads and extends our prior coordinate-free Markovian model with deep sequence modelling and an explicit duration-conditioning signal. Trained on one site and evaluated zero-shot on three unseen sites, it matches the small-turn shape of the empirical turn-rate distributions of held-out telematics; across all four sites, the per-site mean Jensen-Shannon divergence over 100 sampling seeds spans approximately 0.035-0.050 bits under an oracle-length duration-target protocol and approximately 0.032-0.042 bits under a fully zero-shot prior-length protocol, with similar ranges whether out-of-distribution (OOD) duration targets are drawn from each held-out site's empirical length distribution or from the Site A prior. Both protocols stay above the metric's sampling-noise floor (<=0.0049 bits). Paired by site, the oracle-length values are 5.3-6.0x lower than those of a re-implementation of our prior Markovian model under the same 1 Hz protocol. Termination is duration-conditioned rather than spatial: rollouts stop on 100% of trials with a length-tracking error of +0.0 +/- 0.0 s against the sampled target (100 of 100 exactly on target; N=100, T=0.2, untouched in-distribution test split).

</details>


### [51] [Contrastive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22175)
*Bonnie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Contrastive World Models：用 Deep InfoMax 式互信息下界替代像素重建，学习对视觉干扰鲁棒的潜在动力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于像素重建的世界模型在视觉复杂环境中易受无关信息主导，目标被无关细节占据，分散了对规划与控制有用信息的建模，导致表征不鲁棒、迁移到真实世界困难。

Method: 在 Dreamer 框架上，将标准世界模型目标中的观测重建替换为类似 Deep InfoMax 的互信息下界，最大化"状态-动作序列"与"未来观测局部 patch 特征"之间的互信息；不再需要像素解码器。

Result: 在三种视觉复杂度递增的小规模设定中评估：默认设定下与 Dreamer 及动量预测基线相当；引入干扰物或自然视频背景后显著优于两者；因移除像素解码器，训练效率更高。

Conclusion: 对比式、基于信息最大化的目标为构建对视觉干扰因素鲁棒的世界模型提供了有原则且具前景的方向，尤其有利于基于模型的强化学习智能体向真实世界迁移。

Abstract: World models trained via pixel reconstruction can struggle in visually complex environments, where irrelevant information dominates the objective and distract the model from information relevant to planning and control. We present Contrastive World Models, an approach for learning latent dynamics models without pixel reconstruction. Building on Dreamer, we replace observation reconstruction in the standard world model objective with a Deep InfoMax-like lower bound that maximizes the mutual information between state-action sequences and local patch features of future observations, encouraging state representations to retain information that is predictive of the future without requiring the model to reconstruct visually irrelevant details. We evaluate our approach in small-scale experiments across three settings of increasing visual complexity. Our method matches Dreamer and a momentum prediction baseline in the default setting, and substantially outperforms both once distractors or natural video backgrounds are introduced, while also training more efficiently by removing the pixel decoder entirely. Our approach is general and makes minimal assumptions beyond access to state-action sequences and future observations. These results suggest that contrastive, infomax-based objectives are a principled and promising direction for building world models that are robust to visual nuisance factors, a property particularly relevant for transferring model-based RL agents to the real world.

</details>


### [52] [OpenBlock: Constructive and Verified Content Generation for Adaptive Tile-Matching Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22177)
*Jiang Jun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应三消类（tile-matching）内容生成平台，核心为"双轨"架构：确定性规则生成器始终可用，可选学习型生成器需通过统一的可行性验证门；并利用自博弈强化学习放置智能体诊断出后期失败源于棋盘状态退化而非生成难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该品类拥有数亿玩家，但其决定每回合呈现何种棋子的内容生成算法仍属专有，且缺乏研究自适应难度的开放平台。同时需要厘清此类游戏的失败根源（内容难度 vs. 棋盘状态退化），并保证学习型生成不会破坏可放置性这一构造性可行性保证。

Method: 1) 双轨内容生成架构：规则生成器 + 可选学习生成器，两者共同受限于同一验证门，该门通过穷举式顺序放置搜索证明每个交付棋组完全可放置，从而学习轨不会降低规则轨的构造可行性保证。2) 自博弈强化学习放置智能体，辅以暴露"逐形状可放置性"的辅助任务并共享表示。3) 受控仿真与统计检验比较获胜/失败局的生成难度分布。4) 逐形状可放置性监督 vs. 聚合难度特征的消融对比。5) 14天在线灰度发布（48,000名玩家，样本比校验、CUPED校正）。

Result: 234,000+自博弈回合中，智能体胜率35.6%（中位分4,200）。受控仿真显示，棋盘填充率70–75%时，33–56%的长条棋子无合法放置；获胜局与失败局的生成难度分布统计上不可区分，表明后期失败由棋盘状态退化而非内容难度驱动。消融显示，驱动表示增益的是逐形状可放置性监督而非聚合难度特征。14天在线灰度中，相比仅规则轨，第1日留存提升1.8个百分点、会话时长提升7%。

Conclusion: 学习型（神经）内容生成轨在实时游戏中具有非对称上行收益：在规则轨之上可稳定提升留存与会话时长，同时验证门确保可放置性保证不被削弱。研究还表明此类游戏的后期失败根因在于棋盘状态退化，为自适应难度设计提供了开放平台与实证依据。

Abstract: Tile-matching puzzle games serve hundreds of millions of players, yet the content-generation algorithms that decide which pieces to present at each turn remain proprietary, and no open platform exists for studying adaptive difficulty in this genre. We present an adaptive tile-matching platform whose central algorithmic contribution is a dual-track content-generation architecture: a deterministic rule-based generator that is always available, and an optional learned generator, both subject to a common verification gate that establishes, by exhaustive sequential-placement search, that every delivered piece set is fully placeable so the learned track can never degrade the constructive-feasibility guarantee of the rule track. A self-play reinforcement-learning placement agent, supervised by auxiliary tasks that expose per-shape placeability to shared representations, is used to diagnose the game's dominant failure mode: at high board fill, long-bar pieces lose the majority of their legal placements. Across 234,000+ self-play episodes the agent reaches a 35.6\% win rate (median score 4,200), and controlled simulation shows that at board fill rates of 70--75\%, 33--56\% of long-bar pieces have no legal placement, while spawn difficulty distributions are statistically indistinguishable between won and lost games---evidence that board-state degeneration, not content difficulty, drives late-game failure. Head-to-head ablations show that per-shape placeability supervision---not aggregate difficulty features---drives the representation gain, and a 14-day online gray rollout (48,000 players; sample-ratio verified, CUPED-adjusted) lifts day-1 retention by 1.8 percentage points and session duration by 7\% over the rule track alone, quantifying the neural track's asymmetric upside in live play.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Task Completion: Training Capable and Safe Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22178)
*Zeyu Kang,Zhenyun Yin,Yang Zhang,Shan He,Shanzhe Lei,Yanjiu Zhong,Xinquan Chen,Yuhong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE，一种面向计算机使用代理（CUA）的能力与安全联合后训练框架，配合自动数据合成管线SCOPE-Gen与轨迹数据集SATraj-OS，通过SFT加在线RL使模型学会"按风险条件化执行"的策略，在OSWorld与OS-BLIND上取得最佳能力-安全综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA的后训练仅以任务成功率为目标，无法诱导可靠的安全行为。一个可靠的CUA应依据风险条件化其执行：正常任务应完成；存在环境风险但有安全路径时应规避并继续；目标有害或无安全路径时应拒绝。而这类条件化策略缺乏对齐的训练数据与优化方法。

Method: 提出SCOPE（Safety and Capability Optimization for Policy Execution），联合后训练任务执行能力与安全感知决策。数据侧：SCOPE-Gen自动合成可验证的能力任务，并在保持原目标不变的前提下将其转化为成对的环境风险变体；据此构建SATraj-OS轨迹数据集，包含能力示范、安全续行（safe continuation）与显式拒绝三类轨迹。训练侧：先对三类轨迹做监督微调，再用在线强化学习进一步提升任务完成度。以Qwen3.5-9B为基座。

Result: SCOPE-RL在OSWorld上达到54.17%任务成功率，在OS-BLIND上达到64.30%攻击规避率，在受评代理中取得最佳的58.80%能力-安全综合得分。消融实验显示两类安全监督作用不对称但互补：拒绝轨迹贡献了大部分攻击规避增益，而风险处理轨迹在同等攻击规避水平下能保留更高的任务效用。

Conclusion: 以任务成功为单一目标的后训练不足以产生可靠的安全行为；通过在配对风险变体数据上联合优化能力与安全，CUA可以学到条件化执行策略。拒绝监督主要提升安全性，风险处理监督主要维持效用，二者结合可在能力与安全间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have made rapid progress in completing complex tasks through graphical user interfaces, yet post-training centered on task success alone does not induce reliable safety behavior. A reliable CUA must condition its execution on risk: it should complete ordinary benign tasks, avoid environmental hazards and continue when a safe completion path remains, and refuse when the goal is harmful or no safe path exists. To learn this conditional policy, we develop Safety and Capability Optimization for Policy Execution (SCOPE), which jointly post-trains a CUA for task-execution capability and safety-aware decision making. To provide aligned training data for this joint objective, we further introduce SCOPE-Gen, an automated pipeline that synthesizes verifiable capability tasks and converts them into paired environment-risk variants while preserving their original goals.
