Method: 构建音视频结构因果模型（SCM），在构造上使音频独立于视频的干扰性外观。在此框架下，对比直接模型（通过跨注意力或共享隐变量让音频直接读取视频）与共享共同原因隐变量的多种变体（瓶颈、无监督共享/私有分解、忠实共享先验模型）。在给定SCM与干预假设下，证明反事实不变性是识别因果预测因子的充要条件，并在多种设定（特征向量SCM、程序化像素视频、真实图像+频谱图音频与预训练骨干、移动真实数字、条件生成器）中验证机制。

Result: 1）让音频直接读取视频的模型会学到视觉捷径，从外观而非因果事件预测声音，在测试时外观-事件相关性被打破时崩溃，合成错误事件的声音；2）常见补救措施（共享共同原因隐变量，包括瓶颈、无监督共享/私有分解、忠实共享先验）均失效，同样抓住外观代理；3）阻断捷径需要对干扰变量进行干预；4）在真实预训练视频到音频生成器上，输入干预测试显示模型对声音无关编辑（重新着色或灰度化）远非不变，显著改变所生成的声音。

Conclusion: 在所述SCM与干预假设下，反事实不变性是识别因果预测因子的充要条件。仅靠共享隐变量无法消除外观捷径，必须对干扰变量实施干预才能阻断捷径；该结论在多种合成与真实设定中均得到验证。

Abstract: Joint audio--video generators are trained on data in which what an event looks like and what it sounds like are strongly, often spuriously, correlated: a particular material, texture, or object appearance co-occurs with a particular sound. This paper is a controlled causal study of the resulting failure mode. Building an AV structural causal model in which the audio is, by construction, independent of the video's nuisance appearance, we show that models which let audio read video directly-through cross-attention or a shared latent-learn a visual shortcut: they predict sound from appearance rather than from the causal event, and collapse when the appearance-event correlation is broken at test time, literally synthesizing the wrong event's sound. Crucially, the popular remedy of routing both modalities through a shared common-cause latent does not fix this: a bottleneck, an unsupervised shared/private factorization, and a faithful shared-prior model all grab the appearance proxy and fail like the direct model. Blocking the shortcut instead requires an intervention on the nuisance. Under the stated SCM and intervention assumptions we prove that counterfactual invariance is necessary and sufficient to identify the causal predictor, and we verify the mechanism across a feature-vector SCM, procedural pixel video, real images with spectrogram audio and a pretrained backbone, moving real digits, and a conditional generator. On a \emph{real, pretrained} video-to-audio generator, an input-intervention test shows the model is far from invariant to sound-irrelevant edits (recolouring or graying a video substantially changes the sound it generates).

</details>


### [88] [Complex-valued Phase-Coherent Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22415)
*Leona Hioki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出复数Transformer沿用原始复数内积的softmax注意力在非原生复数任务上接近随机水平，提出将匹配分数改为归一化余弦相似度（对q、k做L2归一化并保持分数在一阶尺度），由此构建的相位相干Transformer（PCT）在多种任务上匹配或超越最强实数基线，其中复筛+相位相干递推的成员首次以复数网络解决Path-X（91.6%可训练参数为复数，S4为38.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复数域神经网络长期沿用实数域设计的原始复数内积softmax注意力，但在并非原生复数的任务上表现近似随机，且此前没有复数注意力被证明能纠正这一问题。作者希望找到使复数注意力真正有效的匹配分数形式，从而释放复数网络的表达能力。

Method: 提出将注意力分数设为尺度化的余弦分数：对查询和键做L2归一化，使分数只反映余弦相似度而忽略幅度，并将分数保持在一阶（order-one）尺度。基于此构建相位相干Transformer（PCT）家族，并给出复筛（complex screening）与相位相干递推相结合的成员。通过四个诊断任务、两种门控机制进行消融，对比是否归一化以及分数尺度过小的影响。

Result: 不做归一化时，模型在两种门控下于ListOps和Needle上停留于随机水平，在另外两个任务上远低于基线；归一化但尺度太小同样失败。采用尺度化余弦分数后，PCT在长程记忆、位置检索、层次推理、频域分类和物理复数信号上匹配或超越最强实数基线；深度到20无退化；损失在参数规模61倍范围内呈对数线性下降。复筛+相位相干递推成为首个解决Path-X的真正复数神经网络，其91.6%可训练参数为复数（S4为38.2%）。

Conclusion: 复数注意力的有效性关键取决于匹配分数形式：必须采用L2归一化的尺度化余弦分数。PCT家族展现出复数网络此前未见的泛化迹象，并首次以高比例复数参数解决Path-X，表明复数神经网络具备尚未被充分开发的潜力。

Abstract: Complex-valued Transformers have inherited softmax attention over the raw complex inner product. Outside natively complex domains this standard form stays near chance, and no complex attention had been shown to correct it. We show that the match must be a scaled cosine score: L2-normalise queries and keys, so the score reads their cosine similarity and ignores their magnitudes, and hold that score at order-one scale. With this the same models train on four diagnostic tasks under two different gates; without the normalisation they stay at chance on ListOps and Needle under both gates and fall far below on the other two, and a normalised score placed at too small a scale fails as well. The resulting family of phase-coherent Transformers (\PCT) matches or exceeds the strongest real-valued baseline across long-range memory, positional retrieval, hierarchical reasoning, frequency-domain classification and physical complex signals; it shows no degradation up to depth 20; and its loss decreases log-linearly over a 61-fold range of parameters. A member of the family, complex screening combined with a phase-coherent recurrence, is the first genuinely complex-valued neural network to solve Path-X, with 91.6% of its trainable parameters complex-valued against 38.2% for S4. We record these as signs of generalisation not previously seen in complex-valued neural networks.

</details>


### [89] [Connected Content Retriever: Dense Graph Edge Features Powering Pre-Ranking at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22441)
*Akhilesh Gupta,Sudarshan Srinivasa Ramanujam,Chirag Bhanuprasad Mehta,Reshma Asharaf Beena,Dhritiman Das,Birjodh Singh Tiwana,Bhargavkumar Kanubhai Patel,Mack Lee,Renyi Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LinkedIn 提出 CC Retriever 预排序系统，利用 GPU 上的排序搜索原语，将十亿级候选索引缩至数万活动并在 120ms p99 内完成全深度模型打分，模型参数扩大 50 倍，线上内容停留时长提升 +2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LinkedIn Feed 中来自用户人脉（关注与好友）的内容占曝光和互动的 70% 以上，因此预排序层需向排序层传递尽可能优质的数百个候选。同时，职业知识图谱带来的二度人脉"陌生人爆款"信号导致候选索引超过十亿，需在 120ms p99 延迟预算内从数万活动中筛选打分，传统方案难以兼顾规模与精度。

Method: 提出 Connected Content Retriever（CC Retriever）预排序系统，在 GPU 上以低延迟运行完整深度排序模型。核心是一个排序搜索 GPU 原语，在运行时将稠密的图亲和力特征（viewer 到 author）与文档级特征在 GPU 上做连接，耗时 5-10ms。系统利用 LinkedIn 经济图谱的特征集，并设计了相应模型架构。

Result: GPU 服务化打分使排序模型参数量扩大 50 倍；在线上实验中为 LinkedIn Feed 的内容停留时长带来 +2.5% 提升，显著高于 LinkedIn Feed 实验的典型增益；特征连接在 GPU 上运行时耗时 5-10ms，满足 120ms p99 的延迟预算。

Conclusion: CC Retriever 通过 GPU 上融合排序搜索原语与深度排序模型，解决了大规模人脉内容预排序的规模与延迟难题，验证了 GPU 服务化对模型规模扩展和业务指标提升的有效性，为在线系统扩展提供了可复用的架构范式。

Abstract: In large-scale recommendation systems like the LinkedIn Feed, content generated by a member's network (connections and follows) makes up over 70% of impressions and engagement. It is therefore essential that the pre-ranking layer forwards the best possible few hundred candidates to the ranking layer. LinkedIn's professional knowledge graph carries engagement signals across both the first degree network (connections and follows) and the second-degree network: posts that a 1st-degree connection reacted to, commented on or reshared but did not author (a.k.a. stranger viral). Due to this fan out, the resulting candidate index exceeds one billion; selection of activities from the viewer's network narrows it down to roughly tens of thousands of activities that must be scored within a 120 ms p99 latency budget. We present Connected Content Retriever (CC Retriever), a pre-ranking system that scores these candidates with a full deep ranking model on GPUs at low latency. At its core is a sorted-search GPU primitive that joins dense graph affinity features (viewer to author) with document level features stored on the GPU at runtime in 5-10 ms. The shift to GPU served scoring enabled a 50x scale up of the ranking model's parameters and delivered a +2.5% lift in content time spent on the LinkedIn Feed in online experiments, significantly higher than the typical gains observed in LinkedIn Feed experiments. In this work, we describe the feature set we leverage from LinkedIn's economic graph and the model architecture used for scoring, with a particular emphasis on the online system that scales the stack.

</details>


### [90] [Efficient Mixture-of-Experts with Speculative Decoding via Expert Coactivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22471)
*Kumari Nishu,Han-Byul Kim,Santosh Chilkunda,Maxwell Horton,Arnav Kundu,Mohammad Samragh,Lauren Hannah,Mohammad Sekhavat,Nikhil Bhendawade,Manuel Ciosici,Iman Mirzadeh,Keivan Alizadeh Vahid,David Harrison,Irina Belousova,Mehrdad Farajtabar,Minsik Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE与投机解码结合时，验证token数增加会导致专家从DRAM传输成本上升；通过训练中改进路由器设计提升专家共激活度，可将吞吐量提高21%并保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型与投机解码（SD）结合可加速推理，但SD的加速效果高度依赖被验证的token数量。验证token越多，需从DRAM传输到NPU的专家就越多，而内存传输通常是推理瓶颈，导致运行时受负面影响。因此需要研究MoE路由器设计如何影响SD下的推理速度。

Method: 作者研究了训练阶段MoE路由器设计对基于SD的MoE运行速度的影响，发现专家共激活度高的路由器运行更快，可缓解更多验证token带来的开销。随后在十亿参数级Transformer模型上评估多种路由器设计选择对专家共激活度与运行时的影响，并组合了全局负载均衡损失、共享专家、一致性损失以及自回归专家选择机制进行训练。

Result: 实验发现：将全局负载均衡损失、共享专家、一致性损失与自回归专家选择机制结合，能显著增强专家共激活。增大的共激活度转化为更高的整体运行时吞吐量；最终模型相比MoE基线吞吐量提升21%，同时保持与基线MoE相当的精度。

Conclusion: 在MoE与投机解码结合的推理场景中，路由器设计对专家共激活和运行时性能具有关键影响。通过特定路由器设计组合提升专家共激活度，可在不损失精度的前提下显著提高推理吞吐量。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are increasingly deployed alongside Speculative Decoding (SD) to accelerate inference, but combining the two is challenging. SD improves the inference speed of dense models by verifying groups of tokens in parallel. However, the inference speedup for SD with MoEs depends heavily on the number of tokens being verified. Using more verification tokens results in more experts being transferred from DRAM to the Neural Processing Unit (NPU), which increases the memory transfer cost. This negatively impacts model runtime, as memory transfer is typically the bottleneck in inference. In this work, we investigate the impact of MoE router design during training on the speed of MoEs with SD. We find that routers with high degrees of expert coactivation result in much faster runtimes, mitigating the impact of using more verification tokens. Motivated by this observation, we assess the impact of various router design choices on expert coactivation and runtime using billion-parameter transformer models. We find that combining a global load-balancing loss, shared experts, a consistency loss, and an autoregressive expert selection mechanism during training results in significantly stronger expert coactivation. This increased coactivation translates into higher overall runtime throughput: our exploration yields a model that improves throughput by 21% over MoE baselines, while maintaining on-par accuracy with the baseline MoE.

</details>


### [91] [COREM: Cosine-Relation Momentum Reshaping with Stateful Writeback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22487)
*Yan Wang,Xiaochuan Wang,Yuxiang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出矩阵值优化器状态中蕴含可利用的"关系结构"，定义了 unit-relation-transform 抽象并实例化为 COREM（余弦-关系动量重塑，带状态化回写），在 CIFAR-10 与 enwik8 上相较于 Muon 在中后期训练取得更优或相当表现，且变换 FLOPs 仅为 Muon 的约 13.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵值优化器状态（如动量）可能包含无法通过逐元素独立处理所捕获的关系结构。作者希望探究能否显式利用矩阵状态内部的关系来改进优化过程，并降低现有矩阵优化方法（如 Muon 依赖 Newton-Schulz 迭代）的计算开销。

Method: 提出 unit-relation-transform 抽象：将状态划分为更新单元，在单元间计算关系，并据此重塑状态。实例化为 COREM：1) 将动量状态划分为更新单元；2) 计算单元间的余弦关系；3) 用这些关系重塑动量；4) 将变换后的状态回写到优化器（stateful writeback），使重塑后的动量不仅影响当前更新，也影响未来优化动态。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10（MLP）与 enwik8（Transformer）上评测。相比 Muon：早期步进效率较低，但中后期提升更强；CIFAR-10 最终验证性能更好，enwik8 最终性能相当。enwik8 的谱诊断显示 COREM 持续提升熵有效秩、降低奇异能量在主导模态上的集中度，同时保持各向异性谱。方阵更新下，COREM 变换 FLOPs 约为 Muon（5 次 Newton-Schulz 迭代）的 13.3%。

Conclusion: 矩阵值优化器状态中的关系结构可被有效利用以改进优化；COREM 以状态化回写机制实现关系驱动的动量重塑，在不牺牲谱各向异性的前提下改善中后期训练与最终性能，同时大幅降低变换计算成本。

Abstract: Matrix-valued optimizer states may contain relational structure that is not captured by treating their entries independently. We study whether relations within matrix-valued optimizer states can be exploited to improve optimization. To this end, we introduce a unit-relation-transform abstraction and instantiate it as COREM, a Cosine-Relation Momentum Reshaping method with stateful writeback. COREM partitions the momentum state into update units, computes cosine relations among them, and uses these relations to reshape the momentum before writing the transformed state back to the optimizer. This stateful mechanism allows the reshaped momentum to affect not only the current update but also future optimization dynamics. We evaluate COREM on CIFAR-10 with an MLP and on enwik8 with a Transformer. Compared with Muon, COREM shows lower early-stage step efficiency but stronger improvement in the mid-to-late stages of training, achieving better final validation performance on CIFAR-10 and comparable final performance on enwik8. Spectral diagnostics on enwik8 show that COREM consistently increases entropy effective rank and reduces the concentration of singular energy in dominant modes, while preserving an anisotropic spectrum. For square matrix updates, COREM requires approximately 13.3% of the transformation FLOPs of Muon with five Newton-Schulz iterations.

</details>


### [92] [EmbeddGAN: A Novel GAN Framework Using an Embedding Network and Gini Distance Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22508)
*MaTais Caldwell,Yixin Chen,Xin Dang,Charles Walter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EmbeddGAN 是一种基于依赖性目标的新型对抗训练框架，用嵌入网络与 Gini 距离相关替换传统判别器，在 MNIST、CIFAR-10、CelebA 上取得有竞争力性能且训练更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAN 虽能生成高质量合成数据，但缺乏关于收敛性和学习过程有效性的形式化保证，导致训练不稳定、模式坍塌及对超参数敏感。

Method: 提出 EmbeddGAN：引入嵌入网络，将真实与生成数据投影到共享低维空间，学习一种表示，使样本与其真/假标签之间的统计依赖性最大化，而生成器最小化该依赖性。目标函数采用 Gini 距离相关（gCor），当且仅当嵌入与真/假标签统计独立时为零，从而促使真实样本与生成样本在学习空间中统计上不可区分。采用极小极大训练策略：嵌入网络最大化 gCor，生成器最小化 gCor。

Result: 在 MNIST、CIFAR-10 和 CelebA 数据集上的实验表明，EmbeddGAN 相对已有基线方法取得有竞争力的性能，并在所评估数据集上表现出显著稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: 通过以基于统计依赖性的目标替代传统判别器分类任务，EmbeddGAN 在保持生成质量竞争力的同时缓解了 GAN 训练不稳定的问题。

Abstract: Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) have demonstrated strong performance in generating high-quality synthetic data. However, they are limited by no formal guarantees regarding convergence and the effectiveness of the learning process. In practice, this leads to training instability, mode collapse, and sensitivity to hyperparameters. To address this, we propose EmbeddGAN, a novel adversarial training framework based on a dependence-based objective. Instead of relying on a discriminator that classifies samples as real or fake, EmbeddGAN introduces an embedding network that learns a representation in which statistical dependence between samples and their real/fake labels is maximized, while the generator is trained to minimize this dependence. This objective is implemented using the Gini distance correlation (gCor), which equals zero if and only if the embeddings are statistically independent of the real/fake label. Minimizing this objective therefore encourages real and generated samples to become statistically indistinguishable in the learned embedding space. The embedding network projects both real and generated data into a shared low-dimensional space, where distributional discrepancies can be measured directly through pairwise distances. We adopt a minimax training strategy: the embedding network maximizes the Gini distance correlation (maximizing dependence), while the generator minimizes it (minimizing dependence). Experiments on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CelebA datasets demonstrate that EmbeddGAN achieves competitive performance relative to established baselines while exhibiting notably stable training dynamics on the evaluated datasets.

</details>


### [93] [The Ups and Downs of Backprop Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22554)
*Giuseppe Chindemi,Benjamin F. Grewe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"权重纠缠"问题，即BP无法保证功能组件对应可独立识别的参数集；提出"权重算子"——参数化模块，可在推理时组合以复用功能组件，仅更新被选中算子的参数，并以Vector Networks作为实现验证功能参数可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BP虽使深层网络端到端学习成为可能，但不决定参数应如何组织，导致功能组件无法被选择性复用与适配。例如目标识别与运动预测可能依赖重叠的参数集，难以独立隔离或修改，作者称此为"权重纠缠"。现有架构（门控非线性、注意力、MoE路由）虽能按样本动态选择网络部分，但并不能保证同一功能组件跨越样本始终绑定到可识别的参数集。

Method: 提出权重算子（weight operators）：实现可复用功能组件的参数化模块，可在推理阶段组合成每个样本所需的函数。学习分两阶段：模型先推断所需的算子组合，然后仅更新被选中算子的参数集。Vector Networks（VNs）作为一种实现，将算子选择与每层内局部误差驱动的更新相耦合。

Result: VNs表明学到的算子可在训练中未出现的组合中被复用，同时更新仅局限于被选中的参数集。这为检验功能参数可识别性——即算子在学习过程中是否始终与同一功能组件关联——提供了基础。

Conclusion: 作者主张功能参数可识别性可作为组织原则，用于构建系统性地复用与重组已学函数、且仅适配需要变更组件的模型。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) has driven the remarkable success of modern deep learning by enabling large hierarchical networks to learn complex functions end-to-end. Yet it does not by itself determine how parameters should be organized so that functional components can be reused and adapted selectively. For example, object recognition and motion prediction may depend on overlapping parameter sets, making them difficult to isolate or modify independently. We call this condition weight entanglement. Modern architectures dynamically select which parts of a network process each sample: nonlinearities gate units, attention selects interactions, and Mixture-of-Experts architectures route inputs to modules. Yet such selection does not ensure that the same functional component remains linked to an identifiable parameter set across samples. We propose weight operators: parameterized modules that implement reusable functional components and can be composed at inference to form the function required by each sample. Learning proceeds in two stages: the model first infers the required operator composition, then updates only the selected operators' parameter sets. Vector Networks (VNs) provide one implementation. They couple operator selection to local error-driven updates within each layer and show that learned operators can be reused in combinations absent from training while updates remain restricted to the selected parameter sets. This provides a basis for testing functional parameter identifiability: whether an operator remains linked to the same functional component during learning. We argue that functional parameter identifiability may provide an organizing principle for models that systematically reuse and recombine learned functions while adapting only the components that need to change.

</details>


### [94] [Benchmarking Hybrid Deep Learning Architectures for Predictive Maintenance in Industry 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22583)
*Zhengyang,Gu,Joseph E. Hernandez,Thomas Cook,John Burtenshaw,Sean Scott,Chris Couch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对工业4.0预测性维护模型在真实工厂噪声数据下可靠性不足的问题，通过700余次实验对比六种深度学习架构，发现Transformer在平稳过程中表现优异但对混乱噪声数据易过拟合；LSTM+Transformer混合模型借助LSTM的噪声过滤能力，兼顾准确性与稳定性，是更可靠的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业4.0中的预测性维护依赖传感器与设备数据预测故障，但模型常在实验室表现完美、部署到真实工厂数据时却意外失效，形成"可靠性"鸿沟。因此需要评估主流深度学习架构在含噪真实数据下的稳健性，尤其检验Transformer是否仍优于RNN。

Method: 在700余次实验运行中评估六种深度学习架构，聚焦两类主流方法：逐步处理序列的循环神经网络（RNN）与一次性建模全序列的Transformer，并在数据含噪条件下对比性能，同时考察LSTM层与Transformer层组合的混合方法。

Result: Transformer在跟踪稳定、变化缓慢的过程中表现出色，但在混乱数据上容易反应过度，将传感器噪声误判为有效信号。结合LSTM层与Transformer层的混合方法对工厂车间噪声数据更具韧性：LSTM充当噪声滤波器平滑数据波动，使Transformer能专注于整体趋势。混合模型不仅提升了准确率，还比复杂模型显著更稳定、更一致。

Conclusion: 在含噪的工业预测性维护场景中，单纯追求模型复杂度并非最优，性能的稳定性（可靠性）同样关键。LSTM+Transformer混合架构通过"先滤波、再建模"的分工，在准确性与鲁棒性之间取得平衡，为真实工厂环境提供了更可靠的预测性维护方案。

Abstract: Predictive maintenance in Industry 4.0 refers to using data from sensors, machines, and production systems to estimate when equipment is likely to fail, so maintenance can be planned before a breakdown occurs [1]. However, a model that predicts maintenance may work perfectly in the lab but fail unexpectedly when applied to real factory data [2]. To solve this "reliability" gap, we evaluated six deep learning architectures across more than 700 experimental runs. We focused on the two dominant approaches in the field: Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs), which process data step-by-step, like reading a sentence [3], and Transformers, a recent dominant approach, which look at the entire sequence at once to spot important connections [4]. We examined whether Transformers still outperform recurrent neural networks (RNNs) when the data includes noise [5]. We found that while Transformers excelled at tracking stable, slow-moving processes, they tend to overreact to chaotic data, mistakenly taking sensor noise for meaningful signals [6]. We also found that the hybrid method that combines a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) layer with a Transformer layer is more resilient to noisy data from factory shops [7]. Functioning as a noise filter, the LSTM smooths out data volatility, allowing the Transformer to focus on the bigger picture without being distracted [8]. The hybrid model did not just improve accuracy; it proved to be significantly more consistent than complex models, delivering reliable predictions regardless of how chaotic the underlying system became.

</details>


### [95] [Augmenting PID Control with Deep Reinforcement Learning: A Hybrid Approach to the Industrial Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22584)
*Zhengyang,Gu,Joseph E. Hernandez,John Burtenshaw,Sean Scott,Thomas Cook,Chris Couch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合PID-RL控制器：用TD3智能体在Industrial Benchmark上搜索最优的Gain与Shift参数，再交给调好的PID控制器执行，从而同时获得最佳DRL智能体的Performance与Efficiency以及经典控制器的Reliability。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程日益复杂，具有非线性、多输入动态特性，传统PID控制器往往难以应对；而纯DRL虽能在高维、延迟效应、多目标冲突环境下自主发现非显然的最优策略，但其最终策略常常不稳定、可靠性差。因此动机是取长补短：保留DRL的探索发现能力，同时保证工业控制所需的可靠性。

Method: 1) 使用Industrial Benchmark（IB）作为测试平台，其特点是高维状态空间、延迟效应与相互冲突的多准则目标；2) 设计多目标奖励函数，使DRL在该环境下可行；3) 采用twin-delayed deep deterministic（TD3）智能体作为发现工具，搜索IB中Gain与Shift参数的最优非显然配置；4) 将发现的参数馈入一个简单且经过整定的PID控制器，构成混合PID-RL控制架构。

Result: 混合模型成功同时具备三项特性：达到最优DRL智能体的Performance与Efficiency水平，并具备经典控制器的Reliability；验证了DRL在简单控制器失效的多维空间中自主发现最优非显然策略的能力。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一种用DRL增强而非替代可信工业控制系统的实用方法论：将RL定位于参数发现与整定环节，将PID保留为执行环节，从而在复杂工业场景中平衡性能、效率与可靠性。

Abstract: As industrial processes grow in complexity, traditional Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controllers are often insufficient for handling their non-linear, multi-input dynamics. We propose using advanced Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) to prove its advantages in these complex environments. To do this, we rely on the Industrial Benchmark (IB). The IB is a realistic simulation that tests DRL algorithms against the key challenges of industrial applications: high-dimensional state spaces, delayed effects, and conflicting multi-criterial objectives. This testbed highlights DRL's core trade-off: while its final policies can often be unstable, its unique strength is the ability to autonomously discover optimal, non-obvious policies in multi-dimensional spaces where simple controllers fail. In this paper, we propose a novel hybrid PID-RL controller that leverages DRL's discovery capability while ensuring Reliability. After developing a multi-objective reward function to make DRL viable, we use a twin-delayed deep deterministic (TD3) agent as a discovery tool to find the optimal, non-obvious settings for the IB's 'Gain' and 'Shift' parameters. By feeding these discovered parameters to a simple, tuned PID controller, our hybrid model successfully combines all three characteristics: it achieves the optimal Performance and Efficiency of the best DRL agent with the Reliability of a classical controller. This work demonstrates a practical methodology for using DRL to augment, rather than replace, trusted industrial control systems.

</details>


### [96] [TWIG: A Time-Causal Wavelet Operator for Autoregressive Forecasting on Irregular Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22585)
*Subashree Venkatasubramanian,David A. Barajas-Solano,Chuyang Liu,Daniel M. Tartakovsky,Dipankar Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWIG：面向不规则图自回归代理建模的图原生神经算子，通过因果多尺度时序特征与图小波算子块实现稳定闭环预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不规则图（如区域扩散、三维地下水文、气动流场）上进行稳定自回归预测存在挑战：需要兼顾多尺度时间记忆、图结构传播与闭环预测中的因果性，而现有非时间因果的图神经算子基线在闭环rollout下表现受限。

Method: TWIG将每个节点历史变换为因果多尺度时序特征，将近期变化与逐渐变慢的记忆分量分离；随后通过带门控逐点通道混合的图小波算子块传播这些特征。架构在构造上即为因果，专为闭环预测设计，预测结果被递归复用作为未来输入。

Result: 在三个不规则域预测任务上评估（区域扩散、三维地下水文、气动流场），图规模400至5233节点，模型容量约70k至10M参数。TWIG在地下水文与区域扩散基准上取得最低的聚合rollout误差，在10M参数气动流场基准上排名第二（仅次于GPS Transformer），并在三种设置下均优于对应的非时间因果Graph WNO基线。

Conclusion: TWIG为不规则图上动态场的稳定自回归预测提供了一种有效且可扩展的方法，验证了因果多尺度时序建模与图小波算子结合在闭环预测中的优势。

Abstract: We introduce TWIG (Time-Causal Wavelet Operator for Irregular Graphs), a graph-native neural operator for autoregressive surrogate modeling on static irregular graphs. TWIG transforms each node history into causal multiscale temporal features that separate recent variation from progressively slower memory components, then propagates these features through graph-wavelet operator blocks with gated pointwise channel mixing. The architecture is causal by construction and designed for closed-loop forecasting, where predictions are recursively reused as future inputs. We evaluate TWIG on three irregular-domain forecasting problems spanning regional diffusion, three-dimensional subsurface hydrology, and aerodynamic flow, with graphs ranging from 400 to 5,233 nodes and model capacities from approximately 70k to 10M parameters. TWIG achieves the lowest aggregate rollout errors on the subsurface-hydrology and regional-diffusion benchmarks and ranks second on the 10M-parameter aerodynamic-flow benchmark, behind the GPS Transformer. Across all three settings, TWIG consistently outperforms the corresponding non-time-causal Graph WNO baseline. These results demonstrate that TWIG provides an effective and scalable approach to stable autoregressive forecasting of dynamical fields on irregular graphs.

</details>


### [97] [User-Level Handover Decision Making Based on Machine Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22593)
*João Lima,Alvaro Medeiros,Eduardo Aguiar,Vicente Angelo de Sousa Junior,Tarciana Guerra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在恶劣传播条件下比较了多种机器学习方法用于用户级切换决策，发现SVM和MLP最适合最优切换目标分类，SOFL模糊系统性能相近但处理时间更短；LightGBM在下行时间估计中误差最小且处理时间短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在涉及建筑物、覆盖空洞与阴影效应的恶劣传播场景中，用户级切换决策面临挑战，需评估不同方法在分类与回归任务上的适用性。

Method: 基于ns-3网络仿真平台构建仿真场景，对比经典机器学习方法（KNN、SVM、神经网络/MLP）、先进模糊逻辑系统（如SOFL）以及提升机（Boosting，如LightGBM）。

Result: 最优切换目标分类中，SVM与MLP表现最佳；模糊系统SOFL性能相近但处理时间更低。下行时间估计中，即使在困难传播场景下，LightGBM误差最小且处理时间短。

Conclusion: 机器学习与模糊逻辑方法均可有效支持恶劣传播条件下的用户级切换决策，方法选择需在精度与处理时间之间权衡：分类任务优选SVM/MLP或SOFL，回归任务优选LightGBM。

Abstract: This letter covers a broad comparison of methods for classification and regression applications for a user-level handover decision making in scenarios with adverse propagation conditions involving buildings, coverage holes, and shadowing effects. The simulation campaigns are based on network simulator ns-3. The comparison encompasses classical machine learning approaches, such as KNN, SVM, and neural networks, but also state-of-the-art fuzzy logic systems and latter boosting machines. The results indicate that SVM and MLP are the most suitable for the classification of the best handover target, although fuzzy system SOFL can perform similarly with lower processing time. Additionally, for the download time estimation, LightGBM provides the smallest error with short processing time, even in hard propagation scenarios.

</details>


### [98] [Concurrency-Aware Process Model Forecasting with Causal Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22614)
*Yongbo Yu,Jari Peeperkorn,Johannes De Smedt,Jochen De Weerdt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将流程模型预测（PMF）从直接跟随图扩展到因果网，通过预测关系与绑定计数的时间序列并重建含AND/XOR语义的未来流程模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PMF方法预测直接跟随图，无法显式表示并发关系，因此需要一种能刻画并发语义的未来流程模型预测方法。

Method: 预测关系与绑定（relation and binding）计数的时间序列，利用这些预测重建具有AND/XOR语义的未来流程模型（因果网）；并提出评估协议，处理部分轨迹并构造符合性检查所需的工作流网。

Result: 在四个事件日志上的实验表明，预测模型达到与从对应未来窗口观测中重新挖掘的模型相近的符合性水平；优于静态发现基线（后者在结构稳定的日志上精度高，但在其余三个日志上精度显著下降）。过滤低频绑定可改善多数符合性指标，但同时移除大量并发行为。

Conclusion: 将PMF扩展到因果网能够在模型层面显式表达并发语义，预测模型的符合性接近未来重挖模型并优于静态基线；低频绑定过滤存在符合性与并发表达能力之间的权衡。

Abstract: Process model forecasting (PMF) aims to predict the process model that will characterize a future period, thereby providing a process-level view of how behavior is expected to evolve. Existing PMF methods, however, forecast directly-follows graphs, which cannot explicitly represent concurrency. We extend PMF to causal nets by forecasting time series of relation and binding counts and using these forecasts to reconstruct future process models with AND/XOR semantics. To evaluate the resulting models, we introduce a protocol that accounts for partial traces and constructs the workflow nets required for conformance checking. Experiments on four event logs show that the forecasted models achieve conformance levels close to those of models re-mined from observations in the corresponding future windows. They also outperform static discovery baselines, which retain high precision on the structurally stable log but exhibit substantial precision losses on the other three logs. Filtering infrequent bindings improves most conformance metrics, although it also removes much of the concurrent behavior captured by the models.

</details>


### [99] [Classification with Abstention Under Class-Conditional Error Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22632)
*Mohammadreza M. Kalan,Yuyang Deng,Sanaz Hamidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在分开的类条件错误约束下带弃权的二分类，目标是最小化弃权率并保证两类错误低于阈值，刻画了分布无关的极小极大超额弃权风险率，提出代理损失形式并给出有限样本保证，分析凸情形计算复杂度并实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带弃权的分类中，常需要对两类错误分别设定约束，而现有方法多关注总体错误或单一风险，缺乏对分开类条件错误约束下弃权风险的理论刻画与可计算学习框架。

Method: 在分开的类条件错误约束下研究二分类弃权问题；用假设类复杂度与样本量刻画分布无关的极小极大超额弃权风险率（至对数因子）；引入代理损失形式以适配神经网络等模型，推导超额代理模糊风险的有限样本保证；将学习任务表述为约束优化问题并分析凸情形下的计算复杂度。

Result: 得到分布无关的极小极大超额弃权风险率（至对数因子），给出代理损失的有限样本保证，刻画凸约束优化的计算复杂度，并在多种数据集上与竞争方法比较性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为带弃权且分开类条件错误约束的分类提供了理论率、可计算代理框架与算法，并通过实验验证其有效性。

Abstract: We study binary classification with abstention under separate class-conditional error constraints, with the objective of minimizing abstention while keeping both errors below prescribed thresholds. We characterize the distribution-free minimax rate of excess abstention risk, up to logarithmic factors, in terms of the complexity of the hypothesis class and the sample size. To make the framework amenable to computation with models such as neural networks, we introduce surrogate-loss formulations and derive finite-sample guarantees for excess surrogate ambiguity risk. We formulate the resulting learning task as a constrained optimization problem and characterize its computational complexity in the convex setting. Finally, we evaluate our approach on various datasets and compare its performance with a competing method for this problem.

</details>


### [100] [Monotone-Constrained Diffusion Models for Long-Horizon Production Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22643)
*Temesgen Mikael Abraha,Yves Lucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在仅有序列前段观测时进行长时程油气产量预测的病态问题，提出条件扩散预测器 Physics-SIMS-TS，在3个辖区、超35,000口井上取得最准确的扩散模型结果，并实现构造性单调预测与校准区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭序列最初若干观测预测长时程本身是病态问题：同一段短历史可对应多条未来轨迹。油气产量预测中，约在井生产寿命前五分之一处做出的预测会驱动开发与弃井决策，且可用预测必须呈单调递减。现有扩散预测方法难以同时满足精度、单调性与不确定性校准需求。

Method: 提出 Physics-SIMS-TS，一种条件扩散预测器，包含：针对合成伪影的负向引导（negative guidance）、递减曲线约束、采样期间施加的保序投影（isotonic projection）、空间训练增强，以及产生完整预测分布的集成随机采样器。此外提出骨干网络的可逆实例归一化（reversible-instance-normalization）变体。评估在共享空间、验证集冻结的统一协议下进行。

Result: 在3个辖区、超过35,000口井上，Physics-SIMS-TS 是所比较方法中最准确的扩散预测器，与集成 Transformer 预测器相当但未超越。其预测以构造方式保证单调，均方误差代价至多0.5%；轨迹集成在按辖区拟合单一离散因子后给出校准区间。在六个标准基准上，骨干的可逆实例归一化变体为领先的扩散基线。另量化了四项协议选择对排序结果的影响。

Conclusion: 通过将物理约束（递减曲线、单调性）与扩散采样结合，可在长时程早期预测中获得准确、单调且校准良好的概率预测；方法在扩散类模型中领先，但与集成 Transformer 尚存差距，且评测排名对协议选择敏感。代码与评测产物已公开。

Abstract: Forecasting a long horizon from only the first observations of a sequence is ill-posed: many trajectories are consistent with the same short history. We study this problem in oil and gas production forecasting, where forecasts made after roughly the first fifth of a well's producing life drive development and abandonment decisions, and where a usable forecast must describe a monotone decline. We present Physics-SIMS-TS, a conditional diffusion forecaster that combines negative guidance against synthetic artifacts, decline-curve constraints and an isotonic projection applied during sampling, spatial training augmentation, and an ensembled stochastic sampler yielding a full predictive distribution. Across three jurisdictions and more than 35,000 wells, under a shared-space, validation-frozen protocol, Physics-SIMS-TS is the most accurate diffusion forecaster in the comparison and is competitive with, but not superior to, ensembled transformer forecasters. Its forecasts are monotone by construction at a cost of at most 0.5% in mean squared error, and its trajectory ensemble yields calibrated intervals after one dispersion factor is fitted per jurisdiction. On six standard benchmarks a reversible-instance-normalization variant of the backbone is the leading diffusion baseline. We also quantify four protocol choices on which the measured ranking depends. Code and evaluation artifacts are released.

</details>


### [101] [Multi-Armed Bernoulli Bandits via Minimax Single-Arm Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22690)
*Huikang Liu,Zhengchao Wang,Daniel Kuhn,Wolfram Wiesemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单臂老虎机（SAB）极小极大解的有限时域伯努利多臂老虎机索引策略，达到 $4.45\sqrt{KT}+10.75K$ 的分布无关遗憾上界，并给出有限网格实现与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时域伯努利多臂老虎机需要兼顾最坏情形遗憾与可计算性，现有 minimax 策略（如网格方法）在多臂及较大时域下往往计算复杂或表现受限，作者希望构造一种兼具 minimax 最优阶与良好实证性能的索引型策略。

Method: 将单臂问题（未知 Bernoulli 臂 vs 已知奖励）的最坏情形遗憾最小化建模为精确的半无限线性规划；由最优停止策略导出"累积继续概率"式索引，辅以单调调整与奖励缺口上限；把索引误差与单臂停止策略遗憾相关联以证明遗憾界；通过 Bernoulli 随机化推广至 $[0,1]$ 奖励，并给出带量化近似损失的有限网格实现。

Result: 证明分布无关遗憾上界 $4.45\sqrt{KT}+10.75K$，与文献中的 minimax 最优阶一致；扩展到 $[0,1]$ 奖励；给出有限网格实现的近似损失界。实验表明该索引策略在所有测试的臂数与时域下最坏情形遗憾低于全部基准策略，且在双臂设置下接近基于网格的 MAB minimax 策略。

Conclusion: 该工作从单臂 minimax 视角统一了索引策略设计与遗憾分析，提供了理论最优阶保证、可实现的网格算法及稳定的实证优势，为有限时域伯努利多臂老虎机提供了实用且理论坚实的解法。

Abstract: We develop an index policy for finite-horizon Bernoulli multi-armed bandits from minimax solutions to single-arm bandit (SAB) problems. Each SAB problem involves choosing between an unknown Bernoulli arm and a known reward. We show that minimizing worst-case regret of SAB problems over all non-anticipative policies admits an exact semi-infinite linear programming formulation. The resulting stopping policies offer a natural way to compare arms: the higher the known reward against which a policy continues sampling, the more promising the unknown arm. We turn this intuition into indices based on cumulative continuation probabilities, with a monotone adjustment and a reward-shortfall cap. By relating index errors to the regret of single-arm stopping policies, we establish a distribution-free regret bound of $4.45\sqrt{KT}+10.75K$ for $K$ arms and horizon $T$. This bound matches the minimax-optimal regret order established in the literature. The guarantee extends to rewards supported on $[0,1]$ through Bernoulli randomization. We also provide a finite-grid implementation with quantified approximation loss. In numerical experiments, the SAB-based index policy achieves lower worst-case regret than every tested benchmark policy across all evaluated numbers of arms and horizons, while closely matching the grid-based MAB minimax policy in the two-arm setting.

</details>


### [102] [Autonomous Model Lifecycle Management for Digital Twin-Based Manufacturing Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22701)
*Zhengyang,Gu,Thomas Cook,Fredaljohn Rohrbaugh,Joseph E. Hernandez,Chris Couch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Manufacturing AI systems must autonomously adapt to continuous distributional shift from raw-material variability, ambient changes, and equipment aging, under strict safeguard and operator-trust requirements where model failures risk physical damage. This paper presents a closed-loop Cyber-Physical System (CPS) for autonomous model lifecycle management in automotive manufacturing, deployed since 2023. The system manages product-specialized model pairs: a sequence-to-sequence physics model (LPP) serving as a digital twin, and a deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) control policy (LCP) trained against it. Per retraining cycle, multiple model variants spanning architecture families and RL algorithms compete; only the best-scoring candidate advances. A Conductor orchestrator autonomously manages plant-wide model inventories with dependency-aware retraining and Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) fallback. Reflecting the principle of Human-Centric Intelligence, the LCP composite score embeds an operator-trust gate penalizing policies deviating from established practice; without it, 23% of policies are rejected by operators despite passing accuracy thresholds. Across multiple facilities, LCP-controlled processes achieve process stability improvements of 28-45% over uncontrolled baselines with zero safety incidents.

</details>


### [103] [D-IMPL: A Diffusion-based Solver for Parameterized BBOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22752)
*Yang Hu,Na Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散模型的通用求解器 D-IMPL，用于参数化黑盒优化（BBO），通过引入"最小化策略"概念，用扩散模型学习解分布，实现跨实例摊销计算成本，并给出样本复杂度保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化中，传统方法在每个 BBO 实例上都需要大量额外计算成本，且难以刻画非凸目标的多模态解空间。作者希望利用扩散模型学习复杂分布的能力，在仅能黑盒访问目标函数查询的条件下，设计一种可在推理阶段降低单实例计算开销、同时捕捉多模态结构的通用求解器。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为兼容的生成建模任务，引入"最小化策略"（minimization policy）作为新的解概念，即定义在解空间上的采样分布，使其密度集中于每个 BBO 实例的最小值点集合。提出 Diffusion-based Iterative Minimization Policy Learning (D-IMPL)，用扩散模型学习该最小化策略，使其密度正比于目标值取负后的指数，从而在不同 BBO 实例间摊销计算成本。

Result: 理论上给出样本复杂度保证，证明可在 O(log(1/δ)) 次迭代内有效学习到 δ-近似最小化策略；并通过大量有约束与无约束 BBO 任务的实证评估验证了 D-IMPL 的性能。

Conclusion: D-IMPL 表明扩散模型可作为参数化黑盒优化的高效通用求解器，借助最小化策略的生成建模视角，在保证理论样本复杂度的同时减少了推理阶段的额外计算，并在多类 BBO 任务上取得良好表现。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated strong power in generative modeling tasks across multiple domains, exhibiting a remarkable capability of learning complex distributions from samples. In this paper, we leverage such capability to design an efficient universal diffusion-based solver for parameterized black-box optimizations (BBO), where the optimizer has only black-box access to queries of the objective function at the learning stage, yet is able to reduce the additional computational cost at the inference stage for each BBO instance while also capturing the potential multi-modal landscape of non-convex objectives. To cast our formulation as a compatible generative modeling task, we introduce the notion of minimization policy as a new solution concept, which defines a sampling distribution over the solutions that should concentrate around the minimizer set for each BBO instance. We then propose Diffusion-based Iterative Minimization Policy Learning (D-IMPL), a practical generative-model-based solver for solving parameterized BBOs that employs diffusion models to learn a minimization policy, whose density is proportional to the exponential of the negated objective values, thereby amortizing the computational costs across different BBO instances. Furthermore, we demonstrate the performance of our D-IMPL algorithm by establishing a sample complexity guarantee showing that a $δ$-approximate minimization policy can be effectively learned within $O(\log(1/δ))$ iterations, and by extensive empirical evaluations over a range of constrained and unconstrained BBO tasks.

</details>


### [104] [Look Before You Steer: Geometry Predicts SAE Feature Steerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22782)
*Muhammad Khan,Shlok Channawar,Akshaj Gurugubelli,Girish Gupta,Aditya Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究稀疏自编码器（SAE）本身在干预前即可计算的几何属性能否预测特征的"可引导性"（steerability，即固定行为效果所需的引导强度），发现解码器空间几何（邻居密度与最大余弦相似度）可部分预测特征引导成本，并在多模型、多宽度及跨架构上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE特征引导目前需要逐特征的系数调优，依赖干预扫描（intervention sweeps），成本高昂。作者希望找到一种在任意前向传播之前、仅凭SAE权重矩阵即可计算的属性，用以预测哪些特征引导代价低、哪些代价高，从而在部署前筛选可控特征。

Method: 从SAE解码器权重矩阵中计算几何指标：邻居密度（neighbor density）与到邻近解码方向的最高余弦相似度（maximum cosine similarity），均在干预前可得。用这些指标对特征引导所需强度进行排序，并与行为效果（固定效果下的引导需求）做秩相关、AUROC分析。验证覆盖两个Gemma-2规模（2B、9B）、两种SAE宽度（16K、65K）、跨架构Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct，以及Qwen3-8B的BatchTopK SAE。

Result: 几何与可引导性存在显著秩相关（ρ最高约-0.546，p < 10⁻⁶），AUROC为0.610–0.822（跨条件）；信号为秩基（rank-based），与网格离散性一致。该关系在两个Gemma-2规模、两种SAE宽度上可复现，并在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上跨架构可检测（ρ=-0.266，n=300）。在Qwen3-8B的BatchTopK SAE上，几何只能预测特征是否可引导，无法预测响应特征间的连续排序，揭示了与SAE训练机制相关的边界条件。在两个模型的深层比例深度处信号减弱，此时引导成本超出干预预算，形成一致的深度边界。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，引导前几何可部分指导系数选择，为在部署前筛选可控特征提供了一条路径。但该方法存在边界条件：训练机制（如BatchTopK）与深层深度会限制其有效性，信号本身也是部分而非完全的预测。

Abstract: Steering with SAE features requires per-feature coefficient tuning, which currently demands intervention sweeps. We ask whether properties of the SAE itself, computable before any forward pass, predict which features will be cheap or expensive to steer. We show that variation in SAE feature steerability is partially predicted by decoder-space geometry: neighbor density and maximum cosine similarity to nearby decoder directions, both computable from the SAE weight matrix before any intervention, rank features by how much steering they require for a fixed behavioral effect ($ρ$ up to $-0.546$, $p < 10^{-6}$, AUROC 0.610-0.822 across conditions; the signal is rank-based, consistent with grid discreteness). This geometry-steerability relationship replicates across two Gemma-2 model scales (2B and 9B), two SAE widths (16K and 65K), and is detectable cross-architecturally on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct ($ρ= -0.266$, $n = 300$). On Qwen3-8B with BatchTopK SAEs, geometry predicts whether a feature is steerable at all but not the continuous ordering among responsive features, revealing a boundary condition tied to SAE training regime. The signal weakens at deep proportional layer depth in both models, where the cost of steering exceeds our intervention budget, a consistent depth boundary. These results provide preliminary evidence that pre-steering geometry can partially inform coefficient selection, offering a path toward screening features for controllability before deployment.

</details>


### [105] [Improved Private Sparse Covariance Estimation with Multiscale Threshold Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22783)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究均值零、协方差支撑未知且每行至多 k 个非零项的子高斯分布在算子范数下的差分隐私协方差估计，提出多尺度随机阈值算法，将隐私相关样本复杂度中的 √k 因子去除，并匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在算子范数下对稀疏协方差进行差分隐私估计时，隐私相关项的样本复杂度为 Õ(k^{3/2}√d/(αε))，其额外 √k 因子并非最优，与已知下界 Õ(k^2/α^2 + k√d/(αε)) 之间存在差距，需要改进算法以收紧该权衡。

Method: 提出多尺度随机阈值算法。核心技术包括：对理想重构的中心化涨落直接建立算子范数界，利用条件独立性而非逐行累积逐项误差；采用多尺度阈值检验分配，在重构方差与查询敏感度之间进行平衡。

Result: 在(ε,δ)-差分隐私与误差至多 ασ^2 下，样本复杂度为 Õ(k^2/α^2 + k√d/(αε))，将现有上界中隐私相关项 Õ(k^{3/2}√d/(αε)) 改进 √k 倍，并在适用参数区间内匹配下界 Ω̃(k^2/α^2 + k√d/(αε))。

Conclusion: 通过算子范数直接界与多尺度阈值分配，该方法移除了隐私相关样本复杂度中的额外 √k 因子，收紧了近似误差与隐私保护之间的权衡，在适用参数范围内达到最优。

Abstract: We study differentially private covariance estimation in operator norm for mean-zero sub-Gaussian distributions with unknown covariance support and at most $k$ nonzero entries per row. We develop a multiscale random-threshold algorithm with sample complexity $\ot(k^2/α^2+k\sqrt d/(α\varepsilon))$ for $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy and error at most $ασ^2$, where $d$ is the dimension and $σ$ is a known sub-Gaussian scale. The bound improves the privacy-dependent term of the existing $\ot(k^2/α^2+k^{3/2}\sqrt d/(α\varepsilon))$ \citep{kumar2026curse} upper bound by a factor of $\sqrt k$, and matches the lower bound of $\widetildeΩ(k^2/α^2 + k\sqrt{d}/(α\varepsilon))$ in its applicable parameter regime.
  Our key technical ingredient is a direct operator-norm bound on the centered fluctuations of an ideal reconstruction, exploiting conditional independence rather than accumulating entrywise errors across each row. A multiscale allocation of threshold tests balances reconstruction variance against query sensitivity. Together, these ingredients sharpen the trade-off between approximation error and privacy protection, removing the additional $\sqrt{k}$ factor from the privacy-dependent sample complexity.

</details>


### [106] [Robust Market Making with Hawkes Order Flow and Price Impact via Adversarial Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22785)
*Hao Yang,Zhenguo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将对抗强化学习做市框架扩展至含Hawkes自激订单到达与交易冲击的复杂环境，并引入LSTM建模时序结构，在多数复杂微观结构情形下改善收益左尾表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实限价订单簿中做市面临显著模型不确定性与机制转换风险。已有对抗强化学习方法将Avellaneda–Stoikov做市问题建模为做市商与环境对手的零和博弈以提升鲁棒性，但通常依赖泊松订单到达且忽略交易诱发的价格冲击，难以刻画订单流聚集、自激及交易后价格反馈等高频微观结构效应。

Method: 在对抗式强化学习做市框架中引入Hawkes自激订单到达与交易诱发价格冲击，扩展机制空间；为缓解由此加剧的非平稳性，加入LSTM模块显式建模近期观测的时序结构；通过博弈论分析与数值实验刻画所提框架的均衡性质，并提出聚焦收益分布左尾改善的鲁棒性评估协议。

Result: 在多种市场机制下，所提方法在大多数复杂微观结构环境中取得更优左尾表现，且在Hawkes激活性强、价格冲击中低的机制中增益尤为明显。Bootstrap检验未发现这些改进源于更强的期末方向性库存偏差。

Conclusion: 将对抗训练与时序状态表示相结合，可提升强化学习做市策略在订单流自激、价格冲击与机制不确定性下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Market-making strategies in real limit order book markets face substantial model uncertainty and regime-shift risk. Existing adversarial reinforcement learning approaches improve robustness by formulating the Avellaneda--Stoikov market-making problem as a zero-sum game between a market maker and an environmental adversary. However, these approaches typically rely on Poisson order arrivals and neglect trade-induced price impact, limiting their ability to capture important high-frequency market microstructure effects such as clustered order flow, self-excitation, and post-trade price feedback.
  We extend adversarial reinforcement learning for market making to a more complex environment with Hawkes self-exciting order arrivals and trade-induced price impact. To mitigate the increased non-stationarity introduced by the expanded regime space, we incorporate an LSTM module that explicitly models the temporal structure of recent observations. We further characterize the equilibrium properties of the proposed framework through both game-theoretic analysis and numerical experiments, and introduce a robustness evaluation protocol focused on improvements in the left tail of the return distribution.
  Experimental results across a range of market regimes show that the proposed method achieves improved left-tail performance in most complex microstructure environments. In particular, the gains are pronounced in regimes with strong Hawkes excitation and low-to-moderate price impact. Bootstrap tests provide no evidence that these improvements are obtained through a stronger terminal directional inventory bias. These results suggest that combining adversarial training with temporal state representation can improve the robustness of reinforcement-learning-based market-making strategies under order-flow self-excitation, price impact, and regime uncertainty.

</details>


### [107] [FIRM-WM: State-factorized factual-interventional recurrent modeling for reward-free visual planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22816)
*Yilun Wu,Yunjian Zhang,Aobo Li,Mujiangshan Wang,Haitao Wu,Aqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FIRM-WM：一种将循环状态拆分为"目标可比构型"与 128 维动态纤维的像素世界模型，并结合共同重置干预分支采集反事实动作结果，在无奖励离线设定下提升图像目标任务的规划成功率与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无奖励潜在世界模型可从离线视频学习并按预测潜未来优化动作，但存在两个错配：一是规划状态既要与目标图像坐标可比，又要保留速度、运动趋势、接触等历史依赖信息；二是离线训练中每条轨迹只对应一个事实未来，而采样式规划器需在同一状态下比较大量未执行动作，缺乏反事实监督。

Method: FIRM-WM（Factual–Interventional Recurrent World Model）：循环隐状态显式分离为类型化的、可与目标比对的构型部分，以及仅用于预测、不进入终末目标代价的 128 维动态纤维；训练上利用宽覆盖的事实轨迹提供状态覆盖，并引入共同重置干预分支，在每个分支执行前重置环境并通过环境的 state-setting 接口恢复同一记录状态值，从而为备选动作序列提供观测到的结果。

Result: 在匹配的 CEM 规划与三个独立全流程随机种子下，TwoRoom 达 99.0±1.0%、Reacher 达 92.7±2.1%、OGBench-Cube 达 88.0±3.0%，对比 LeWM 的 89.0%、88.0%、70.0%；部署模型规模 2.98–3.42M 参数，规划时间降低 2.13–11.60 倍。

Conclusion: 通过状态解耦与干预式数据采集弥合规划状态可比性与反事实监督两处缺口，FIRM-WM 以紧凑参数量在多个图像目标任务上显著优于基线，并大幅降低规划耗时。

Abstract: Reward-free latent world models can learn from offline videos and solve new image--goal tasks by optimizing actions through predicted latent futures. This setting places two demands on the planning state: its coordinates must be comparable with a goal image. Moreover, its dynamics must retain velocity, motion trend, contact, and other history--dependent information beyond those goal coordinates. Offline training creates a second mismatch: each recorded trajectory reveals one factual future, whereas a sampling--based planner compares many actions that were not taken from the same state. We introduce FIRM-WM (Factual--Interventional Recurrent World Model), a compact pixel world model designed around these two gaps. Its recurrent state separates a typed, goal--comparable configuration from a 128-dimensional dynamic fiber used for prediction but excluded from the terminal goal cost. Broad factual trajectories provide state coverage, while common--reset intervention branches provide observed outcomes for alternative action sequences. Before executing each branch, we reset the environment and restore the same recorded values exposed by the environment's state--setting interface. Under matched CEM planning and three independent full-pipeline seeds, FIRM-WM reaches 99.0$\pm$1.0% on TwoRoom, 92.7$\pm$2.1% on Reacher, and 88.0$\pm$3.0% on OGBench-Cube, compared with 89.0%, 88.0%, and 70.0% for LeWM. The deployed model uses 2.98--3.42M parameters and records 2.13--11.60$\times$ lower planning time on these tasks.

</details>


### [108] [Counterfactual Tool Ranking under Utility, Cost, and Privilege Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22819)
*Jiapeng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以11个可执行企业级工具、精确倾向日志和真实本地MCP传输为实验平台，构建一套可证伪的反事实工具评估方法，用于区分评估结果的权威性、历史支持度与比较所估计的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实工具评估中常混淆三类问题：结果的权威性来源、历史数据支持范围、以及比较实际估计的因果量。作者希望厘清这些区别，并通过独立公开证据检验评估结论是否稳健，而非提出新的DR估计量或生产级安全结论。

Method: 使用11个企业启发的可执行工具与精确倾向日志、真实本地Model Context Protocol传输；先做45次合成实验，再用30次实现回报对照实验和15组基于1,930个独立发布的BFCL任务的实验进行检验；在函数名分组不相交的BFCL派生划分上比较直接回归与DR选择器；并评估两个固定的本地Qwen2.5模型在200个留出任务上的表现，包括缺失支持下的策略差异与保持分歧的兜底方案。

Result: 全回报直接回归在设定下反转了最初有利的DR结果：MAE为0.0139（直接回归）对0.0272（DR）；但在偏移环境下DR保持优势，误差0.0227对0.0948。函数名分组不相交划分上，直接与DR选择器的平衡准确率分别为81.85%与79.83%。两个本地Qwen2.5模型在固定提示下表现出强烈的不弃权失败。在缺失支持下，两策略共享的不支持动作会相互抵消，即使绝对量不可识别，仍可点识别增量变化；保持分歧的兜底在全部5个支持缺口实验中实现该性质，但保守采样边界无法证明部署改进。

Conclusion: 论文的贡献是提出一种可证伪的评估方法并提供独立公开证据，而非新DR估计量、官方BFCL榜单成绩或生产智能体安全声明；评估结论对设定与支持条件高度敏感，因此需要区分权威性、历史支持与实际估计目标。

Abstract: Counterfactual tool evaluation must distinguish authority, historical support, and what a comparison actually estimates. We study these distinctions with eleven executable enterprise-inspired tools, exact-propensity logs, and real local Model Context Protocol transport. An initial 45-run synthetic study is retained, then challenged by 30 realized-return control runs and 15 experiments on 1,930 independently released Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard (BFCL) tasks. Full-return direct regression reverses an initially favorable doubly robust (DR) evaluation result in the linear setting: mean absolute errors are 0.0139 for direct regression and 0.0272 for DR. Under a shifted environment, DR retains an advantage, with errors 0.0227 versus 0.0948. On function-name-group-disjoint BFCL-derived splits, direct and DR selectors obtain balanced accuracies of 81.85% and 79.83%. Two pinned local Qwen2.5 models are evaluated on the same 200 held-out tasks, exposing a strong failure to abstain under the fixed prompt. We further characterize policy differences under missing support: unsupported actions shared by two policies cancel, allowing point identification of an incremental change when neither absolute value is identifiable. A disagreement-preserving fallback achieves this property in all five support-gap runs, but conservative sampling bounds do not certify deployment improvement. The contribution is a falsifiable evaluation method and independent public evidence, not a new DR estimator, official BFCL leaderboard score, or production-agent safety claim.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Average Error through Oracle-Informed Stress Tests for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22820)
*Xu Lin,Runheng Zuo,Shengxuan Xu,Qitai Tan,Hongyu Lin,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“配对、机制可控的压力测试”，把各预测步长下期望平方误差的变化分解为环境风险与预测-神谕距离，并用3个已知归因的端到端对照验证流程，随后在24个可部署预测器及多变量关系迁移场景中评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均平方误差本身无法区分预测性能下降是源于未来变得更不可预测（环境变化），还是源于预测值偏离条件均值（模型误差）。因此需要一个能对误差变化进行机制级、组件级归因的评测基准。

Method: 构造配对、机制可控的端到端压力测试：在每个lead time上将期望平方误差变化分解为环境风险与环境-神谕距离（使用评估模型无法获得的、以origin为条件的预测神谕）。设置null、environmental-only、information-gap三类已知归因对照检验流程正确性；在24个可部署预测器上测试frequent switching与outlier-variance feedback；并扩展到多变量关系迁移，跨6个模型与3种耦合强度，考察8通道系统及Ring/Block/Hub关系的匹配难度审计；最后对独立DGP实现做预设对比稳定性审计。

Result: 对照检验通过：流程将变化归因到正确组件。频繁切换下14个方法的实际MSE更高但oracle distance更低；离群方差反馈下19个方法MSE更高但尺度标准化MSE更低。短/长lead压力响应排名Spearman相关0.624，显示明显horizon-dependent重排。多变量关系中oracle distance仅占分解期望风险增量的0.7–3.9%，环境风险占主导，并在8通道系统和匹配难度审计中持续。预设对比显示趋势累积与假设的switching reversal等发现不可复现。

Conclusion: 该基准将组件化诊断与留出稳定性审计结合，能区分环境风险与模型-神谕差距；它补充而非替代真实数据OOD评估——后者在无法获得精确oracle归因时衡量现实迁移下的性能。

Abstract: Average squared error cannot reveal whether forecasting performance degrades because the future becomes less predictable or because forecasts move farther from the conditional mean. We introduce paired, mechanism-controlled stress tests that decompose changes in expected squared error at each lead time into environmental risk and forecast-oracle distance, using an origin-conditioned predictive oracle unavailable to the evaluated models. Three end-to-end controls have known attribution. Specifically, the null, environmental-only, and information-gap controls verify that the pipeline assigns changes to the correct component. We then apply the benchmark to 24 deployable forecasters. Under frequent switching, 14 methods have higher realized MSE but lower oracle distance; under outlier-variance feedback, 19 have higher MSE but lower scale-standardized MSE. Short- and long-lead stress-response rankings have Spearman correlation 0.624, revealing substantial horizon-dependent reordering. We then study multivariate relation shifts. Across six models and three coupling severities, oracle distance accounts for only 0.7-3.9% of the decomposed expected-risk increase, and environmental-risk majority persists in an eight-channel system and a matched-difficulty audit of Ring, Block, and Hub relations. Finally, prespecified contrasts on independent data-generating process (DGP) realizations show that several visually compelling discovery profiles, including trend accumulation and the hypothesized switching reversal, do not replicate. The benchmark thus combines component-wise diagnosis with a held-out stability audit. It complements real-data out-of-distribution evaluation, which measures performance under realistic shifts when exact oracle attribution is unavailable.

</details>


### [110] [Personalized Federated Reinforcement Learning via Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning: Convergence of Exact and Hessian-Free Meta-Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22833)
*Ali Beikmohammadi,Sarit Khirirat,Sindri Magnússon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Per-FedAvg-PG，分析个性化联邦强化学习的一阶收敛性与 Hessian-free 变体的偏差和驻点下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 n 个各自处于独立 Markov 决策过程的智能体，通过服务器协作学习共享的 MAML 风格策略初始化，使单个智能体只需一步本地策略梯度即可个性化。需要解决该设定下的收敛性刻画，以及精确元梯度依赖内循环 Hessian 带来的实际计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出 Per-FedAvg-PG：智能体在通信轮之间执行 τ 步局部随机元策略梯度。证明其在 K=O(ε^{-3/2}) 轮、τ=Θ(ε^{-1/2}) 局部步数下达到个性化目标的 ε-近似一阶驻点。利用强化学习结构性质（标准策略类正则性下各智能体目标梯度与 Hessian 一致有界且常数显式），使监督学习理论中的有界梯度与有界异质性条件自动满足，无需单独异质性假设。进一步分析 Hessian-free 变体并界定其偏差。

Result: 得到 K=O(ε^{-3/2}) 轮、τ=Θ(ε^{-1/2}) 的收敛率；证明 Hessian-free 变体的偏差有界，并构造出元梯度非零且量级为 α 的不动点，表明驻点下限是方法本身性质而非界所致。在表格型与神经导航实验中验证了预测行为，并显示对未见智能体的迁移在样本成本上比独立训练低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果将自适应步长识别为可调的个人化旋钮，并将曲率估计识别为决定精确元梯度是否可负担的关键量。

Abstract: We study personalized federated reinforcement learning, in which $n$ agents, each acting in its own Markov decision process, collaborate through a server to learn a shared MAML-style policy initialization that becomes effective for an individual agent once that agent adapts it with a single local policy-gradient step. We propose Per-FedAvg-PG, in which agents take $τ$ local stochastic meta-policy-gradient steps between communication rounds, and prove that it reaches an $\varepsilon$-approximate first-order stationary point of the personalized objective in $K=\mathcal O(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$ rounds with $τ=Θ(\varepsilon^{-1/2})$ local steps. The analysis rests on a structural feature of the reinforcement learning setting: under standard policy-class regularity, the per-agent objectives have uniformly bounded gradients and Hessians with explicit constants, so the bounded-gradient and bounded-heterogeneity conditions imposed by the supervised theory hold automatically and no separate heterogeneity assumption is needed. The exact meta-gradient requires the inner-loop policy Hessian, which our experiments identify as the practical bottleneck. We therefore analyze the Hessian-free variant, bound its bias, and exhibit fixed points at which the meta-gradient is nonzero and of order $α$, showing that the resulting stationarity floor is a property of the method rather than of the bound. Experiments on tabular and neural navigation confirm the predicted behavior and show transfer to unseen agents at an order of magnitude lower sample cost than independent training. Together these results identify the adaptation step size as a tunable personalization knob and the curvature estimate as the quantity that governs whether exact meta-gradients are affordable.

</details>


### [111] [A Hybrid Attention Model Learning Unified Time-aware Patch Representation for Irregular Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22836)
*Zhihao Lin,Li Lin,Qi Zhang,Kaiwen Xia,Shuai Wang,Jialin Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合注意力模型，学习统一的时间感知patch表示用于不规则多元时间序列（IMTS）预测，无需插补，并构建30B观测的VersaTSA预训练数据集，在三项IMTS基准上取得最优零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常依赖不规则多元时间序列，其存在观察间隔不一致、变量间异步采样及信息性缺失。现有时间序列基础模型要么通过插补注入虚假值，要么用基于索引的位置编码忽略连续时间，缺乏遵循原始IMTS模式的基础模型。

Method: 1）时间感知patch编码：将patch内可变数量的时间戳映射为固定大小嵌入，无需插补即可统一不规则patch格式；2）时间偏置注意力：以辅助注意力偏移校准patch间时间错位与异步跨通道依赖；3）基于decoder-only Transformer，采用混合因果掩码，对历史上下文保持双向全视图，对预测区间保持严格自回归；4）构建VersaTSA，包含30B观测并保留源数据原生采样稀疏性，用于大规模预训练。

Result: 在三项IMTS基准和一项标准规则MTS基准上实验，模型在IMTS上取得最先进的零样本性能，迁移到规则预测时也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了遵循原始IMTS模式的基础模型空白，通过时间感知patch表示、时间偏置注意力和混合因果掩码实现统一不规则建模，并验证了大规模不规则数据预训练的有效性与跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have recently delivered impressive zero-shot performance across diverse forecasting tasks. However, real-world decision-making frequently relies on \emph{irregular multivariate time series} (IMTS), where inconsistent inter-observation intervals and asynchronous sampling across variables coexist with informative missingness. Existing TSFMs handle such inputs either through imputation that injects spurious values or through index-based positional encodings that ignore continuous time. There is still a gap in the foundation model that follows the original IMTS patterns. In this paper, we propose a hybrid attention model that learns a unified time-aware patch representation for IMTS forecasting. We first design a \emph{time-aware patch encoding} that maps a variable number of intra-patch timestamps into a fixed-size embedding, producing a uniform format for irregular patches without resorting to imputation. We then introduce a \emph{time bias attention} mechanism that calibrates inter-patch temporal misalignment and asynchronous cross-channel dependencies as auxiliary attention offset. Finally, on top of a decoder-only Transformer backbone, we adopt a \emph{hybrid causal mask} that preserves a bidirectional full view over the historical context while keeping the forecast horizon strictly autoregressive. To support large-scale pretraining under irregular settings, we also curate VersaTSA, an archive of $30$B observations that retains the native sampling sparsity of its sources. Experiments on three IMTS benchmarks and a standard regular-MTS benchmark show that our model achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on IMTS and remains competitive when transferred to regular forecasting.

</details>


### [112] [Testing the Construct Validity of a Functional Valence Axis in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22850)
*Weihan Li,Xinlei Chen,Yuhan Song,Xiaofeng Lin,Tianshi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究"好-坏结果方向"这一对比激活方向究竟表征了何种构念：通过控制干预分离"实际结果"与"获知结果的告知历史"，发现方向可跨显式编码迁移，但受先前是否被预告的显著影响，故支持功能性价值相关解释，而非历史不变的标量效价状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比激活方向常被按"其解码内容"或"其引导行为的强度"来解读，但缺乏充分证据说明它表征的是构念本身，还是提取该对比时所用对比项的一个相关特征（correlated feature）。作者希望厘清：什么样的证据才足以识别方向所代表的构念。

Method: 在迷宫任务中研究good–bad结果方向。设计受控干预，将"已实现结果"（realised outcome）与"该结果得以被知晓的信息历史"（informational history）分离，并对比显式结果编码的不同形式，以及"有预告"与"无预告"历史下到达同一结果的情形；在多个LLM检查点（checkpoints）上做跨形式迁移测试，并配合一次匹配的迷宫强化学习（maze-RL）运行进行对照。

Result: 1）在多个LLM检查点上，基于一种显式结果编码拟合的方向可良好迁移到另一种编码，说明该读出并不绑定于表层形式；2）但同一实际结果经"有预告"与"无预告"两种历史到达时，迁移显著退化：即使两种历史随后接收到相同的显式结果，事后读出仍强烈条件于先前的预告；3）在匹配的迷宫RL运行中，RL后方向对参考MDP剩余回报（reference-MDP remaining return）的预测性大幅增强，策略在被测试位点上对其依赖更强，而上述历史依赖依然存在。

Conclusion: 结果支持对该方向作功能性、与价值相关的解读，但不支持将其等同于一个历史不变的标量效价状态（history-invariant scalar valence state）。

Abstract: Contrastive activation directions are often interpreted from what they decode or how strongly they steer behavior. But what evidence is sufficient to identify the construct represented by such a direction, rather than a correlated feature of the contrast used to extract it? We study this question for a good--bad outcome direction in a maze task, using controlled interventions that separate the realised outcome from the informational history through which it became known. Across multiple LLM checkpoints, directions fitted on one explicit outcome encoding transfer well to another, indicating that the readout is not tied to surface form. In contrast, when the same realised outcome is reached through announced and unannounced histories, transfer degrades substantially: even after both histories receive the same explicit outcome, the post-event readout remains strongly conditioned on the earlier announcement. In a matched maze-RL run, the post-RL direction becomes substantially more predictive of reference-MDP remaining return and the policy becomes more dependent on it at the tested sites, while this history dependence persists. These results support a functional, value-related interpretation of the direction, but not its identification with a history-invariant scalar valence state.

</details>


### [113] [CurvFlow-DTA: dual-graph discrete Ricci curvature flow for drug--target affinity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22862)
*Jicheng Ma,Yunyan Yang,Juan Zhao,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CurvFlow-DTA用分子图与蛋白残基接触图上的加权Forman曲率流替代静态曲率，配以成对条件选择器和双分支Flow-GINE，在Davis、KIBA的暖启动与冷启动场景均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曲率感知DTA方法多为药物图上的静态曲率，蛋白端主要依赖序列派生特征，成对自适应的图几何利用不足，难以适应冷启动中的未见实体。

Method: 在药物分子图与蛋白残基-残基接触图上计算加权Forman曲率流；为每个实体预计算与标签无关的曲率流轨迹；用成对条件选择器决定双分支Flow-GINE所读的时间尺度（horizons）；冻结的ESM-2提供残基级表示与接触分数以构建蛋白图；推理仅需SMILES与蛋白序列，无需结合复合物结构。

Result: Davis和KIBA上，所有暖启动与冷启动设置均优于协议对齐的Ricci-GraphDTA基线。暖启动MSE分别下降19.9%和18.9%；六个冷启动比较中MSE下降14.3%–27.4%，CI全程更高。在汇编的文献基线集合内，CurvFlow-DTA在两个暖启动基准上取得最低MSE，并在六个冷启动设置中的四个取得最低MSE。

Conclusion: 成对自适应、基于曲率流的图几何建模可有效提升DTA预测，并在冷启动泛化上展现优势，且不依赖复合物结构即可推理。

Abstract: Graph neural networks are widely used for drug--target affinity (DTA) prediction, and discrete Ricci curvature has recently been used to characterize molecular graph geometry. Existing curvature-aware DTA approaches mainly use static curvature on the drug graph while representing proteins primarily with sequence-derived features. This leaves pair-adaptive use of graph geometry underexplored, which may limit adaptation to unseen entities in cold-start settings relevant to practical screening.
  We present CurvFlow-DTA, which replaces a single static curvature representation with weighted Forman curvature flow on both molecular and protein residue--residue contact graphs. A label-independent flow trajectory is precomputed for each entity, and a pair-conditioned selector determines the horizons read by a dual-branch Flow-GINE. A frozen ESM-2 supplies residue-level representations and contact scores used to construct the protein graph. Inference requires only SMILES strings and protein sequences, without a bound complex structure.
  On Davis and KIBA, CurvFlow-DTA improves on the protocol-matched Ricci-GraphDTA baseline in every warm and cold-start setting. Warm-split mean squared error (MSE) decreases by $19.9\%$ on Davis and $18.9\%$ on KIBA. Across the six cold-start comparisons, MSE decreases by $14.3$--$27.4\%$, with higher concordance index (CI) throughout. Within our compiled set of literature baselines, CurvFlow-DTA achieves the lowest MSE on both warm benchmarks and across four out of six cold-start evaluation settings.

</details>


### [114] [Causilo Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22866)
*Minyong Cho,Minho Jeong,Dooho Lee,Jinmo Lee,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表格基础模型 Causilo，通过引入行内精炼模块并复用固定数量摘要 token 的交叉注意力，实现前沿预测性能与极快推理速度的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型难以同时满足前沿预测性能与快速推理，性能与效率之间存在权衡，需要一种能同时占据性能-效率帕累托前沿的方法。

Method: 采用 TabICL 的“先列后行”架构，在行压缩前新增行精炼模块：在列编码后于每行内部交换单元格表示信息，精炼后的单元再经额外列阶段重新访问上下文集，随后压缩为行嵌入。两个行阶段均通过固定数量的摘要 token 做交叉注意力，使注意力开销随特征数线性增长。

Result: 在 TabArena 上取得 1785.4 Elo，每 1K 测试样本推理时间中位数为 0.10 秒，相比 TabPFN-3.5-Fast 推理时间减少 31.6%，位于性能-效率帕累托前沿；在 TabArena、BeyondArena 和 ScoringBench 上均取得前沿级表现，训练数据约 3.6 千万张合成表格。

Conclusion: Causilo 在保持前沿水平预测性能的同时显著提升推理效率，验证了行精炼结构与线性复杂度注意力设计在表格基础模型中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Causilo, a tabular foundation model (TFM) that combines frontier predictive performance with exceptionally fast inference. On TabArena, Causilo achieves 1785.4 Elo, at a median inference time of 0.10 seconds per 1K test samples. It outperforms TabPFN-3.5-Fast with 31.6% less inference time, placing it on the performance--efficiency Pareto frontier. Causilo follows TabICL's column-then-row architecture but introduces another row-refinement module before row compression. This module exchanges information among cell representations within each row after column encoding. The refined cells then visit the context set again through an additional column stage before being compressed into row embeddings. For inference efficiency, both row stages use cross-attention through a fixed number of summary tokens, keeping their attention cost linear in the number of features. Pretrained on approximately 36M synthetic tables, Causilo delivers strong benchmark results across TabArena, BeyondArena, and ScoringBench, achieving frontier-level performance with substantially faster inference.

</details>


### [115] [Leveraging Inference-Time Compute for Diffusion Models via Global Scheduling of Denoising Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22867)
*Yuan Cao,Yifu Tang,Hangqi Li,Zeyu Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将扩散模型推理中verifier引导的多候选采样形式化为计算预算分配问题，证明最优分配具有可分离凹整数规划的水填充结构，并提出离线锚定加在线校正的算法，在三个人工采样器族上以少20%–50%的函数评估达到均匀分配的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型部署时可通过在去噪轨迹每步采样多个候选、用verifier打分并保留最优候选来提升样本质量，但每个候选都需一次网络评估。在固定函数评估预算下，如何把这些搜索努力分配到轨迹各步之前没有系统的理论刻画，实践中通常采用均匀分配，可能造成预算浪费。

Method: 1) 将逐步多候选搜索建模为计算预算分配问题，在步长的首阶近似下，把在某步评估K个候选的期望增益分解为“内生的步敏感度参数 × 与步无关的通用样本量因子（K个标准正态抽样期望最优值）”。2) 给定敏感度剖面，证明最优分配是具水填充结构的可分离凹整数规划，并在总敏感度固定时，用majorization序刻画：敏感度越分散，相对均匀分配的优势越大。3) 证明敏感度随实例变化时，任何自适应策略的最坏情况遗憾随轨迹长度线性增长，据此设计离线锚定分配、在线仅回收实例松弛的算法。4) 将分析从独立随机搜索推广到更广的局部搜索算子族，并实例化为可实现的算法。

Result: 理论结果包括：期望增益的敏感度—样本量因子分解、最优分配的水填充/凹整数规划刻画、以及基于majorization序的分散度优势结论；同时给出自适应策略的最坏情况线性遗憾下界。实验在三个人工采样器族上验证，所提分配方法在达到均匀基准质量的同时减少20%–50%的函数评估次数。

Conclusion: 扩散采样中的多候选verifier搜索可归结为具有水填充结构的最优预算分配问题；由于自适应策略存在随轨迹长度线性增长的遗憾下界，稳健方案应采用离线锚定并仅在线校正松弛。该框架可推广至一般局部搜索算子，并在实验中显著降低达到同等样本质量所需的函数评估成本。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate a sample by traversing a denoising trajectory, a sequence of stochastic noise-reduction steps that transforms pure noise into a draw from a target distribution. At deployment time, additional computation can improve sample quality without retraining: at each step, the sampler draws several candidate noise samples, scores the resulting predictions with a quality criterion called the verifier, and retains the best candidate at the cost of one network evaluation per candidate. This raises a resource allocation question: given a fixed budget of function evaluations, how should search effort be distributed across the steps of the denoising trajectory? We formulate this as a computational budget allocation problem. First, we show that, to leading order in the step size, the expected gain from evaluating $K$ candidates at a step factorizes into an endogenous, step-specific sensitivity parameter times a universal sample-size factor equal to the expected best of $K$ standard-normal draws. Second, for a fixed sensitivity profile, the optimal allocation solves a separable concave integer program with water-filling structure; at fixed total sensitivity, its advantage over uniform allocation increases with sensitivity dispersion in the majorization order. Third, we prove that when sensitivities vary across instances, no adaptive policy can avoid worst-case regret that grows linearly in the trajectory length, which motivates a design that anchors the allocation offline and adapts online only to recover instance-specific slack. We extend the analysis from independent random search to a broader family of local search operators, and instantiate it as an implementable algorithm. Experiments on three families of diffusion samplers show that the proposed allocation attains the quality of the uniform benchmark with 20 to 50 percent fewer function evaluations.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Full Pipeline FP8 Reinforcement Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22870)
*Fanchao Chen,Ziheng Jiang,Ziyun Wei,Zheng Zhong,Du Li,Chi Zhang,Haibin Lin,Shivaram Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示全流程FP8强化学习存在严重的训练不稳定问题，其根源是复合FP8量化噪声扭曲重要性比率；提出Calibrated Clipping动态方法对齐FP8裁剪边界，成功消除熵激增并恢复至BF16基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP8量化虽能加速LLM的RL训练，但全流程FP8 RL仍难以保持稳定。以往工作多聚焦于用TIS等校正技术解决训练-推理失配，然而作者发现全流程FP8 RL依然会出现训练中期的熵异常激增与输出乱码。作者追溯到一个此前被忽视的原因：复合FP8量化噪声扭曲重要性比率，将负优势token不成比例地推出信任域并错误地将其梯度置零，导致病态输出未受惩罚并随训练累积。

Method: 提出Calibrated Clipping（校准裁剪）：一种动态方法，通过匹配下界裁剪分位数并相应重新平衡上界，使FP8裁剪边界与高精度BF16分布对齐。

Result: 在GRPO与DAPO算法、8B至32B多种模型规模、以及多种FP8缩放粒度上的大量实验表明，该方法成功消除熵激增，并恢复到与BF16基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 复合FP8量化噪声是导致全流程FP8 RL不稳定的关键且被忽视的因素，通过Calibrated Clipping校准裁剪边界可有效稳定FP8 RL训练并保持性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key technique for improving the reasoning and agentic abilities of large language models (LLMs). Although FP8 quantization can accelerate RL training, maintaining stability throughout an FP8 RL pipeline remains challenging. While previous works have focused on resolving train-inference mismatches using correction techniques like TIS, we reveal that full-pipeline FP8 RL still suffers from severe training instability, manifesting as anomalous mid-training entropy surges and garbled outputs. We trace this instability to a previously overlooked cause: compounded FP8 quantization noise distorts the importance ratio, disproportionately pushing negative-advantage tokens outside the trust region and erroneously zeroing out their gradients. As a result, pathological outputs are not properly penalized and accumulate over the course of training. To address this, we propose Calibrated Clipping, a dynamic method that aligns the FP8 clipping bounds with high-precision BF16 distributions by matching the lower-bound clipping quantile and rebalancing the upper bound accordingly. Extensive experiments across GRPO and DAPO algorithms, model scales from 8B to 32B, and multiple FP8 scaling granularities demonstrate that our approach successfully eliminates entropy surges and restores performance comparable to the BF16 baseline.

</details>


### [117] [Prioritized Rollouts for Efficient World Model-based Vision-Language-Action Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22879)
*Yifei Sheng,Haoxiang Ren,Zhilong Zhang,Haonan Wang,Runjie Xu,Yihao Sun,Nan Tang,Zhichao Wu,Lei Yuan,Haoxin Lin,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出U-GROW：一种轻量、即插即用的采样层，利用策略不确定性识别对策略改进更有价值的状态，将更多世界模型rollout导向这些高信息状态，从而在VLA的MBRL微调中降低计算成本并提升效率。仅修改分支起始分布，不改变策略优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型具备强具身智能潜力，但用RL微调受限于真实机器人交互成本；MBRL虽用学习到的世界模型降低交互成本，却在VLA策略与世界模型规模化时计算代价高昂。现有方法平等对待各状态，忽视了不同状态对策略改进价值的显著差异。

Method: 发现策略不确定性可识别更具改进潜力的状态：策略仅在一小部分状态上呈现高不确定性，常出现在决策敏感阶段（微小动作差异即可改变任务结果）。据此设计U-GROW——轻量、即插即用的采样层，通过修改模型rollout的分支起始分布，将更多rollout分配给这些高信息状态，可直接嵌入现有MBRL流程，不改变策略优化目标。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界操作任务上的实验验证了U-GROW的高效性与有效性，支持用策略不确定性指导经验生成。

Conclusion: 策略不确定性可作为经验生成的有效引导信号；U-GROW以极小改动实现采样聚焦，为VLA的MBRL微调提供了高效、通用且易集成的方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for embodied intelligence, but fine-tuning them with reinforcement learning (RL) remains constrained by the cost of real-world robot interaction. Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) reduces this cost by using a learned world model to generate rollouts for policy optimization. However, it becomes computationally expensive as VLA policies and world models scale. Existing methods typically treat states equally, overlooking substantial differences in their utility for policy improvement. In this paper, we show that policy uncertainty helps identify states with greater potential for policy improvement. The policy exhibits high uncertainty at only a small subset of states, often during decision-sensitive stages where small action differences can alter task outcomes, suggesting that policy improvements at these states could be particularly valuable. Building on these findings, we introduce U-GROW, a lightweight, plug-and-play sampling layer that directs more model rollouts to these informative states. By modifying only the branched-start distribution, U-GROW can be integrated into existing MBRL pipelines without changing the policy optimization objective. Experiments in both simulated and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate the efficiency and effectiveness of U-GROW, supporting the use of policy uncertainty to guide experience generation.

</details>


### [118] [Merge++: Universal Merge Refinement Through Data-Free Checkpoint Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22886)
*Aditya Pola,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Merge++，一种无需数据的后处理模型合并方法，通过反转专家检查点合成任务代表性图像，并据此将专家知识蒸馏进合并模型，可通用地提升各类权重空间合并算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无数据模型合并方法完全在权重空间操作，仅对参数做算术运算，从未观察每个专家的实际行为。而行为信号只能通过前向评估产生，需要输入数据，这与无数据设定相冲突。

Method: Merge++ 在合并后作为独立的精炼阶段：先反转专家检查点合成任务代表性图像，得到可评估的输入；再利用这些图像把专家知识蒸馏进合并模型。它除检查点外不需任何额外数据，与底层权重空间合并算法解耦，通用适配各类合并算法。

Result: 方法稳定提升从简单任务算术到最新谱方法的多种合并算法，平均增益 +2 至 +8 点，个别配置最高提升 +25.9 点。

Conclusion: Merge++ 通过在合并流程中引入行为信号，弥补了纯权重空间方法的局限，作为互补的后处理精炼步骤，可普遍且显著地增强现有模型合并算法，且保持无数据约束。

Abstract: Model merging consolidates fine-tuned experts into one multi-task model without retraining. All existing data-free methods approach this problem entirely in weight space. Restricted to arithmetic on parameters, these methods never observe how each expert behaves, a signal that only emerges through forward evaluation. Accessing this behavioral signal requires inputs to evaluate on, which the data-free setting prohibits. We propose Merge++, a post-hoc method that addresses this by inverting the expert checkpoints to synthesize task-representative images, then distilling expert knowledge into the merged model using those images. Merge++ requires no additional data beyond the checkpoints themselves. It applies universally across merging algorithms and operates as a complementary refinement stage independent of the underlying weight-space method. The method consistently improves merging algorithms ranging from simple task arithmetic to state-of-the-art spectral methods, with average gains of +2 to +8 points and up to +25.9 on individual configurations.

</details>


### [119] [Are Coreset Selection Methods Worth Their Cost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22894)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出端到端的coreset选择基准，将选择与训练成本统一计入可审计的墙钟预算，在4个数据集、11种选择器、5个比例、3个随机种子上共1500+次运行。结果显示在各预算锚点下获胜者均为类均衡随机采样、重复随机采样或全量训练，无复杂选择器胜出；ImageNet-1K上少轮次全量训练优于所有选择策略且成本最低。选择成本被固定的全数据集扫描主导，无法通过减小选择比例摊销，且不跨数据集外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有coreset选择方法通常以下游固定子集规模下的准确率评估，既忽略了选择子集所耗时间，也未固定训练配方，导致比较不公平、结论不可比。已有重复采样研究表明预算感知评估已偏向随机策略，本文检验各选择器在最优操作点下该结论是否仍成立。

Method: 构建标准化端到端基准：统一下游训练流程，并将选择与训练计入同一可审计墙钟预算；覆盖CIFAR-10到ImageNet-1K的4个数据集、11种选择器、5个选择比例、3个随机种子，发布1500+次运行。开展预算研究：在CIFAR-10与Tiny ImageNet各8个墙钟预算锚点上对比各选择器最优操作点；在ImageNet-1K进行固定预算对决；并进行逐数据集成本审计，分析选择成本构成、可摊销性及跨数据集外推性，同时量化子集复用下的收益，并修复常用代码库的9处正确性问题。

Result: CIFAR-10与Tiny ImageNet的8个预算锚点无一由复杂选择器获胜，获胜者均为类均衡随机采样、重复随机采样或全量训练；ImageNet-1K固定预算对决中，少轮次全量训练优于所有被测选择策略且成本最低。成本审计显示选择成本在各规模下由固定全数据集扫描主导，无法通过选择更小比例摊销，其绝对规模也不可从一数据集外推到另一数据集。9处修复中一处使标准Herding基线变化近6个百分点。

Conclusion: 选择时间并非免费预处理，忽略它的评估度量了错误的量；预算感知的端到端评估表明，在当前方法下复杂coreset选择难以在真实墙钟预算下胜过简单随机或全量训练。

Abstract: Coreset selection picks a representative subset of the labeled training set to make training cheaper. However, it is usually evaluated by downstream accuracy at a fixed subset size, ignoring both the time spent selecting the subset and the training recipe behind each reported number. We introduce an end-to-end benchmark that standardizes downstream training and charges selection and training to the same auditable wall-clock budget, spanning 4 datasets from CIFAR-10 to ImageNet-1K, 11 selectors, 5 fractions, and 3 seeds, with over 1,500 released runs. Repeated-sampling work has shown that budget-aware evaluation already favors random strategies. Our two budget studies test whether that verdict survives when every selector is granted its most favorable operating point. Across eight wall-clock budget anchors on each of CIFAR-10 and Tiny ImageNet, no anchor is won by a sophisticated selector: every winner is class-balanced random sampling, repeated random sampling, or full-data training. In fixed-budget duels on ImageNet-1K, training on all data for fewer epochs beats every selection strategy we probe while also costing the least. A per-dataset cost audit shows that selection cost is dominated at every scale by a fixed full-dataset scan, so it cannot be amortized away by selecting a smaller fraction, and its absolute size does not extrapolate from one dataset to another. We further quantify when selection does pay back through subset reuse, and document 9 correctness fixes to a widely used codebase, one of which shifts a standard Herding baseline by nearly 6 points. Selection time is not free preprocessing, and an evaluation that ignores it measures the wrong quantity.

</details>


### [120] [Computationally efficient safe exploration in reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22919)
*Shreeram Murali,Shankar A. Deka,Dominik Baumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Nadaraya-Watson估计器的轻量级安全强化学习算法CoLSafe-MDP，用于约束MDP的安全探索与优化，计算复杂度为常数时间，并在栅格环境和火星地形数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中的强化学习需要在探索阶段提供安全保证。传统强化学习算法缺乏此类保证，而依赖高斯过程（GP）的改进方法虽能提供保证，却面临巨大计算开销（随数据点数量三次方增长），难以规模化应用。

Method: 基于Nadaraya-Watson估计器构建算法CoLSafe-MDP，在约束马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中进行安全探索与优化。该估计器具备估计值边界，且计算复杂度为常数时间，相较GP方法（三次方复杂度）显著降低。

Result: 在栅格（grid）环境中评估算法性能，并进一步在观测性火星地形数据上进行实验验证。

Conclusion: CoLSafe-MDP以常数时间计算代价实现了安全探索，克服了GP方法计算昂贵的瓶颈，展示了在真实场景（如火星地形）中应用的可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in real-life applications requires safety guarantees during exploration. Typical reinforcement learning algorithms do not provide such guarantees, and many modifications that do rely on Gaussian processes (GPs), which have a large computational cost. We propose a computationally lightweight algorithm based on the Nadaraya-Watson estimator that safely explores and optimizes constrained Markov decision processes (MDPs). Our algorithm, \textsc{CoLSafe-MDP}, uses an estimator that scales in constant-time with bounds on the estimates, a significant improvement from its GP-based counterparts that scale cubically with the number of data points. We then evaluate its performance in a grid-based environment and on observational Martian terrain data.

</details>


### [121] [Joint Domain-Class Modeling for Federated Learning Under Feature Skew](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22932)
