*Sina Najafi,Mostafa Tavassolipour,Seyed Pooya Shariatpanahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JDFL，一种轻量、与优化器无关的联邦学习方法，通过推断伪域并将分类头扩展为域-类联合logits，配合相似度感知软标签与随机目标分配两种监督策略，在特征偏移下提升全局测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端共享标签但条件输入分布p_i(x|y)因客户端特定的隐式外观因素而变化，导致特征偏移并使性能下降。作者希望在不共享原始数据的前提下，让这种隐式域变化可被模型利用。

Method: JDFL先从简短的本地更新信号推断域聚类（伪域）；再将分类头扩展为M×C的联合（域-类）logits以表示域条件外观，同时保持共享主干。为训练扩展头，引入两种互补监督：相似度感知软标签（在相近伪域间迁移证据，同时允许域特定特化）和逐样本随机目标分配（在联合输出上扰动监督，作为低成本训练期正则）。该扩展与FedAvg、SCAFFOLD等标准FL方法兼容，改动极小。

Result: 在标准域偏移图像基准上，两种监督模式均能持续提升全局测试精度；消融与敏感性研究表明增益来源于所提监督与参数化方式，而非单纯的容量增加。

Conclusion: JDFL以轻量、优化器无关的方式把隐式域变化转化为可用的域-类联合建模，缓解特征偏移，且易于集成到现有联邦学习框架中。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without centralizing private data, but performance often degrades under feature skew: clients share labels while the conditional input distributions $p_i(x\!\mid\!y)$ vary due to latent, client-specific appearance factors. We propose Joint Domain-Class Federated Learning (JDFL), a lightweight, optimizer-agnostic extension that makes this latent domain variation usable without sharing raw data. JDFL first infers domain clusters called pseudo-domains from brief local update signals. It then expands the classifier head to output $M\times C$, joint (domain-class) logits. This allows the model to represent domain-conditioned appearance while keeping a shared backbone. To train the expanded head we introduce two complementary supervision strategies based on simple intuitions: a similarity-aware soft-labeling that transfers evidence between nearby inferred domains while allowing domain-specific specialization, and a per-sample randomized target assignment that perturbs supervision across the joint outputs and serves as a low-cost training-time regularizer. JDFL integrates with existing standard FL methods (e.g., FedAvg, SCAFFOLD) with minimal changes. Empirically, both supervision modes consistently improve global test accuracy on standard domain-shifted image benchmarks; ablations and sensitivity studies show the gains stem from the proposed supervision and parametrization rather than mere capacity increase.

</details>


### [122] [Token Utility Is Selection-Conditioned: Coupled Selection of Prompt Context and Response Supervision for Efficient Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22943)
*Can Wu,Xinrui Chen,Ou Wu,Yi Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRIDGE 提出预算化响应-提示交互的方向性梯度高效令牌选择方法，通过共享验证导向交互代理与交替选择，解决独立选择导致的“选择状态失配”，在数学推理、代码生成与指令跟随上优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效LLM指令微调需要在保留提示上下文的条件下选择响应监督；现有方法对提示与响应两侧分别估值，导致估值所用状态与最终保留训练子集状态不一致，即选择状态失配，从而影响选择质量。

Method: BRIDGE（Budgeted Response-Prompt Interaction via Directional Gradient-guided Efficient Token Selection）：以共享的验证导向交互代理，在另一侧保留状态下评估每侧令牌效用，获得选择条件化的令牌效用；采用预算化交替选择，将预计算交互在当前对侧子集上聚合，以更新条件分数并协同两侧保留子集；再用结构感知投影把条件响应分数转成连贯监督片段。

Result: 在三个模型家族上，BRIDGE 在数学推理、代码生成和指令跟随任务中总体上优于参与比较的选择方法；在数学推理中，相对独立选择方法的优势随压缩率提高而增大。

Conclusion: 通过建模响应-提示交互与交替预算选择，BRIDGE 缓解选择状态失配，并在多种任务与模型家族上取得更优的选择效果，尤其在更高压缩下保持更强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Efficient large language model (LLM) instruction tuning requires selecting response supervision with supporting prompt context. Existing methods typically value both sides separately, risking selection-state mismatch between valuation and retained training subsets. BRIDGE (Budgeted Response-Prompt Interaction via Directional Gradient-guided Efficient Token Selection) captures selection-conditioned token utility through a shared validation-directed interaction surrogate valuing each side under the other's retained state. Budgeted alternating selection coordinates retained subsets by aggregating precomputed interactions over the current opposite-side subset to update conditional scores. Structure-aware projection converts conditional response scores into coherent supervision spans. Across three model families, BRIDGE leads compared selection methods overall in mathematical reasoning, code generation, and instruction following. In mathematical reasoning, its advantage over independent selection grows with compression.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Similarity: Coverage-Aware Prompt Selection for Time Series Forecasting with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22977)
*Daeun Ji,Minkyoung Kim,Dongkuk Kim,Yohan Lee,Beomsoo Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对基于相似度的检索在提示式时间序列预测中导致的“近似重复偏差”，提出 CASP-LLM，通过使用量追踪与饱和门控构成的覆盖正则项（不增加可学习参数）来缓解提示选择偏差，并在多数数据集-预测步长设置上匹配或优于相似度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似度检索是 LLM 上下文学习、检索增强生成及提示式时间序列预测中的主流条件化规则，但其倾向于集中在近重复候选上，导致偏向主导性时序模式、忽略稀有但具信息量的事件。尽管已有面向多样性的检索研究，该问题在其他检索条件化流程中仍未获充分检验。

Method: 以提示式时间序列预测为试验平台，其中学习型提示池按相似度检索。主流方法按余弦相似度取 Top-K 且无冗余控制。作者提出 CASP-LLM：将使用量追踪（usage-tracking）与饱和门控（saturating-gate）结合为覆盖正则化器，且不引入可学习参数。

Result: 在六个长期基准与 M4 短期基准上，CASP-LLM 在多数数据集-预测步长设置上匹配或优于基于相似度的 LLM 预测器；例外为 Electricity、M4-Monthly 及少样本长时程设置。受控实验表明失败模式位于跨批次使用量层面，而非单次检索冗余：如 MMR 的检索内多样化无帮助，而跨训练正则化锚点使用量有效。

Conclusion: 提示选择的偏差本质是跨批次使用层面的问题，需在使用量维度而非单次检索内做多样化；CASP-LLM 以无参数的覆盖正则化有效缓解该偏差，并界定了其适用边界。

Abstract: Similarity-based retrieval is the dominant rule for conditioning large language models (LLMs) in in-context learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and prompt-based time series forecasting. The rule concentrates on near-duplicate candidates, an issue that has motivated diversity-aware retrieval but remains unexamined in other retrieval-conditioned pipelines. We study this issue using prompt-based time series forecasting as a test bed, where a learned prompt pool is retrieved by similarity. Dominant methods in this setting retrieve top-K entries by cosine similarity without redundancy control, producing a bias toward dominant temporal patterns while overlooking rare but informative events. We propose CASP-LLM, a coverage-aware semantic prompting framework that addresses this prompt selection bias by combining usage-tracking and saturating-gate techniques into a coverage regularizer that adds no learnable parameters. On six long-term benchmarks and the M4 short-term benchmark, CASP-LLM matches or improves on similarity-based LLM forecasters on most dataset-horizon settings, with the exceptions of Electricity, M4-Monthly, and the few-shot long-horizon setting. A controlled study locates the failure mode at the cross-batch usage level rather than per-retrieval redundancy: within-retrieval diversification such as MMR does not help, whereas regularizing anchor usage across training does.

</details>


### [124] [LPINNs: First-Layer Gated Localization for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22984)
*Lakshay Chawla,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在未改变结构的PINN第一隐藏层激活上乘以输入相关的局部化函数,为第一层单元赋予感受野,可在长域与高阶问题上显著降低解误差,但收益高度依赖局部化函数族的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINN在整个计算域上共享单一表示,在长域和高阶算子问题上优化困难。作者希望寻找一种比域分解更轻量的替代方案,即在不划分域、不引入界面损失的前提下赋予第一层单元局部感受野。

Method: 将原本不变的稠密PINN的第一个隐藏层激活乘以输入相关的局部化函数。系统筛选13个局部化函数族,每个族最多3种参数化方式,并在非线性谐振子(HO)、长空间区间热方程以及构造的四维(4D)四阶问题上进行测试,全程采用10组配对随机种子。

Result: 在相同预算下三种配置取得大幅误差下降:(i)固定高斯局部化在2π谐振子域上,3k轮将平均解RMSE从4.8369e-1降至8.83e-3;(ii)带可学习中心和宽度的反二次族在8π热域上,10k轮从2.896e-1降至3.06e-2;(iii)固定bump局部化在4π的4D域上,10k轮从1.75947e1降至2.260e-1。三组对比中所有配对种子均有改善。该机制并非稳赢:谐振子上13族中仅2族优于基线,其余11族中10族差9至23倍;4D上4族结果非有限,5族比基线差三个数量级以上。反二次族是唯一在三类方程上均优于基线的族。

Conclusion: 第一层局部化可为基线PINN在长域和高阶问题上带来可测量的改进,但效果强烈依赖于所选局部化函数族,反二次族表现出最好的通用性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) use one shared representation over the computational domain, which can become difficult to optimize on long domains and for high-order operators. We study a minimal alternative: multiply the first hidden activation of an otherwise unchanged dense PINN by input-dependent localization functions, giving first-layer units receptive fields without partitioning the domain or adding interface losses. We screen 13 families of localization functions, in up to three parameterizations each, on a nonlinear harmonic oscillator (HO), a heat equation on a long spatial interval, and a manufactured four-dimensional (4D) fourth-order problem, with ten paired seeds throughout. Three configurations give large reductions in solution error at matched budgets: (i) Fixed Gaussian localization functions on the $2π$ HO domain cut mean solution RMSE from $4.8369\times10^{-1}$ to $8.83\times10^{-3}$ at 3k epochs. (ii) The inverse-quadratic family with learnable centers and widths cuts it from $2.896\times10^{-1}$ to $3.06\times10^{-2}$ on the $8π$ heat domain at 10k epochs. (iii) Fixed bump localization functions cut it from $1.75947\times10^{1}$ to $2.260\times10^{-1}$ on the $4π$ 4D domain at 10k epochs. Every paired seed improves in these three comparisons. The screen also shows that the mechanism is not a free win: on HO only 2 of 13 families beat the baseline, and 10 of the remaining 11 are 9 to 23 times worse; on 4D four families are non-finite and five are more than three orders of magnitude worse than the baseline. The inverse-quadratic family is the only one that beats the baseline on all three equations. Overall, these results show that first-layer localization can provide measurable improvements to baseline PINNs on long-domain and high-order problems.

</details>


### [125] [On attention heads and bilinear forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22990)
*Andrew O'Desky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究训练后大语言模型注意力头中双线性形式的对称与反对称部分，引入正交不变剖面映射，发现其剖面聚集于秩一双线性形式的剖面附近，并证明在Zariski稠密子集上对称部分可分解为双曲型与零型之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解训练后大语言模型注意力机制中双线性形式的代数结构，为注意力头的几何与代数分析提供理论工具。

Method: 将实双线性形式通过正交不变的剖面映射映入三维单纯形，分析训练后形式的剖面分布；并对Zariski稠密子集上的query-key矩阵证明对称部分的分类定理。

Result: 训练后双线性形式的剖面聚集于秩一形式的剖面附近；对称部分可表示为双曲型形式与零型形式之和。

Conclusion: 注意力头中的双线性形式在训练后呈现低秩结构特征，其对称部分具有清晰的代数分解，揭示了注意力机制潜在的秩一倾向与几何规律。

Abstract: We study the symmetric and antisymmetric parts of bilinear forms in the attention heads of trained large language models. We introduce an orthogonally invariant profile map from real bilinear forms to a three-dimensional simplex and observe that profiles of trained bilinear forms accumulate near profiles of rank-one bilinear forms. We prove that the symmetric part of a bilinear form in an attention head is the sum of a hyperbolic form and a zero form for a Zariski-dense subset of query-key matrices.

</details>


### [126] [Interpretable Multi-Hypersphere Deep Anomaly Detection for Open-set Supervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23008)
*Zhiji Yang,Fangyong Wang,Yue Li,Xianli Pan,Jianhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IMHD-AD，为每个已知正常类构建独立超球，将类特定超球中心与半径嵌入最终网络层联合优化，用跨超球最小带符号边界分数判定开集接受/拒绝，实现可解释多类开集异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类开集异常检测需仅用已知正常类的类标签样本刻画由多个异质子分布构成的正常接受域，并在测试时识别未见异常。现有单超球方法无法显式表达类特定位置与接受范围；现有多超球或多类方法未充分整合类间边界约束、可学习接受范围与可解释决策。

Method: IMHD-AD在共享特征空间中为每个已知正常类构建独立超球；采用目标在内、非目标在外的约束，将类特定超球中心与半径直接嵌入网络最终层，并与共享表示联合优化；以跨各超球的最小带符号边界分数同时决定开集接受或拒绝，并为每个决策提供几何解释。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST与CIFAR-10上，IMHD-AD在30项开集比较中的28项取得最高AUC；二维合成实验进一步表明，模型架构需在已知正常类的紧致性与未知异常的可分性之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: IMHD-AD通过多超球嵌入与联合优化，统一了类间边界约束、可学习接受范围和可解释决策，在多类开集异常检测中表现优异，并揭示了紧致性与可分性之间的架构权衡。

Abstract: Multi-class open-set anomaly detection requires a model to characterize the normal acceptance domain formed by multiple heterogeneous subdistributions using only class-labeled samples from known normal classes, and to identify previously unseen anomalies at test time. Existing single-hypersphere methods cannot explicitly represent class-specific locations and acceptance ranges, while current multi-hypersphere or multi-class approaches do not fully integrate inter-class boundary constraints, learnable acceptance ranges, and interpretable decisions. To address these limitations, we propose Interpretable Multi-Hypersphere Deep Anomaly Detection (IMHD-AD). IMHD-AD constructs an independent hypersphere for each known normal class in a shared feature space. With target-inside and non-target-outside constraints, IMHD-AD embeds the class-specific hypersphere centers and radii directly into the final network layer and jointly optimizes them with the shared representation. The minimum signed boundary score across hyperspheres simultaneously determines open-set acceptance or rejection and provides a faithful geometric explanation of each decision. On MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10, IMHD-AD achieves the highest AUC in 28 of 30 open-set comparisons. A two-dimensional synthetic study further shows that model architecture must balance the compactness of known normal classes against the separability of unknown anomalies.

</details>


### [127] [WaveFront Decoding: Parallelized Self-Speculative Decoding for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23033)
*Hyeongju Ha,Jae-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练自推测解码框架 Wavefront Decoding（WFD），通过将不同深度与位置的 token 状态组织为对角波前，在同一循环调用中协同批处理草稿与验证，从而显著降低循环语言模型的解码延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过重复应用权重共享块来增加有效深度而不增加参数量，但每生成一个 token 需执行 T 次顺序循环块调用，导致解码延迟大幅上升。现有草稿-验证分离的推测解码方案未能充分利用该架构特性。

Method: WFD 利用循环语言模型的两个特性：中间循环输出可作为有效草稿预测；权重共享使不同位置、不同循环深度的 token 状态可在一次批处理循环块调用中处理。WFD 将这些混合深度状态组织成对角波前，在浅层持续草拟新位置，同时将较早位置推进至全深度验证。与草稿-验证分阶段调度不同，WFD 在同一循环调用内协同批处理草稿与验证，被拒绝的草稿则用全深度预测进行修正；并引入跨循环 KV 共享以降低波前 KV 流量。

Result: 在 Spec-Bench 六个任务类别上，WFD 相较自回归解码在 Ouro-2.6B 上实现 2.42 倍加速，在 Huginn-3.5B 上实现 3.54 倍加速，持续优于草稿-验证方案；跨循环 KV 共享进一步减少波前 KV 流量，使 Huginn-3.5B 上加速比提升至 4.81 倍。

Conclusion: WFD 是一种面向循环语言模型的免训练自推测解码框架，通过波前式混合深度批处理实现草稿与验证的协同执行，在不增加参数与训练成本的前提下大幅提升解码效率，并可通过 KV 共享进一步优化。

Abstract: Looped language models repeatedly apply a weight-shared block to increase effective depth without increasing parameter count, but the resulting T sequential recurrent-block calls per generated token substantially increase decoding latency. To address the issue, we introduce Wavefront Decoding (WFD), a training-free self-speculative decoding framework designed for looped language models. WFD exploits two properties of these architectures: intermediate recurrence outputs provide effective draft predictions, and weight sharing allows token states at different positions and recurrence depths to be processed in one batched recurrent-block call. WFD organizes these mixed-depth states into a diagonal wavefront, continuously drafting new positions at shallow depth while advancing earlier positions toward full-depth verification. Unlike the phase-separated draft-then-verify schedule, WFD therefore co-batches drafting and verification within the same recurrent calls, while rejected drafts are corrected using full-depth predictions. Across six Spec-Bench task categories, WFD achieves 2.42x speedup on Ouro-2.6B and 3.54x on Huginn-3.5B over autoregressive decoding, consistently outperforming draft-then-verify. Cross-recurrence KV sharing further reduces wavefront KV traffic and increases WFD's speedup to 4.81x on Huginn-3.5B.

</details>


### [128] [Optimizers for Diffusion Models: A Controlled Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23055)
*Arman Bolatov,Egor Shulgin,David Li,Abduragim Shtanchaev,Sebastian U. Stich,Maxim Panov,Eric Moulines,Peter Richtárik,Martin Takáč*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对离散扩散模型进行受控优化器基准测试，在四种扩散形式与多个任务上比较七种优化器，发现AdamW虽为强默认但并非总是最优，最优优化器随扩散形式变化，且自回归预训练验证过的方法（Muon、MARS-M、SOAP）可有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型已在多个基准上媲美自回归语言模型，但其训练优化器的选择长期被忽视：优化器往往沿袭前作而未加比较。同时，新优化器的验证几乎只基于自回归预训练，其目标函数与损失曲面均不同，因此有必要针对离散扩散开展系统性的优化器基准研究。

Method: 作者构建了跨四种扩散形式的受控优化器基准：在掩码扩散（text8）、均匀扩散（QM9，以及通过高斯对偶的LM1B）和图像高斯扩散（CelebA-64）上，比较七种优化器（AdamW、Lion、Muon、SOAP、MARS、MARS-M、Schedule-Free）。每个任务均有公开参考值，所有优化器采用相同搜索协议，且每个获胜配置在完整预算下用三个随机种子重新训练。

Result: AdamW是强默认选择但并非总是正确：在四个任务中的两个上被显著超越，且获胜优化器随扩散形式而变。值得注意的是，在自回归语言模型预训练上验证的方法迁移良好：Muon、MARS-M和SOAP各自在至少一种扩散形式上优于调优后的AdamW。

Conclusion: 优化器选择应与训练配方其余部分同等重视，不能简单沿用默认。基准、所有运行和全部图表均可通过 https://github.com/armanbolatov/diffusion-baselines 的代码端到端复现。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models now match autoregressive language models on several benchmarks, while the question of how best to train them has received far less attention: the optimizer is inherited from one paper to the next and never compared. New optimizers, meanwhile, are validated almost exclusively on autoregressive pretraining, a different objective on a different loss surface. We present a controlled optimizer benchmark across four diffusion formulations, to our knowledge the first for discrete diffusion: seven optimizers (AdamW, Lion, Muon, SOAP, MARS, MARS-M, Schedule-Free) on masked diffusion (text8), uniform diffusion (QM9, and LM1B through the Gaussian duality) and Gaussian diffusion on images (CelebA-64), each on a task with published reference values. Every optimizer receives the same search protocol, and every winner is retrained at the full budget with three seeds. AdamW is a strong default but not always the right choice: it is beaten by a resolved margin on two of the four tasks, and the winner changes with the formulation, so the optimizer deserves the same care as the rest of the training recipe. Notably, methods validated on autoregressive language model pretraining transfer well: Muon, MARS-M and SOAP each beat the tuned AdamW on at least one diffusion formulation. The benchmark, all runs and every figure are reproducible end to end from the released code at https://github.com/armanbolatov/diffusion-baselines.

</details>


### [129] [MolSC: Leveraging Substituent Contributions to Enhance Fine-grained Molecular Understanding in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23073)
*Hyuntae Park,Sooyeon Kim,Jiwon Park,SangKeun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MolSC数据集与MolSC-Bench基准，用以刻画"取代基贡献"（取代基连接至分子骨架所引起的性质变化），涵盖结构警示、靶点特异性生物活性与理化描述符，含181K训练样本及1,541条严格不重叠的评测样本；结果显示现有分子LLM及GPT-5.2、Gemini-3-Flash等强模型在该任务上可靠性有限，而在MolSC上训练可显著提升该能力并改善多项下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子大语言模型虽在多种化学任务上表现优异，但难以捕捉细粒度结构—性质关系，尤其是局部微小修饰如何改变分子行为。为弥补这一不足，作者提出以"取代基贡献"为切入点，将修饰效应显式建模为可学习对象。

Method: 1) 构建MolSC数据集：从人工标注的生物活性记录中整理，定义取代基贡献为取代基连接至分子骨架所引发的性质变化，覆盖结构警示风险、靶点特异性生物活性与理化描述符，含181K条取代基级训练样本；2) 构建MolSC-Bench评测基准：1,541条留出样本，在骨架、取代基、分子三个层面与MolSC完全不相交，避免信息泄漏；3) 在现有分子LLM与GPT-5.2、Gemini-3-Flash等强模型上进行对比实验，并验证在MolSC上训练后的效果与下游迁移能力。

Result: 现有分子LLM以及GPT-5.2、Gemini-3-Flash等强专有模型在取代基贡献预测上可靠性有限；而在MolSC上训练可显著提升该能力，并在多种下游分子任务上取得强劲表现。

Conclusion: 取代基贡献学习是细粒度分子理解的关键组成部分，显式建模局部修饰效应能有效弥补现有分子LLM在结构—性质关系上的短板。

Abstract: Recent advances in natural language processing have led to molecular Large Language Models (LLMs) with strong performance across diverse chemistry tasks. However, they still struggle to capture fine-grained structure-property relationships, particularly how small, localized modifications alter a molecule's behavior. To address this limitation, we introduce MolSC, a dataset of substituent contributions, defined as property changes induced by attaching specific substituents to molecular scaffolds. Curated from manually annotated bioactivity records, MolSC spans structural-alert liability, target-specific bioactivity, and physicochemical descriptors, and contains 181K substituent-level examples for training. We further propose MolSC-Bench, a held-out evaluation benchmark of 1,541 examples disjoint from MolSC at the scaffold, substituent, and molecule levels. Our experiments show that existing molecular LLMs and strong proprietary models such as GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3-Flash show limited reliability in substituent contribution prediction. In contrast, training on MolSC substantially improves this ability and achieves strong performance across diverse downstream molecular tasks. These results highlight substituent contribution learning as a key component of fine-grained molecular understanding.

</details>


### [130] [AirGC-CD: Gaussian-Circulant Precoding for Exactly Debiasable PAPR Reduction in Over-the-Air Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23084)
*Jonggyu Jang,Hyeonsu Lyu,Hyun Jong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AirGC-CD：在裁剪前用部分高斯循环矩阵对本地更新预编码，使裁剪输出严格高斯、可闭式反演，聚合并保证无偏，并兼作压缩器降低传输维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中联邦学习中，叠加波形的PAPR随模型维度增长，抑制PAPR需在裁剪（引入不可去除的接收端偏差）与功率回退（损失平均SNR）之间权衡；此外传输未压缩，每参数占用一次信道使用，难以支撑大模型训练。

Method: AirGC-CD对每个本地更新先用部分高斯循环矩阵预编码再裁剪。预编码输出严格服从高斯分布（不依赖更新的稀疏性），因此裁剪函数针对已知分布设计；利用单一标量Bussgang增益闭式地平均反演裁剪，证明聚合结果严格无偏、裁剪仅增加方差。将裁剪比作为唯一自由参数，闭式推导其近似最优值以平衡裁剪方差与回退SNR损失。预编码器为线性，兼作压缩器，将传输维度由d降至m，借助两次FFT以O(d log d)实现（高斯草图需O(md)）。

Result: 在五个图像数据集上的实验表明，AirGC-CD在多数设置下优于基线空中联邦学习方案，尤其在低SNR时表现更佳，且每轮使用更少的信道使用次数。

Conclusion: 通过预编码将裁剪反演问题转化为已知高斯分布下的闭式无偏聚合，并同时实现降维压缩，AirGC-CD在低SNR与大模型场景下兼具通信效率与学习性能优势。

Abstract: Over-the-air federated learning lets edge devices transmit their local updates simultaneously, reducing the communication overhead. The resulting waveform, however, has a peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) that grows with the model dimension, and keeping the amplifier in its linear range leaves two remedies: clipping the peaks or backing off the transmit power. Neither remedy is without cost: i) the clipping distortion appears at the receiver as a bias that cannot be removed, and ii) back-off keeps the signal intact but degrades the average signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Independent of this trade-off, the transmission remains uncompressed, spending one channel use per model parameter, which keeps large-model training out of reach. To address these challenges, we propose AirGC-CD, an over-the-air scheme that precodes each local update with a partial Gaussian circulant matrix before clipping. In AirGC-CD, the precoder's output is exactly Gaussian regardless of the update's sparsity, so the clipping function is designed for a known distribution instead of inheriting it from the data. This enables the clipping to be inverted on average by a single scalar Bussgang gain in closed form, and we prove that the resulting aggregate is exactly unbiased, with clipping adding only variance. The clipping ratio is then the only free parameter left, trading the variance of the clipping against the SNR loss from back-off, and we derive its near-optimum in closed form. Since the precoder is linear, it also acts as a compressor, reducing the transmission from the model dimension d to the sketch dimension m at a cost of only O(dlog d) via two fast Fourier transforms, whereas a Gaussian sketch costs O(md). Experiments on five image datasets show that AirGC-CD outperforms baseline over-the-air FL schemes in most settings, particularly at low SNR, while using fewer channel uses per round.

</details>


### [131] [Neural Spectral Capacity: Measuring and Designing Architectures from Network Specification Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23087)
*Chenyu Zhu,Ruoyu Zhao,Zhichao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Neural Spectral Capacity（NSC）：基于权重矩阵奇异值谱的闭式标量，可在不实例化模型的情况下度量架构容量，并配套 NSC-DP 精确动态规划求解器，在资源约束下秒级求出全局最优架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer 设计与压缩本质上是预算下的容量分配问题，而 #Params 与 #FLOPs 只刻画规模与计算量，不反映架构结构。参数量预算相同但深度-宽度、注意力头、FFN 分配不同的架构得分相同却表现迥异，因此需要一个能体现结构差异的容量标量。

Method: NSC 以每个权重矩阵的奇异值谱为基础构造闭式标量；在标准随机初始化下，利用 Marchenko-Pastur 定律使 NSC 仅凭架构规格即可计算，无需模型实例化、数据或梯度。其逐层可加结构支持 NSC-DP：在资源约束下用精确动态规划全局最大化 NSC，CPU 上秒级求解。

Result: 在七个 Transformer 与 CNN 家族中，NSC 在排序上优于 #Params、#FLOPs 及代表性免训练代理；在 FlexiBERT 上参数量差异小于 10% 的配对中 τ=0.505，而 #Params 仅为 0.082。NSC-DP 在 2 秒内于 WikiText-103 上发现超越人工设计基线的 Transformer-XL 架构；并将 LLaMA-7B 剪枝为在八个常识推理任务上最优的 5.7B 模型，无需校准数据，比最强免训练代理基线快约 5900 倍。

Conclusion: NSC 提供了一个仅依赖架构规格、免数据免梯度的结构感知容量度量，配合精确动态规划可在秒级获得资源约束下的全局最优架构，在架构排序、设计与剪枝任务上均显著优于传统标量与免训练代理方法。

Abstract: Modern Transformer design and compression both reduce to allocating capacity under a budget. The standard scalars for these decisions, #Params and #FLOPs, capture size and compute but not architectural structure: two architectures with identical parameter budgets but different depth-width, head, or FFN allocations receive identical scores yet behave differently. We propose Neural Spectral Capacity (NSC), a closed-form scalar grounded in the singular-value spectrum of each weight matrix. Under standard random initialization, the Marchenko-Pastur law renders NSC computable from the architectural specification alone, with no model instantiation, data, or gradients. Its layer-wise additive structure admits NSC-DP, an exact dynamic-programming solver returning the architecture globally maximizing NSC under resource constraints in seconds on a CPU -- a guarantee that black-box search over existing training-free proxies cannot provide. Empirically, NSC outperforms #Params, #FLOPs, and representative training-free proxies in ranking across seven Transformer and CNN families (on FlexiBERT, $τ= 0.505$ on pairs differing in #Params by less than 10%, where #Params collapses to 0.082); NSC-DP discovers a Transformer-XL architecture on WikiText-103 that beats the human-designed baseline in 2 seconds; and prunes LLaMA-7B to the best 5.7B model across eight commonsense reasoning tasks without any calibration data, about 5900x faster than the strongest training-free proxy baseline.

</details>


### [132] [The Role of Coordinates in Pareto Regret for Adversarial Multi-Objective Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23092)
*Changkun Guan,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究对抗性多目标赌博机中坐标难易程度对Pareto遗憾的影响，证明未知难易信息下无法利用较小累积损失改善最坏情况阶数，并给出匹配下界的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论表明，某坐标累积损失越小（越"容易"）越可能降低Pareto遗憾。但实际中事先不知道哪个坐标更容易，这种信息缺失是否消除了利用简单坐标的可能性，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 作者定义损失为1减奖励，用某坐标上各臂最小累积损失衡量该坐标的"容易度"（越小越容易）。负向结果通过构造对手证明：即使知道L₀=min_d L_d，最优坐标信息也无法改善最坏情况阶数。正向结果将Poly-INF应用于固定坐标得到上界；在不知道L₀时，提出奖励倍增版Poly-INF以自适应未知量。

Result: 对K≥4臂、T≥6轮、至少2个坐标，证明极小极大期望Pareto遗憾为Ω(min{T−L₀, √(K(T−L₀))})，且随L₀单调递减。当L₀已知时，对固定坐标应用Poly-INF得到同阶上界并与下界匹配；当L₀未知时，奖励倍增版Poly-INF仍达到匹配的极小极大率。

Conclusion: 未知坐标难易信息会消除利用简单坐标改善最坏情况Pareto遗憾的可能；但其他坐标亦可达到最优Pareto遗憾率。所提算法无需额外log T因子，且与坐标数量无关。

Abstract: Adversarial multi-objective bandits hold the potential to help us optimize choices (arms) whose reward is a multidimensional vector chosen by an adversary and whose performance is measured by Pareto regret. We define loss as one minus reward and measure the easiness of a coordinate by the smallest cumulative loss of the arms on it, and call the coordinate easier when this quantity is smaller. Existing work suggests that in theory an easier coordinate may reduce Pareto regret. However, in practice, one may not know which coordinate is easier. On the negative side, we show that this lack of information eliminates the possibility: a smaller cumulative loss does not improve the worst-case order of Pareto regret. Precisely, let \(L_d\) be the smallest cumulative loss along coordinate $d$ over $T$ rounds. For \(K\ge4\) arms, \(T\ge6\) rounds, and at least 2 coordinates, we prove that the minimax expected Pareto regret is \(Ω(\min\{T-L_0,\sqrt{K(T-L_0)}\})\). It is monotonically decreasing in \(L_0\), even when \(L_0=\min_d L_d\) itself is known. On the positive side, this result motivates the possibility that other coordinates, not just the easy one, may suffice to attain the optimal rate of Pareto regret. When $L_0$ is known, we apply Poly-INF to a fixed coordinate and obtain an upper bound on Pareto regret that exhibits the same order and thus matches the lower bound. Without such knowledge, we develop a reward-doubling version of Poly-INF that adapts to this unknown quantity while still attaining the matching minimax rate. Another implication is that it has no extra \(\log T\) factor and is independent of the number of coordinates.

</details>


### [133] [When Does Adversarial Refinement Help? A Negative Result and Open Problem in Adapting R3GAN to Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23102)
*Yufeng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文尝试用稳定化的R3GAN（配粗到细精修与频域判别器）复现对抗式多变量时间序列插补，通过对3个数据集14个已保存配置的审计，得出否定性结论：在唯一严格的**非遗留线性起始配置**上，均值变化仅-0.7%（-3.0%至+1.1%），对抗信号无益甚至有害；作者主张"GAN优化分布性目标"这一常见解释不成立（扩散模型同样优化分布目标却达到SOTA），并提出可学习判别器为何无法提供有效精修梯度为开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与Transformer已取代GAN成为多变量时间序列插补主流方法，主要理由是GAN训练不稳定。R3GAN（NeurIPS 2024）通过带可证收敛性的正则化相对论损失消除了该不稳定性，由此产生一个自然问题：稳定、现代的GAN能否让对抗式插补重新可行？作者希望检验"训练不稳定是GAN在插补任务上失利的根因"这一假设，并厘清对抗精修在何种条件下才值得使用。

Method: 将R3GAN适配到1D时序数据，采用粗到细（coarse-to-fine）精修框架，并引入频域判别器。对3个数据集上共14个已保存配置进行审计。因这些配置属异质性的单次运行，证据为描述性而非匹配的因果消融。此外进行了重建权重扫描，并做了一次独立的Weather数据集对比（单独R3GAN-1D vs BRITS）。作者明确指出，真正能识别对抗信号因果贡献的关键实验——去除判别器的匹配消融——尚未完成。

Result: 报告为否定性结果。（1）5个已保存的均值起始/零起始配置全部改善48.4%–70.2%；（2）在8个符合条件、非遗留的线性起始配置中，平均变化为-0.7%（范围-3.0%至+1.1%）；（3）另有一处-21.9%的遗留日志记录异常，保留以溯源性但排除于上述汇总之外；（4）在已保存的Weather对比中，单独R3GAN-1D比BRITS差5.8倍；（5）重建权重扫描结果与"对抗信号惰性或有害"一致，但无法确定其因果贡献。

Conclusion: 稳定化GAN未能复现对抗式插补的优势：在严格的线性起始配置上平均无改善（-0.7%）。作者反驳了"GAN因优化分布性而非逐点目标故不适用于插补"的常见解释，理由是扩散模型同样优化分布性目标却取得SOTA插补效果。作者将"可学习判别器为何无法提供有用精修梯度（而可学习扩散去噪器可以）"确立为开放问题，并给出关于何时值得采用对抗精修的实践建议。受限于异质单次运行与缺失匹配消融，结论为描述性；后续首要实验是去除判别器的匹配消融。

Abstract: Diffusion models and transformers have supplanted GANs for multivariate time series imputation, largely on grounds of GAN training instability. R3GAN (NeurIPS 2024) removes that instability via regularized relativistic losses with provable convergence, raising a natural question: do stable, modern GANs revive adversarial imputation? We adapt R3GAN to 1D temporal data with a coarse-to-fine refinement framework and a frequency-domain discriminator, and audit 14 saved configurations across 3 datasets. Because these are heterogeneous single runs, the evidence is descriptive rather than a matched causal ablation. We report a negative result. All five saved mean/zero-start configurations improve by 48.4-70.2%. Among eight eligible non-legacy linear-start configurations, the mean change is -0.7% (range -3.0% to +1.1%); a separate -21.9% legacy logging anomaly is retained for provenance but excluded from that aggregate. In a saved Weather comparison, standalone R3GAN-1D underperforms BRITS by 5.8x. Crucially, we argue the common explanation (that GANs optimize distributional rather than point-wise objectives) cannot be the whole story, since diffusion models also optimize distributional objectives yet achieve state-of-the-art imputation. Our saved reconstruction-weight sweep is consistent with the adversarial signal being inert or harmful, but cannot identify its causal contribution; a matched discriminator-removed ablation is the key next experiment. We frame the precise reason a learned discriminator fails to provide useful refinement gradients (where a learned diffusion denoiser succeeds) as an open problem, and offer practical guidance on when adversarial refinement is worthwhile.

</details>


### [134] [Whitening Inverts the Hierarchy: What the Norm of a Whitened Embedding Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23117)
*Mohammed Ahnouch,Lotfi Elaachak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 白化后嵌入的平方范数常被当作免训练似然代理，但本文证明其并非高斯联合分布，而是语义非典型性的马氏度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清并纠正"白化坐标近似标准正态→平方范数可作似然代理"这一流行假设的统计基础与理论边界。

Method: 用投影中心极限定理分析白化坐标边缘正态性的来源；在多个编码器与三种训练目标下比较白化半径与高斯参考分布，并与具有相同均值、协方差的分布克隆对照；从两个矩估计主导尺度，无自由参数预测不相交谱半区间的交叉依赖。

Result: 白化半径系统性过度离散，即使对同均值同协方差的分布克隆亦如此；经验与理论范数统计的一致性只是样本内白化的代数后果，不构成高斯性证据。机制为白化逆转编码器谱层级，使平方范数贡献转向近退化、以噪声为主的方向，其主导变异由单一输入相关尺度支配；据此估计的尺度可准确预测交叉依赖。该统计量能与非参数密度估计一致地排序与检测非典型样本，但高斯尾部阈值可能偏差达数量级。

Conclusion: 平方白化范数应解释为语义非典型性的马氏度量而非对数似然，这同时解释了其实际有效性与校准失败。

Abstract: Whitening a foundation-model embedding and using its squared norm as a training-free likelihood surrogate is motivated by the observation that whitened coordinates often appear approximately standard normal. We show that this observation follows from the projection central limit theorem and therefore does not imply a Gaussian joint distribution. Across multiple encoders and three training objectives, we find systematic over-dispersion of the whitened radius relative to the Gaussian reference, including against distributional clones with identical mean and covariance. We further show that the commonly reported agreement between empirical and theoretical norm statistics is an algebraic consequence of in-sample whitening and does not constitute evidence for Gaussianity.
  We identify the mechanism behind this behavior: whitening reverses the encoder's spectral hierarchy, shifting the contribution to the squared norm toward near-degenerate directions that encode predominantly noise. In these directions, the dominant variability is governed by a single input-dependent scale. We estimate this scale from two moments and use it to predict, without additional free parameters, the cross-dependence between disjoint spectral halves. These results indicate that the squared whitened norm is better interpreted as a Mahalanobis measure of semantic atypicality than as a log-likelihood. This interpretation explains both its practical effectiveness and its calibration failures: the statistic can rank and detect atypical samples consistently with nonparametric density estimates and across encoders trained with different objectives, while Gaussian tail thresholds can be inaccurate by orders of magnitude. etc.

</details>


### [135] [Perplexity Cost Understates What Activation Quantisation Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23125)
*Anish Sathyanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 困惑度是可靠的聚合信号，但会掩盖不同计算能力受损的结构性差异：同等级别下归纳能力几乎不受损，而检索能力已显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流激活量化评估依赖困惑度这一聚合指标，但其是否能够识别量化器具体破坏了哪些计算任务尚不清楚，本文旨在检验聚合指标与具体能力损伤之间的对应关系。

Method: 在来自四个家族、最多32B参数的12个模型上开展780组模型内比较；将困惑度偏好与归纳、检索两类能力对比；并通过匹配的高斯噪声控制、仅随机化量化误差符号、以及在旋转基下量化等干预，分离误差幅度与坐标对齐的作用。

Result: 困惑度偏好所选方案在98%以上比较中同时保留更多归纳与检索能力；但当困惑度仅上升1.2至1.5倍时，归纳保留0.959而检索降至0.554。匹配幅度的高斯噪声与符号随机化均基本无害，旋转基量化在单块干预下将归纳从0.001恢复至0.980（检索为0.694）；端到端4比特下归纳0.968、检索0.534，结论在AWQ部署配置下亦成立。

Conclusion: 困惑度目标只约束激活变换的平均代价，无法单独证明哪些计算得以幸存；评估量化需引入任务层面的细粒度诊断。

Abstract: Activation quantisation is usually evaluated with an aggregate metric, perplexity, averaged over every token a model predicts. We ask whether that average identifies which computations a quantiser damages. Perplexity turns out to be a reliable aggregate signal: across 12 models from four families and 780 within-model comparisons, the arm perplexity prefers also retains more induction and more retrieval in all but 2.1 and 4.0 percent of cases respectively. But where perplexity has risen by only a factor of 1.2 to 1.5, induction still keeps 0.959 of its intact accuracy while retrieval has already fallen to 0.554, a gap the aggregate number does not surface. This gap has structure, not just size: a matched Gaussian-noise control of the same per-channel magnitude leaves it largely intact, and randomising only the sign of the quantisation error, every magnitude held fixed, is nearly as harmless, so magnitude alone does not explain the damage. Quantising in a rotated basis, which changes coordinate alignment without changing error magnitude, restores induction from 0.001 to 0.980 at three average bits per token in a single-block intervention, though retrieval recovers less completely at the same setting (0.694); end-to-end at four average bits, induction reaches 0.968 and retrieval 0.534. The pattern holds on two further models up to 32B parameters and, in the deployed configurations we tested, under AWQ once activations are pushed to 4 bits. A perplexity target bounds the average cost of a transformation applied to the activation; it does not, by itself, show which computations survived.

</details>


### [136] [Provably Efficient Reinforcement Learning in Continuous-Time Episodic MDPs with Poisson Decision Epochs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23127)
*Kenny Guo,Valentio Iverson,Sahan Wijetunga,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究事件驱动的连续时间episodic MDP，在时间Lipschitz平滑假设下将UCRL与Q-learning推广到泊松决策epoch场景，证明模型基与无模型算法均达到Õ(T^{2/3})遗憾，并给出匹配的Õ(T^{2/3}) minimax下界，表明该速率在对数因子内最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实RL问题常处于连续时间，决策发生在不规则的事件驱动时刻而非固定离散步，而现有理论多针对离散时间设定，缺乏对泊松决策epoch与时间平滑动态的紧致遗憾保证。

Method: 针对episodic连续时间MDP，决策epoch由齐次泊松过程控制，奖励与转移动态随时间平滑变化；考虑每episode固定跳跃数与固定时间预算（随机泊松epoch数）两种设定。在时间Lipschitz连续性假设下，利用局部平滑性进行离散化，将UCRL（Auer and Ortner 2006）与Q-learning（Jin et al. 2018）扩展到该场景。

Result: 为模型基与无模型算法均证明了Õ(T^{2/3})遗憾上界；同时建立匹配的Õ(T^{2/3}) minimax下界，说明速率在对数因子内最优。

Conclusion: 给出了Lipschitz平滑、泊松决策epoch的连续时间episodic MDP首个紧致遗憾保证，填补了连续时间事件驱动RL理论中速率最优性的空白。

Abstract: Many real-world reinforcement learning (RL) problems evolve in continuous time, where decisions occur at irregular, event-driven intervals rather than at fixed discrete steps. We study episodic continuous-time Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) in which decision epochs are governed by a homogeneous Poisson process and the reward and transition dynamics vary smoothly over time. We consider both a fixed number of jumps per episode and a fixed time budget with a random number of Poisson decision epochs. Under a Lipschitz continuity assumption in time, we exploit local smoothness through discretization and extend both UCRL (Auer and Ortner 2006) and Q-learning (Jin et al. 2018) to this setting, proving $\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret bounds for both model-based and model-free algorithms. Finally, we establish matching $\widetildeΩ(T^{2/3})$ minimax lower bounds, showing that the rate is optimal up to logarithmic factors. These results provide the first tight regret guarantees for Lipschitz-smooth continuous-time episodic MDPs with Poisson decision epochs.

</details>


### [137] [Ask for Any Appliance: A Prompt-Programmable Foundation Model for Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23146)
*Xudong Wang,Jiacheng Cui,Junyu Xue,Tongxin Li,Guoming Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FM4NILM，一个可通过提示编程的单一基础模型，实现非侵入式负载监测，无需为每个电器训练专用网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NILM依赖电器专用模型与固定输出清单，扩展覆盖范围成本高，需要一种共享的、可灵活扩展的模型。

Method: 基于轻量级节律感知Transformer，在来自7个公开语料库的645k序列（采样间隔1-60秒）上通过掩码重建预训练；用观测掩码损失对齐电器请求以处理部分标注家庭；采用Bernoulli-lognormal解码器分离活动检测与条件功率估计；以自然语言描述和可选激活示例作为提示。

Result: 在REDD、UK-DALE、REFIT的留出家庭与时间段上，单个冻结文本提示模型服务12个电器-语料请求，事件F1达0.556，AUPRC为0.625，活跃窗口MAE最低（251.8W）；流式分数聚合将事件F1提升至0.582（60秒聚合延迟）；类别留出评估中，添加10个激活示例将微波炉AUPRC从0.132提升至0.214（无需参数更新）。

Conclusion: 单一共享模型可实现有竞争力的负荷分解，支持通过提示与示例而非新增专用网络来扩展电器覆盖范围。

Abstract: Non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM) estimates appliance-level consumption from a whole-home meter, but appliance-specific models and fixed output inventories make coverage costly to extend. We present FM4NILM (Foundation Model for NILM), a single prompt-programmable model that estimates a requested appliance's power trajectory from aggregate measurements, a natural-language description, and optional activation exemplars. A lightweight cadence-aware transformer is pretrained by masked reconstruction on 645k sequences from seven public corpora spanning 1-60 s sampling intervals, then aligned with appliance requests using observation-masked losses for partially labeled households. A Bernoulli-lognormal decoder separates activity detection from conditional power estimation. On held-out households and time periods from REDD, UK-DALE, and REFIT, one frozen text-prompted model serves twelve appliance-corpus requests, achieving 0.556 event F1, 0.625 AUPRC, and the lowest active-window MAE (251.8 W) among seven appliance-specific baselines. Streaming score aggregation raises event F1 to 0.582 with a 60 s aggregation delay. In a separate category-held-out evaluation, adding ten activation exemplars raises microwave AUPRC from 0.132 to 0.214 without parameter updates. Input-intervention ablations probe the model's dependence on appliance requests and aggregate measurements. These results demonstrate competitive disaggregation with one shared model and support extending appliance coverage through prompts and examples rather than additional specialist networks.

</details>


### [138] [K-TRAIL: Simulator-Guided Generative Design of EM/RF Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23183)
*Piyush Saha,Evan Newell,Hanna O'Leary,Arun Natarajan,Alireza Aghasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K-TRAIL：一种仿真器引导的生成式框架，用扩散模型结合无导数集合卡尔曼引导，实现RF/EM电路的自动化逆向设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RF/EM电路逆向设计困难：电路布局与电响应关系非唯一（non-unique），且全波仿真计算代价高昂。现有方法依赖伴随灵敏度或可微求解器，限制了实用性。

Method: K-TRAIL将基于扩散的布局生成与无导数的集合卡尔曼（ensemble Kalman）引导相结合，利用黑箱EM仿真器的反馈在生成过程中精炼候选布局，无需伴随灵敏度或可微求解器模型。支持两种合成模式：由给定S参数响应合成，以及直接由RF性能约束合成。

Result: 在多层RFIC结构上的实验表明，仿真器引导的生成提高了与目标响应的吻合度，并能发现满足电路级设计要求的、结构上存在差异的多种布局。

Conclusion: 该方法为生成式、可验证感知（verification-aware）的RF电路设计提供了切实可行的路径，同时保留了探索多样布局拓扑的灵活性。

Abstract: Inverse design of RF and electromagnetic (EM) circuits is challenging because the relationship between circuit layout and electrical response is non-unique, and full-wave simulation is computationally expensive. This paper presents K-TRAIL, a simulator-guided generative framework for automated EM/RF circuit synthesis. K-TRAIL combines diffusion-based layout generation with derivative-free ensemble Kalman guidance, allowing feedback from a black-box EM simulator to refine candidate layouts during generation without requiring adjoint sensitivities or differentiable solver models. The framework supports both synthesis from prescribed S-parameter responses and synthesis directly from RF performance constraints. Experiments on multi-layer RFIC structures show that simulator-guided generation improves agreement with target responses and can identify structurally distinct layouts that satisfy circuit-level design requirements. The proposed approach provides a practical path toward generative, verification-aware RF circuit design while retaining the flexibility to explore diverse layout topologies.

</details>


### [139] [Neural Residual Modeling for Scientific Data Compression under Guaranteed Error Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23185)
*Surya Majumder,Liangji Zhu,Sanjay Ranka,Anand Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种后处理流水线：在RVQ压缩器后接U-Net预测并校正像素空间残差，再用GAE阶段将残余误差投影到分块PCA基上以强制满足用户指定的块级误差界。在S3D、JHTDB、E3SM上NRMSE与压缩比均优于RVQ-only及常规残差校正基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学仿真数据的有损压缩日益依赖RVQ等学习式潜空间架构，其通过迭代量化基表示与残差来逐步降低重建误差。但RVQ仅在潜空间完成残差建模，重建在像素空间的误差结构基本未被处理，导致误差缺乏针对性校正，且难以同时满足科学数据所需的严格误差保证。

Method: 在RVQ压缩器基础上增加U-Net后处理网络，直接预测并校正原始体数据与RVQ重建之间的像素空间残差；分析表明该残差具有空间结构性，而非由局部强度或梯度特征驱动，因此需深度空间模型而非简单统计校正。U-Net校正后的重建再送入GAE阶段，将剩余残差投影到逐块PCA基上，以强制满足用户指定的分块误差界。

Result: 在S3D、JHTDB和E3SM数据集上评估，相比RVQ-only和标准残差校正基线，NRMSE与压缩比均获得一致提升，同时保持科学数据保真度所需的严格误差保证。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个在单一框架内结合潜空间RVQ、基于深度空间后处理网络的显式像素空间残差校正，以及GAE误差界保证的科学数据压缩流水线，可在提升压缩性能的同时维持严格误差约束。

Abstract: Lossy compression of scientific simulation data increasingly relies on learned, latent-space architectures such as Residual Vector Quantization (RVQ), which iteratively quantize a base representation and its residuals to progressively reduce reconstruction error. While effective, RVQ performs this residual modeling entirely in latent space, leaving the pixel-space error structure of the reconstruction largely unaddressed. In this work, we propose a post-processing pipeline that augments an RVQ-based compressor with a U-Net trained to predict and correct pixel-space residuals between the original volume and its RVQ reconstruction. We show that these residuals are spatially structured rather than driven by local intensity or gradient features, motivating the need for a deep spatial model rather than simple statistical correction. The U-Net-corrected reconstruction is then passed through a Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE) stage, which projects the remaining residual onto a per-block PCA basis to enforce a user-specified block-wise error bound. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first pipeline to combine latent-space RVQ, explicit pixel-space residual correction via a deep spatial post-processing network, and GAE-based error-bound guarantees within a single framework for scientific data compression. We evaluate our approach on S3D, JHTDB and E3SM datasets, demonstrating consistent improvements in NRMSE, compression ratio] over RVQ-only and standard residual-correction baselines, while maintaining strict error guarantees required for scientific data fidelity.

</details>


### [140] [Triggers and Diagnostics for LLM-Based Interpretability Failures in Active Inference Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23215)
*Param Raval,Rohit Shenoy,Archana Vaidheeswaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了作为智能体运行时监督的LLM解释器，发现其在三种后端上均会流畅地叙述被污染的信念、对错误行动提供谄媚合理化，并易受观测元数据中的攻击者文本操控与数据外泄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM解释器被越来越多地附加到自主智能体上作为运行时监督，操作者依赖生成的自然语言叙述而非内部状态来判断智能体的信念与行动。然而，解释器架构中没有任何机制在操作者据此行动前检验解释是否为真，因此亟需对解释本身进行审计。

Method: 将跟踪德国电网需求并调节发电的Active Inference（AIF）智能体与LLM解释器配对，后端使用GPT-4o、Claude-3-Opus和Gemini；用三种黑盒触发器探测该组合。包括以每步600 MW污染观测流、在智能体行动客观错误的时间步上检验解释、以及在观测元数据字段注入攻击者控制文本。

Result: 每步600 MW的观测污染使智能体后验偏移490 MW（约占电网容量0.9%）；注入期间产生的30条解释在既定评分标准下均未报告任何异常，且都流畅地叙述了被污染信念。在智能体行动客观错误的时间步上，三种解释器有80-95%的概率产生谄媚合理化（每后端n=20）。观测元数据中的攻击者控制文本可操控解释器，且易感性因提供商而异，数据外泄在三种后端上均成功。

Conclusion: 所观察到的每一次失败中，解释都是流畅但错误的。解释器架构中没有任何机制在操作者据其行动前检验解释的真实性。因此，对解释器进行测试应成为任何智能体部署审计的组成部分。作者为每种失败提出了缓解措施，但未对其进行评估。

Abstract: LLM explainers are increasingly attached to autonomous agents as runtime oversight, with operators reading a generated account of the agent's beliefs and actions rather than its internal state. We audit the account itself, pairing an Active Inference (AIF) agent that tracks German grid demand and adjusts generation with an LLM explainer on three backends (GPT-4o, Claude-3-Opus, Gemini), and probing the pair with three black-box triggers. Corrupting the observation stream by 600 MW per step moves the agent's posterior by 490 MW, roughly 0.9% of grid capacity. None of the 30 explanations produced during the injection flag anything under a stated rubric, and each narrates the corrupted belief fluently. On timesteps where the agent takes an objectively wrong action, all three explainers produce a sycophantic rationalization 80-95% of the time (n = 20 per backend). Attacker-controlled text in the observation metadata field steers the explainer, with susceptibility differing by provider and data exfiltration succeeding on all three. We propose mitigations for each failure but do not evaluate them. In every failure we observed, the explanation was fluent and wrong. Moreover, nothing in the explainer architecture checks whether an explanation is true before an operator acts on it. Testing the explainer therefore belongs in any audit of an agentic deployment.

</details>


### [141] [Causal Inference with Unobserved Confounding: A Mixture Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23219)
*Mansi Sood,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从混合学习的视角处理观测数据因果推断中的未观测混杂问题，将潜在混杂因子视为诱导混合结构的异质性来源，并在结构性与可识别性假设下恢复混合分布与成分机制，从而估计干预分布与因果量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未观测混杂是观测数据因果推断的根本挑战，传统方法难以在存在潜在混杂因子时识别因果效应，因此需要新的建模范式将潜在混杂结构纳入可识别框架。

Method: 提出混合学习视角，将潜在混杂因子建模为产生观测数据混合结构的异质性来源；以伯努利混合的多种变体为示例，梳理混合学习技术及其结构性假设，并联系面板数据场景下的因果推断，包括潜在因子模型与合成干预；进一步考虑具有相依结果轨迹的高维指数族混合，从反事实均值拓展到反事实分布建模。

Result: 在合适的结构与可识别性假设下，恢复混合分布和成分机制能够支持干预分布及因果量的估计；高维指数族混合可用于建模反事实分布，拓展了仅关注反事实均值的传统框架，并与其它处理未观测混杂的互补方法形成对照。

Conclusion: 该视角在混合学习与因果推断之间架起桥梁，把近期高维混合学习进展与可扩展的因果效应识别和估计相连接，同时为混合学习提出新的挑战。

Abstract: Unobserved confounding is a fundamental challenge in causal inference from observational data. This article develops a mixture-learning perspective, viewing latent confounders as sources of heterogeneity that induce mixture structure in observed data. Under suitable structural and identifiability assumptions, recovering the mixing distribution and component mechanisms enables estimation of interventional distributions and causal estimands. Using variants of Bernoulli mixtures as a running example, we contextualize mixture-learning techniques and their structural assumptions, and connect them to causal inference in panel-data settings, including latent factor models and synthetic interventions.We then consider high-dimensional exponential-family mixtures with dependent outcome trajectories, moving beyond counterfactual means to model counterfactual distributions. We situate this perspective relative to complementary approaches for unobserved confounding. Together, these ideas provide a bridge between mixture learning and causal inference, connecting recent advances in high-dimensional mixture learning to scalable identification and estimation of causal effects while raising new challenges for mixture learning.

</details>


### [142] [Proximal Residual Value Functions for Consistent Planning and Real-Time Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23242)
*Harrison Waldon,Carson Eisenach,Akhil Bagaria,Daniel Russo,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alisha Zachariah,Dean Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出近端残差价值函数与端到端强化学习框架，用于两层时间尺度决策系统，并以库存放置为应用验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两层时间尺度系统中，规划层需周期性向实时优化器提供延续价值函数，以实现资源分配决策的一致性；库存放置是典型场景，需要兼顾实时凸优化可执行性与端到端学习能力。

Method: 提出近端残差价值函数，由决策后库存的严格凸势函数与可学习凸残差组合而成；该形式保证优化层良定义，支持端到端微分，同时保留实时执行的显式凸目标。并刻画光滑价值函数产生跨规划与执行时间尺度一致决策的充要条件。

Result: 基于大型电商零售商历史库存到达与需求模式的离线仿真中，学习到的近端残差价值函数相较历史生产系统代理，使总路由与调拨成本降低5.0%。

Conclusion: 近端残差价值函数为两层时间尺度决策系统提供了一种兼顾凸优化结构与端到端学习的价值函数学习方案，并在真实历史数据仿真中验证了成本改进效果。

Abstract: We study two-timescale decision systems in which a planning layer periodically supplies a continuation-value function to a real-time optimizer that allocates arriving resources, with inventory placement as our motivating application. We propose an end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) method for learning this function using \emph{proximal residual value functions}, which combine a strictly convex potential of post-decision inventory with a learned convex residual. This general form yields a well-posed optimization layer that supports end-to-end differentiation while preserving an explicit convex objective for real-time execution. We characterize the necessary and sufficient conditions under which a smooth value function yields decisions that are consistent across the planning and execution timescales. In an offline simulation using historical inventory arrival and demand patterns from a large e-commerce retailer, learned proximal residual value functions reduce total routing and transfer cost relative to a historical-production-system proxy by 5.0%.

</details>


### [143] [The Price of Self-Calibration: Exact Evidence Budgets and Manufactured Blind Sets in Adaptive Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23254)
*Abdou-Raouf Atarmla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文给出了自校准监控器在任意漂移下保证长程虚警率的"代价"的精确刻画：保证本身是会计恒等式；对在线分位数跟踪器给出两条精确的代价恒等式、一条关于窗式报警率波动的精确定律，以及任意漂移类下监控器必然"盲"于 $\mathcal{D}-\mathcal{D}$ 中故障的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自校准监控器通过在线调整阈值来在任意漂移下保证预设的长程虚警率，但这一保证的代价（检测能力的损失）此前缺乏精确、无拟合参数的理论刻画。作者希望在标注每条定律确切适用域的前提下，量化该保证的价格，并揭示容忍漂移类所导致的根本性盲区。

Method: 理论分析：将保证表述为会计恒等式；对在线分位数跟踪器推导两条证据恒等式，分别针对高度 $\delta$ 的持续阶跃与斜率 $c$ 的斜坡，并与报警率上限 $c=\eta(1-\alpha)$ 对应；利用窗式质量望远镜求和性（telescoping）到紧内部状态的差，导出窗式报警率标准差为 $O(1/L)$ 的闭式常数；用两点论证证明对漂移类 $\mathcal{D}$ 的容忍等价于对 $\mathcal{D}-\mathcal{D}$ 的盲性；并给出比 Pinsker 更紧、永不失效的高斯投影界来量化盲区之外的检测功效。

Result: 保证为与检测目标无关的会计恒等式；阶跃 $\delta$ 在一个报警内产生 $\delta/\eta$ 的超额报警质量，当 $\delta$ 为增益 $\eta$ 的格点倍数时路径处处恰为 $\delta/\eta$；斜坡斜率 $c$ 产生恰好 $c/\eta$ 的稳态超额率，与累积量无关，边界 $c=\eta(1-\alpha)$ 与报警率上限重合。窗式报警率标准差为 $1/L$ 阶而非二项式的 $1/\sqrt{L}$，闭式常数无需拟合即被验证；按二项式尺度校准的检测器误校准因子为 $\sqrt{\eta\varphi(q_0)L}$，正确校准使检测窗从故障速度倒数的二次变为线性。任何容忍漂移类 $\mathcal{D}$ 的监控器（任意视界、任意规则）对 $\mathcal{D}-\mathcal{D}$ 中所有故障盲；对速度有界类，盲集恰为倍速类，跟踪器吸收由自身增益固定的速度类，于是在将吸收漂移声明为正常的认证制度下，监控器"制造"出 $\mathcal{D}$。高斯投影界精确、比 Pinsker 更紧且永不失效。

Conclusion: 自校准虚警率保证是一种与检测目标无关的会计恒等式，其检测代价可被精确表达：阶跃与斜坡的代价分别由 $\delta/\eta$ 与 $c/\eta$ 刻画，窗式报警率波动为 $1/L$ 阶，误校准与检测窗尺度随之改变。更根本的是，容忍某漂移类必然导致对其差类的盲性，速度有界类的盲集恰为倍速类，监控器在认证制度下会自行制造被声明为正常的漂移类。

Abstract: Self-calibrating monitors adapt their threshold online to guarantee a prescribed long-run false-alarm rate under arbitrary drift. We compute the price of that guarantee, stating every law with its exact domain of validity. First, the guarantee is an accounting identity, insensitive to what the monitor is meant to detect. Two evidence identities make the cost exact for the online quantile tracker: a persistent step of height $δ$ yields excess alarm mass within one alarm of $δ/η$, and exactly $δ/η$ pathwise when $δ$ is a lattice multiple of the gain $η$; a ramp of slope $c$ yields a stationary excess rate of exactly $c/η$, independent of accumulated size, up to a boundary $c=η(1-α)$ coinciding with the alarm-rate cap. Second, the certificate's own fluctuation obeys an exact law: the windowed alarm rate has standard deviation of order $1/L$, not the binomial $1/\sqrt{L}$, since the windowed mass telescopes to a difference of a tight internal state; the closed-form constant is validated with no fitted parameter. Detectors calibrated on the binomial scale are miscalibrated by $\sqrt{η\varphi(q_0)L}$, and correct calibration turns detection windows from quadratic to linear in the inverse fault speed. Third, any monitor required to tolerate a drift class $\mathcal{D}$ is blind, at any horizon and for any rule, to every fault in $\mathcal{D}-\mathcal{D}$; the proof is a deliberately elementary two-point argument and the contribution is the object it identifies: for speed-bounded classes the blind set is exactly the doubled-speed class, and the tracker absorbs a speed class fixed by its own gain, so that under a certification regime declaring absorbed drift normal, the monitor manufactures $\mathcal{D}$. An exact Gaussian projection bound, sharper than Pinsker and never vacuous, quantifies power outside it.

</details>


### [144] [CTRL: Control-Based Time Series Forecasting with LLM-Guided Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23257)
*Minkyoung Kim,Daeun Ji,Yohan Lee,Beomsoo Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CTRL框架，将语义推理与定量预测解耦：冻结骨干网络生成基础预测，LLM智能体作为控制器分析趋势/季节/不规则分量误差并输出紧凑控制信号，由轻量残差解码器转为预测修正；含无标签测试时自适应，仅需3-24次LLM调用即可应对分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序预测方法在利用LLM时存在两难：一是将LLM降级为数值预测器，未发挥其推理优势；二是让LLM直接生成预测，在非平稳条件下预测不稳定。

Method: CTRL将语义推理与定量预测解耦：冻结骨干网络产生基础预测；专门的LLM智能体充当控制器，通过分解趋势、季节和不规则分量来分析骨干预测误差，将推理锚定在可解释的时间结构上；每个智能体输出紧凑控制信号，由轻量残差解码器转化为预测修正；并引入无标签测试时自适应，仅凭输入统计量检测分布偏移，借助缓存以3-24次LLM调用重新适配控制信号。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验数值；作者声明CTRL在非平稳时序动态与分布偏移下提升鲁棒性，并在高度平稳的时序上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦推理与预测、引入分量分解与紧凑控制信号及低成本测试时自适应，CTRL在非平稳和分布偏移场景下增强鲁棒性，同时在平稳场景保持竞争力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting underpins critical decision-making across diverse domains. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising reasoning capabilities, existing LLM-based time series forecasting approaches either reduce them to numerical predictors that bypass their strengths, or allow direct forecast generation that destabilizes predictions in non-stationary settings. We introduce CTRL, a framework that decouples semantic reasoning from quantitative prediction. A frozen backbone generates base forecasts, while specialized LLM agents function as controllers that analyze backbone prediction errors through decomposed trend, seasonal, and irregular components, grounding reasoning in interpretable temporal structure. Each agent outputs compact control signals that a lightweight residual decoder translates into forecast corrections. CTRL incorporates label-free test-time adaptation that detects distribution shift from input statistics alone and readapts control signals with only 3-24 LLM calls via caching. CTRL is explicitly designed to improve robustness under non-stationary temporal dynamics and distribution shift, while remaining competitive on highly stationary time series where adaptive correction provides limited additional benefit.

</details>


### [145] [Why Ghost Outputs Teach: A Kernel-Based Understanding of Subliminal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23260)
*Zhe Li,Bicheng Ying,Chaosheng Dong,Haibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从学习动力学视角对“潜意识学习”（SL）现象给出机制性解释：推导出链式跨任务核，将“幽灵输出”监督与任务预测变化通过共享主干表示建立显式联系，并统一解释三个核心经验谜题，实验验证全部理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜意识学习（SL）已被发现存在（学生模型通过匹配看似无关的教师辅助输出即可获得下游任务能力，而从未见过任务标签、任务特定输出或原始训练数据），但其背后的优化机制仍不清楚。已有研究只定位了潜意识信号可能所在的位置，缺乏对现象成因的严格理论刻画。作者希望填补这一空白，从学习动力学角度解释 SL 为何以及如何发生。

Method: 作者在学习动力学框架下进行分析，推导出一个“链式跨任务核”（chained cross-task kernel），该核通过共享主干表示，将幽灵输出（ghost-output）监督显式地连接到任务预测的变化上。由此建立一个统一的分析框架，对 SL 中三个核心经验谜题给出严格数学解释。

Result: 理论给出三点结论：(i) 在共享初始化下，迁移算子形成严格半正定（PSD）结构，保证幽灵输出优化能在无显式标签暴露的情况下，使学生与教师真实任务目标对齐；(ii) 幽灵输出维度充当显式秩瓶颈（rank bottleneck），控制任务相关特征的迁移；(iii) 合成高熵输入作为宽带探针（broadband probe），最大化跨任务核重叠，从而解释为何随机噪声在潜意识迁移中持续优于结构化数据。在经典幽灵输出设置上的实验验证了全部三项理论预测。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个基于学习动力学的理论解释，说明幽灵输出监督如何导致潜意识学习：通过链式跨任务核与 PSD 迁移算子、秩瓶颈及宽带探针机制，统一阐明了 SL 的产生机理，并得到实验支持。

Abstract: Subliminal Learning (SL) is a recently identified phenomenon in which a student model acquires downstream task capabilities by matching seemingly unrelated auxiliary outputs from a teacher, despite never observing task labels, task-specific outputs, or the original training data. While recent studies have identified where subliminal signals may reside, the optimization mechanism underlying this phenomenon remains poorly understood. In this work, we provide a mechanistic understanding of SL through the lens of learning dynamics. Specifically, we derive a chained cross-task kernel that explicitly links ghost-output supervision to changes in task predictions through shared backbone representations. Our unified analytical framework provides a rigorous mathematical explanation for three central empirical puzzles in SL: (i) under shared initialization, the transfer operator forms a strictly Positive Semi-Definite (PSD) structure, guaranteeing that ghost-output optimization aligns the student with the teacher's true task objective without explicit label exposure; (ii) the ghost-output dimensionality acts as an explicit rank bottleneck governing the transfer of task-relevant features; and (iii) synthetic, high-entropy inputs function as broadband probes that maximize cross-task kernel overlap, explaining why random noise consistently outperforms structured data for subliminal transfer. Experiments on the canonical ghost-output setting validate all three theoretical predictions, providing the first learning-dynamics-based theoretical explanation of how ghost-output supervision gives rise to subliminal learning.

</details>


### [146] [Optimal No-Regret Learning for Repeated Prophet Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23265)
*Kun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究前缀反馈下的重复预言机不等式问题，提出高效算法实现 $\widetilde O(\sqrt{T})$ 期望遗憾，匹配下界（差对数因子），解决了 Liu 等人(2025)的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复预言机不等式在前缀反馈下，学习者需在每轮从固定顺序的 $n$ 个盒子中选择并不可撤销地接受一个值，且只能观察到停止盒子之前的前缀。已有方法对盒子数 $n$ 存在多项式依赖，效率不足，Liu 等人(2025)提出该开放问题。

Method: 算法直接通过近最优策略进行探索，将经验反向归纳与盒子特定的到达奖励(reach bonuses)相结合；随后采用相对下降聚合规则(relative-drop aggregation rule)，利用观测前缀的嵌套结构来保持探索，从而消除对盒子数 $n$ 的多项式依赖。

Result: 实现了 $\widetilde O(\sqrt{T})$ 的期望遗憾，与下界在对数因子上匹配，且消除了对盒子数 $n$ 的多项式依赖。

Conclusion: 该算法有效解决了前缀反馈下重复预言机不等式的开放问题，达到近最优遗憾并显著改善对盒子数的依赖。

Abstract: We study repeated prophet inequalities under prefix feedback. In each of $T$ rounds, a learner encounters fresh values drawn independently from $n$ boxes with unknown $[0,1]$-supported distributions in a fixed order and must irrevocably accept one, observing only the prefix up to its stopping box. Regret is measured against the optimal stopping policy that knows the distributions. We give an efficient algorithm achieving $\widetilde O(\sqrt{T})$ expected regret, matching the lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Our algorithm explores directly through near-optimal policies, combining empirical backward induction with box-specific reach bonuses. A relative-drop aggregation rule then exploits the nesting structure of observed prefixes to preserve exploration, thereby removing the polynomial dependence on the box number $n$. This resolves an open question posed by Liu et al. (2025).

</details>


### [147] [Optimal Multi-way Decision Trees for Stratified Sampling in Online Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23308)
*Tomoka Takei,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出OMST（Optimal Multi-way Stratification Trees），将分层抽样中的分层构建问题形式化为特征图上的路径选择问题，并用精确的方差最小化二元优化求解，同时引入监督最优分箱与冗余路径/分配约束约简，从而在不增加样本量的前提下提升A/B实验的统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线对照实验（A/B测试）的核心挑战是在不扩大实验样本量的前提下提升实验灵敏度（统计功效）。分层抽样是经典的方差缩减技术，但其效果高度依赖于分层（strata）的构造方式，因此需要一种能自动优化分层结构的方法。

Method: 提出OMST框架，基于最优多路决策树进行分层：将分层表述为特征图上的路径选择问题，所选路径即具可解释性的分层规则；在连续比例分配与Neyman型最优分配下，用精确的方差最小化二元优化进行求解；引入监督式最优分箱为数值特征生成与结果相关的候选切分；并设计冗余候选路径与分配约束的约简流程以显著缩小优化规模。

Result: 在真实数据集与模拟数据集上的实验表明，OMST在方差缩减上达到与现有方法相当或更优的效果，同时保持浅层且可解释的分层树。

Conclusion: OMST将分层抽样设计转化为可精确求解的优化问题，兼顾方差缩减性能与模型可解释性，为提升A/B实验灵敏度提供了一种有效的分层构建方案。

Abstract: Online controlled experiments, or A/B tests, are widely used to estimate causal effects on digital platforms. A central challenge is to improve experimental sensitivity, or statistical power, without increasing the experimental sample size. Stratified sampling is a classical variance reduction technique; however, its effectiveness depends critically on how the strata are constructed. We thus propose an optimization-based stratification framework for stratified sampling using optimal multi-way decision trees. Our method, called Optimal Multi-way Stratification Trees (OMST), formulates stratification as a path-selection problem over a feature graph. The selected paths define interpretable stratification rules and are optimized using an exact variance-minimizing binary optimization formulation under continuous proportional allocation and a Neyman-type optimal allocation. We incorporate supervised optimal binning to generate outcome-relevant candidate splits for numerical features. Furthermore, we introduce reduction procedures for redundant candidate paths and assignment constraints, substantially reducing the optimization problem size. Experiments on both a real-world and a simulated dataset demonstrate that OMST achieves comparable or superior variance reduction to existing methods while maintaining shallow and interpretable stratification trees.

</details>


### [148] [ValueDiff: Value-Geometric KV Cache Eviction for Sink-Suppressed LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23314)
*Junyoung Park,Jungwook Choi,Mingu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ValueDiff：一种基于 value 向量几何的 KV cache 驱逐方法，按 value 向量相对缓存均值的 L2 偏差排序 token，在 sink 被抑制的现代 LLM 上显著优于依赖 attention sink 的既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 采用 QK-normalization、gated attention、可学习 attention sink 或 logit softcapping，导致持续 attention sink 减弱，而现有 KV cache 驱逐方法主要依赖此类 sink。作者观察到在这些模型中，弱 sink 与 value 向量相对 key 向量更高的离散度同时出现，因此转向 value 侧几何信息设计驱逐准则。

Method: ValueDiff 按 token 的 value 向量与 cache 均值的 L2 偏差进行排序驱逐；该分数在关于未来 attention 的最大熵假设下等价于最小扰动驱逐。评测采用固定 cache 预算，prefill 阶段每个 block 边界执行驱逐，generation 阶段每个解码步执行驱逐。

Result: RULER 在 2k 紧预算下，ValueDiff 在七个 sink 被抑制模型上保留 dense 的 88--99%，其中 6/7 为最优；LongBench 在 4k 预算下平均保留 92%，最强基线为 83%；MATH-500 在 25% cache 预算下，在所有测试的 sink 抑制模型上均为最强非 dense 方法，在 gated-attention 模型上领先已有方法最多约 20 分。

Conclusion: 在三个基准上，value 几何均表现为 sink 被抑制模型中更可靠的 query-invariant 驱逐信号。

Abstract: Modern LLMs with QK-normalization, gated attention, learned attention sinks, or logit softcapping exhibit weaker persistent attention sinks, on which existing KV cache eviction methods primarily rely. We observe that across these models, weaker sinks co-occur with greater value-vector dispersion relative to key-vector dispersion. Motivated by this value-side dispersion, we present ValueDiff, a value-geometric eviction that ranks tokens by the L2 deviation of their value vectors from the cache mean. The same score arises as the minimal-disturbance eviction under a max-entropy assumption about future attention. We evaluate under fixed cache budgets, with eviction at every block boundary during prefill and at every decoding step during generation. On RULER at a tight 2k token budget, ValueDiff retains 88--99\% of dense across seven sink-suppressed models (best on 6 out of 7). On LongBench at the 4k budget, ValueDiff averages 92\% retention across sink-suppressed models versus 83\% for the strongest prior baseline. On MATH-500, ValueDiff is the strongest non-dense method on every sink-suppressed model tested at the 25\% cache budget, outperforming prior methods by up to $\sim$20 points on gated-attention models. Across all three benchmarks, value geometry emerges as the more reliable query-invariant eviction signal for sink-suppressed models.

</details>


### [149] [CSC: Calibrated Simplicity for Conflict-Aware Social Bot Detection in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23320)
*Yipeng Qian,Pengjie Zhao,Chaoxi Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出CSC（Calibrated-Simplicity）框架，用于解决LLM时代社交机器人检测中的模态冲突问题。通过简化原型引导图专家、校准单纯形约束融合和轻量不一致性专家三个设计，在TwiBot-22、TwiBot-20和MGStBot-large上提升了校准工作点决策质量，并保持外部基准竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型使社交机器人能以廉价、流畅、可扩展的方式实现语义伪装，导致仅靠文本难以检测。核心挑战是模态冲突：账号在语义上看似人类，但在图结构、画像属性或跨模态一致性上可疑。近期图方法通过增加稀疏原型选择、自适应门控等图侧复杂度来应对，但实验表明单纯增加复杂度并非解决此类冲突的可靠方法。

Method: 提出CSC校准-简化框架，包含三个设计：(1) 简化的原型引导图专家，保留有用结构偏置并去除不稳定的图侧启发式；(2) 校准的单纯形约束融合，在后期融合前对齐异构置信空间；(3) 轻量不一致性专家，建模跨模态分歧。

Result: 在TwiBot-22、TwiBot-20和MGStBot-large上，CSC提升了校准工作点的决策质量，同时在外部基准上保持竞争力。分析显示：校准提升置信可靠性；不一致性专家主要在高冲突或近阈值区域提供局部修正；简化图侧控制带来更好的稳定性-成本权衡。语义伪装压力测试表明，将选定机器人文本替换为匹配的人类文本会显著削弱独立文本专家，而图与融合证据在平衡挑战集上保持稳定。

Conclusion: 解决LLM时代社交机器人检测的模态冲突，关键不在于增加图侧架构复杂度，而在于校准与简化：通过置信校准、跨模态不一致性建模和精简图侧控制，可在保持竞争力的同时获得更可靠的决策与更优的稳定性-成本权衡。

Abstract: Social bot detection is essential for protecting online platforms from misinformation amplification, coordinated manipulation, and distorted public discourse. However, large language models have made social bots much harder to detect from text alone because semantic camouflage is now cheap, fluent, and scalable. The resulting challenge is modality conflict: an account may look human-like in semantics while remaining suspicious in graph structure, profile attributes, or cross-modal consistency. Recent graph-based detectors tackle this limitation by adding graph-side complexity, such as sparse prototype selection, adaptive gating, or architecture-specific control logic, yet our experiments suggest that complexity alone is not the most reliable way to resolve such conflict.
  We therefore propose CSC, a calibrated-simplicity framework for conflict-aware LLM-era social bot detection. The framework combines three design choices: a simplified prototype-guided graph expert that retains useful structural biases while removing unstable graph-side heuristics, calibrated simplex-constrained fusion that aligns heterogeneous confidence spaces before late fusion, and a lightweight inconsistency expert that models cross-modal disagreement. Experiments on TwiBot-22, TwiBot-20, and MGStBot-large show that \textsc{CSC} improves calibrated operating-point decision quality while remaining competitive across external benchmarks. Further analyses show that calibration improves confidence reliability, the inconsistency expert mainly provides localized corrections in high-conflict or near-threshold regions, and simplified graph-side control yields a better stability-cost trade-off. A targeted semantic-camouflage stress test further shows that replacing selected bot text with matched human text sharply degrades the standalone text expert while leaving graph and fused evidence stable on a balanced challenge set.

</details>


### [150] [Rethinking Class Imbalance for Single-Cell Foundation Models: A Systematic Benchmark Across Architectures and Long-Tail Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23325)
*Zeyu Dong,Jiahui Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对单细胞基础模型（scGPT、scBERT、Geneformer）在长尾/不平衡细胞类型上的表现进行系统基准测试：在3种架构×3个数据集×6种长尾损失×3个随机种子共162次训练中，发现总体准确率（最高97.5%）会掩盖稀有细胞类型的系统性失败，且失败可分为两种具有不同嵌入几何特征的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞基础模型的细胞类型分类总体准确率很高（实验中最高达97.5%），但这一聚合指标可能掩盖对稀有、常与疾病相关的细胞群体的系统性失败；尽管长尾损失函数被普遍认为能缓解该问题，其实际有效性缺乏系统、可控的验证。

Method: 构建系统性基准：在3种架构（scGPT、scBERT、Geneformer）、3个数据集（Multiple Sclerosis、Zheng68K、human Pancreas）上，评估6种长尾损失函数（交叉熵、加权CE、类平衡损失、focal loss、LDAM、logit-adjusted softmax），共进行162次受控训练（3×3×6×3）。比较总体准确率、Macro-F1与稀有类召回率，并分析不同损失的嵌入空间几何特征。

Result: 在全部9种（架构×数据集）设置中，普通交叉熵下总体准确率、Macro-F1与稀有类召回率之间存在一致差距，且由数据集结构而非预训练驱动。稀有类失败分为两种机制：一种可被合适的损失函数恢复，另一种虽保持线性可分性，却在所有损失与架构下被吸收进无关类的邻域。对于可恢复类别，重加权效果由类别的绝对训练集规模预测，而非其数据占比或数据集整体不平衡比。类平衡损失与LDAM在9种设置中表现最稳定；logit adjustment仅以牺牲稀有类精确率换取召回率，未能同时提升两者。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可复用的基准和基于机制的实际指导，用于将单细胞基础模型与不平衡生物数据结合；同时指出稀有类失败具有可预判的嵌入几何特征，且应依据绝对样本量而非相对占比来选择重加权策略。

Abstract: Single-cell foundation models (scGPT, scBERT, Geneformer) achieve cell-type classification accuracy up to 97.5% in our experiments, yet this aggregate accuracy can mask systematic failure on rare, often disease-relevant cell populations that long-tail loss functions are widely assumed to address. We present a systematic benchmark of six long-tail loss functions (cross-entropy, weighted CE, class-balanced loss, focal loss, LDAM, logit-adjusted softmax) across three architectures and three datasets (Multiple Sclerosis, Zheng68K, human Pancreas), totaling 162 controlled training runs (3 backbones x 3 datasets x 6 losses x 3 seeds). The gap between overall accuracy, Macro-F1, and rare-class recall under plain cross-entropy is consistent across all nine (architecture, dataset) settings, driven by dataset structure rather than pretraining. Rare-class failure itself splits into two regimes with distinct embedding-geometry signatures, visible before any loss is chosen: some classes are recoverable by the right loss, while others retain linear separability yet are absorbed into unrelated classes' neighborhoods under every evaluated loss and architecture. Among the recoverable classes, the efficacy of reweighting is predicted by a class's absolute training-set size, rather than its share of the dataset or the dataset's overall imbalance ratio. Class-balanced loss and LDAM are the most consistent choices across all nine settings, while logit adjustment trades rare-class precision for recall rather than improving both. Our results give both a reusable benchmark and mechanism-grounded practical guidelines for combining foundation models with imbalanced biological data.

</details>


### [151] [A Patient World Model for Early Forecasting of Digital Health Campaign Outcomes: Capabilities and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23333)
*Yunlong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将数字DTC健康活动的动态预测建模为动态系统问题，构建患者隐状态世界模型，联合学习暴露条件下的状态动态与周度转化风险，并向前滚动预测转化曲线。在14.7万患者、520万风险人周的数据上，第4周截止时预测至第52周相对误差2.9%，第8–26周截止时为0.8–2.6%，显著优于池化风险梯度提升和分horizon分类器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字DTC健康活动通常只能事后测量，进行中预测多依赖针对每个截断点和预测horizon单独训练的分类器，模型冗余、效率低且缺乏动态系统视角。论文希望用统一的动态系统建模患者状态演化与转化风险，实现紧凑、可滚动的前瞻预测。

Method: 将任务视为动态系统问题，构建紧凑的患者世界模型：为每位患者维护隐状态，学习暴露条件下的状态动态，并与周度转化风险（hazard）联合训练；模型可向前滚动生成未来转化曲线。加入Fisher信息分析以论证在转化稀有情况下需要密集的下一暴露监督，并设置辅助目标。评估包括回顾性预测（以记录的未来暴露为条件）和情景模拟（如关闭全部未来暴露）。

Result: 在美国活动数据（147,173名患者、520万风险人周）上，以记录的未来暴露为条件的回顾评估中，从第4周截断预测至第52周的剩余新品牌处方量相对误差为2.9%，第8–26周截断为0.8–2.6%。最强非循环基线（同等生存滚动与信息的池化风险梯度提升）相对误差为13.6–33.1%，分horizon分类器表现更差。移除密集下一暴露监督的辅助目标使处方量误差增加约2–14倍，且在更常见的专科就诊结局上无一致劣势。情景模拟中关闭全部未来暴露使预测转化率从0.31升至0.89，与观察性暴露数据中的选择效应一致。

Conclusion: 患者世界模型在动态DTC活动的前瞻预测中显著优于非循环基线和分horizon分类器，密集下一暴露监督对稀有转化事件至关重要。但暴露条件滚动预测不能因果解释，关闭未来暴露导致转化上升的现象更可能反映选择效应，提示该类模型的因果解释存在局限。

Abstract: Digital direct-to-consumer (DTC) health campaigns are usually measured after the fact. In-flight forecasting commonly relies on a separate classifier for every cutoff and horizon. We treat this task as a dynamic-system problem and build a compact patient world model. The architecture maintains a latent state per patient, learns exposure-conditioned state dynamics jointly with a weekly conversion hazard, and rolls forward into future conversion curves. We evaluate it on a US campaign dataset with 147{,}173 patients and 5.2 million at-risk person-weeks. In a retrospective evaluation conditioned on recorded future exposures, the model forecasts the remaining new-to-brand prescription volume through week 52 with a relative error of 2.9\% from a week-4 cutoff and 0.8--2.6\% from cutoffs at weeks 8--26. The strongest non-recurrent baseline, a pooled-hazard gradient boosting model given the same survival rollout and information, has relative errors of 13.6--33.1\%. Per-horizon classifiers perform substantially worse. A Fisher-information analysis motivates dense next-exposure supervision when conversions are rare. Removing this auxiliary objective increases prescription-volume error by approximately $2$--$14\times$, while providing no consistent disadvantage on the more common specialist-visit outcome. We also evaluate scenario simulation. Switching all future exposure off raises predicted conversion from 0.31 to 0.89, a pattern consistent with selection effects in observational exposure data. This result highlights the limits of interpreting exposure-conditioned rollouts causally.

</details>


### [152] [What Can a Recurrent State Safely Forget?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23366)
*Linzhe Zhang,Changming Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文通过"预测商"刻画循环模型记忆与收缩的边界，并给出有限未来审计框架及最优探测复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环模型中，保持影响未来行为的信息与抑制隐状态误差存在冲突：收缩提升稳定性，但沿"区分未来"方向的收缩会破坏记忆。作者希望形式化这一边界，并在可审计条件下量化语义保持的纠正能力与探测代价。

Method: 提出隐状态空间的预测商：当两隐状态诱导相同条件未来时视为等价，其等价类构成预测纤维；证明任何语义保持的精确纠正器在商空间上为恒等映射。在隐维d、预测维k的正则点处，最多消除d−k个独立方向。进而构建有限未来可审计框架：以部署库W与独立审计库A（W⊆A）在声明的纠正域上评估，借助生成式探测访问与审计度量覆盖，用有限随机rollout给出分离边际Ω_{W|A}(δ)的高概率证书；并给出内在审计维k下所需探测结果的O(M·Ω^{-(k+2)})标度及匹配的极小极大下界，同时用完备性模数推广到连续未来。

Result: 建立离散—连续边界：有限预测状态具有正半径的精确纠正盆地，而不可数连续的未来可区分状态在有限维欧氏空间中经受任意正半径扰动后无法解码。证书化边际内保持W预测可保证有界审计语义失真；探测复杂度标度为O(M·Ω^{-(k+2)})，且该指数由匹配下界证明最优。控制实验验证了证书边际、标度律与自动探测细化，并采用安全优先评估范式。

Conclusion: 该工作以预测商统一刻画循环表示的稳定性—记忆权衡，给出可审计的有限未来正确性证书、最优探测复杂度及连续未来的扩展模数，为安全优先的循环模型评估与纠正提供理论基础与实验支持。

Abstract: Recurrent models must preserve information that changes future behavior while suppressing hidden-state error. These objectives conflict: contraction improves stability, but contraction along a future-distinguishing direction destroys memory. We formalize this boundary through the predictive quotient of a recurrent state space. Two hidden states are equivalent when they induce the same conditional future; their equivalence classes form predictive fibers. Every exact semantics-preserving corrector acts as the identity on this quotient. At a regular point with hidden dimension d and predictive dimension k, it can eliminate at most d - k independent directions. This establishes a discrete-continuous boundary: finite predictive states admit positive-radius exact correction basins, whereas an uncountable continuum of future-distinguishable states cannot be decoded after arbitrary positive-radius perturbations in finite-dimensional Euclidean space.
  To operationalize this principle, we develop an auditable finite-future framework. A compact deployment bank W is evaluated against an independent audit bank A (W subseteq A) on a declared correction domain. Under generative probe access and audit-metric coverage, finite stochastic rollouts furnish a high-probability certificate for the separation margin Omega_{W|A}(delta). Preserving learned W-predictions within this certified margin guarantees bounded audit-semantic distortion. For intrinsic audit dimension k, the required probe outcomes scale as O(M * Omega^{-(k+2)}), where M = |A|; a matching minimax lower bound proves this exponent is optimal. Extending guarantees to continuous futures is achieved via an explicit completeness modulus. Controlled experiments validate the certified margins, scaling laws, and automated probe refinement under a safety-first evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [153] [The Evidence Ladder for Reinforcement Learning in Healthcare: From Retrospective Policies to Trusted Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23374)
*Yunfan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述以"证据阶梯"（问题建模→回顾性识别→策略估计→压力测试→前瞻性评估→生命周期监测）为组织框架，系统梳理医疗保健强化学习研究，指出历史数据上得分高的策略并不等于能改善临床照护，并呼吁将医疗RL视为嵌入动态社会技术系统中的干预来评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习适合刻画结果随时间展开的医疗决策，但多数研究进展仍难以转化为常规临床干预。既有综述多按算法或临床应用分类，缺乏对证据强度层级的审视，因此需要一个以证据等级为主线的综述框架。

Method: 采用"证据阶梯"式综述方法，将医疗RL研究分为六个层级：问题建模、回顾性识别、策略估计、压力测试、前瞻性评估与生命周期监测；对每一层级综合其假设与失效模式，辨析哪些证据可跨场景迁移、哪些不可迁移，并提出面向累积性评估的报告规范；同时将restless bandits作为其中一个特例而非组织性框架纳入讨论。

Result: 揭示了该领域反复出现的证据缺口：策略在历史数据集上评分高，并不能证明其能改善照护。综述同时明确了各证据层级的假设与失效模式，并厘清了可迁移与不可迁移证据的边界。

Conclusion: 医疗RL应作为嵌入在持续变化的社会技术系统中的干预来评估，而不能仅被视作对回顾性奖励的优化器；研究应遵循面向累积性评估的报告实践，以支撑从回顾性证据到前瞻性临床验证的推进。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a natural language for healthcare decisions whose conse- quences unfold over time, yet most reported progress remains far from routine intervention. Ex- isting surveys organize the field by algorithm or clinical application. We instead review healthcare RL through an evidence ladder: problem formulation, retrospective identification, policy estima- tion, stress testing, prospective evaluation, and lifecycle monitoring. This view connects clinical treatment, patient engagement, and health-system operations while exposing a recurring gap: evi- dence that a policy scores well in a historical dataset is not evidence that it will improve care. We synthesize the assumptions and failure modes at each rung, identify what evidence can and can- not transfer across settings, and propose reporting practices for cumulative evaluation. Restless bandits are included as one special case, not as the organizing framework. The central lesson is that healthcare RL should be evaluated as an intervention embedded in a changing sociotechnical system, rather than only as an optimizer of a retrospective reward.

</details>


### [154] [Discovering Physical Representation Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23381)
*Linzhe Zhang,Changming Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"物理表示-语言发现"问题：在匿名受控实验下，直接从观测中恢复隐藏的物理本体（载体、微分序列、测量类型、对偶结构、规范等价等），给出可识别性理论、多项式时间构造性流程，以及端到端两阶段测量界与匹配的极小极大率，并在Maxwell与RLC系统上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器要发现物理定律，首先必须发现其测量本身意味着什么：哪些观测定义在胞元上、哪些是强度量或广延量、哪些扇区互为对偶、哪些区分只是规范冗余。现有科学机器学习大多在人类给定的语言中学习定律，而非发现使定律可表达的"语言"，因此缺乏对观测可识别性极限的理论刻画。

Method: 提出物理表示-语言发现框架：将物理干扰转化为交换子（commutant），用精化（refinement）把"量"与"坐标"分离，先恢复表示再选择物理。给出可识别性理论与多项式时间构造性算法，用于恢复载体与微分序列、测量类型与定向扭转、不可逆精化语义、原始-对偶Maxwell图以及残余等价。对认证有限实验族，推导两阶段测量界与极小极大率。

Result: 对认证有限实验族证明了端到端两阶段测量界，并在维度、精度与置信度上得到匹配的极小极大率。盲Maxwell实验在规则与非结构化载体、联合损坏观测下可恢复完整的原始/相对对偶本体；独立非结构化RLC系统表明结果不局限于Maxwell。框架可扩展至每载体数万胞元，压力审计显示其在非马尔可夫记忆、非线性、非局域性及复杂本构迟滞等严苛物理情形下仍具鲁棒性；公开FDTD审计展示了从不完整场数据中涌现匿名旋度结构，并刻画了完全恢复的信息论前提。

Conclusion: 该工作把科学ML从"在人类给定语言中学习定律"推进到"发现使定律可表达的語言"，并为观测可识别性建立了精确的理论极限。其物理表示-语言发现框架在多种系统与严苛条件下均具普适性与鲁棒性，可作为匿名实验下恢复隐藏物理本体的通用方法论。

Abstract: Before a machine can discover a physical law, it must discover what its measurements are: which observations live on cells, which are intensive or extensive, which sectors are dual, and which distinctions are merely gauge. We introduce physical representation-language discovery, the problem of recovering this hidden ontology directly from anonymous controlled experiments. We give an identifiability theory and constructive polynomial-time procedure that recovers a carrier and differential sequence, measurement types and orientation twist, noninvertible refinement semantics, primal-dual Maxwell diagrams, and the residual equivalences that no permitted experiment can break. The theory turns material nuisance into a commutant, uses refinement to separate quantities from coordinates, and selects physics only after its representation has been recovered. For a certified finite experiment family, we prove an end-to-end two-stage measurement bound and a matching minimax rate in dimension, accuracy, and confidence. Blind Maxwell experiments recover complete primal/relative-dual ontologies on regular and unstructured carriers under jointly corrupted observations; an independent unstructured RLC system demonstrates that the result is not specific to Maxwell. The framework scales to tens of thousands of cells per carrier, while stress audits demonstrate robustness across severe physical regimes - including non-Markovian memory, nonlinearities, nonlocality, and complex constitutive hysteresis.
