A public FDTD audit demonstrates the emergence of anonymous curl structure from incomplete field data, while characterizing the informational prerequisites for complete recovery. The goal is to move scientific ML from learning laws in a human-supplied language to discovering the language in which laws become expressible, establishing exact theoretical limits on observational identifiability.

</details>


### [155] [Blind Thermodynamic Ontology Discovery from Anonymous Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23387)
*Linzhe Zhang,Changming Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何从"匿名"受控实验中直接发现隐藏的热力学本体（广延/强度标度扇区、共轭配对、凹势与储量系综拟阵），提出可操作可辨识性理论与多项式时间构造算法，并证明残余观测等价类，在范德华流体、居里-外斯磁体及六种真实流体上验证了稳健恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在学习热力学状态方程前，必须先弄清测量量的物理含义：哪些通道随系统尺度缩放、哪些是强度共轭量、扇区如何通过接触配对、哪个势决定稳定性。当传感器只暴露广延态与强度响应的未知线性混合时，被动观测无法区分物理量与坐标伪影，因此需要主动的受控实验来发现这一隐藏的热力学本体。

Method: 将问题形式化为从匿名受控实验中发现隐藏热力学本体；提出可操作的可辨识性理论；设计多项式时间构造算法：从复制对比中提取广延与强度标度扇区，从热接触与互易性恢复其共轭余切配对，通过离散循环凹性验证全局可行凹势，并确定储量系综的不变拟阵；同时证明残余观测等价类为 (x, λ) ~ (A x, a A^{-T} λ + β)。

Result: 在范德华流体与居里-外斯磁体上的盲评估表明，即使在病态混合条件下也能稳健恢复，并正确解析匿名麦克斯韦联结线、拒绝非平衡延拓；在 NIST WebBook 六种真实流体上的外部验证显示，操作型本体发现可跨真实物质迁移且无坐标泄漏。

Conclusion: 论文给出了从匿名受控实验中发现热力学本体的可辨识性理论与构造算法，并刻画了任何允许实验都无法突破的尖锐观测极限，证明该方法可从合成模型迁移到真实物理物质。

Abstract: Before a machine learning model can learn a thermodynamic equation of state, it must discover what its measurements represent: which channels scale with system size, which are intensive conjugates, how sectors pair through contact, and which potential governs stability. When sensors expose only an unknown linear mixture of extensive states and intensive responses, passive observations cannot disentangle physical quantities from coordinate artifacts. We formulate the problem of discovering this hidden thermodynamic ontology directly from anonymous controlled experiments. We present an operational identifiability theory and a constructive polynomial-time algorithm that extracts extensive and intensive scaling sectors from replication contrasts, recovers their dual cotangent pairing from thermal contact and reciprocity, verifies a globally admissible concave potential via discrete cyclic concavity, and determines an invariant matroid of reservoir ensembles. We prove that the residual observational equivalence is strictly (x, lambda) ~ (A x, a A^{-T} lambda + beta), establishing the sharp observational limit that no permitted experiment can break. Blind evaluations on van der Waals fluids and Curie-Weiss magnets confirm robust recovery under ill-conditioned mixing, correctly resolving anonymous Maxwell tie-lines while rejecting non-equilibrium continuations. External validation across six real fluids from the NIST WebBook demonstrates that operational ontology discovery transfers across real physical substances without coordinate leakage.

</details>


### [156] [Tool-Augmented On-Policy Distillation for LLM Domain Adaptation in Sequence-Based Omics Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23435)
*Jie Ying,Zhefan Wang,Zihong Chen,Zhengqing Li,Jinzhe Li,Gang Li,Jian Liu,Fang Hu,Tao Luo,Zhonghang Yuan,Wanli Ouyang,Stan Z. Li,Fan Yang,Nanqing Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmicsBench——首个面向多组学序列的推理基准（含 1,160 道专家验证题目、覆盖 DNA 调控/RNA 加工/蛋白功能六类任务），并基于其实证发现提出 TA-OPD 训练方法以对齐预测与证据链推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学序列蕴含复杂生物模式，LLM 在其上做自动化科学发现时，现有基准仅用分类/回归指标衡量预测结果，忽略了模型是否掌握背后的生物学证据，导致无法诊断其推理缺陷。

Method: 1) 构建 OmicsBench：1,160 道专家验证问题，覆盖 DNA 调控、RNA 加工、蛋白功能三类领域共六项任务，要求提供可追溯的证据链，并使用与领域专家共同开发的实例级评分标准（instance-specific rubrics）进行评估；2) 评测 17 个 LLM；3) 提出工具增强的在线策略蒸馏（TA-OPD），一种后训练方法，在 0.8B–27B 的五个 Qwen3.5 模型上对齐序列预测与基于证据的生物学推理。

Result: 评测 17 个 LLM 发现反向关系：科学专用 LLM 在序列分类准确率上优于通用 LLM，但无法给出支持其预测的有效证据，可能源于捷径学习（依赖统计模式而非生物学机制）。TA-OPD 在五个 Qwen3.5 模型上一致增强证据 grounding，并在多数任务上提升预测性能，且增益跨模型规模保持。

Conclusion: 更强的序列推理并非仅来自模型容量提升，而可通过证据感知训练获得；OmicsBench 与 TA-OPD 共同构成诊断多组学 LLM 推理失败并通向"预测由生物学证据支撑"的框架。

Abstract: Multi-omics sequences contain complex biological patterns, yet deciphering their mechanisms for automated scientific discovery remains challenging. As large language models (LLMs) interpret these sequences, evaluating both predictions and scientific reasoning is critical. However, existing benchmarks for multi-omics sequence tasks rely on classification and regression metrics, neglecting whether models grasp the underlying biological evidence. We introduce OmicsBench, the first reasoning benchmark for multi-omics sequences, comprising 1,160 expert-validated questions across six tasks spanning DNA regulation, RNA processing, and protein function. OmicsBench requires traceable evidence chains, evaluated using instance-specific rubrics developed with domain experts. Evaluating 17 LLMs reveals an inverse relationship: while scientific LLMs outperform general-purpose LLMs in sequence classification accuracy, they fail to provide valid evidence to support their predictions. One plausible interpretation is shortcut learning: specialized models may rely on statistical patterns rather than the biological mechanisms needed for scientific discovery. Motivated by this finding, we introduce tool-augmented on-policy distillation (TA-OPD), a post-training method to align sequence prediction with evidence-grounded biological reasoning. Across five Qwen3.5 models spanning 0.8B to 27B parameters, TA-OPD consistently strengthens biological evidence grounding while improving predictive performance on most tasks. These gains persist across model scales, indicating that stronger sequence reasoning does not arise solely from increased model capacity, but can be improved through evidence-aware training. Together, OmicsBench and TA-OPD provide a framework for diagnosing reasoning failures in multi-omics LLMs and a path toward models whose predictions are better grounded in biologically meaningful evidence.

</details>


### [157] [RLVR$^{2}$: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rubric-based Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23457)
*Hao Li,Zhengkun Zhang,Gangqiang Hu,Zhen Zhang,Yude Gao,Dai Dai,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RLVR²，将多维度评分标准转化为组内序数比较，从比较矩阵恢复潜在效用并融合为单一训练信号，避免异构评分尺度的标定问题，在3个模型规模、16个基准上持续优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR正从数学、代码等具有明确正确性信号的任务，扩展到由多维度评分标准（rubric）规定的综合性质量要求。策略优化每次采样只能消费一个标量，因此基于rubric的流程必须把多个准则分数聚合成标量奖励。这一聚合常被当作简单的分数缩放，实则隐含决定了训练中各质量维度之间的权衡方式。主流做法（各准则归一化后线性加权）假设不同准则的基数分数差可比、且某一准则的提升能补偿另一准则的失败；当准则在语义上异质时，这两个假设均不可靠。

Method: 提出RLVR²（Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rubric-based Ranking），一种面向rubric-based RLVR的可验证排序范式。对每个准则，将rubric分数转换为准则特定的组内序数结果，从所得比较矩阵中恢复潜在效用，再将这些效用合并为单一训练信号。仅保留组内排序、丢弃原始分数幅值，从而无需标定异质评分尺度；并支持保持目标的属性调整——与观测排序相关但并非训练目标的辅助属性可参与估计，而无需扩展rubric或直接对其给予奖励。

Result: 在3个模型规模和16个基准上，RLVR²持续优于代表性的rubric-based基线，在每个规模下多数基准取得最佳总体性能。分析表明，该方法在保持质量目标的同时，能控制与推理效率和回答格式相关的系统性效应。

Conclusion: 以序数效用替代跨准则的基数分数聚合，为异质rubric下的RLVR提供了无需尺度标定的可验证训练信号，并在多规模、多基准上验证了其有效性与对效率、格式等干扰因素的控制能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is expanding from tasks with well-defined correctness signals, such as mathematics and code, toward multifaceted quality requirements specified by multi-dimensional rubrics. Since policy optimization consumes one scalar per rollout, rubric-based pipelines must map multiple criterion scores into a scalar reward. This aggregation is often treated as score scaling, but it implicitly determines how quality dimensions trade off during training. The prevailing practice, normalizing each criterion and taking a linear combination, assumes that cardinal score differences are comparable across criteria and that gains on one criterion compensate for failures on another; both assumptions are unreliable when criteria are semantically heterogeneous. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rubric-based Ranking (RLVR$^2$), a verifiable ranking paradigm for rubric-based RLVR. For each criterion, RLVR$^2$ converts rubric scores into criterion-specific within-group ordinal outcomes, recovers a latent utility from the resulting comparison matrix, and merges these utilities into one training signal. By retaining only within-group ordering and discarding raw score magnitudes, RLVR$^2$ avoids calibrating heterogeneous rubric scales. It further supports objective-preserving attribute adjustment: auxiliary attributes that correlate with observed rankings but are not training objectives can enter the estimation without expanding the rubric or rewarding them directly. Across three model scales and 16 benchmarks, RLVR$^2$ consistently outperforms representative rubric-based baselines, achieving the best overall performance on most benchmarks at every scale. Analysis shows it controls systematic effects tied to reasoning efficiency and response formatting while preserving the quality objective.

</details>


### [158] [TRACE: Tractable Routing Autoencoder for Clinical ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23460)
*Shunbo Jia,Runze Ma,Haonan Lyu,Haijin Zhang,Qiang Yang,Caizhi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE 是一种将 32 维临床潜空间预先按领域知识划分为灌注、结构、传导子空间的可溯源 ECG 诊断框架，每个子空间路由至独立诊断头，兼顾高精度与决策路径可审核性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高精度 ECG 深度学习模型（尤其是基于数百万记录预训练的基础模型）缺乏决策路径可审核性：临床医生无法将诊断追溯到生理通路，也无法对某一通路进行干预。作者希望在保持甚至超越现有精度的同时，提供可解释、可干预的诊断路径。

Method: 提出 TRACE（Tractable Routing Autoencoder for Clinical ECG）。其 32 维临床潜空间由领域知识预先指定而非优化发现，被划分为灌注、结构、传导三个子空间；每个子空间按设计路由到专属诊断头，并用正交性惩罚正则化该划分；解码器支持潜变量扰动以重建 ECG。评估包括：PTB-XL 与 Georgia 数据集上的线性探针（冻结特征）、CPSC2018 九标签队列的迁移（仅重设路由表为灌注/节奏/传导划分）、联合探针、擦除与扰动分析，以及消融和容量匹配的排列对照。

Result: 在 PTB-XL 和 Georgia 上，TRACE 超过无约束分类器，并在参数量约为八分之一的情况下，优于在千万级记录上预训练的 ECG 基础模型（线性探针评估）。在无结构类别的 CPSC2018 九标签队列上，仅重设路由表即可迁移。探针、擦除与扰动分析验证了路由契约；扰动去极化与复极化通路可调节重建波形。移除指定划分及正交性惩罚在 PTB-XL 上损失 1.70 AUC 与 11.30 macro-F1，在 Georgia 上损失 2.76 AUC 与 16.92 macro-F1。容量匹配的排列对照中，任意分配与本体路由的 AUC 差距在 0.34 以内，macro-F1 无统计差异（p=0.619）。

Conclusion: 本体驱动的路由在不牺牲 macro-F1 的前提下提供了决策路径可审核性；任意排列虽可获得相近的判别性能，但只有基于领域本体指定的子空间划分才能保证诊断可追溯到生理通路并支持干预。

Abstract: Deep learning has advanced automated electrocardiogram (ECG) diagnosis, but the field's most accurate models, foundation models pretrained on millions of recordings, are not decision-pathway auditable: a clinician cannot trace a diagnosis to a physiological pathway or intervene on one. We propose TRACE, a Tractable Routing Autoencoder for Clinical ECG, whose 32-dimensional clinical latent space is specified in advance from domain knowledge rather than discovered by optimization. TRACE partitions this space into perfusion, structure, and conduction subspaces, routes each to its own diagnostic head by design, regularizes the partition with an orthogonality penalty, and reconstructs the ECG through a decoder that permits latent perturbation. On PTB-XL and Georgia, TRACE exceeds unconstrained classifiers and stays ahead of an ECG foundation model pretrained on ten million recordings, evaluated by linear probe on frozen features, at roughly an eighth of the parameter count. On the nine-label CPSC2018 cohort, which carries no structural class, the framework transfers with only the routing table re-specified to a perfusion/rhythm/conduction partition. Joint probe, erasure, and perturbation analyses verify the routing contract, and perturbing the depolarization and repolarization pathways modulates the reconstructed waveform. Removing the specified partition and its orthogonality penalty costs 1.70 AUC and 11.30 macro-F1 points on PTB-XL, and 2.76 AUC and 16.92 macro-F1 points on Georgia. A capacity-matched permutation control places arbitrary assignments within 0.34 AUC points of the ontology routing and leaves macro-F1 statistically level (p=0.619): the ontology supplies decision-pathway auditability at no macro-F1 cost.

</details>


### [159] [ITSY: Causal Discovery From Irregular Time-Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23516)
*Wenbo Xu,Yue He,Yunhai Wang,Yueguo Chen,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出ITSY，首个面向不规则时间序列（含缺失观测）的线性结构因果模型连续优化因果发现方法。它通过重构结构方程使用最近可用历史进行预测，并联合补全缺失值、学习同期与滞后因果图。实验表明其在多种缺失/规模/图密度/噪声条件下及真实基准上均优于代表性SCM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列结构因果模型可恢复同期与滞后效应，但多数方法要求完整观测窗口，面对样本缺失时会出现模型误设，因而无法有效处理不规则时间序列。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: ITSY是首个针对不规则时间序列线性模型的连续优化因果发现方法。其核心是重写结构方程，使预测依据最近可用的历史而非可能缺失的当前切片；在此框架下联合补全缺失值并同时学习同期图与滞后图；并引入加权重构目标以校正该重构带来的噪声变换。

Result: 在变化缺失率、数据规模、图密度和噪声的合成场景以及一个真实世界基准上，ITSY在图恢复性能上持续优于代表性的基于SCM的基线方法，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为不规则线性一阶动力学提供了一个针对性的解决方案，并明确了向非线性或高阶扩展所需的假设条件。

Abstract: Structural causal models for time series recover contemporaneous and lagged effects, but most methods require complete observation windows and become misspecified when samples are missing. We introduce ITSY, the first continuous-optimization method for causal discovery from irregular time series under a linear model. ITSY reformulates the structural equation so that prediction uses the nearest available history rather than the possibly missing current slice, and jointly imputes missing values while learning both graphs. A weighted reconstruction objective corrects the noise transformation induced by this reformulation. Across synthetic regimes varying missingness, scale, graph density, and noise, and on a real world benchmark, ITSY consistently improves graph recovery over representative SCM-based baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed method. The results establish a focused solution for irregular linear first-order dynamics and clarify the assumptions required for nonlinear or higher-order extensions.

</details>


### [160] [Feature Suppression and Differential Privacy for Residential Traffic Classification: A Two-Home Federated Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23521)
*Márton Pál Lipcsey-Magyar,Adrian Pekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在双客户端联邦学习仿真中比较全特征基线、特征抑制（FS）与DP-SGD：FS-mild（移除4个时序特征）在宏F1与最差组F1上稳定优于该DP-SGD配置，但无形式化隐私保证；DP-SGD在少数类（尤其较小家庭）损失明显；成员推断探测接近随机水平但未确立等价隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅流量分类服务于服务管理，但跨家庭学习需应对流量异构与隐私约束；隐私感知训练在不同流量类别上可能带来不均衡代价，因此需研究隐私与效用（尤其类别公平性）之间的权衡。

Method: 基于网关采集的162万条预处理流量、6个类别，构建双客户端联邦学习仿真；对比全特征基线、特征抑制（FS-mild，从16个模型输入中排除4个时序特征）和固定记录级隐私设置下的DP-SGD；采用按规模比例聚合，在两种模型容量、分层划分与时序划分下运行5个随机种子，并以宏F1与最差组F1（各家庭中的最小类别F1）为指标；此外用基于损失与影子模型的成员推断探测评估隐私。

Result: 按规模比例聚合时，FS-mild在全部5个种子、两种模型容量、分层与时序划分下，其合并宏F1与最差组F1均高于DP-SGD；测试的DP-SGD配置导致明显的少数类性能损失，在较小家庭中尤为突出；FS-mild相对全特征基线并非一致提升；在分层划分模型上，两类成员推断探测的聚合判别接近随机水平，且探测间排序不一致。

Conclusion: FS可作为输入最小化的基线方法，用于缓解隐私感知训练中的类别不均衡代价，但不能替代形式化隐私保证；成员推断探测的近随机结果不足以确立等价隐私，需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Residential traffic classification supports service management, but learning across homes must account for heterogeneous traffic and privacy constraints. Privacy-aware training may impose uneven costs across traffic categories. We study this tradeoff in simulated two-client federated learning using 1.62 million preprocessed gateway-collected flows across six categories. We compare a full-feature baseline, feature suppression (FS), and differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) under one fixed record-level privacy setting. FS-mild excludes four timing features from 16 model inputs; it provides no formal privacy guarantee. With size-proportional aggregation, FS-mild achieves higher combined macro-F1 and worst-group F1 (the minimum per-class F1 across homes) than DP-SGD in all five seeds at both model capacities under stratified and temporal splits. The tested DP-SGD configuration incurs pronounced minority-category losses, especially in the smaller home, but FS-mild does not uniformly improve on the full-feature baseline. On stratified-split models, loss-based and shadow-model membership probes show near-chance aggregate discrimination without a consistent ranking across probes; this does not establish equivalent privacy. These findings support FS as an input-minimization baseline, not a substitute for formal privacy.

</details>


### [161] [Predicting Out-of-Distribution Generalization of Neural Operators via Observable Spectral Error Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23529)
*Hang-Cheng Dong,Pengcheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结构保持框架，将学习到的解算子参数化为作用于椭圆算子特征值的谱滤波器 h_θ(λ)，从而把能量范数误差精确分解为谱逼近项与分布相关的谱加权项，并据此导出可在无真解条件下计算的三种OOD诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子求解PDE时在分布偏移下可靠性不足，而现有OOD泛化方法多为经验性、黑箱式，只报告聚合误差指标，无法解释误差来源或预测误差何时增大，阻碍了实际部署。

Method: 将学习解算子参数化为作用在椭圆算子特征值上的谱滤波器 h_θ(λ)，用切比雪夫多项式展开实现并以弱形式目标训练；该参数化允许将能量范数误差精确分解为模型相关的谱逼近项和输入诱导的分布相关谱加权项，进而导出保守带内上确界 ε_sup、全局RMS代理 ε_rms 与样本相关有效度量 ε_eff(f) 三种诊断。

Result: 四组受控实验表明，在分布内、带内谱偏移、带外尾部及复合偏移情形下，ε_eff(f)‖f‖ 能一致地预测能量误差，而全局指标可能系统性地产生误导；三种诊断均无需真解即可计算。

Conclusion: 该框架将神经算子的OOD评估从黑箱基准测试转变为算子结构诊断，为可审计的科学机器学习提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as powerful surrogates for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), yet their reliability under distribution shift remains a critical barrier to deployment. Existing approaches to out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization in operator learning are largely empirical and black-box: they report aggregate error metrics without explaining why errors arise or when they will grow. We propose a structure-preserving framework that makes OOD generalization predictable and auditable. Our key idea is to parameterize the learned solution operator as a spectral filter $h_θ(λ)$ acting on the eigenvalues of the underlying elliptic operator, implemented via Chebyshev polynomial expansions and trained with a weak-form objective. This parameterization admits an exact decomposition of the energy-norm error into two observable components: a model-dependent spectral approximation term and a distribution-dependent spectral weighting term induced by the input. From this decomposition we derive three diagnostics: a conservative in-band supremum $\vareps_{\mathrm{sup}}$, a global RMS proxy $\vareps_{\mathrm{rms}}$, and a sample-dependent effective metric $\vareps_{\mathrm{eff}}(f)$. These diagnostics can be computed without access to ground-truth solutions. Through four controlled experiments, we show that $\vareps_{\mathrm{eff}}(f)\|f\|$ consistently predicts energy error under in-distribution, in-band spectral shift, out-of-band tail, and compound shifts, whereas global metrics can be systematically misleading. Our framework shifts OOD assessment of neural operators from black-box benchmarking to operator-structure diagnostics, providing a practical route to auditable scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [162] [Decoupled Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23535)
*Zhengkang Guan,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦因果发现（DCD），通过加权函数解耦非目标变量直接识别马尔可夫边界（MB），并利用MB内的结构不对称性迭代构建CPDAG，理论上证明了可识别性、可靠性与完备性，在噪声场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现是科学研究中的基础且具挑战性的任务。现有方法主要依赖条件独立性检验、结构评分或受限的函数假设，存在局限，因此需要一种不依赖这些方法的新视角。

Method: 提出DCD方法，基于解耦视角：通过加权函数解耦非目标变量，使得在解耦分布下只有MB内变量与目标保持依赖，从而直接识别MB；在此基础上利用MB内的结构不对称性迭代构建CPDAG。

Result: 理论上建立了DCD的可识别性、可靠性与完备性；实证评估表明DCD取得强劲性能，尤其在具有挑战性的噪声机制下表现突出。

Conclusion: DCD提供了一种不依赖条件独立性检验、结构评分或受限函数假设的因果发现新范式，兼具理论保证与实证优势。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data is a fundamental yet challenging task in scientific research. While existing approaches are primarily based on conditional independence tests, structure scores, or restrictive functional assumptions, we propose Decoupled Causal Discovery (DCD), a novel decoupling-based perspective that does not rely on these methodologies. DCD directly identifies the Markov boundary (MB) by decoupling non-target variables via weighting functions, such that only variables within the MB preserve dependence with the target under the decoupled distribution. Building on this, DCD iteratively constructs the Completed Partially Directed Acyclic Graph (CPDAG) by exploiting structural asymmetries within the MBs. We establish the theoretical identifiability, soundness, and completeness of DCD. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that DCD achieves strong performance, particularly excelling in challenging noise regimes.

</details>


### [163] [Preserving Geometric Integrity in Graph Prompting via Measure-Constrained Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23547)
*Xiangyu Wang,Shuo Wang,Ruiyi Fang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MINT将图提示学习中节点到提示的分配建模为熵正则化最优传输问题，用预设的提示侧边际约束全局提示利用率，并给出精确方差分解与稳定性界，在少样本引文与异质图上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图提示学习让路由决策日益节点自适应，但各自独立优化的局部决策会共同把分配质量集中到有限共享提示库中的少数提示上，导致全局提示利用率失衡，即便每个节点—提示匹配在局部仍然合理。

Method: 提出MINT（Measure-INtegrity Transport），一个熵正则化最优传输框架，把节点到提示的适配表述为全局耦合的分配问题：传输代价偏好局部几何兼容性，而规定的提示侧边际显式控制全图提示利用率。进一步推导精确方差分解，将提示侧几何方差分离为保留的提示更新变异与节点内重心离散度，并给出冻结编码器前向映射的条件稳定性界。

Result: 在标准引文网络及额外异质图上，MINT在少样本适配中保持竞争力。受控实验与端到端实验区分了路由与拓扑的作用：固定边际路由控制全图提示利用率，并在引文网络上产生可测量的端到端效果；拓扑增强则提供互补的、依赖图结构的机制来应对结构失配。

Conclusion: 将节点—提示适配提升为全局耦合的熵正则最优传输问题，可通过提示侧边际直接调控全图提示利用率，并借方差分解与稳定性分析获得可解释性；路由控制与拓扑增强在图提示学习中承担不同且互补的角色。代码已开源。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning enables parameter-efficient adaptation of frozen Graph Neural Networks to downstream tasks through lightweight prompt parameters. As routing becomes increasingly node-adaptive, however, independently optimized local decisions can collectively concentrate assignment mass on a small subset of a finite shared prompt bank, even when individual node--prompt matches remain locally meaningful. We propose MINT (Measure-INtegrity Transport), an entropically regularized optimal transport framework that formulates node-to-prompt adaptation as a globally coupled allocation problem. The transport cost favors local geometric compatibility, while a prescribed prompt-side marginal explicitly controls graph-wide prompt utilization. We further derive an exact variance decomposition that separates prompt-side geometric variance into retained prompt-update variation and within-node barycentric dispersion, together with a conditional stability bound for the frozen-encoder forward map. Across standard citation networks and additional heterophilic graphs, MINT remains competitive in few-shot adaptation. Controlled and end-to-end experiments further distinguish the roles of routing and topology: fixed-marginal routing controls graph-wide prompt utilization and has measurable end-to-end effects on citation networks, while topology augmentation provides a complementary, graph-dependent mechanism for addressing structural mismatch. Code is available at https://github.com/Ga1axy0051/MINT.

</details>


### [164] [Physics-residual machine learning predicts oxygen-evolution catalyst activity beyond the training range from sparse polarization measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23549)
*Yong-Woon Kim,Jihyeok Lee,Sungtae Park,Sooseok Choi,Yung-Cheol Byun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理残差机器学习方法预测超出测量窗口的析氧催化活性，用Tafel外推加距离衰减残差校正，误差显著低于纯数据驱动模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合库筛选析氧催化剂时，需要决定哪些候选材料进行后续测量，但决定性的活性位于测量电位窗口之外、且常高于拟合中记录的所有活性，现有方法难以外推预测。

Method: 物理残差机器学习：Tafel方程外推候选自身的实测电流与斜率；学习到的残差按特征空间距离衰减以校正幅度；适用域评分在测量前识别超出训练范围的预测。

Result: 在独立制备的322候选库中（282个高于训练最大值），每候选两次测量下平均绝对误差0.203 mA cm⁻²，而选定的数据驱动机器学习模型为1.330；训练范围内的误差相当，35个标注催化剂即可拟合。在两个独立数据集中，该构建将过电位误差降低29%至52%。

Conclusion: 该策略可在缩短单次测量的同时仍对最活跃组分进行排序，加速催化剂筛选campaign。

Abstract: Screening oxygen-evolution catalysts on combinatorial libraries requires deciding which candidates receive the remaining measurements. The deciding activity lies beyond each candidate's measured potential window and often above every activity recorded during fitting. We predict it by physics-residual machine learning: the Tafel equation extrapolates the candidate's own measured current and slope, a learned residual attenuated with feature-space distance corrects the magnitude, and an applicability-domain score identifies predictions above the training range before measurement. In a separately fabricated 322-candidate library, 282 above the training maximum, two measurements per candidate gave a mean absolute error of 0.203 mA cm$^{-2}$ against 1.330 for the selected data-driven machine-learning model. Errors inside the training range remained comparable, and 35 labelled catalysts were enough to fit it. In two independent datasets the same construction lowered the overpotential error by 29 to 52%. Campaigns can therefore shorten each measurement and still rank the most active compositions.

</details>


### [165] [Global Ranks Survive, Selected Heads Shift: BOS-Sink Topology under 4-bit Weight-Only Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23585)
*Kuanlin Chen,Chen-Wei Kuo,Cheng-En Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过 STC 指标检验"先识别 sink 头、再复用于 4-bit 量化"这一捷径的安全性，发现全局秩保持良好但离散头集合、层局部策略与跨域校准不可直接迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sink-aware 部署通常假设量化前识别的重要首 token 注意力头映射在量化后仍可复用，但该假设缺乏系统验证；作者希望明确这种复用在何种条件下安全。

Method: 提出 Sink Topology Consistency (STC) 指标，分离全局秩保持、top-k 集合重叠、逐层 sink 质量漂移，并区分逐输入敏感性与校准映射迁移；在 Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B 与 Llama-3.2-1B 上开展 4-bit NF4 权重-only PTQ 实验，涉及 4,096 token 序列、C4 到 LongBench 的跨域迁移及匹配域重校准。

Result: 全局 bf16 到 4-bit 秩保持很高（$ρ_s \geq 0.980$），但 top-k Jaccard 仅 0.619-0.793（成员保留率 76.5-88.5%）；全局统计掩盖局部失败，Qwen 末端层漂移达模型均值的 6.2-7.9 倍，而 Llama 漂移低且近乎均匀；跨域重叠退化在 Qwen 上比同域更严重，Llama 则不明显；匹配域重校准在 n=8（Qwen）与 n=32（Llama）达到稳定性平台 90%，非尖锐阈值；Qwen 仅更新部分层无法满足全图稳定性；Jetson Orin NX 上 16 样本负载仅需数秒。

Conclusion: 全局排序通常可迁移，但离散头集合、逐层局部策略与跨域校准必须在量化后重新验证。

Abstract: Sink-aware deployment may identify important first-token attention heads before a model is quantized, then reuse that map at the edge. We test when this shortcut is safe for 4-bit NF4 weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ). Our Sink Topology Consistency (STC) metrics separate global rank preservation, top-$k$ set overlap, and layerwise sink-mass shift, and distinguish per-input sensitivity from calibration-map transfer. Across Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Llama-3.2-1B, global bf16-to-4-bit ranks remain high at 4,096 tokens ($ρ_s \geq 0.980$), yet top-$k$ Jaccard overlap is only 0.619-0.793, corresponding to 76.5-88.5% membership retention. The global statistic also masks local failures: terminal Qwen layers shift by 6.2-7.9x their model means, whereas Llama-3.2-1B shows low, nearly uniform drift. Under a C4-to-LongBench shift, cross-domain overlap degrades more than the within-domain precision comparison for both Qwen models, but not for Llama-3.2-1B. Matched-domain 4-bit recalibration reaches 90% of a split-half stability plateau at the smallest tested $n=8$ for both Qwen models and $n=32$ for Llama-3.2-1B, though not as a sharp threshold; for the two Qwen models, updating only selected layers does not reach the full-map stability criterion. On Jetson Orin NX, the 16-sample workload takes seconds for the two models with valid on-device sink measurements. The practical message is precise: global rankings often transfer, but discrete head sets, layer-local policies, and cross-domain calibration should be revalidated after quantization.

</details>


### [166] [Cost-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Action Masking and Projection for Battery Energy Storage Dispatch under Suppressed-Spread Market Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23590)
*Kuanlin Chen,Chen-Wei Kuo,Cheng-En Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究PPO电池储能调度控制器，将物理动作掩码与紧急投影同经济建议解耦，通过五种子、M0-M6消融及配对诊断进行严格评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 价差收窄压缩储能循环收益空间，需在保证运行可行性的同时优化调度经济性；现有方法对"经济筛选"与"可行性执行"的职责划分不清，缺乏对预测依赖与消融的系统验证。

Method: 构建PPO控制器：预选物理动作掩码与紧急投影负责可行性，因果的预测驱动经济顾问负责收益筛选；所有依赖预测的方法共享同一24步因果预测与电网侧结算。开展五种子的T1/T2窗口对比、代理成本MPC基准、日度/周度/混合/持续性季节预测的不相交周块测试，以及M0-M6消融和配对诊断。

Result: 建议开启时净利为30.59（T1）与18.04（T2）USD/336小时，低于代理成本MPC的36.77与22.94 USD；建议使T2利润由16.45升至18.04 USD并降低吞吐量，但对T1无实质影响。PPO在日度/周度/混合预测下稳定，在持续性预测下减弱，两期均低于MPC。诊断结果将变化定位于5-10 USD/MWh价差区间，SoC依赖效应混杂。消融显示移除掩码会使数千次不可行请求进入投影，同时移除两层则暴露爬坡违规。

Conclusion: 该工作将经济筛选与可行性执行明确分离，但未主张形式化安全性、生命周期最优老化或RL的优越性。

Abstract: Battery energy storage system (BESS) dispatch must preserve operational feasibility while declining price spreads reduce the margin available to pay for cycling. We study a proximal policy optimization (PPO) controller whose pre-selection physical action mask and emergency projection are separated from a causal, forecast-informed economic advisory. All forecast-dependent methods receive the same causal 24-step forecast and grid-side settlement. Across five PPO seeds, advice-on net profit is 30.59 and 18.04 USD per 336-hour T1 and T2 window, versus 36.77 and 22.94 USD for proxy-cost MPC; PPO remains below this reference in both periods. Advice raises T2 profit from 16.45 to 18.04 USD while reducing throughput, but is immaterial in T1. On disjoint weekly blocks, PPO is stable under daily, weekly, and blended seasonal forecasts, weakens under persistence, and remains below proxy-cost MPC. Paired diagnostics localize changes to the observed 5-10 USD/MWh regime with mixed SoC-dependent effects. An M0-M6 ablation shows that mask removal sends thousands of infeasible requests to projection, while removing both physical layers exposes ramp violations. The evidence separates economic screening from feasibility enforcement without claiming formal safety, lifecycle-optimal aging, or RL dominance.

</details>


### [167] [Bilinear Optimization Divergence: Diagnosing Factor-Constrained LoRA Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23594)
*YongShun Wang,JianLin Su,Yong Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出BOD诊断，揭示LoRA正交性本身不足以保护历史特征，需由任务起始状态、参数化与优化器位移共同决定；据此给出SFOR与WRP，共享适配器历史响应从19.12%降至0.005%，累积族从7.72%降至0.002%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA因子间正交并不直接说明组合更新保护了什么，其效果依赖任务起始状态、参数化方式和优化器实际位移。论文旨在从锚点相对视角刻画这一"保护"的本质，明确不同架构下应施加何种约束。

Method: 提出双线性优化散度（BOD）作为锚点相对的有效更新响应诊断；有限步分析区分共享适配器与全新零输出块两种情形，导出半冻结正交路由（SFOR）与累积O-LoRA当前块硬保护，并用权重残差投影（WRP）在优化器步后强制执行所需位移。

Result: 两任务受控轨迹验证了预测的残差路径，共享族归一化历史响应由19.12%降至0.005%，累积族由7.72%降至0.002%；Qwen3-8B四任务实验中，SFOR将BWT从-2.47提升至-0.86且AA基本不变，O-LoRA硬保护将三阶平均AA由80.27%升至81.30%、FM由2.20降至0.43；组件控制显示更严格的可行性未必提升最终任务表现。

Conclusion: 正交性只有结合架构条件才具保护意义；论文给出"该约束什么、如何约束、如何解读其经验价值"的架构条件化解释，并说明约束强度与最终性能并不单调对应。

Abstract: Orthogonality in a LoRA factor does not by itself specify what the composed update protects: the answer depends on the task-start state, the parameterization, and the realized optimizer displacement. We formalize this question through Bilinear Optimization Divergence (BOD), an anchor-relative diagnostic of effective-update response on selected historical features. The finite-step analysis distinguishes two cases. In a shared adapter, protecting the routing displacement leaves a learned-anchor residual through the changing companion factor. In a fresh zero-output block, a feasible routing state can protect the composed update while both current factors remain trainable. These conditions yield Semi-Frozen Orthogonal Routing (SFOR) for shared adapters and current-block hard protection for cumulative O-LoRA; Weight Residual Projection (WRP) enforces the required displacement after the optimizer step. Controlled two-task traces verify the predicted residual paths, reducing normalized historical response from 19.12% to 0.005% in the shared family and from 7.72% to 0.002% in the cumulative family. Four-task experiments on Qwen3-8B characterize the resulting trade-offs: SFOR improves backward transfer (BWT) from -2.47 to -0.86 with nearly unchanged average accuracy (AA), while O-LoRA hard protection improves three-order mean AA from 80.27% to 81.30% and forgetting measure (FM) from 2.20 to 0.43. Component controls also show that stricter feasibility need not improve final task performance. Together, the analysis and evidence provide an architecture-conditioned account of which constraint to enforce, how to enforce it, and how to interpret its empirical value.

</details>


### [168] [ETH-TraceBench: A Large-Scale Event-Stream Benchmark for Ethereum DeFi under Temporal, Protocol, and Contract Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23659)
*Kemal Kirtac,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ETH-TraceBench，一个用于评估以太坊 DeFi 表征在时间、协议、池/基础设施与符号偏移下表现的基准，强调困难迁移条件而非单一聚合分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以太坊 DeFi 提供公开、带时间戳的交易级事件流，但相同的公开符号会给机器学习带来强捷径，导致高聚合分数可能掩盖模型缺乏真正泛化能力的问题。

Method: 构建覆盖 2021 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月、含 13.5 亿笔交易日志与 50.1 亿条原始日志行的事件宇宙；在固定 911,267 条监督样本上评估，2021–2024 训练、2025H1 选型、2025H2 测试；设置时间、协议、池/基础设施、符号四类偏移条件，并进行 emitter/topic 联合掩码与自然流行度审计。

Result: 聚合时间测试中 TraceStats-GB 达 0.953 macro-F1，TopicEmitterHashMLP 达 0.959；协议新颖性下显著下降至 0.794、0.743、0.766；严格未见池分别为 0.927、0.897、0.935；Uniswap v4 与 Ekubo v1 明显更难；联合掩码 emitter 与 topic 使 DEX 降至 0.916、清算降至 0.774；标准 Transformer 相对确定性打乱无一致优势。2025H2 DEX 流行度约 22.5%，400 笔审计与标签源一致。

Conclusion: ETH-TraceBench 主张以困难迁移与受控输入条件作为核心评估目标，而非依赖单一聚合分数；高聚合表现可能不依赖复杂时序建模。

Abstract: Ethereum decentralized finance (DeFi) provides a public, time-stamped record of transaction-level event streams, but the same public symbols can create strong machine-learning shortcuts. We introduce ETH-TraceBench, a benchmark for evaluating Ethereum DeFi representations under temporal, protocol, pool/infrastructure, and symbolic shift. The raw event universe covers January 2021-December 2025 and contains 1.35 billion transactions with logs and 5.01 billion raw log rows. Model evaluation uses a fixed 911,267-instance supervised sample, training on 2021-2024, selecting models on 2025H1, and testing on 2025H2. Simple models perform strongly on the aggregate temporal test: TraceStats-GB reaches 0.953 macro-F1 and TopicEmitterHashMLP 0.959 on the canonical DEX test set. Performance drops sharply under protocol novelty, with macro-F1 of 0.794, 0.743, and 0.766 for TraceStats-GB, TopicEmitterTrace-SGD, and TopicEmitterHashMLP, while strict unseen-pool scores remain 0.927, 0.897, and 0.935. Uniswap v4 and Ekubo v1, both absent from supervised training, are materially harder than the full test. Jointly masking emitter and topic identity reduces DEX macro-F1 to 0.916 and liquidation macro-F1 to 0.774 for TopicEmitterTrace-SGD. A standard Transformer over log-index-ordered events provides no consistent advantage over a deterministic shuffle of the same events, indicating that high aggregate scores can arise without sophisticated chronological modeling. A natural-prevalence audit estimates 2025H2 DEX prevalence among logged Ethereum transactions at about 22.5%, and a deterministic 400-transaction audit finds complete agreement with task label sources and independently re-queried raw-log counts. ETH-TraceBench therefore treats difficult transfer and controlled-input conditions, rather than a single aggregate score, as the main evaluation target.

</details>


### [169] [One Patch, Three Roles: What Is Actually Coupled in Autoregressive Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23686)
*Ziang Li,Yue Huang,Guoxu Zhou,Na Han,Jie Wen,Lunke Fei,Xiaozhao Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究分块自回归时间序列预测中表示、转移与执行三个角色能否解耦，提出ATD实现可选执行步数，并提出Spectrum Tangent校正残差投影，在不增加参数与调用的情况下提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块自回归预测通常将输入表示、学习到的状态转移与递归执行绑定在单一分块长度上，限制了执行效率与精度的独立优化。作者希望厘清哪些角色可以被单独调整。

Method: 通过原子编码研究考察模型宽度与原子分组的敏感性；提出自回归轨迹蒸馏（ATD），以轻量并行出口拟合冻结父模型的递归轨迹，实现ATD-1/2/4/8可选执行，其中ATD-1精确还原父模型；进一步发现沿训练选定的周期历史方向存在可校正的残差投影，提出Spectrum Tangent，在不增加神经参数和Transformer调用的情况下施加该校正。

Result: 配对四数据集比较中ATD-8达到5.54倍端到端加速且各宽度下质量稳定；ATD在全部21次种子运行中提升轨迹保真度，但在与干净未来监督匹配时仅15次提升预测精度；在horizon 720、七个数据集与两个输出宽度上，Spectrum Tangent使MSE和MAE分别降低2.54%与2.33%，且比递归推理快3.24倍。水平与形状投影有时不一致。轨迹可压缩性、保真度-精度错配与校正现象在三个公开AR父模型上重复出现。

Conclusion: 表示、转移与执行可作为自回归设计的独立轴；ATD支持可选择的执行步数并保持质量，Spectrum Tangent以无额外参数和调用的方式改善精度，相关现象具有跨模型的可重复性。

Abstract: Patch-based autoregressive time-series forecasting often ties input representation, learned transitions, and recursive execution to one patch length. We ask which of these roles can be adjusted separately. A supporting atomic-encoding study finds greater sensitivity to model width than to atom grouping on the evaluated grid. Our main finding is that a frozen parent's recursive trajectory is easier to fit than the observed future with lightweight parallel exits. Autoregressive Trajectory Distillation (ATD) turns this into selectable ATD-1/2/4/8 execution, with ATD-1 exactly recovering the parent. On a paired four-data-set comparison, ATD-8 reaches $5.54\times$ end-to-end speedup with stable quality across widths. Fewer calls do not automatically remove the parent's existing forecast error: ATD improves trajectory fidelity in all 21 seed runs but forecast accuracy in only 15 against matched clean-future supervision. We further find a correctable residual projection along a train-selected periodic history direction. Spectrum Tangent applies this correction without adding neural parameters or Transformer calls. At horizon 720, it reduces mean squared error (MSE) and mean absolute error (MAE) by 2.54% and 2.33% over seven data sets and two output widths, while remaining $3.24\times$ faster than recursive inference. Level and shape projections sometimes disagree. Trajectory compressibility, the fidelity-accuracy mismatch, and the correction recur across three public AR parents. Together these results separate representation, transition, and execution as AR design axes. Code is available at https://github.com/RowanFFF/ATD-Spectrum-Tangent.

</details>


### [170] [A multi-temporal dataset for mapping burned areas in the Brazilian Cerrado using time series of remote sensing imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23687)
*Alisson Cleiton de Oliveira,Thales Sehn Körting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文基于CBERS-4A、CBERS-4与AMAZONIA-1卫星WFI传感器影像，构建了2020—2022年巴西Chapada dos Veadeiros国家公园的多时相表格数据集，用于火烧迹地制图，并采用随机森林分类器进行建模与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对巴西Cerrado地区火烧迹地制图需求，探索无需星间光谱定标的WFI传感器数据在火烧迹地识别中的可用性，并构建可复用的多时相表格数据集。

Method: 数据集包含蓝、绿、红、近红外波段及BAI、EVI、GEMI、NDVI、NDWI光谱指数，按规则网格组织；样本标注为完全烧毁（TB）、部分烧毁（PB）和非烧毁（NB）。采用随机森林分类器，测试两种分类策略（二分类与三分类），并通过七种验证方案评估不同后分类组合，指标包括accuracy、precision、recall与IoU。

Result: 当TB、PB、NB作为独立类别、且将TB重分类为BA、PB与NB合并为非烧毁时，IoU最高。与MCD64A1产品对比，BA类漏分误差2020年22%、2021年28%、2022年59%；错分误差分别为46%、43%、46%。

Conclusion: WFI传感器在无需星间光谱定标条件下可用于火烧迹地制图；建议进一步探索其他机器学习算法，以评估该数据集潜力与局限。

Abstract: This paper introduces a multi-temporal tabular dataset derived from satellite images to map burned areas in the Chapada dos Veadeiros National Park, in Goiás, Brazil, covering the years 2020 to 2022. The dataset contains blue, green, red, and near-infrared bands, as well as the BAI, EVI, GEMI, NDVI, and NDWI spectral indices from the WFI sensor on the CBERS-4A, CBERS-4, and AMAZONIA-1 satellites, organized into a regular grid. We applied the Random Forest classifier to develop and validate models based on samples labeled as totally burned, partially burned, and non-burned. Two classification approaches were tested: one combining burned and non-burned areas into binary classes and another distinguishing between totally burned (TB), partially burned (PB), and non-burned (NB) classes. Seven validation approaches assessed different post-classification combinations, focusing on accuracy, precision, recall, and intersection over union (IoU) metrics. Results showed higher IoU when TB, PB, and NB were used as individual classes and TB was reclassified as burned area (BA) while PB and NB were grouped as non-burned. Comparing the annual results of this approach to the MCD64A1 product, the errors of omission for the BA class were 22% in 2020, 28% in 2021 and 59% in 2022, while the errors of commission were 46%, 43% and 46%, respectively. The study highlights the utility of the WFI sensor for burned area mapping without inter-satellite spectral calibration and suggests further exploration with other machine learning algorithms to evaluate the dataset potential and limitations.

</details>


### [171] [Tail-Weight Control and Localized Generalization in Nearly Low-Rank Adversarial Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23688)
*Kunyu Wang,Dehan Wang,Wenjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在高斯模型（低维信息子空间＋独立噪声尾）下研究欧氏对抗扰动中的范数约束线性分类，证明主空间 witness 可强制近最优预测器具有小尾部权重，并给出有限样本泛化保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注对抗鲁棒分类中"信息在主方向、噪声在尾部"这一结构性设定下的理论刻画，旨在揭示主维度与尾部能量如何共同决定近最优预测器的行为与泛化性能，并避免对尾部方差正性的额外假设。

Method: 采用高斯建模（低维信息子空间＋独立噪声尾），针对有界 ramp 损失做理论分析：证明主空间 witness 在风险小于 1/2 时迫使近最优预测器尾部权重小；利用路径特定密度界获得不依赖正尾部方差的常数；在各向同性主协方差下建立唯一总体最小化器与联合局部增长；通过边界归一化从中心化 margin 中移除公共攻击惩罚，给出由主维度与总尾部能量控制的局部有限样本保证；进一步做全局化增长以去除入口条件，并给出模型感知比较以保留局部保证。

Result: 理论上刻画了近最优预测器的小尾部权重性质、唯一总体最小化器与联合局部增长，并给出局部有限样本保证；实验中 20 组配对重复显示超额风险与尾部使用随样本量增加而下降，且当尾部维度在总能量固定下增大时行为几乎不变；纯噪声对照与优化器诊断进一步界定结论适用范围与局限性。

Conclusion: 在信息集中于低维主空间、噪声集中在尾部的设定下，对抗鲁棒线性分类的近最优解可被主空间 witness 有效约束，其泛化由主维度与总尾部能量主导，尾部维度本身影响有限；结论在较弱常数与去除入口条件下仍可维持局部保证。

Abstract: We study norm-constrained linear classification under Eu clidean adversarial perturbations in a Gaussian model with a low-dimen sional informative subspace and an independent noise tail. For bounded ramp loss, we prove that a principal-space witness with risk below one half forces every near-optimal predictor to have small tail weight. A path-specific density bound yields constants without requiring positive tail variance. Under isotropic principal covariance, we establish a unique population minimizer and joint local growth. Boundary normalization then removes the common attack penalty from centered margins, giving localized finite-sample guarantees governed by principal dimension and total tail energy. Globalized growth removes the entrance condition at weaker constants; a model-aware comparison retains local guarantees. Experiments with twenty paired repetitions show decreasing excess risk and tail use with sample size, and nearly unchanged behavior when tail dimension grows at fixed total energy. Pure-noise controls and optimizer diagnostics clarify the scope and limitations of these conclusions.

</details>


### [172] [GenVoid: Uncertainty-Aware Learning of Subsurface Material Defects with an Experimentally Validated Physics-Informed Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23761)
*Trishit Mondal,Prajwal Bharadwaj,Nikhil Karanjgaokar,Ameya D. Jagtap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GenVoid——一个物理信息生成模型框架，仅凭表面位移测量即可识别二维/三维固体内部空洞，并显式建模测量噪声与不确定性，输出概率化重构结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部空洞在制造结构中普遍存在，但其几何被隐藏，只能通过可及测量间接推断，传统表征方法面临困难，且缺乏对测量噪声、不确定性和可观测性的定量刻画。

Method: 将固体力学控制方程嵌入生成式推断框架（GenVoid），支持线弹性、超弹性和塑性材料行为及复杂二/三维几何；同时引入可观测性度量，刻画边界测量对内部局部刚度扰动的敏感性。

Result: 在高保真合成数据集与原位力学实验实测位移场上验证了方法；可观测性度量建立了缺陷位置、传感器配置与重构精度之间的直接联系，量化了边界测量数量与空间分布对识别精度的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了物理信息、不确定性感知的隐藏缺陷无损表征途径，并为固体力学反问题的测量策略设计提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Internal voids are ubiquitous defects in manufactured structures, yet their characterization remains challenging because their geometry is hidden and can only be inferred indirectly from accessible measurements. Here we introduce \textit{GenVoid}, a physics-informed generative model-based framework for identifying internal voids in complex two- and three-dimensional solids from surface displacement measurements alone. By incorporating the governing mechanics into a generative inference framework, \textit{GenVoid} enables void identification across linear elastic, hyperelastic and plastic material behaviours and accommodates complex two- and three-dimensional structural geometries. Importantly, the framework explicitly accounts for uncertainty and noise in displacement measurements, producing probabilistic reconstructions of internal void geometry rather than a single deterministic estimate. We demonstrate the approach using high-fidelity synthetic datasets and experimentally measured displacement fields obtained from in-situ mechanical experiments, establishing its ability to infer hidden voids from realistic displacement measurements. To quantify the fundamental limits of such inference, we further introduce an observability measure that characterizes the sensitivity of boundary measurements to localized stiffness perturbations within the interior under an ensemble of applied loads. This framework provides a direct connection between defect location, sensor configuration and reconstruction fidelity, enabling systematic assessment of how the number and spatial distribution of boundary measurements govern void-identification accuracy. To this end, these results establish a physics-informed and uncertainty-aware approach for non-invasive characterization of hidden defects and provide a quantitative basis for designing measurement strategies for inverse problems in solid mechanics.

</details>


### [173] [Statistical Convergence of Transformer Encoder-Accelerated Robust Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23775)
*Suman Banerjee,Hiroyasu Tsukamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为鲁棒强化学习算法提供了统计严格的收敛性结果，该算法由基于Transformer的动作价值函数预测进行热启动，并利用保形预测构建轨迹级非一致性得分，导出一个预认证的停止准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大状态-动作空间中求解马尔可夫决策过程的最优动作价值函数计算代价极高，因此需要既能借助先验知识加速收敛、又能对不确定性提供严格统计保证的算法。

Method: 采用R-污染模型刻画状态转移核的不确定性；用自然语言提示编码任务规范，由Transformer预测动作价值函数实现热启动；利用保形预测，以收缩贝尔曼残差构造轨迹级非一致性得分，得到同时覆盖所有迭代的保形分位数界及预认证停止准则。

Result: 在规模与污染程度各异的扰动迷宫环境上的数值实验表明，Transformer热启动可显著降低初始误差并加速收敛；所提保形界比现有保证更紧地跟踪真实误差轨迹。

Conclusion: 该方法将Transformer先验与保形预测结合，在几乎不需要真实转移核知识的前提下，为鲁棒价值函数估计提供了同时覆盖所有迭代的收敛认证与可靠停止准则。

Abstract: Obtaining the optimal action-value function in Markov decision processes is computationally intensive in large state--action spaces. In this study, we present statistically rigorous convergence results for a robust reinforcement learning algorithm warm-started by a transformer-based action-value function prediction, where natural language prompts encode task specifications. Our framework adopts the R-contamination model to characterize uncertainty in the state transition kernel, and employs conformal prediction to certify convergence via trajectory-level nonconformity scores constructed from the contracting Bellman residual. The resulting conformal quantile bounds the gap between the running and optimal action-value functions simultaneously over all iterations, thereby yielding a pre-certified stopping rule that requires little knowledge of the true transition kernel. Numerical case studies on perturbed maze environments of varying size and contamination level confirm that the transformer-based warm start measurably reduces the initial error and accelerates convergence, while the proposed conformal bounds track the true error trajectory more tightly than existing guarantees.

</details>


### [174] [Falling Trees: A Model Class for Interpretable Risk Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23780)
*Varun Babbar,Zachery Boner,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 falling trees——在保持单调风险约束的前提下引入树形分支的可解释模型，并给出 GRAVITree 动态规划算法学习其 Rashomon 集，在临床与公开基准上匹配或超越 FRL 等基线，且对高风险样本给出更稀疏的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常需优先处理高风险案例（如临床中先处理高危患者）。FRL（falling rule lists）以规则列表+风险单调递减的形式提供了可解释方案，但其单路径结构使模型类别过于受限，表达能力不足，难以在保持可解释性的同时获得足够灵活性。

Method: 提出 falling trees：在同样强制风险单调约束的前提下允许树状分支，从而在规则列表与完整决策树之间插值，可按用户需求调节模型表达力。提出 GRAVITree：一种带界限的动态规划算法，在深度与分支约束下学习 falling trees 的 Rashomon 集。

Result: 在一个新的临床数据集以及多个公开分类基准上，falling trees 匹配或优于 FRL 及其他可解释基线，并常对高风险实例产生更稀疏的决策。

Conclusion: falling trees 在高风险场景下于可解释性、表达能力与风险优先级之间取得了实用平衡，且 GRAVITree 能有效枚举受约束的 Rashomon 集。

Abstract: Many real-world decisions require prioritizing high-risk cases, such as clinicians prioritizing high-risk patients before lower-risk ones. Falling rule lists (FRLs), which are ordered if--then rules with monotonically decreasing risks, provide an interpretable framework for such tasks; however, their single-path structure yields a highly restricted model class. We introduce falling trees, a new family of interpretable models that enforces the same monotonic risk constraint while permitting tree-structured branching. We present GRAVITree, a novel dynamic-programming-with-bounds algorithm for learning the Rashomon set of falling trees under depth and branching constraints. Our formulation can interpolate between rule lists and full decision trees, enabling user-desired model expressivity. In a new clinical dataset and in many public classification benchmarks, falling trees match or outperform FRLs and other interpretable baselines, often producing more sparse decisions for high-risk instances. Our results show that falling trees strike a practical balance between interpretability, expressiveness, and risk prioritization for high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [175] [Belted Engression: Sufficient Dimension Reduction for Generative Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23789)
*Wenxi Tan,Bing Li,Lingzhou Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Belted Engression，一个统一且参数高效的生成式分布回归框架，将充分降维（SDR）嵌入生成架构，形成"先压缩后生成"的端到端范式，并给出理论与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代条件生成模型在学习复杂协变量依赖时面临困难；传统 SDR 虽能压缩这类依赖，但并未针对条件生成任务进行构建，二者之间存在理论和方法上的空白。

Method: 提出 Belted Engression：以充分表示学习为驱动，在生成架构中嵌入结构性瓶颈，构成端到端 compress-then-generate 范式，并在总体 Belted Engression 目标下求解。

Result: 理论上证明标准 SDR 条件等价于保律生成因子分解，且在该目标全局最优处实现；通过揭示 energy-score 损失的局部 Bernstein 型控制，得到比现有结果更快的有限样本收敛速率；并证明该瓶颈结构严格更小，参数数量相对无结构基线渐近可忽略。模拟与真实应用显示其在分布预测和 SDR 恢复上更优且可训练参数更少。

Conclusion: Belted Engression 统一了 SDR 与条件生成，可在更少参数下实现更优的分布预测与 SDR 恢复，兼具理论保证与实证优势。

Abstract: Modern conditional generative models face significant challenges when learning complex covariate dependencies. While sufficient dimension reduction (SDR) provides a principled approach to compress these dependencies, traditional SDR frameworks were not formulated for conditional generation. To bridge this gap, we propose Belted Engression, a unified and architecturally parameter-efficient framework for generative distributional regression. Our approach establishes an end-to-end compress-then-generate paradigm driven by sufficient representation learning, embedding a structural bottleneck into the generative architecture. Theoretically, we prove that the standard SDR condition is equivalent to a law-preserving generative factorization, which is achieved at the global optimum of the population Belted Engression objective. Furthermore, by uncovering a localized Bernstein-type control for the energy-score loss, we establish finite-sample convergence rates that are sharper than those of existing results. We also prove that this belted architecture is strictly smaller, operating with an asymptotically vanishing parameter count relative to the unstructured baseline. Extensive simulations and real-world applications demonstrate that Belted Engression achieves superior distributional prediction and SDR recovery with fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [176] [Iterative Atom Refinement: A Monotonicity Principle for Dictionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23812)
*Alexander Christie,Miguel Moscoso,Alexei Novikov,George Papanicolaou,Chrysoula Tsogka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代原子精炼（IAR）算法，从稀疏观测中逐个恢复字典原子，并给出严格的收敛理论，证明在模型假设下仅需三步即可识别生成原子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典学习旨在从观测 y_i = A x_i（x_i 稀疏）中恢复未知字典 A。已有方法多关注整体字典恢复，缺少对单个原子恢复过程的理论保证。本文希望为逐个精炼原子的简单迭代过程建立严格的收敛性分析。

Method: 提出 Iterative Atom Refinement（IAR）算法：从随机方向出发，反复选取与当前迭代方向相关性最强的观测，并对这些被选数据进行平均以更新方向。理论分析运用高维概率估计，并提出关于原子选择概率的新单调性原理。

Result: 证明某一原子的初始微小优势会被不断放大，直至该原子被单独分离出来；在模型假设下，IAR 仅需三步即可识别出生成原子。数值实验支持理论结果，表明所刻画的动力学能准确反映字典精炼中的实际行为。

Conclusion: IAR 是一种简单且具有理论保证的字典原子恢复方法，其快速收敛特性与数值实验一致，为字典精炼过程提供了严格的理论解释。

Abstract: Dictionary learning seeks to recover an unknown dictionary $A$ from observations ${\bf y}_i = A{\bf x}_i$ with sparse coefficient vectors ${\bf x}_i$. We introduce the \emph{Iterative Atom Refinement} (IAR) algorithm, a simple procedure for recovering individual dictionary atoms. Starting from a random direction, IAR repeatedly selects the observations most strongly correlated with the current iterate and updates the direction by averaging the selected data. Our main contribution is a rigorous convergence theory of IAR. Using high-dimensional probabilistic estimates and a novel monotonicity principle for atom-selection probabilities, we show that a small initial advantage of one atom is amplified until that atom is isolated. Under our model assumptions, IAR identifies a generating atom after only three refinement steps. Numerical experiments support the theory and show that the resulting dynamics accurately capture the behavior observed in dictionary refinement.

</details>


### [177] [Real-time Generalizable Heart Valve Mechanics for Clinical Disease Assessment via a Physics-Conditioned Neural Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23826)
*Shawn Koohy,Wensi Wu,Matthew A Jolley,Paris Perdikaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理条件神经算子PCNO，用Transformer代理有限元仿真，快速预测二尖瓣/三尖瓣力学场，提速最高15260倍且精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二尖瓣反流是全球最常见的心脏瓣膜疾病，影响超2%人口、75岁以上成人至少10%，造成约15%的瓣膜性心脏病相关死亡，但仅少数重症患者接受矫正手术。快速评估瓣膜力学有望实现更早、更精准的干预，而传统有限元仿真速度过慢，无法满足临床时间要求和参数扫描需求。

Method: 提出物理条件神经算子（PCNO），一种基于Transformer的代理模型，以收缩期血压和组织属性为条件，预测二尖瓣与三尖瓣几何上的瓣叶位移、应变和应力场。训练数据涵盖功能正常、反流及病理瓣膜，包括tethering（牵拉）、P2脱垂和瓣环扩张。

Result: 相比精细网格有限元仿真，PCNO实现最高15,260倍加速且精度相当；能够识别病理类别；在分布外外推条件下，诊断指标误差控制在3.5%以内。

Conclusion: PCNO作为快速、准确的瓣膜力学代理模型，兼具高加速比与诊断级精度，有望支持更早、更精准的临床干预与参数化评估。

Abstract: Mitral regurgitation is the most common heart valve disorder worldwide, affecting over 2% of the global population, rising to at least 10% in adults over 75, and causing approximately 15% of valvular heart disease-related deaths. Yet only a minority of patients with severe disease undergo corrective surgery. Rapid assessment of valve mechanics could enable earlier, more precise intervention, but traditional finite element simulations remain too slow for clinical timelines and parameter sweeps. We introduce the Physics-Conditioned Neural Operator (PCNO), a transformer-based surrogate that predicts leaflet displacement, strain, and stress fields across mitral and tricuspid geometries, conditioned on systolic blood pressure and tissue properties. Trained on functional, regurgitated, and pathological valves, including tethering, P2 prolapse, and annular dilation, PCNO achieves up to a 15,260x speedup over fine mesh finite element simulations with comparable accuracy, identifies pathology class, and resolves diagnostic metrics within 3.5% error under out-of-distribution extrapolation.

</details>


### [178] [Actionable Insights from Observational Data: The Case of Advanced Classes in K-12 Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23836)
*Nabit Bajwa,Seth B. Hunter,Sanmay Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用美国某公立学区引入可选开放入学制初中高阶课程这一政策变化前后的丰富观测数据，考察修读高阶/挑战性课程对K-12学生学业成绩的影响，发现高阶英语课程有净正向但幅度温和的效果，且收益分布不均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12教育中一个根本性难题是修读更高阶或更具挑战性课程的效果。该问题尤为复杂，因为学生（或其家长）自行选择是否选修这些课程，导致因果分析困难。核心识别挑战在于：影响高阶课程选修的因素同时也能预测学业结果，因此选修与未选修学生的简单比较存在混杂，朴素估计可能反映的是学生能力、动机或支持等潜在差异，而非课程本身的影响。

Method: 利用美国某公立学区的新颖数据集，该数据集记录了学生的选课决策、既往学业历史、人口学特征及后续结果，时间跨度覆盖学区范围内引入可选开放入学制初中高阶课程（按学科领域）的政策变化。这是一个丰富的观测数据集，但高阶课程选修由学生特征与选择驱动而非随机分配，因此作者针对这一核心识别挑战展开因果分析。

Result: 修读高阶英语课程对学生学业成绩结果具有净正向但幅度温和的影响。然而这些收益分布不均：一些预测收益相对较大的学生（即往年表现属于"中等成就者"）反而更不可能选修；其他群体（如黑人学生和低社会经济地位学生）也表现出显著更低的选修倾向。

Conclusion: 预测收益与实际选修之间的差距表明，细致的数据分析能够从大型观测数据集中提取可操作的洞见，包括识别出看似具备良好受益条件却未选择高阶课程的学生。

Abstract: A fundamentally challenging question in K-12 education is about the effects of taking more advanced or challenging classes. It is particularly complex because students (and/or their parents) choose whether to enroll in these classes, making causal analysis challenging. In this paper, we begin to tackle this question by taking advantage of a novel dataset from a public school system in the US. This dataset records students' course enrollment decisions, prior academic histories, demographics, and subsequent outcomes around the time of a district-wide change that introduced optional open-enrollment advanced middle-school courses in subject areas. This is a rich observational dataset, but enrollment in advanced classes is driven by student characteristics and choices rather than random assignment. This creates a core identification challenge: the same factors that influence enrollment in advanced courses are also predictive of academic outcomes. As a result, simple comparisons between enrolled and non-enrolled students are confounded, and naive estimates may reflect underlying differences in student ability, motivation, or support rather than the impact of coursework itself. Our analysis shows that enrolling in advanced English courses has a net positive but modest effect on student achievement outcomes. However, these benefits are unevenly distributed: some students with relatively large predicted gains ("middle achievers" in prior years) are less likely to enroll than others. Some other groups (e.g. Black students and those with lower socio-economic status) also demonstrate significantly lower propensity to enroll. This gap between predicted benefit and observed enrollment illustrates how careful data analysis can extract actionable insights from large observational datasets, including identifying students who appear well-positioned to benefit but do not select into advanced options.

</details>


### [179] [From Regional to Global: Transfer Learning for Atmospheric Transport Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23838)
*Jeff Clark,Elena Fillola,Nawid Keshtmand,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Matthew Rigby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估大气传输模拟器（footprint代理模型）在全球不同区域间的空间可迁移性，通过区域专属模型、多区域模型及留一区域实验，在四个世界区域检验跨区域泛化与迁移学习能力，以支撑高效的全球温室气体排放估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反演法依赖物理驱动的 Lagrangian Particle Dispersion Models（LPDMs），计算成本高、难以扩展至现代高分辨率卫星数据。此前已开发出在南美区域比英国气象局 LPDM 快约1000倍的大气传输模拟器，但大气传输具有区域异质性，直接向全球扩展并不容易，因此需要评估模型跨区域迁移能力。

Method: 在四个世界区域（南美、东亚、南亚、北非）上评估空间可迁移性，采用区域专属模型与多区域模型，并设计 leave-one-region-out 实验；同时结合输入变量分布与输出 footprint 分布来刻画区域差异。

Result: 论文评估了模型跨四个区域的空间迁移表现，通过区域专属、多区域以及留一区域实验揭示不同区域间泛化能力差异，并从输入变量和输出 footprint 分布角度对区域差异进行了刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作为跨区域泛化与迁移学习提供了直觉认识，有助于提升各区域表现，从而推动高效全球排放估计的实现。

Abstract: Greenhouse gas emissions estimates can be derived using inverse methods by combining atmospheric concentration observations with chemical transport models. The latter traditionally use physics-driven simulators such as Lagrangian Particle Dispersion Models (LPDMs), which are expensive to run and do not scale well to modern satellites' high resolution data. Previously we developed a performant atmospheric transport emulator that approximates LPDM outputs ("footprints") over South America ~1,000X faster than the UK Met Office's LPDM. Expanding towards global emulation is not straightforward, as atmospheric transport is regionally heterogeneous. This paper evaluates spatial transferability capabilities of models across four world regions: South America, East Asia, South Asia, North Africa using both region-specific and multi-region models, and leave-one-region-out experiments. Regional differences are characterised in the context of input variable and output footprint distributions. This work builds intuition in cross-region generalisation and transfer learning, aiding regional performance towards efficient global emissions estimates.

</details>


### [180] [Adaptive Determinantal Client Scheduling in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23843)
*Wen Xu,Ben Liang,Gary Boudreau,Hamza Sokun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用行列式点过程（DPP）建模并增强联邦学习客户端调度中的多样性，设计了基于梯度信息与质量分数的DPP核矩阵，并提出自适应行列式客户端调度（ADCS）算法，实现质量-多样性灵活权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据与系统异构性使客户端调度至关重要。已有工作多聚焦于被调度客户端的质量（加快收敛、缩短墙钟收敛时间或提升平均模型性能），却很少考虑客户端多样性，而多样性对抵抗异构性、改善最差客户端性能非常重要。

Method: 使用行列式点过程（DPP）建模并增强调度多样性；利用梯度信息和质量分数设计DPP核矩阵，从而天然实现灵活的质量-多样性权衡；通过对DPP进行快速MAP推断，提出自适应行列式客户端调度（ADCS）；进一步量化ADCS的梯度近似误差，并针对非凸损失函数下的一般有偏客户端选择建立收敛性分析。

Result: 对比数值实验表明，ADCS优于当前最先进的客户端调度算法，包括基于质量的算法和基于多样性的算法。

Conclusion: ADCS通过DPP有效建模并增强客户端多样性，兼具灵活的质量-多样性权衡，并在理论上提供了梯度近似误差量化与非凸损失下的收敛分析，实验上超越了现有先进调度方法。

Abstract: Scheduling clients for model training is critical in federated learning due to both data and system heterogeneity. Most previous works focus on the quality of the scheduled clients to achieve faster convergence, shorter wall-clock convergence time, or better average model performance. They rarely consider the diversity of clients, which is important to counter heterogeneity and improve performance for the worst-off clients. In this work, we advocate the use of determinantal point processes (DPPs) to model and enhance the diversity in client scheduling. We first design the kernel matrices of DPPs using gradient information and quality scores, which inherently enables a flexible quality-diversity trade-off. Applying fast MAP inference over DPPs, we propose Adaptive Determinantal Client Scheduling (ADCS) in FL. We further quantify the gradient approximation error of ADCS and develop convergence analysis for general biased client selection in FL with non-convex loss functions. We conduct comparative numerical experiments showing that ADCS outperforms state-of-the-art client scheduling algorithms, including both quality-based and diversity-based ones.

</details>


### [181] [PROSE: A Theory of Optimal Stopping with Perishable Evidence for Peer Selection in Intermittently Connected Decentralised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23845)
*Christos Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对去中心化联邦学习中"同伴证据易逝"的同伴选择问题，建立了有限时域马尔可夫最优停止理论，证明最优策略具有保留值（Snell包络）结构，并给出集中不等式、信息价值停止规则、等待价值闭式解与短视最优性定理，最终实例化为本地策略PROSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习取消了聚合服务器，但协作依赖同伴的瞬时可用性。在移动与间歇连接场景中，评估一个潜在同伴会消耗接触时间，甚至导致交互机会本身消失；且所学到的同伴证据具有易逝性——既因链路过期而衰减，也因同伴模型在旧测量老化期间发生漂移而失效。

Method: 将接收方在单次接触内的决策形式化为带昂贵信息获取和未来到达外部选项的有限时域马尔可夫最优停止问题，证明其最优策略可由保留值刻画（Snell包络结构），并围绕该框架证明四项结果：(i) 阶段一致、漂移感知的集中不等式与高概率正确的极大极小认证规则及有限样本识别界；(ii) 移动感知的信息价值停止规则及比较静态，表明链路风险越高则继续探测价值越低、停止域越大；(iii) 标记泊松接触到达下的等待价值闭式解及搜索理论保留值比较静态；(iv) 短视最优性定理，证明在足够波动的单调移动机制下，单步置信安全规则是有效代理且不会过早停止。方法为纯解析推演。

Result: 得到最优停止策略的保留值/Snell包络刻画；建立漂移感知集中与极大极小认证（含有限样本界）；给出信息价值停止规则及"链路风险↑→停止域↑"的比较静态；推导标记泊松到达下等待价值闭式解与搜索理论保留值；证明波动移动机制下单步置信安全规则的短视最优性。理论实例化为轻量、纯本地策略PROSE，并刻画出退化为经典序贯决策问题的静态接触与无漂移极限。

Conclusion: 本文为去中心化联邦学习中的同伴选择提供了自洽的最优停止理论，以保留值为核心统一了认证、信息价值与等待价值，并证明在特定流动机制下简单规则即最优。PROSE作为纯本地策略具备轻量可部署性，且经典序贯决策问题可在特定极限下被恢复。全文为解析性工作，未涉及实验验证。

Abstract: Decentralised federated learning removes the aggregation server but makes collaboration dependent on transient peer availability. In mobile and intermittently connected systems, evaluating a promising peer consumes contact time and may cause the exchange opportunity itself to vanish, so that the evidence a learner gathers about a peer is perishable: it decays because links expire and because peer models drift while old measurements age. This paper develops a self-contained theory of optimal stopping for the resulting peer-selection problem. We formalise a receiver's within-contact decision as a finite-horizon Markov optimal-stopping problem with costly information acquisition and a future-arrival outside option, and prove that it admits an optimal policy characterised by a reservation value (Snell-envelope structure). Around this formulation we prove: (i) stage-uniform, drift-aware concentration and a maximin certification rule that is correct with high probability together with a finite-sample identification bound; (ii) a mobility-aware value of-information stopping rule and comparative statics showing that higher link hazard lowers the value of continued probing and enlarges the stopping region; (iii) a closed-form value of waiting under marked-Poisson contact arrivals, together with a search-theoretic reservation value whose comparative statics we characterise; and (iv) a myopic-optimality theorem establishing that, in sufficiently volatile (monotone) mobility regimes, the one-step confidence-safe rule is a sound surrogate for the optimal policy and never stops prematurely. We instantiate the theory as PROSE (Perishable-evidence Reservation-value Optimal Stopping for Exchange), a lightweight, fully local policy, and delineate the static contact and drift-free limits in which classical sequential decision problems are recovered. The development is entirely analytical.

</details>


### [182] [VISTA: An Attention-Based Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Architecture for Space Situational Awareness Sensor Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23875)
*Miguel Leiva-Vélez,Adalberto Claudio Quiros,Nicolas Gaston Rozado,Hodei Urrutxua,Víctor Rodríguez-Fernández*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VISTA架构，用可变实体深度强化学习解决大规模空间态势感知传感器调度问题，实现观测与动作空间与目录规模解耦，在多场景下显著优于经典与固定维度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驻留空间物体快速增长，使空间态势感知传感器调度复杂度剧增；有限、异构、分布式传感资源需在不断扩大目录中分配，经典优化方法难以应对。现有深度强化学习在简化场景有效，但固定维度的状态与动作表示限制其扩展至大规模动态目录与分布式传感网络。

Method: VISTA（Variable-Entity Intelligent Sensor Tasking Architecture），一种可扩展的深度强化学习架构，面向变动物体规模与传感器配置下的持续不确定性驱动目录维护。其结合物理与任务信息引导的top-K检索、以实体为中心的注意力机制、循环记忆以及基于指针的动作解码，使每个智能体的观测空间与动作空间独立于目录规模。

Result: 在固定规模单传感器基准到大规模天基调度与异构协同感知等多场景评估：30个目标时，目录恢复比固定维度循环基线快31.2%；大规模下五小时不确定性较最强经典参考降低97.5%，较循环学习器降低99.3%；零样本测试至20000个物体，显示传感容量、目录规模与恢复时域近似线性关系；学习策略展现出传感器模态自适应及对目标数量与初始不确定性变化的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VISTA为跨大型、分布式、异构地基与天基传感器网络的自适应空间态势感知传感器调度提供了可扩展框架。

Abstract: The rapid growth of resident space objects is increasing the complexity of space situational awareness sensor tasking, challenging classical optimization methods as they allocate finite, heterogeneous, and distributed sensing resources across ever-larger catalogues. Existing deep reinforcement learning approaches show promise in reduced settings, but fixed-dimensional state and action representations limit their ability to scale to large, dynamic catalogues and distributed sensing networks. We introduce VISTA (Variable-Entity Intelligent Sensor Tasking Architecture), a scalable deep reinforcement learning architecture for persistent uncertainty-driven catalogue maintenance across variable object populations and sensor configurations. VISTA combines physics- and mission-informed top-K retrieval with entity-centric attention, recurrent memory, and pointer-based action decoding, thereby keeping each agent's observation and action spaces independent of catalogue size. We evaluate VISTA across different scenarios, from fixed-size single-sensor benchmarks to large-scale space-based tasking and heterogeneous cooperative sensing. With 30 orbiting targets, VISTA recovers the catalogue 31.2% faster than the fixed-dimensional recurrent baseline. In the large-scale regime, VISTA reduces five-hour uncertainty by 97.5% relative to the strongest classical reference and by 99.3% relative to the recurrent learner. Zero-shot tests up to 20,000 objects reveal near-linear relations between sensing capacity, catalogue size, and recovery horizon. Learned policies also exhibit sensor modality adaptation and generalization to population and initial-uncertainty shifts. Together, these results demonstrate that VISTA provides a scalable framework for adaptive space situational awareness sensor tasking across large, distributed networks of heterogeneous ground- and space-based sensors.

</details>


### [183] [GLR-MM: Graph-Based Global-Local Reconstruction for Robust Multimodal Chest X-ray and EHR Representation Learning under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23876)
*Surbhi Sharma,Nikhil Manali,Devesh Maheshwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GLR-MM——一种基于图的全局-局部重建框架，用于ICU早期死亡预测中不完整多模态（CXR-EHR）输入的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床多模态模型常需在全部胸部X光（CXR）与电子健康记录（EHR）输入到齐前做出预测。现有方法或对齐已观测表征、或建模缺失性、或进行跨模态重建，但未联合利用患者内证据与临床上相似的跨患者证据。

Method: 将五种CXR-EHR模态映射到共享空间；通过互补的局部跨模态分支与全局图注意力分支重建缺失嵌入，并自适应融合两者估计；联合优化类别平衡的预测、重建与对比目标。

Result: 在9,620例MIMIC来源ICU住院数据上，以共享确定性掩码评估10%、30%、50%随机模态缺失。轻度与中度缺失下MUSE表现更优；50%缺失时GLR-MM的AUROC与AUPRC分别高出0.0088和0.0249。

Conclusion: 图引导的重建在输入严重不完整时最为有效。

Abstract: Clinical multimodal models must often predict before all chest X-ray (CXR) and electronic health record (EHR) inputs are available. Existing approaches align observed representations, model missingness, or reconstruct across modalities, but do not jointly exploit within-patient and clinically similar inter-patient evidence. We propose GLR-MM, a Graph-Based Global-Local Reconstruction framework for early ICU mortality prediction. It maps five CXR-EHR modalities to a shared space, reconstructs missing embeddings through complementary local cross-modal and global graph-attention branches, adaptively fuses their estimates, and optimizes class-balanced prediction, reconstruction, and contrastive objectives. On 9,620 MIMIC-derived ICU stays, we evaluate 10%, 30%, and 50% random modality missingness with shared deterministic masks. MUSE performs better under mild and moderate missingness, whereas GLR-MM achieves higher AUROC and AUPRC at 50% by 0.0088 and 0.0249, respectively. These results indicate that graph-guided reconstruction is most useful when inputs are severely incomplete.

</details>


### [184] [Collaborative Streaming Anomaly Detection with Interactive Explanations and Ensemble Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23883)
*Diogo Risca,Afonso Lourenço,Ricardo Martins,Goreti Marreiros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种人机协作的流式异常检测系统，通过多检测器加权共识、伪影感知规则和代理模型提升高速数据流检测的鲁棒性与可解释性，并支持分析师介入调整决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速数据流场景下的异常检测面临检测稳定性、部署伪影干扰以及模型可解释性不足等问题，纯自动化方法难以满足实际需求，因此需要将人类分析师显式纳入决策回路，以增强检测可靠性与可操作性。

Method: 系统集成异构检测器，采用基于归一化的加权共识机制融合输出，并引入伪影感知规则以稳定部署环境下的异常评分；同时构建代理模型近似集成共识，暴露与异常行为相关的可读传感器条件，支持分析师审阅异常片段、调整共识行为并优化代理规则，形成人机调整后的集成模型。

Result: 在包含260,000个事件和3个异常片段的工业数据流上进行评估，系统展现出稳健的检测性能以及可操作的人机交互效果。

Conclusion: 将人类分析师显式融入决策回路，结合异构检测器共识与可解释代理模型，能够有效提升高速数据流异常检测的鲁棒性与实用性，实现可操作的人机协同。

Abstract: We present a collaborative streaming anomaly detection system for high-speed data streams that explicitly integrates human analysts into the decision loop. The system combines heterogeneous detectors and aggregates their outputs through a normalization-based weighted consensus, complemented by artifact-aware rules to stabilize anomaly scoring under deployment. To improve interpretability, it derives surrogate models that approximate the ensemble consensus and expose human-readable sensor conditions associated with anomalous behavior. Analysts can actively intervene by reviewing anomaly episodes, adjusting consensus behavior, and refining surrogate rules used for anomaly prediction, producing a human-adjusted ensemble. We evaluate the approach on an industrial stream with 260\,000 events and 3 anomalous episodes, showing robust detection and actionable human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [185] [Circuit-Diff: Factual Edit-based Intervention Method for Localizing Knowledge in Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23892)
*Edward G. Friedman,Xiangchen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Circuit-Diff，通过对模型施加低秩事实编辑，识别归因图中角色发生变化的特征节点，从而定位与编辑知识相关的电路，并附带开源实现与两个配套工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性以特征为基本单元、以电路为计算子图，但单个神经元存在多义性，Cross-Layer Transcoders (CLTs) 通过生成归因图来近似电路。然而归因图节点为无标签特征，使用前需剪枝并人工推断每个存活节点的含义，导致 CLTs 用于电路发现时门槛高、效率低。

Method: Circuit-Diff 直接对模型本身施加低秩事实编辑，将归因图中角色在该编辑下发生变化的特征视为与所编辑知识相关。方法被形式化定义，并评估经事实编辑后冻结 CLT 的可靠性；通过把选出的节点在最多 24 个 CounterFact 编辑上进行 patch 来做因果检验，同时给出案例研究。实现基于 circuit-tracer 包开源，并附带多提示聚合与基于规则的超节点标注两个工具。

Result: 在所考察的编辑上，被标记节点不仅是对物体 token 的检测器；通过读取 CLT 已发布的特征仪表板可见，这些节点还包含围绕新旧物体的历史、地理与关联特征。方法在 CounterFact 编辑上的因果 patch 测试中检验了所选节点的有效性。

Conclusion: Circuit-Diff 通过低秩事实编辑驱动的归因图变化来筛选相关特征，使 CLTs 更易用于电路发现，并降低人工解读无标签节点的负担；配套开源实现与工具进一步提升了实用性。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability defines features as the fundamental units of a neural network and circuits as the weighted subgraphs that carry out its computation. Because individual neurons are polysemantic, Cross-Layer Transcoders (CLTs) were introduced as a way to approximate a model's circuits by generating an attribution graph. The nodes of that graph, however, are unlabeled features: reading a graph means pruning it and then working out by hand what each surviving node means. To make CLTs easier to use for circuit discovery, we introduce Circuit-Diff, which intervenes on the model itself with a low-rank factual edit and takes the features whose role in the attribution graph changes under that edit as related to the edited knowledge. On the edits we examine, the flagged nodes are not only detectors of the object token: read off the CLT's released feature dashboards, they include features for the history, geography and associations surrounding the old and new objects. We formalize the method, measure how reliable a frozen CLT remains after a factual edit, test the selected nodes causally by patching them on up to 24 CounterFact edits, give a case study, and release an open-source implementation built on the circuit-tracer package, together with two further tools (multi-prompt aggregation and rule-based supernode labeling).

</details>


### [186] [GDN Tree-Scan: Served Tree Verification for Recurrent-Hybrid Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23900)
*Zhiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GDN Tree-Scan——一个集成于 vLLM 的 Gated-DeltaNet 混合语言模型树形推测解码验证器，解决了循环状态在树形验证中无法沿路径正确传播的问题，并在 Qwen3.6-27B-FP8 上取得 27.0% 的 token 加权解码吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力型 Transformer 的树形推测解码验证只需祖先掩码即可，但循环混合语言模型打破了这一前提：候选行必须携带其根到节点路径上原生顺序解码本应产生的循环状态。否则验证器即便使用正确的注意力掩码，仍会基于不可能的循环历史进行条件化，导致验证失效。

Method: 将 GDN Tree-Scan 作为服务化验证器集成进 vLLM，融合四类机制：FlashAttention-2 树偏置注意力、分支局部的 GDN 扫描/重放、设备端多草稿提交（multidraft commitment），以及仅对已接受链进行状态发布（accepted-chain-only state publication）。

Result: 在公开的 Qwen3.6-27B-FP8 检查点上，B=1、温度 0.6 的 SWE/Codex 解码门控中，六节点根分支树使每次提交 token 数提升 17.2%，验证前向时间接近原生水平；token 加权解码吞吐达 23.88 tokens/s，对比原生五步 MTP（E5）的 18.80，提升 27.0%。单请求等时延视角为 +4.0%，端到端任务墙钟时间仍以预填充为主。等价性证据仅限循环 oracle 概率重打分（p-rescore）在观测原生翻转下限内的闭合，并非完整的分布距离证明。

Conclusion: GDN Tree-Scan 有效支撑了循环混合模型的树形推测解码，在保持近原生验证开销的同时显著提升解码吞吐，但递归状态等价性的形式化保证仍是待解决的问题。

Abstract: Tree speculative decoding verifies multiple candidate continuations in one target forward pass. For attention-only transformers, the verifier mainly needs an ancestry mask. Recurrent-hybrid language models break this assumption: a candidate row must also carry the recurrent state that native sequential decode would have produced along its root-to-node path. Otherwise, a verifier can use a correct attention mask while still conditioning on an impossible recurrent history.
  We present GDN Tree-Scan, a served verifier for Gated-DeltaNet hybrid language models integrated into vLLM. The system combines FlashAttention-2 tree-bias attention, branch-local GDN scan/replay, device-side multidraft commitment, and accepted-chain-only state publication. On the public Qwen3.6-27B-FP8 checkpoint, in a clean batch-one (B=1) SWE/Codex decode gate at temperature 0.6, a six-node root-branch tree increases committed tokens/event by 17.2% at near-native verify-forward time and reaches 23.88 token-weighted decode tokens/s versus 18.80 for native five-step MTP (E5), a 27.0% token-weighted decode-throughput gain. The per-request-equal latency view is +4.0%, and end-to-end task wall time remains prefill-heavy. Empirical equivalence evidence is scoped to recurrent-oracle probability-rescore (p-rescore) closure within the observed native flip floor, not a full distribution-distance proof.

</details>


### [187] [Misaligned Clinical Risk Classification and Cost Asymmetry in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23999)
*Star S. D. Liu,Xiyu Ding,Robert B. Barrett,Alberto Santamaria-Pang,Nic Dobbins,Harold P. Lehmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究考察四个开源权重LLM（Qwen-2.5-7B/32B、Llama-3.1-8B/70B）如何在内部表征临床成本权衡。结果：模型能线性恢复患者风险（AUC≈0.83）与成本方向，但仅两个大模型的表征变化与输出变化一致，成本幅度响应多为方向无关；12个模型-措辞组合中仅2个表现出成本正确的决策。LLM编码但未能可靠整合风险与成本信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床决策中日益重要，但其如何将患者风险与临床成本权衡（假阴性/假阳性代价）整合进决策尚不清楚。研究旨在探究LLM内部是否表征成本权衡、这些表征与临床预测的关系，以及决策是否随成本方向与幅度按预期变化，从而评估其临床部署的可靠性。

Method: 使用公开糖尿病数据集，对四个开源权重LLM（Qwen-2.5-7B/32B、Llama-3.1-8B/70B）设置11种假阴性(FN)对假阳性(FP)成本比，并采用三种措辞表述。通过线性探测等方法考察模型内部表征，结合行为输出来检验成本方向与幅度的可恢复性及决策一致性，并检验镜像对称编码假设。

Result: 患者风险可线性恢复，性能与常规分类器相当（AUC≈0.83）；每个模型均能恢复成本方向。但成本方向的表征变化仅在两个较大模型中与输出变化一致；对成本幅度的响应主要为方向无关。12个模型-措辞组合中仅2个同时表现出对FN与FP成本增加的对立响应及成本正确排序。此外，在一侧成本上拟合的方向迁移到另一侧时未发生反转，不符合镜像对称编码预期。

Conclusion: LLM能够编码风险与成本信息，但无法可靠地将其整合为成本正确的决策。临床评估除预测性能外，还应纳入成本权衡测试、措辞敏感性分析及默认操作点考察。

Abstract: How large language models (LLMs) integrate patient risk with clinical cost tradeoffs remains poorly understood. We investigated how four open-weight LLMs (Qwen-2.5-7B/32B and Llama-3.1-8B/70B) internally represent cost tradeoffs, how these representations relate to clinical predictions, and whether decisions shift as predicted by the specified cost direction and magnitude. Using a public diabetes dataset, we varied 11 false-negative (FN) to false-positive (FP) cost ratios across three phrasings and examined representations and behavioral outputs. Patient risk was linearly recoverable on par with conventional classifiers (AUC $\approx 0.83$), and cost direction was recoverable in every model. However, representational shifts in cost direction tracked output changes only in the two larger models, and responses to cost magnitude were predominantly direction-agnostic. Only 2 of 12 model-phrasings showed both opposing responses to increasing FN versus FP costs and cost-correct ordering. Representationally, a direction fitted on one cost side did not invert when transferred to the other, as expected under mirror-symmetric encoding. These findings suggest that LLMs encode risk and cost information but do not reliably integrate them into cost-correct decisions. Clinical evaluations should therefore include tradeoff tests, phrasing sensitivity, and default operating points alongside predictive performance.

</details>


### [188] [Multivariate quantile regression via Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23906)
*Andrew Polar,Michael Poluektov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测随机系统中向量值目标条件联合分布的新算法，采用KAN集成作为函数逼近器，并提出新的联合分布差异度量及基于其的拟合优度检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机系统的随机性为内在随机性，而非源于观测误差或加性噪声，传统方法难以刻画此类向量值目标的条件联合分布；多元分位数回归虽涉及条件联合分布建模但任务相对简单。

Method: 采用Kolmogorov–Arnold网络（KAN）集成作为灵活函数逼近器实现分布识别；提出新的联合分布差异度量，并基于该度量构建拟合优度检验用于验证与校准识别技术；方法理论上不限于KAN。

Result: 所提识别技术结合训练流程与拟合优度检验，可对内在随机系统的向量值目标条件联合分布进行建模；拟合优度检验在本研究中以ad hoc方式用于验证与校准。

Conclusion: 该算法与差异度量及GoF检验具有更广泛的适用性，检验可进一步制表以供通用，但本文未开展该工作。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel algorithm for predicting conditional joint distributions of vector-valued targets in stochastic systems whose randomness is intrinsic rather than arising from observation errors or additive noise. Multivariate quantile regression also involves modeling conditional joint distributions but represents a less challenging task. It predicts the probability that vector-valued targets fall within predefined regions, identifies regions corresponding to predefined probability levels, or performs both tasks simultaneously. The proposed identification technique employs ensembles of Kolmogorov--Arnold networks (KANs) as flexible function approximators. Although the suggested technique is not theoretically restricted to KANs, KANs are particularly well suited to the proposed construction and are therefore used throughout this study. In addition to the training procedure, this work introduces a new discrepancy measure for joint distributions and a goodness-of-fit (GoF) test based on it. This GoF test was initially developed to validate and calibrate the proposed identification technique and is used here in an ad hoc manner. Although the test could be tabulated for broader use, such a tabulation is not pursued in this work. The test is also applicable more generally.

</details>


### [189] [FlashBoB: I/O-Efficient Exact Backward-over-Backward for Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24089)
*Anthony Givans,Michael Crawshaw,Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashBoB：一种用于softmax注意力反向传播再求导（BoB）的精确、I/O高效算法，避免生成N×N中间张量，将精确二阶注意力扩展至N=262K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: softmax注意力是长上下文工作负载的瓶颈。FlashAttention虽使前向与一阶反向I/O高效，但不支持backward-over-backward（BoB），而BoB对二阶优化、test-time training、基于梯度的记忆和元学习等应用至关重要。现有BoB实现要么材料化大型中间张量，要么在长序列下耗尽GPU显存。
