Method: 核心洞察是softmax二次反向传播具有分层仿射结构：两个逐行标量通过仿射变换决定所有输出。据此设计两遍调度方案，将计算保持在片上tile内，避免所有N×N中间张量，片上SRAM占用有界，片外HBM流量最小。

Result: 达到Θ(N²d²/M)的HBM流量（d为头维度，M为内存大小）；在标准FlashAttention风格分数重计算模型下，匹配精确前向注意力继承的大缓存下界。单张A100 80GB GPU上可将精确注意力BoB扩展至N=262K，而先前PyTorch精确基线在N=16K即失败；相比FlashBack最高快6.3倍。

Conclusion: FlashBoB使精确二阶注意力在长上下文序列长度下变得实用，此前实现在该场景下无法高效运行。

Abstract: Transformer models built on the attention mechanism have become a central building block in modern deep learning, yet softmax attention remains a major bottleneck for long-context workloads. While FlashAttention makes the forward and first backward passes I/O-efficient, it does not support backward-over-backward (BoB), which enables exact differentiation through the backward pass for applications such as second-order optimization, test-time training, gradient-based memory, and meta-learning. Existing BoB implementations either materialize large intermediate tensors or exhaust GPU memory at long sequence lengths. We present FlashBoB, an exact, I/O-efficient algorithm for BoB in softmax attention that keeps computation within on-chip tiles and avoids all $N \times N$ intermediate tensors, where $N$ is the sequence length. The key insight is a hierarchical affine structure in the softmax double backward: two row-wise scalars determine all outputs through affine transformations. This yields a two-pass schedule with bounded on-chip static random-access memory (SRAM) usage and minimal off-chip high-bandwidth memory (HBM) traffic. FlashBoB achieves $Θ(N^2 d^2/M)$ HBM traffic ($d$ is the head dimension and $M$ is the memory size) and, within the standard FlashAttention-style score-recomputation model, matches the inherited large-cache lower bound for exact forward attention. Empirically, it scales exact attention BoB to $N=262\text{K}$ on a single A100 80GB GPU, where prior PyTorch exact baselines fail by $N=16\text{K}$, and is up to $6.3\times$ faster than FlashBack. These results make exact second-order attention practical at long-context sequence lengths where prior implementations cannot run efficiently.

</details>


### [190] [A discrete generative model of neuronal spiking activity on microelectrode arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23907)
*Md Sayed Tanveer,Mohammed A. Mostajo-Radji,Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于离散时空motif词表的生成模型，用于高密度微电极阵列的稀疏全阵列二进制脉冲活动建模，无需特定实验参数即可跨组织泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经活动生成模型通常假设固定的已排序神经元集合，而高密度微电极阵列产生的全阵列二进制脉冲体极其稀疏，且不同实验中所观测到的电极子集不断变化，导致已有方法难以适用，亟需可复用、不依赖特定实验参数的表示方法。

Method: 构建离散生成模型，使用共享的时空motif词表表示活动。采用残差向量量化自编码器学习motif词表，并用因子化掩码Transformer预测活动发生位置及每个活跃位置对应的motif。在涵盖人脑类器官与急性离体人海马组织的31项实验上进行评估。

Result: motif被广泛复用：实验身份仅解释motif使用熵的9%，跨组织类型的motif重叠度与组织内部相当。在独立于生成先验的表示质量评估中，体素级重建平均精度为匹配的扁平分词器的5.2倍；在掩码补全与自由生成任务上，完整模型达到匹配生成基线站点级平均精度的1.4–2.6倍，并在全部四类生成指标上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为全阵列脉冲活动建立了紧凑、可复用且无需学习实验特定参数的表示，为跨多种神经制备的生成建模提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Generative models of neural activity could help characterize tissue dynamics, compare experimental conditions, and simulate population activity for applications ranging from disease and drug-response studies to closed-loop experimentation. Existing approaches, however, typically assume a fixed set of sorted neurons, whereas high-density microelectrode arrays produce extremely sparse, array-wide binary spike volumes in which the observed subset of electrodes varies across assays. We introduce a discrete generative model that represents this activity using a shared vocabulary of spatiotemporal motifs. A residual vector-quantized autoencoder learns the motif vocabulary, while a factorized masked transformer predicts where activity occurs and which motif appears at each active location. We evaluate the model on 31 assays spanning human brain organoids and acute \emph{ex vivo} human hippocampal tissue. The learned motifs are broadly reused: assay identity explains only $9%$ of the entropy in motif use, and motif overlap across tissue types is comparable to overlap within them. When representation quality is evaluated independently of the generative prior, our approach achieves $5.2\times$ the voxel-level reconstruction average precision of a matched flat tokenizer. For masked completion and free generation, the full model achieves $1.4$--$2.6\times$ the site-level average precision of the matched generative baseline and outperforms it across all four families of generation metrics. These results establish a compact, reusable representation for array-wide spiking activity without learned assay-specific parameters, providing a scalable foundation for generative modeling across diverse neural preparations.

</details>


### [191] [Reinforcement Learning under State and Outcome Uncertainty: A Foundational Distributional Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24103)
*Larry Preuett,Qiuyi Zhang,Muhammad Aurangzeb Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将分布式强化学习（DistRL）推广到部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），提出新的分布式贝尔曼算子并证明其收敛性，通过psi-vectors给出收益分布的有限表示，进而提出DPBVI算法，为部分可观测下的风险敏感控制奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实规划任务中智能体需同时应对环境状态的不确定性与所选策略结果的可变性；现有DistRL仅建模全可观测域下的收益分布，无法处理部分可观测场景，而部分可观测下的风险敏感控制需要收益分布信息。

Method: 将DistRL扩展至POMDP，使智能体学习每个条件计划的收益分布；引入面向部分可观测的新分布式贝尔曼算子，并在supremum p-Wasserstein度量下证明其收敛性；提出psi-vectors对收益分布进行有限表示，推广经典alpha-vectors；在此基础上将psi-vectors融入标准point-based backup流程，提出分布点基值迭代（DPBVI）算法。

Result: 给出了部分可观测下分布式贝尔曼算子的收敛性证明；psi-vectors实现了收益分布的有限表示；DPBVI成功将DistRL与POMDP规划相衔接，可追踪收益分布，为风险敏感控制提供支撑，并公开源代码。

Conclusion: 该工作连接了分布式强化学习与POMDP规划，通过追踪收益分布为部分可观测域中罕见高影响事件的风险敏感控制奠定基础，并开源代码以促进鲁棒决策研究。

Abstract: In many real-world planning tasks, agents must tackle uncertainty about the environment's state and variability in the outcomes of any chosen policy. We address both forms of uncertainty as a first step toward safer algorithms in partially observable settings. Specifically, we extend Distributional Reinforcement Learning (DistRL)-which models the entire return distribution for fully observable domains-to Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs), allowing an agent to learn the distribution of returns for each conditional plan. Concretely, we introduce new distributional Bellman operators for partial observability and prove their convergence under the supremum p-Wasserstein metric. We also propose a finite representation of these return distributions via psi-vectors, generalizing the classical alpha-vectors in POMDP solvers. Building on this, we develop Distributional Point-Based Value Iteration (DPBVI), which integrates psi-vectors into a standard point-based backup procedure-bridging DistRL and POMDP planning. By tracking return distributions, DPBVI lays the foundation for future risk-sensitive control in domains where rare, high-impact events must be carefully managed. We provide source code to foster further research in robust decision-making under partial observability.

</details>


### [192] [Matched-Input Estimates Differ in Sign Across Architectures: Auditing EEG Foundation Models on Motor Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23924)
*Kevin Zhou,Sparsh Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在严格锁定验证集的协议下审计LaBraM与CBraMod：四分类BCI IV-2a上所有监督基线均优于所有基础模型配置；宽带输入带来的差异符号随架构而变；四分类劣势在BNCI2014-004上不可复现；温度缩放可将基础模型校准误差拉回监督模型区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练EEG基础模型被广泛宣传为脑机接口的通用编码器，但近期基准在"其表征何时能迁移到下游任务"上结论相互矛盾。作者希望排除方法选择泄漏与输入管线不匹配等混杂因素，给出更可靠的比较证据。

Method: 采用validation-locked协议审计LaBraM与CBraMod的运动想象表现：预处理、架构、优化、冻结深度、检查点、温度与方法选择均仅用训练会话数据确定。数据集为四分类BCI Competition IV-2a与二分类BNCI2014-004。核心混杂检验：将三种监督架构（含ATCNet、EEG Conformer）在基础模型所用的宽带数组上重训，构造匹配输入对比，并做多重比较校正（n=9）。另用验证集拟合的温度缩放评估校准。

Result: BCI IV-2a四分类中，所有监督对比方法均优于所有基础模型配置（含验证集选出的微调方案）。匹配输入后，宽带输入使ATCNet准确率提高0.078，却使EEG Conformer下降0.088，符号相反；但三个匹配输入项经多重比较校正后在n=9下均不显著，故仅作描述性解读而非正式的"架构×管线"交互。四分类劣势在数据集间不一致：二分类BNCI2014-004上未能检测到微调CBraMod与监督基线间同样的分离。温度缩放虽使分类精度大幅偏低，却把基础模型校准误差带回监督模型区间。

Conclusion: 单一对比方法可能无法给出与架构无关的"预训练对监督"性能差距分解；基础模型相对监督基线的短板既依赖架构也依赖数据集与评估指标（精度vs校准），当前关于EEG基础模型迁移优势的结论应谨慎表述。

Abstract: Pretrained EEG foundation models are increasingly proposed as general-purpose encoders for brain-computer interfaces, yet recent benchmarks disagree about when their representations transfer to downstream tasks. We audit LaBraM and CBraMod on motor imagery under a validation-locked protocol in which preprocessing, architecture, optimization, freeze depth, checkpoint, temperature, and method selection are determined using training-session data only. On four-class BCI Competition IV-2a, every supervised comparator evaluated here outperforms every foundation-model configuration, including validation-selected fine-tuning. We then examine a key confound: foundation models and task-specific decoders are normally evaluated using different input pipelines. Retraining three supervised architectures on the broadband arrays consumed by the foundation models produces matched-input accuracy differences of opposite sign across architectures: broadband input improves ATCNet by 0.078 accuracy while reducing EEG Conformer accuracy by 0.088. None of the three individual matched-input terms is significant after multiple-comparison correction at n = 9, so we treat the sign variation descriptively rather than as a formal architecture-by-pipeline interaction. These observed sign differences suggest that a single comparator may not provide an architecture-invariant decomposition of a pretrained-versus-supervised performance gap. The four-class deficit also does not reproduce uniformly across motor-imagery datasets: on two-class BNCI2014-004 we cannot detect the same separation between fine-tuned CBraMod and the supervised comparators. Finally, validation-fitted temperature scaling returns foundation-model calibration error to the supervised range despite substantially lower four-class accuracy.

</details>


### [193] [The Neural Forcing for Three-Dimensional Incompressible Navier-Stokes finite time blowup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23934)
*Beibei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两部分神经网络框架，用于受迫三维不可压Navier–Stokes流动：第一部分构建外力生成与优化系统，第二部分建立连续介质层面的数学认证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为受迫三维不可压Navier–Stokes流动构造可控的外力轨迹，并在连续介质层面为有限时间光滑性丧失提供严格的数学认证，桥接神经候选发现与连续介质分析。

Method: Part I：物理信息神经网络生成结构化外力轨迹，经可微PDE rollout或PPO-Clip优化候选，选定外力冻结后由独立定力重放校验；Part II：将神经候选发现与连续介质分析分离，推导积分互易涡量判据以蕴含Riccati型增长与有限时间光滑延拓丧失，发展经验证的计算到连续介质迁移策略，并为非退化神经输出律建立条件性正概率闭合。

Result: 在连续介质层面完成证明；给出可蕴含Riccati型增长与有限时间光滑延拓丧失的积分互易涡量判据，并建立计算到连续介质的经验证迁移与条件性正概率闭合。

Conclusion: 该框架通过两阶段设计实现神经外力发现与连续介质严格认证的结合，证明在连续介质层面成立，为受迫Navier–Stokes流的有限时间奇异性/光滑性丧失研究提供条件性理论基础。

Abstract: We present a two-part neural framework for forced three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes flow. Part~I develops the computational forcing system. A physics-informed neural model generates structured external-force trajectories, candidates are optimized through differentiable PDE rollouts or PPO-Clip, and selected forcings are frozen and checked by independent fixed-force replay. Part~II provides the mathematical certification layer. It separates neural candidate discovery from continuum analysis, derives integrated reciprocal-vorticity criteria that imply Riccati-type growth and finite-time loss of smooth continuation, develops a validated computational-to-continuum transfer strategy, and establishes a conditional positive-probability closure for a nondegenerate neural output law. The proof is complete at the continuum level.

</details>


### [194] [Opinion Leader Dynamics: How Sparse Attention Shapes Token Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24202)
*Jingkun Liu,Yue Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将token建模为单位球面上的粒子，提出"意见领袖动力学"框架，用反向Wasserstein梯度流刻画稀疏注意力下token表示的演化，理论证明指数收敛，并在前沿稀疏注意力LLM的投影token表示中验证了多群体结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力在降低全局自注意力二次开销的同时保持较好性能，但其受限交互如何塑造token表示的演化，在理论上仍缺乏深入探索。作者希望为稀疏注意力下token群体"内部趋同、群体间分离"的现象提供动力学解释。

Method: 将token视为单位球面上的粒子，提出"意见领袖动力学"框架，包含两种机制：(1)显式模型——固定代表（意见领袖）诱导势场，吸引token趋向不同局部极大值；(2)隐式模型——不连通的交互群体各自演化到独立的共识方向。两者均被形式化为反向Wasserstein梯度流，并在适当条件下证明指数收敛。随后将理论预测与前沿稀疏注意力LLM的token演化相对照，并在四个基准上比较Kimi-K3、MiniMax-M3、DeepSeek-V4-Flash与稠密注意力模型GLM-4.7-Flash的投影token表示。

Result: 在四个基准上，Kimi-K3、MiniMax-M3、DeepSeek-V4-Flash的投影token表示比稠密注意力模型GLM-4.7-Flash呈现更清晰的簇分离和更高的聚类分数；有限粒子模拟展示了理论所预言的指数收敛行为。

Conclusion: 受限的token交互可对应到不同的群体级吸引子，为稀疏注意力如何在维持群体内部对齐的同时保持群体间分离提供了动力学层面的解释；观察结果支持预测的多群体结构与已训练前沿LLM的相关性。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the quadratic cost of global self-attention while retaining strong empirical performance, but how its restricted interactions shape the evolution of token representations remains theoretically underexplored. Modeling tokens as particles on the unit sphere, we introduce opinion leader dynamics, a framework that identifies two mechanisms through which token groups converge internally while maintaining distinct limiting directions. In the explicit model, fixed representatives induce a potential that attracts tokens toward distinct local maxima. In the implicit model, disconnected interaction groups evolve toward separate consensus directions. We formulate both models as reverse Wasserstein gradient flows and establish exponential convergence under suitable conditions. We further connect these theoretical predictions to token evolution in frontier sparse-attention LLMs that motivate our framework. Across four benchmarks, Kimi-K3, MiniMax-M3, and DeepSeek-V4-Flash consistently exhibit clearer cluster separation and higher clustering scores than the dense-attention model GLM-4.7-Flash in projected token representations. These observations support the relevance of the predicted multiple-group structure to trained frontier LLMs, while finite-particle simulations illustrate the theoretical convergence behavior. Together, our results connect restricted token interactions to distinct group-level attractors, providing a dynamical account of how sparse attention can support alignment within groups while preserving separation between them.

</details>


### [195] [Hessian Rank Constraint for Learning Structure of Nonlinear Latent Variable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24241)
*Zijian Li,Ruichu Cai,Feng Xie,Xinshuai Dong,Haoyue Dai,Yuewen Sun,Yujia Zheng,Guangyi Chen,Yingyao Hu,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 cross-Hessian Rank Constraint (HRC)，一种基于秩的非线性潜变量因果发现准则，利用观测数据对数密度的 cross-Hessian 秩来刻画潜变量维度，并将其应用于潜变量定位与因果结构恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据中发现潜变量及其因果关系是基础但困难的问题；现有方法通常依赖线性关系或可逆混合函数等强假设，难以适用于一般非线性混合场景。

Method: 提出 HRC 条件：当两组观测变量被一组低维潜变量 d-分离时，在条件对数密度导数的温和仿射导数假设下，cross-Hessian 的秩等于潜变量维度；该秩性质在线性高斯情形下退化为 Tetrad 约束。并将 HRC 实例化于纯单因子测量设定，用于定位潜变量并恢复其因果结构至马尔可夫等价类。

Result: 理论证明了非线性情形下观测数据对数密度的 cross-Hessian 会产生秩性质并揭示潜变量信息，且在线性高斯情形下归约为 Tetrad 约束；合成数据与真实数据实验均支持理论主张。

Conclusion: HRC 为一般非线性混合下的潜变量因果发现提供了基于秩的基本工具，在低噪声或中等非线性条件下该假设易于满足，并在单因子测量场景中实现了潜变量定位与因果结构恢复。

Abstract: Uncovering latent variables and their causal relations from observed data is a fundamental yet challenging problem. Existing methods often rely on restrictive assumptions, such as linear relations or invertible mixing functions. To better address this problem under general nonlinear mixing procedures, we propose a condition called the cross-Hessian Rank Constraint (HRC), which serves as a primitive rank-based tool for nonlinear latent causal discovery. In particular, we show that a rank-based property arises from the cross-Hessian of the observed-data log-density in the nonlinear case, revealing information about the latent variables, and reduces to the Tetrad constraints in the linear Gaussian case. More specifically, when two groups of observed variables are d-separated by a set of lower-dimensional latent variables, the rank of this cross-Hessian is equal to the dimension of the latent variables, under a mild affine derivative assumption on the conditional log-density derivatives. This assumption can be naturally satisfied when the noise level is low or the relevant nonlinearity is moderate. As a downstream application, we instantiate HRC in the pure one-factor measurement setting for locating latent variables and recovering their causal structure up to Markov equivalence. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets support the theoretical claims.

</details>


### [196] [Simpler Methods Work Better for L1 Penalized Logistic Models and Large Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23995)
*Edward Raff,James Holt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对现有L1正则线性模型求解器进行大规模实证测试，发现旧方法反而优于被标榜为"最先进"的新方法，并提出一个基于LBFGS在次梯度上的简单基线，经微调后在工业级生产环境中更具实用性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L1正则线性模型在高维（d>1,000,000）任务中仍是业界主流方法，但许多现有L1求解器通用性差、速度极慢且难以并行化，导致在MLOps流水线中难以训练大规模工业语料。作者希望澄清哪些求解器真正适合生产使用。

Method: 作者复现并测试了文献中若干被宣称"最先进"的求解器，与较旧的经典方法进行对比；同时提出并评估一个在次梯度上使用LBFGS的新简单基线，辅以少量工程微调。

Result: 旧方法在当前通用场景下远优于新提出的"最先进"方法；而尽管文献中以理论不收敛为由否定了次梯度LBFGS方案，实验表明该简单基线经微调后效果极佳。

Conclusion: 作者建议学术界在研究中应避免得出过度自信的错误结论，以免阻碍成果向生产转化；同时推荐采用次梯度LBFGS基线作为更易支持、更易扩展的生产级方案。

Abstract: Linear models with an $L_1$-norm penalty remain state-of-the-art for high-dimensional ($d > 1,000,000$) tasks, offering a straightforward method for solving real-world industry problems. Despite their widespread use in industry and utility, many $L_1$ solvers are not effective for general use, are prohibitively slow, and are ineffective in parallelization. This makes them difficult to train in an MLOps pipeline on large industry-scale corpora. In this work, we test several proposed ``state-of-the-art'' solutions from the literature and find that older methods are currently far superior for general use. We also identify several recommendations for academics to perform research that avoids erroneously overconfident results, which can prevent the transition to production use. Equally surprising, we find that a new and simple baseline, using LBFGS on a sub-gradient, is highly effective with minor tweaks, despite being dismissed in the literature for theoretical non-convergence. In practice, we find it is an easier-to-support and easier-to-scale method for production use.

</details>


### [197] [MemCalib: Benchmarking and Optimizing Memory Use in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24259)
*Ruike Cao,Fanyu Zhao,Fugen Yao,Liang Dong,Jian Xu,Guanjun Jiang,Yifei Zhao,Han Zhang,Li Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MemCalib基准评估LLM对上下文中记忆影响力的校准能力，发现前沿模型普遍存在过度使用或使用不足的问题，并提出有序双向反事实信用分配算法MemCalib-RL以分离并定位两类偏差信号，在多个模型家族与规模上取得最佳整体表现并更平衡地处理记忆使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体记忆的有效性取决于底层LLM能否为上下文中的每条记忆赋予恰当的影响力，但该能力此前被广泛忽视，缺乏相应的评估基准与优化方法，且现有后训练算法存在方向性偏斜。

Method: 构建基于真实记忆系统场景的MemCalib基准，用于评估记忆使用并推进优化算法；随后提出MemCalib-RL——一种有序双向反事实信用分配算法，通过精确原子消融分离过度使用与使用不足信号，并将信用定位到响应token上。

Result: 在MemCalib测试集上，前沿开源与闭源模型均难以恰当使用记忆，常出现过度或不足使用，导致有偏、低质量响应；常见后训练算法（如GRPO与在线自蒸馏）呈现明显的方向性偏斜，一个方向改善而另一方向退化。MemCalib-RL在Qwen3-8B、Ministral-3-8B-Instruct与Qwen3.5-35B-A3B等模型家族和规模上取得最佳整体性能，更好地平衡过度与不足使用，且增益可泛化至外部基准。

Conclusion: 记忆影响力校准是LLM智能体记忆能力中被忽视的关键维度；MemCalib为评估提供了现实场景基础，而MemCalib-RL通过双向反事实信用分配有效缓解方向性偏斜，其设计选择与鲁棒性得到进一步实验支持，并揭示了训练动态。

Abstract: The effectiveness of agent memory ultimately depends on whether the underlying LLM gives each memory in context an appropriate degree of influence over its response. Yet this capability has remained largely overlooked. To assess this capability, we introduce MemCalib, a benchmark grounded in realistic memory-system scenarios for evaluating memory use and advancing optimization algorithms. Results on the MemCalib test set reveal that frontier open- and closed-source models struggle to use memory appropriately. They frequently over-use or under-use memory rather than matching each proposition's actual use to its target level, leading to biased, low-quality responses. Experiments with common post-training algorithms, including group relative policy optimization and on-policy self-distillation, further reveal a clear directional skew: trained models improve in one direction while deteriorating in the other. We therefore propose MemCalib-RL, an ordered bidirectional counterfactual credit-assignment algorithm that separates over- and under-use signals and localizes their credit to response tokens through exact atom ablation. Results across model families and scales (Qwen3-8B, Ministral-3-8B-Instruct, and Qwen3.5-35B-A3B) show that MemCalib-RL achieves the best overall performance while better balancing over-use and under-use, with gains generalizing beyond MemCalib in external benchmark evaluation. Further experiments support its design choices and robustness and provide insight into its training dynamics.

</details>


### [198] [TTSE: A Two-Track Online Self-Evolution Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24289)
*Ruimin Pei,Yongkang Wu,Shangyi Zheng,Yaqing Zhang,Deyang Li,Jianjun Tao,Xinyu Zhang,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TTSE（Two-Track Self-Evolution），一种双轨在线自进化框架，将智能体可进化知识解耦为环境事实（FACT）与任务条件化实现流程（TIP），并从决策论角度分析其优势，在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在持续交互环境中需要实现长期自主性，其自身能力的进化是核心问题。现有做法将环境知识视为外部固定输入，而非智能体持续进化的一部分；强化学习方法虽通过环境交互优化策略，但通常只能适应固定任务分布或单一环境，缺乏对任务条件变化的适应能力。

Method: 提出 TTSE 双轨在线自进化框架，将进化知识分离为 FACT（环境事实，其可靠性通过交互证据持续验证）和 TIP（任务条件化的实现流程）。理论上从决策论视角将智能体超额风险分解为环境表征遗憾与条件执行遗憾，刻画环境条件化策略严格优于条件无关策略的条件，并以 FACT 识别误差与跨条件失配代价来界定下游风险。实现上兼容现有技能自进化方法，并通过检索式注入集成到通用智能体框架中。

Result: 在 GDPevo 上的消融实验验证了双轨进化的优势；在经典智能体任务基准 ALFWorld 与 ScienceWorld 上表现出更优的任务适应性；与 Bayesian-Agent 算法结合后，单轨消融实验验证了双轨优势，在 SOPBench 五大领域的总分经过三次独立重复显著提升；在真实端到端任务基准 PinchBench 上，通过检索式注入集成到通用智能体框架，在三次独立运行中稳定优于基线。

Conclusion: TTSE 通过将知识解耦为环境事实与任务条件化流程的双轨自进化机制，在理论与实验层面均证明了双轨相对单轨的优势，并具备良好的兼容性与跨基准的泛化能力，为 LLM 智能体的长期自主能力进化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents are applied in continuously interactive environments, driving the evolution of their own capabilities becomes a core problem for achieving long-term autonomy. Currently, environmental knowledge is typically treated as an external fixed input rather than as part of the agent's ongoing evolution. Reinforcement learning methods usually optimize policies through environmental interaction but tend to adapt only to fixed task distributions or single environments. This paper proposes TTSE (Two-Track Self-Evolution), a dual-track online self-evolution framework that separates evolving knowledge into FACT (environmental facts, whose reliability is continuously verified through interaction evidence) and TIP (task-conditioned implementation procedures). From a decision-theoretic perspective, we decompose the agent's excess risk into environment-representation regret and conditional-execution regret, characterize the conditions under which environment-conditioned policies strictly outperform condition-agnostic policies, and bound the downstream risk in terms of FACT identification error and cross-condition mismatch cost. In practice, TTSE's ablation experiments on GDPevo validate the advantage of dual-track evolution. On the classic agent task benchmarks ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, TTSE further demonstrates superior task adaptation. Moreover, TTSE is broadly compatible with existing skill self-evolution methods; combined with the Bayesian-Agent algorithm, a single-track ablation validates the dual-track advantage, substantially improving the aggregate score across the five major domains of SOPBench over three independent repetitions. Finally, on the real end-to-end task benchmark PinchBench, TTSE is integrated into a general agent framework via retrieval-based injection and stably outperforms the baseline across three independent runs.

</details>


### [199] [ShapeLex: Decoupling Local Shape Symbolization and Global Scale Modeling for Text-Controlled Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24003)
*Subo Wei,Jianqi Gao,Mingyan Fan,Shaorong Xie,Xinzhi Wang,Yongpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ShapeLex，将文本控制的时间序列生成解耦为局部形状的离散符号化与全局属性的连续建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范式将语义理解与序列建模耦合于单一连续隐空间，缺乏显式局部语义锚点，且未分离全局连续属性与局部离散形状，导致关键局部结构被平滑、遗漏或错位。

Method: ShapeLex首先从训练数据中归纳出可复用的离散形状单元词表（如上升、尖峰、骤降），形成可解释的符号空间；随后由自回归生成器依据文本描述选择形状，调整位置、时长等属性并按时间顺序组合成形状骨架；最后用混合密度尺度头建模并采样整体水平与波动性，以恢复真实的全局尺度。

Result: 在十二个公开数据集、真实用户撰写文本及下游预测任务上，ShapeLex生成的序列比现有方法更贴合真实数据分布；同时借助所学词表自动合成配对监督，避免随数据规模增长的标注成本，提升可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过离散形状词表与连续全局属性建模的解耦，ShapeLex在生成质量与可扩展性上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Text-controlled time series generation aims to synthesize sequences that follow natural-language descriptions while remaining faithful to real data distributions. Existing paradigms often couple semantic understanding and sequence modeling in a single continuous latent space, lacking explicit local semantic anchors and separation between global continuous attributes and local discrete shapes. As a result, key local structures may be smoothed, missed, or misplaced. We propose Shape Lexicon (ShapeLex), which decouples text-to-sequence generation into discrete symbolization of local shapes and continuous modeling of global attributes. ShapeLex first induces a reusable vocabulary of discrete shape units, such as rises, spikes, and sharp drops, from training data, forming an interpretable symbolic space. An autoregressive generator then selects shapes according to the textual description, adjusts attributes such as position and duration, and composes them in temporal order into a shape skeleton. Finally, a mixture-density scale head models and samples the overall level and volatility to restore realistic global scale. Experiments on twelve public datasets, real user-written text, and downstream forecasting tasks show that ShapeLex generates series that better match real data distributions than existing methods. In addition, paired supervision is automatically synthesized from the learned vocabulary, avoiding annotation costs that grow with dataset size and improving scalability.

</details>


### [200] [KV-COBRA: KV Cache Compression via Co-Optimized Bit-Rank Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24298)
*Sihyeon Ha,Jaeho Lee,Yo-Seb Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV-COBRA提出按注意力头协同优化秩截断与比特宽度的KV-cache压缩资源分配方法，在极低比特率下优于均匀分配方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-cache压缩方法在各注意力头上统一使用相同的秩和比特宽度，忽略了不同头最优的秩截断与量化组合存在差异，这在极端低比特率下成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 将每头的秩截断与比特宽度协同优化形式化为资源分配问题：在头内平衡秩截断损失与量化损失，再在头间重新分配预算以最小化总失真；采用融合Hadamard旋转均衡各通道方差，并按注意力-KL重要性重排SVD基使求解器具备查询感知能力；同一分配器可扩展至K+V联合压缩。

Result: 在困惑度、零样本和长上下文基准上，于0.5至4 bpd范围内，KV-COBRA在低bpd时相比评估方法呈现最小的精度退化，且无逐token额外开销。

Conclusion: 限制KV-cache极端压缩的关键在于预算在注意力头间的分配方式而非压缩方案本身；按头协同优化秩与比特宽度可显著提升低比特率下的压缩效果。

Abstract: What limits KV-cache compression at extreme bit-rates? We argue that it is not the choice of compression scheme, but how its budget is allocated across attention heads. Existing methods apply rank and bit-width uniformly, ignoring that each head has a different optimal mix of rank truncation and quantization. We show that co-optimizing rank and bit-width per head, using only standard low-rank projection and scalar quantization, dominates uniform allocation, with the largest gains at low bit-rates. Our method, KV-COBRA (Co-Optimized Bit-Rank Allocation), formalizes this as a resource-allocation problem: it balances rank-truncation loss against quantization loss within each head, then redistributes budget across heads to minimize total distortion. A fused Hadamard rotation equalizes per-channel variance, and reordering the SVD basis by attention-KL importance makes the solver query-aware. The same allocator extends to joint $K{+}V$ compression. On perplexity, zero-shot, and long-context benchmarks from $0.5$ to $4$ bits per dimension (bpd), KV-COBRA shows the smallest accuracy degradation among evaluated methods at low bpd, with no per-token overhead.

</details>


### [201] [Graph-to-Grid (G2G): Continuous-Coordinate Feature Painting for Soccer Pass Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24040)
*Kaan Günay,Orhun Gun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文以2022世界杯53,628次传球为数据，验证"双线性散布"（painting）替代栅格化（raster）作为特征接口，可将传球选择似然提升约四分之一nat，并定位增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球稠密传球面（dense pass surfaces）需要为每个格点给出：该处传球能否到位、持球者是否会选择它、以及控球价值。现有网络（LiDAR检测器、鸟瞰感知、图气象模型）将实体特征映射到栅格时采用binning或学习的transfer，丢失了球员在格内的精确坐标；这些系统只是"采用"某种接口，却未度量接口本身的影响。

Method: 提出插值式接口（painting）：将每名球员的特征按其测量坐标双线性散布到栅格上，使传球面损失能端到端训练逐球员编码器。基于2022世界杯53,628次传球，用八折交叉验证（每折、每臂5个种子调参），并与纯栅格基线比较；另在另一提供方的7场德甲及德乙比赛上重训练。开展13项预注册研究定位增益来源，并对比原有SoccerMap与标准U-Net。

Result: painting相较同核心的纯栅格输入，将选择似然提升约四分之一nat，在八折交叉验证的每场比赛中均成立，跨五种子调参稳健，并在跨提供方重训练后保持。painting九个原始球员特征（无编码器）即贡献约四分之三的增益；学习编码器与消息传递带来较小但显著的增量。painting对原始SoccerMap和标准U-Net亦有帮助，而偏移通道、更细栅格、注意力painter及无栅格解码器均无此效果。跨提供方冻结参数时似然优势消失，注入跟踪误差会压缩该增益。

Conclusion: 双线性painting是一种更优的栅格特征接口，其增益主要来自保留球员精确坐标的原始特征散布，而非编码器复杂度；但该优势依赖跨域微调且对跟踪噪声敏感。需强调：结论仅针对观测终点预测，不适用于对假设性传球的校准评估。

Abstract: Dense pass surfaces give, for every pitch cell, whether a pass played there would arrive, whether the carrier would choose it, and what the possession would then be worth. The networks that draw them read the state as a raster of per-cell counts, losing where inside a cell each player stands. LiDAR detectors, bird's-eye-view perception and graph weather models move entity features onto a grid, binning each entity to a cell or learning the transfer. We evaluate the interpolated form: each player's features are scattered bilinearly onto the grid at the player's measured coordinates, so the surface loss trains the per-player encoder end to end. Those systems adopt an interface; this paper measures one. On 53,628 passes from the 2022 World Cup, painting improves selection likelihood over the same core fed rasters alone by about a quarter of a nat: in every match of an eight-fold cross-validation, with every arm tuned over five seeds, and after retraining on seven Bundesliga and 2. Bundesliga matches from another provider. Thirteen pre-specified studies locate the gain: painting the nine raw player features with no encoder carries three quarters of it, and the learned encoder and message passing add a smaller, resolved increment. Painting also helps the original SoccerMap and a canonical U-Net, whereas offset channels, a finer raster, an attention painter and a raster-free decoder do not. Frozen across the provider boundary the likelihood advantage is lost; injected tracking error compresses it. These results concern observed-endpoint prediction, not calibrated evaluation of hypothetical passes.

</details>


### [202] [The Undetected Damage of Quantization on Retrieval and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24322)
*Luca Zhou,Alessandro Zirilli,Daniele Solombrino,Roberto Dessì,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 量化模型在保持分类精度时，其top-1检索结果仍有14%~46%发生改变，且聚合排序指标只揭示了部分损害。作者将这一失败归因于最高两个分数之间的间隔，并据此判断量化答案是否可信及何处需要额外精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常以分类精度或聚合排序指标衡量量化模型的可靠性，但这类指标掩盖了量化对具体检索结果（尤其是top-1）造成的扰动。作者希望找到一个无需标签、可事前预测并可逐输入判断量化是否可信的度量，以指导更经济的精度分配。

Method: 作者以最高两分数之间的间隔为分析工具：当该间隔超过最大舍入误差的两倍时，top-1结果可保证在量化后幸存。该间隔无需标签即可测量，可事前预测哪些模型在量化下会失效，并逐输入判断量化答案是否与全精度答案一致。基于该诊断，检索任务中把额外位宽分配给使间隔移动最大的层，分类任务中将少数低间隔输入路由到全精度。

Result: 量化模型保持分类精度时仍改变14%~46%的top-1检索结果，聚合排序指标仅反映部分损害。大多数分类输入的间隔足以信任量化答案，而少数检索查询满足该条件。检索中针对性的额外位宽分配以约一半成本回收了额外一位收益的四分之三；分类中将少数低间隔输入路由至全精度，以极低代价恢复了大部分丢失的精度。

Conclusion: 最高两分数之间的间隔是量化可靠性的关键指标，能无需标签地预测与逐输入判断量化后top-1是否保持。该间隔在分类与检索中呈现不同分布，因而对应不同的修复策略：检索中针对性分配位宽，分类中路由低间隔输入至全精度。

Abstract: We show that a quantized model that keeps its classification accuracy still changes $14$ to $46\%$ of its top-1 retrieval results, and that aggregate ranking metrics reveal only part of this damage. We tie this failure to the gap between the two highest scores and use that gap to decide when a quantized answer can be trusted and where additional precision should be spent. We show that the top-1 result is guaranteed to survive quantization only when this gap exceeds twice the largest rounding error. In classification, scores are the logits, and the loss function pushes the correct class away from other classes, encouraging this gap. In retrieval, scores are query-document scores, and nothing separates the top-1 item from the second. This gap can be measured without labels. Before deployment, it predicts which models will break under quantization, and at deployment time it tells, per input, whether the quantized answer still matches the full-precision answer. Most classification inputs have a gap wide enough to trust the quantized answer, but few retrieval queries do. That gap motivates a different fix in each task. In retrieval, spending extra bit-width on the layers whose quantization moves the gap most recovers up to three-quarters of an extra bit's benefit for half its cost. In classification, routing the few low-gap inputs to full precision recovers most of the lost accuracy at a fraction of the cost.

</details>


### [203] [Q-DEQ: Discrete Solving and Quantization for Deep Equilibrium Models in Time Series Forecasting under Edge Deployment Coding Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24042)
*Ruotong Yang,Hongdong Zhu,Qi Gao,Yin Ma,Hai Wei,Kai Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-DEQ 将深度平衡模型前向求解中的局部更新表述为离散优化问题，通过二进制编码构造 QUBO 并由 SA 或 CIM 求解，结合 W8A8 量化，在保持预测精度的同时大幅压缩参数量与存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署要求预测模型具备紧凑的参数存储与低位表示。DEQ 通过重复应用共享层获得隐式深度，降低了显式层堆叠的参数成本，但其常用的 Anderson 求解器在连续实数域中搜索更新系数，未必适配低位/离散化的部署需求。

Method: 提出 Q-DEQ：将 DEQ 前向求解中的局部更新建模为离散优化问题。候选方向由当前状态与迭代历史构造，使用局部二次残差模型评估其组合；对方向系数进行二进制编码，得到可用模拟退火（SA）或相干伊辛机（CIM）求解的 QUBO 问题。不动点求解后，通过一次 re-forward pass 对共享层权重与激活施加 W8A8 伪量化。

Result: 在 iTransformer 骨干与五个多变量时间序列预测数据集上评估：相对显式多层基线的 MSE 差异范围为 -1.16% 至 +2.90%，其中两个数据集 MSE 更低。DEQ 参数共享使参数量降低 1.80×–3.82×；结合 W8A8，静态权重存储降低 4.3×–12.8×。基于 CPU 的 SA 与 Kaiwu CIM 物理后端求解的局部 QUBO 问题产生高度一致的下游预测结果。

Conclusion: 局部离散求解可作为 DEQ 时间序列预测的可行组件，并为通过不同组合优化后端执行不动点更新提供了路径。

Abstract: Edge deployment motivates forecasting models with compact parameter storage and low-bit representations. Deep equilibrium models (DEQs) obtain implicit depth by repeatedly applying a shared layer, reducing the parameter cost of explicit layer stacking. Their usual Anderson solver, however, searches for update coefficients in the continuous real domain. We propose Q-DEQ, which formulates local updates in DEQ forward solving as discrete optimization problems. Candidate directions are constructed from the current state and iteration history, and a local quadratic residual model is used to evaluate their combinations. Binary encoding of the direction coefficients yields a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem that can be solved by simulated annealing (SA) or a coherent Ising machine (CIM). After fixed-point solving, a re-forward pass applies W8A8 fake quantization to the shared layer's weights and activations. We evaluate Q-DEQ with an iTransformer backbone on five multivariate time series forecasting datasets. Relative MSE differences from the explicit multi-layer baseline range from $-1.16\%$ to $+2.90\%$, with lower MSE on two datasets. DEQ parameter sharing reduces parameter counts by factors of $1.80\times$--$3.82\times$; combined with W8A8, static weight storage is reduced by factors of $4.3\times$--$12.8\times$. Local QUBO problems solved using CPU-based SA and the Kaiwu CIM physical backend produce closely matching downstream forecasts. These results establish local discrete solving as a viable component of DEQ time series forecasting and provide a route for executing fixed-point updates through different combinatorial optimization backends.

</details>


### [204] [Information-Time Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24380)
*Yongcheng Zeng,Xinyu Cui,Yan Song,Guoqing Liu,Hongsheng Xin,Kaike Zhang,Cheng Deng,Kun Zhan,Jian Ying,Jian Zhao,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出InfoPPO，用信息密度而非token数量重参数化MDP的时间推进，统一时间信用传播与策略更新的状态依赖结构，并在Qwen3上验证其推理性能与稳定性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法以逐token生成为时间推进单位，但自回归轨迹中的信息流高度不均匀，导致时间信用分配与折扣机制在长序列推理中失效（终端监督被过度衰减）。需要一个更符合信息分布的时间参数化方式。

Method: 以信息密度（state-wise information density）替代原始token计数重参数化时间推进，形成信息时间MDP；由此导出时间信用传播与策略更新的共同状态依赖结构；据此实现自适应裁剪（将裁剪阈值按token位置对应状态的信息密度调整），并扩展到信息时间下的performance-difference与policy-improvement分析。

Result: 在五个竞赛级数学推理基准上，基于Qwen3模型相对竞争基线取得一致性提升；在非平凡折扣设置下保持稳定的准确率与回复长度，而token-time PPO则出现退化。

Conclusion: 信息密度作为时间参数化可恢复长期推理中折扣机制的有效性，兼顾有效horizon收缩与终端监督保留，并提供理论支撑与实用的自适应更新机制。

Abstract: RLVR has substantially improved the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. However, existing methods typically parameterize temporal progression in the Markov Decision Process by token-by-token generation, despite the highly non-uniform information flow along autoregressive trajectories. In this paper, we propose InfoPPO, which reparameterizes temporal progression using information density rather than raw token count. This reparameterization induces a common state-dependent structure for both temporal credit propagation and policy updates. InfoPPO restores the effectiveness of non-trivial discounting in long-horizon reasoning, retaining effective-horizon contraction while avoiding excessive attenuation of terminal supervision over long token sequences. Moreover, the information-time policy-improvement analysis naturally leads to a state-dependent update constraint, which we implement through adaptive clipping. By adapting the clipping threshold at each token position to the information density of its corresponding state, this mechanism enables more targeted policy updates while preserving proximal control. Theoretically, we extend performance-difference and policy-improvement analyses to the information-time MDP, deriving a policy-improvement lower bound when policy changes are regulated by information density. We further connect the general information-time analysis to practical LLM policy optimization by relating state-wise information density to local policy movement, while also providing theoretical grounding for the adaptive update mechanism. Experiments on Qwen3 models demonstrate consistent gains over competitive baselines across five challenging competition-style mathematical reasoning benchmarks. InfoPPO also maintains stable accuracy and response length across non-trivial discount settings under which token-time PPO deteriorates.

</details>


### [205] [Artificial Structure Function Search: Preserving Artificial Functional Connectivity for Structured Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24401)
*Mindula Illeperuma,Rafael Pina,Charuka Herath,Sharmarke A. Gabayre,Varuna De Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化剪枝框架ASF-S及其候选选择准则PGI，借鉴大脑结构-功能关系，通过尊重输出层拓扑组织定义AFC，实现70%参数压缩且剪枝层无需重训即可恢复基线精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在资源受限设备上部署计算成本高；现有主流剪枝方法依赖不透明的启发式或基于权重的准则，无法揭示网络中的结构依赖关系。

Method: 提出结构化剪枝框架ASF-S，其中使用新型剪枝候选选择准则PGI（Principle Gradient Importance，受大脑结构-功能关系启发）；通过保证剪枝后模型结构尊重输出层的拓扑组织，为人工神经网络定义AFC（Artificial Functional Connectivity，人工功能连接）。

Result: 以PGI作为选择准则、ASF-S作为剪枝框架，与近期基准方法对比，模型变体实现70%参数压缩，且剪枝层无需重训练即可恢复基线精度。AFC为"通过精细的剪枝候选选择准则可找到准确的小型网络"提供证据。

Conclusion: ASF-S通过引入生物学启发的候选选择准则和人工功能连接概念，证明了谨慎的剪枝候选选择能导向高精度的小型网络，为结构化剪枝提供了兼具可解释性与高效性的新范式。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a model compression technique that is used to reduce the computational cost of deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained devices. Popular methods of pruning rely on opaque heuristics or weight-based criteria that give no indication as to the structural dependencies in the network. To address these limitations we present Artificial Structure Function Search (ASF-S): a novel structured pruning framework. ASF-S utilizes Principle Gradient Importance (PGI): a novel prune-candidate selection criteria that is inspired by structure-function relationships in the brain. By ensuring the pruned structure of the model respects topographical organization of the output layer, we define Artificial Functional Connectivity (AFC) for artificial neural networks. AFC provides evidence to demonstrate that accurate smaller networks can be found using careful prune candidate selection criteria. We present results for PGI as a selection criterion and for ASF-S as a pruning framework against recent benchmarks, demonstrating that our method yields model variants with 70\% parameter reduction, that can recover baseline accuracy without re-training the pruned layers.

</details>


### [206] [SPeaR: Test-Time Adaptation with Steering Primitives for Realigning Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24111)
*Muhammad Sudipto Siam Dip,Ali Etemad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPeaR 冻结预训练网络，在阶段边界插入轻量可学习模块以校准中间表征，仅用测试流优化，在多个损坏数据集上以极少量参数达到或超越大规模参数适配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时适配（TTA）通过更新预训练模型参数来应对分布偏移，这限制了可适配的对象以及网络中适配发生的位置。作者希望在不改动预训练权重的前提下实现更灵活、更有效的适配。

Method: 将预训练网络保持冻结，改为"引导"其中间表征。提出 SPeaR（Steering Primitive for Realigning Representations），在阶段边界插入轻量可学习模块，直接从测试流优化，无需源数据与监督预热。每个原语采用门控目标（仅在适配有益时降低不确定性）、多样性正则项（防止坍塌）与多深度锚点（稳定适配）进行优化。

Result: 引导早期表征最为有效；同一原语可在卷积与 Transformer 架构间迁移。在 CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C 与 ImageNet-C 上，SPeaR 一致地达到或超越参数量高出数个数量级的适配方法，在广泛批大小范围内保持稳健，并在持续适配中保留源域性能。

Conclusion: 通过冻结主干、引导中间表征的范式，TTA 可用极少参数实现强适配性、跨架构迁移性与稳定性，为分布偏移下的模型适配提供了新的高效方向。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) addresses distribution shift using only unlabeled test data. Existing methods typically adapt pretrained models by updating their parameters, limiting both what is adapted and where adaptation can occur within the network. We instead keep the pretrained network frozen and steer its intermediate representations. We introduce SPeaR (Steering Primitive for Realigning Representations), which inserts lightweight learnable modules at stage boundaries and optimizes them directly from the test stream, requiring neither source data nor supervised warm-up. Each primitive is optimized using a gated objective that reduces uncertainty only when adaptation is beneficial, along with a diversity regularizer to prevent collapse, and a multi-depth anchor to stabilize adaptation. We show that steering early representations is the most effective strategy, and that the same primitive transfers across convolutional and Transformer architectures. Across CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-C, SPeaR consistently matches or outperforms methods that adapt orders of magnitude more parameters, remains robust across a wide range of batch sizes, and preserves source-domain performance during continual adaptation.

</details>


### [207] [ARM: Attention with Routed-Memory for Learnable Sparse Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24417)
*Qiuhao Zeng,Jerry Huang,Peng Lu,Ruiyi Fang,Gezheng Xu,Zihao Jing,Yufei Cui,Charles Ling,Gang Niu,Boyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Attention with Routed Memory (ARM)，一种可微、固定大小的分层路由 KV 缓存结构，用 Gumbel-Softmax 软选择内存槽并以 sigmoid 门控软更新，取代硬性驱逐，并训练策略在推理时动态分配内存量，在短/长上下文任务上兼顾性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型长上下文推理受限于必要的 KV 缓存机制；现有的选择性 token 驱逐与剪枝虽能缓解缓存增长问题，但往往丢弃关键信息，造成信息损失。

Method: 提出 ARM（Attention with Routed Memory）：一种新的 KV 缓存结构，构建全可微、固定大小的内存系统，组织为分层路由器；通过 Gumbel-Softmax 学习选择内存槽，并用 sigmoid 门控更新，软性融合新信息与已存信息，避免硬驱逐；进一步训练策略，在推理时动态选择不同规模的内存访问，以适应简单上下文与需深度推理的输入。

Result: 在标准常识与长上下文推理基准上，ARM 相比固定 KV 缓存方法取得了更优的性能与效率，同时在内存占用与生成延迟方面保持高效、可扩展。

Conclusion: ARM 通过可微、固定大小的路由内存机制替代硬驱逐，既减少信息损失，又能按输入难度自适应分配内存，从而在短、长上下文场景中实现更可扩展且有效的检索。

Abstract: Despite advances in long-context inference, large language models (LLMs) remain fundamentally limited by the key-value (KV) caching mechanisms that are necessary for stable computation. Techniques such as selective token eviction and pruning have vastly mitigated these issues, but often discard core information to manage the growing cache. In this paper, we propose Attention with Routed Memory (ARM) a novel KV caching structure that introduces a fully differentiable, fixed-size memory system organized as a hierarchical router. Via a Gumbel-Softmax, ARM learns to select memory slots and perform sigmoid-gated updates that softly combine new and stored information, avoiding hard eviction and reducing information loss. By further training a policy to dynamically select varying amounts of memory at inference, ARM adapts its accesses for both simple contexts and inputs that require deeper reasoning, enabling more scalable and effective retrieval on both short- and long-contexts. Experimental results on standard commonsense and long-context reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that ARM achieves superior performance and efficiency compared to fixed KV-caching approaches, while remaining efficient and scalable in terms of both memory and generation latency.

</details>


### [208] [PAC-Bayesian Meta-Learning for Few-Shot Identification of Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24117)
*Chenfeng Huang,George Michailidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PBML-LTI，一个面向少样本LTI系统辨识的PAC-Bayes元学习框架，学习可迁移先验并进行贝叶斯自适应，通过鞅PAC-Bayes分析处理时序依赖，给出支持-查询预测风险界及拟合-KL训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短轨迹、噪声大或高维场景下LTI系统辨识困难；传统系统辨识将各系统独立处理，无法利用相关系统间的共享结构。同时，LTI轨迹违反i.i.d.假设，使多数PAC-Bayes元学习理论分析失效。

Method: 将每个任务视为未知LTI系统，用训练轨迹学习转移矩阵上的数据依赖先验；对新系统在少样本下做贝叶斯自适应得到任务特定后验。针对时序依赖引入鞅PAC-Bayes分析，推导支持-查询预测风险界，据此设计拟合-KL元训练目标，并给出转移矩阵恢复与多步轨迹预测的推论。

Result: 得到依赖轨迹损失下的支持-查询预测风险界，界中显式刻画经验拟合、后验复杂度与先验质量的作用；进一步推导转移矩阵恢复和多步轨迹预测的有限样本保证，实现不确定性感知的元辨识。

Conclusion: PBML-LTI在保留任务异质性的同时实现跨系统迁移，配合鞅PAC-Bayes分析为序列依赖下的少样本LTI辨识提供准确估计、原则性不确定性量化及有限样本理论保证。

Abstract: Identifying linear time-invariant (LTI) dynamical systems is challenging when trajectories are short, noisy, or high-dimensional. Traditional system identification typically treats each system independently and cannot exploit shared structure across related systems. We propose PBML-LTI, a PAC-Bayesian meta-learning framework for few-shot LTI system identification that learns a transferable prior over task-specific dynamics while preserving task heterogeneity. Each task corresponds to an unknown LTI system, and the meta-learner uses training trajectories to learn a data-dependent prior over transition matrices. For a new system with limited data, PBML-LTI performs Bayesian adaptation under this prior to obtain a task-specific posterior, providing accurate estimates and principled uncertainty quantification.
  A key challenge is temporal dependence, since LTI trajectories violate the i.i.d. assumptions underlying most PAC-Bayes meta-learning analyses. We address this with a martingale PAC-Bayes analysis for dependent trajectory losses and derive a support-query predictive-risk bound that motivates a fit-KL meta-training objective. The bound clarifies the roles of empirical fit, posterior complexity, and prior quality in few-shot adaptation under sequential dependence. We further derive corollaries for transition-matrix recovery and multi-step trajectory prediction, connecting uncertainty-aware meta-identification with finite-sample guarantees for dependent dynamical data.

</details>


### [209] [WPBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Wind Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24444)
*Yuhan Zhu,Jilin Hu,Xinying Cai,Yingshan Li,Li Ma,Xiangfei Qiu Linsen Li,Kai Zhang,Yao Fu,Weihao Jiang,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WPBench，一个面向风电功率预测的综合、公平、可扩展基准，涵盖26个公开数据集、19个代表性模型，并提供多维度评估与结构化诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电功率预测对电力系统调度、可再生能源并网和电力市场运行至关重要，而该领域进展依赖于全面实证的基准测试。现有基准存在四方面不足：1）风电场景覆盖有限（风机规模、变量组成、空间结构）；2）预测模型族覆盖不完整；3）评估指标与风电需求不匹配；4）缺乏超越单一时间模式的结构感知诊断。

Method: 提出WPBench基准：整合26个公开数据集，按风机规模和变量组成组织，覆盖单风机、多风机、单变量和多变量场景；在统一的数据处理、训练和评估协议下，对19个代表性模型进行基准测试，涵盖传统方法、深度时序模型、时空模型和基础模型；除逐点误差外，还评估预测曲线保真度和计算效率，并从时间、变量依赖、空间依赖三个视角提供结构化诊断。

Result: WPBench在统一协议下完成对19个模型、26个数据集的系统评测，覆盖多类风电场景，并在逐点误差之外提供曲线保真度、计算效率及时间/变量/空间结构化诊断结果。

Conclusion: WPBench支持跨多样风电场景的系统性模型比较，并为未来研究提供可复用的平台。

Abstract: Accurate, reliable, and deployable wind power forecasting is critical for power system dispatch, renewable energy integration, and electricity market operations. Progress in this field hinges on the ability to empirically and comprehensively benchmark forecasting methods. Yet existing benchmarks fall short of supporting systematic evaluation in four key aspects: 1) limited coverage of wind power scenarios across turbine scale, variable composition, and spatial structure; 2) incomplete coverage of forecasting model families; 3) evaluation metrics misaligned with wind power requirements; and 4) limited structure-aware diagnostics beyond individual temporal patterns. To address these limitations, we propose WPBench, a comprehensive, fair, and extensible benchmark for wind power forecasting. WPBench integrates 26 public datasets organized by turbine scale and variable composition, spanning single-turbine, multi-turbine, univariate, and multivariate settings. Under unified processing, training, and evaluation protocols, it benchmarks 19 representative models covering traditional methods, deep temporal models, spatio-temporal models, and foundation models. Beyond point-wise errors, WPBench assesses forecast-curve fidelity and computational efficiency, and delivers structure-aware diagnostics across temporal, variable-dependency, and spatial-dependency perspectives. Together, these capabilities enable systematic model comparison across diverse wind scenarios and provide a reusable platform for future research.

</details>


### [210] [CLOOPD: Closing the Learner Loop in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24141)
*Keye Zheng,Hanyu Li,Zhan Cheng,Yuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLOOPD 将教师信号获取与学生实现解耦，在冻结已评分批次后多次执行 actor 更新并测量实现度，在相近教师 token 预算下将宏准确率从 15.41 提升至 17.78（两次）与 19.36（三次）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中每个新批次需付出两次代价：学生生成轨迹、更强教师打分。现有方法聚焦于改进"哪些轨迹被评分"和"教师信号如何构造"，却通常只用一次 actor 更新来消费该信号，导致教师 token 的利用效率不足。

Method: 提出闭环框架 CLOOPD，将教师信号获取与学生侧实现分离：在 KL 包络内选择自适应 α 路径点，冻结已评分的批次及其优势值，每次 actor 更新后重新前向学生以度量实现情况，并在独立 token 预算下分配 actor 计算。框架包含确定性的两趟/三趟策略、按 token 计价的 CLOOPD-TPMR，以及预算匹配对照。

Result: 在 8-H20 节点上的六次 300 步运行中，所有 CLOOPD 策略在相近教师 token 规模下均优于单趟 TOP-D 基线：宏准确率由 15.41 提升至 CLOOPD-Fixed2 的 17.78、CLOOPD-Fixed3 的 19.36；第 100 步时 CLOOPD-Fixed3 达 15.35，接近 TOP-D 第 300 步水平，同时教师评分 token 减少 67.2%、GPU 小时减少 28.0%。早期 8-A100 消融显示自适应 α 消除了观察到的信任包络违规，第三趟带来额外余量。

Conclusion: CLOOPD 将"学生从教师评分 token 中学习到何种程度"纳入可预算的框架设计维度，表明在不增加教师 token 成本的前提下，通过闭环分配 actor 计算可显著提升蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) pays twice for each fresh batch: the student generates trajectories and a stronger teacher scores them. Existing methods improve which trajectories are scored and how the teacher signal is constructed, but usually consume it with one actor update. We introduce CLOOPD, a closed-loop framework separating teacher-signal acquisition from student-side realization. CLOOPD selects an adaptive $α$ waypoint inside a KL envelope, freezes the scored batch and its advantages, re-forwards the student after each actor pass, measures realization, and allocates actor work under a separate token budget. The framework includes deterministic two- and three-pass policies, token-priced CLOOPD-TPMR, and a budget-matched control. Across six 300-step runs on an 8-H20 node, every CLOOPD policy improves the one-pass TOP-D anchor at comparable teacher-token scale: macro accuracy rises from 15.41 to 17.78 with CLOOPD-Fixed2 and 19.36 with CLOOPD-Fixed3. At step 100, CLOOPD-Fixed3 reaches 15.35, nearly matching TOP-D at step 300 while using 67.2% fewer teacher-scored tokens and 28.0% fewer GPU-hours. Earlier 8-A100 ablations show adaptive $α$ eliminates observed trust-envelope violations; a third pass adds headroom. These results position CLOOPD as a framework for budgeting how fully students learn from teacher-scored tokens.

</details>


### [211] [Lifted Bellman Linear Programming for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24489)
*Hyukjun Yang,Jongchan Park,Narim Jeong,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lifted Bellman Linear Program（LBLP），将 Bellman 最优性的线性规划刻画提升到联合 (Q,V) 空间，使约束仅涉及数据集内状态-动作对；其松弛形式 ALBUM 用铰链惩罚与停止梯度实现，无需目标网络或 EMA，在 OGBench 上以最少参数和最低显存达到与 FQL 相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线 RL 通常依赖对 bootstrap 目标做回归、并用 EMA 目标网络稳定训练；多步目标包含行为策略动作，需要 off-policy 修正。作者希望避免这些复杂性（目标网络、EMA、off-policy 修正、动作分块），改为直接在数据集内的样本上施加 Bellman 最优性约束。

Method: 1) 构造 LBLP：将 Bellman 最优性的 LP 形式提升到 (Q,V) 联合空间，使每条约束仅含数据集中的状态-动作对；2) 证明其唯一极小元为样本内最优对，且沿数据集轨迹的 K 步片段约束不改变该极小元（对任意 rollout 策略与步长成立）；3) 在确定性动力学下，该极小元位于数据集最优回报与最优值之间；4) 将约束松弛为 hinge 惩罚，在表格情形下当惩罚系数超过有限阈值时解不变；5) 用神经网络实现 ALBUM，对 K 步 rollout 目标做 stop-gradient 分离，目标函数不含对 bootstrap 目标的平方回归，因此无需目标网络与 EMA；6) 理论上证明确定性动力学下 LBLP 解在某个与 γ、K 无关的系数条件下是 detached 更新的驻点。

Result: 在 OGBench 上，ALBUM 使用单一 critic 与高斯策略，平均性能与 FQL 相当，并与近期动作分块方法可比；同时在所有对比方法中参数量最少、峰值 GPU 显存最低。

Conclusion: 通过将 Bellman 最优性以不等式约束形式直接施加于样本内数据，LBLP/ALBUM 提供了一条无需目标网络、EMA 与 off-policy 修正的离线 RL 路径，且数据集轨迹的折扣回报可直接作为下界使用；实验表明其在性能与资源效率之间取得了良好折中。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) typically trains a critic by minimizing a regression loss against bootstrapped value targets stabilized by target networks with exponential moving average (EMA) updates. Multi-step targets incorporate behavior-policy actions and therefore require off-policy correction. We instead impose in-sample Bellman optimality on the critic through inequality constraints. We formulate the Lifted Bellman Linear Program (LBLP), which lifts the linear programming characterization of Bellman optimality to the joint $(Q,V)$ space so that every constraint involves only state-action pairs in the dataset. Its unique minimizer is the in-sample optimal pair, and constraints along $K$-step segments of dataset trajectories leave this minimizer unchanged for any rollout policy and horizon. Under deterministic dynamics, this minimizer lies between the best dataset return and the optimal value. Relaxing the constraints into hinge penalties recovers the same solution above a finite penalty coefficient in the tabular case. Approximate Lifted Bellman Unconstrained Minimization (ALBUM) implements this relaxation with neural networks and detaches the $K$-step rollout targets by stop gradient. Its objective contains no squared regression onto bootstrapped targets, so it can be trained without target networks or EMA updates. Under deterministic dynamics, the LBLP solution is a stationary point of the detached update under a coefficient condition independent of $γ$ and $K$, and the inequality constraints allow discounted returns along dataset trajectories to serve as lower bounds without off-policy correction or action chunking. On OGBench, ALBUM uses a single critic with a Gaussian policy, matches the average performance of FQL, and is comparable to recent action-chunking methods, while using the fewest parameters and the least peak GPU memory among all compared methods.

</details>


### [212] [Luck Is Not Skill: When Do Paired Rollouts Help Group-Relative RL of LLM Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24144)
*Nazmus Sakib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究成对rollout（组内共享event-keyed噪声调度）对组相对强化学习的影响，发现配对消除奖励对比方差中的组间分量但未必降低梯度方差，实验显示在工具故障下提升最终测试成功率但未达预注册学习曲线标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习通过比较同一prompt的多次rollout来估计优势，但独立的环境噪声会干扰这种比较，使奖励对比和梯度估计带有额外方差，因此需要探究配对rollout能否降低方差并带来学习收益。

Method: 研究paired rollouts：组内共享event-keyed噪声调度，同时保持每个rollout的边缘分布不变。针对单边评分器噪声推导方差下降的精确条件，并构造奖励对比改善但梯度方差上升的反例。进行对照实验，训练2B工具使用智能体，设置工具故障与评分器翻转两类噪声、每种设计三个随机种子，并采用预注册协议（含已披露的先前试点）。另在来自两条故障训练轨迹的八个不同checkpoint上做梯度探针。

Result: 工具故障下配对使最终含噪测试成功率平均提升+5.1个百分点，三个种子差异均为正，但未满足预注册的学习曲线标准；评分器翻转下同样未达标，验证AUC差异为+0.003（95%区间[-0.029,+0.033]）。梯度探针发现两类噪声下中心化协方差迹均更低：评分器翻转为21%至30%，工具故障为40%至63%。

Conclusion: 有限样本测量支持配对降低估计器方差的机制，但不足以确立普遍的学习速度收益；研究结果区分了改善奖励对比、降低估计器方差与改善学习这三件不同的事。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning compares rollouts of the same prompt, but independent environment noise can obscure these comparisons. We study paired rollouts, which share an event-keyed noise schedule within each group while preserving each rollout's marginal distribution. Pairing removes the between-schedule component of reward-contrast variance, but need not reduce gradient variance. For one-sided grader noise, we derive an exact condition for reduction and give a counterexample in which reward contrasts improve while gradient variance increases. A controlled study trains a 2B tool-use agent under tool faults and grader flips, with three seeds per design. The protocol was registered with a disclosed, previously completed pilot. Under tool faults, pairing improves final noisy-test success by +5.1 percentage points on average, with all three seed differences positive, but misses the registered learning-curve criterion. The criterion is also missed under grader flips: the validation-AUC difference is +0.003 (95% interval [-0.029, +0.033]). A gradient probe on eight distinct checkpoints from two fault-trained trajectories finds lower mean-centered covariance traces under both noise types: 21 to 30% for grader flips and 40 to 63% for tool faults. These finite-sample measurements support the variance mechanism without establishing a general learning-speed benefit. The results distinguish improving reward comparisons, reducing estimator variance, and improving learning.

</details>


### [213] [On Emergent Capabilities and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24504)
*Luca Zhou,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型合并（权重算术）对涌现能力的传递规律与对训练能力不同：父母均具备的涌现能力可被保留；父母均不具备的涌现能力无法通过合并超加性产生；仅单亲具备的涌现能力被稀释得比伴随的训练能力更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调检查点与适配器大量涌入公共仓库，模型合并成为最常用的权重算术操作，用于低成本组装能力。但该操作对"涌现能力"——从未作为显式训练目标的行为——的影响尚不明确，作者据此提出问题：合并对涌现能力做了什么。

Method: 在两个独立测试平台（激活预言机、涌现失配模型）上，跨三个模型家族，系统考察权重混合范围内涌现能力的保留、产生与稀释情况，并与伴随的训练能力作对比。

Result: 呈现三重结论：一、当父母双方均携带某涌现能力时，合并可在整个混合范围内保留其大部分表现（如两个失配检查点合并后仍保留大部分广泛失配）；二、合并不具有超加性，无法创造出父母双方均不具备的涌现能力（两个单任务预言机的任何加权合并都达不到联合训练预言机的审计能力）；三、当仅单亲具备该涌现能力时，合并对其稀释速度快于对伴随训练能力的稀释速度，且在多数设置下差距显著。

Conclusion: 制品的涌现行为并不像其训练能力那样可组合，即涌现能力与训练能力在权重合并下遵循不同的传递与衰减规律。

Abstract: Fine-tuned checkpoints and adapters now fill public repositories, and the most common operation applied to these artifacts is model merging: arithmetic on their weights that assembles capabilities cheaply. We ask what this operation does to emergent capabilities: behaviors an artifact carries that were never an explicit training target. Studying two independent testbeds (activation oracles and emergent-misaligned models) across three model families, we find that the answer is threefold. First, merging preserves an emergent capability that both parents carry: merging two misaligned checkpoints retains most of their broad misalignment across the whole mixing range. Second, merging cannot create an emergent capability that is superadditive in its parents: no weighted merge of two single-task oracles reaches the jointly-trained oracle's auditing ability. Third, when only one parent carries the capability, merging dilutes it faster than the trained capability that accompanies it: the gap is significant in most settings. In short, emergent behaviors of an artifact do not compose the way its trained capability does.

</details>


### [214] [Mind or Message? Auditing Theory of Mind in Multi-Agent Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24146)
*Cong Li,Cheng Chen,Thomas Fung,Alex Rossi,Yi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过已知真实偏好与帕累托前沿的多议题谈判模拟，检验语言模型智能体是否真正建模伙伴心智。结果显示智能体社交表现流畅但经济效率极低，其推断主要来自自我中心投射而非对伙伴的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体越来越多用于模拟社会互动，其对话记录看似表明智能体相互理解。作者要检验这种表面理解究竟源于对伙伴心智的建模，还是仅基于伙伴话语的表面记录。

Method: 构建40个多议题谈判，隐藏偏好权重与完整帕累托前沿均为已知。两个模型家族进行160组双人谈判，所有对话记录在测量前冻结。随后用2880次反事实探测，在字节级固定证据的情况下逐一变动读者自身收益、伙伴语气、身份标签与递归顺序等因素。

Result: 智能体社交流畅但经济表现差：96.2%的双人组达成协议且无协议失败，但仅0.7%的协议落在帕累托前沿，未获取20.5%的联合价值，76.6%的交易错过利益完全一致的关键议题；在帕累托前沿与该一致议题上，随机抽取双方均可接受的方案表现相当。仅更换读者自身收益表即令推断的首要优先级变动15.0个百分点，表明为自我中心投射而非推断；语气改写带来5.3个百分点变动，身份标签为0.0。智能体预测伙伴对自身的看法有72.5%正确率，而该伙伴的信念本身仅51.2%正确。

Conclusion: 智能体对对话的追踪远好于对对话背后心智的追踪，其表面社会理解依赖话语表面记录与自我中心投射，而非伙伴心智模型。

Abstract: Language model agents are increasingly used to simulate social interaction, and the resulting transcripts read as though the agents understand one another. We ask whether that appearance rests on a model of the partner's mind or on the surface record of what the partner said. We build a social simulation in which both questions have exact answers: 40 multi-issue negotiations whose hidden preference weights and whose full Pareto frontier are known by construction. Two model families negotiate across 160 dyads, every transcript is frozen before any measurement, and 2880 counterfactual probes then hold the evidence byte identical while moving one factor at a time: the reader's own stake, the partner's tone, an identity label, and the order of recursion. The agents are socially fluent and economically poor. They reach agreement in 96.2% of dyads with 0 protocol failures, yet only 0.7% of deals land on the Pareto frontier, they leave 20.5% of the available joint value unclaimed, and they miss the one issue on which their interests are perfectly aligned in 76.6% of deals; on the frontier and on that aligned issue, a package drawn at random from the set both sides would accept does as well. The probes locate the failure. Swapping only the reader's own payoff sheet, while the partner's words and offers stay identical, moves the inferred top priority by 15.0 percentage points, which is egocentric projection rather than inference, while a tone rewrite moves it by 5.3 percentage points and an identity label by 0.0. Most tellingly, an agent predicts what its partner believes about it 72.5% of the time while that partner's belief is itself correct only 51.2% of the time: the agents track the conversation far better than they track the mind behind it.

</details>


### [215] [$t_0$: A Time-Series Foundation Model for Forecasting with Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24559)
*Lucas Meyer,Claudio Sole,Huikan Xiang,Nicolas Li,Lucas Franceschino,Arnau Quera-Bofarull,Maarten P. Scholl,Joachim Fainberg,Geoffrey Négiar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出开源多变量上下文预测基础模型家族t0，发布102M的t0-alpha与256M的t0-beta，采用时间/变量交替注意力与分位数概率预测，在GIFT-Eval、fev-bench等基准上接近最优零样本TSFM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型多面向单变量或需任务特定重训练，难以在零样本条件下同时利用目标历史、历史协变量与已知未来协变量进行多变量概率预测，因此需要构建通用且开源的多变量上下文预测模型。设计动机还在于验证已知未来协变量对预测技能的增益。

Method: 构建t0系列开源权重基础模型（t0-alpha 102M、t0-beta 256M），无需任务特定重训练即可基于目标历史、过去协变量和已知未来协变量进行条件预测。Transformer层在时间维与变量维交替施加注意力；通过分位数预测输出概率预测。预训练数据包括精选公开数据与合成的、刻意包含协变量到目标依赖关系的生成器数据族。分析涵盖校准性、长时程滚动策略与缺失数据鲁棒性。

Result: GIFT-Eval上t0-alpha聚合CRPS为0.4941；t0-beta CRPS 0.4738、MASE 0.6865，两项均列第三，距最佳零样本TSFM在4.0%以内。fev-bench技能分分别为42.2与46.7，后者第三、落后榜首2.0分。已知未来协变量在30个任务上使t0-alpha技能提升6.3个百分点。维多利亚电力需求基准上t0-beta在近一年上下文下属最准确模型之列；独立Macrocosm对29个月ERCOT小时电价评估中，两者将滞后价格基线MAE降低38%。

Conclusion: t0证明了在多变量上下文、含已知未来协变量的零样本概率预测上可达到接近最优TSFM的性能，且开源权重与统一架构具备通用性；已知未来协变量带来显著增益，模型在长时程与缺失数据场景下表现稳健。

Abstract: We present $t_0$, a family of open-weights foundation models for forecasting with multivariate context. We release its first two members: $\texttt{t0-alpha}$ and $\texttt{t0-beta}$, respectively 102M and 256M parameters. Both condition their forecasts on target history, past covariates, and known-future covariates, without task-specific retraining. Their transformer layers alternate attention along time and across variates. They produce probabilistic forecasts through quantile predictions. Pretraining combines curated public data with synthetic generator families constructed to contain covariate-to-target dependencies. On GIFT-Eval, $\texttt{t0-alpha}$ reaches an aggregate CRPS of 0.4941, and $\texttt{t0-beta}$ a CRPS of 0.4738 and a MASE of 0.6865, third on both and within 4.0% of the best zero-shot TSFM. On fev-bench they score 42.2 and 46.7 in skill, the latter third again and 2.0 points behind the leader. We analyze $\texttt{t0-alpha}$ in depth. Known-future covariates raise its skill by 6.3 percentage points across 30 tasks. The report also examines its calibration, its rollout strategy on long horizons, and its robustness to missing data. On the Victoria electricity-demand benchmark, $\texttt{t0-beta}$ is among the most accurate models with a context of nearly a year. In an independent Macrocosm evaluation of hourly ERCOT prices over 29 months, both cut the MAE of the lagged-price baseline by 38%.

</details>


### [216] [Acceptance-Aware Draft Model Training for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24150)
*Tianhua Xia,Mugilan Ganesan,Yifei Feng,Haiyu Wang,Maximilian Egger,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向接受长度的训练损失（EAL、WTV），直接优化推测解码的期望接受长度，并可按贪心/采样解码模式定制，还可结合GRPO强化学习阶段，实验表明显著优于KL类分布匹配目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码（speculative decoding）的加速比主要由接受长度决定，但现有草稿模型训练方法主要优化交叉熵或KL散度作为代理目标。这类目标只鼓励分布匹配，并不直接优化接受长度；同时贪心验证与采样解码的接受机制不同，统一代理目标难以适配。

Method: 提出直接优化推测窗口内期望接受token数的接受长度感知损失：1）针对贪心验证，推导期望接受长度（EAL）损失，显式最大化期望接受长度；2）针对采样解码，提出窗口总变差（WTV）损失，在考虑序列接受依赖的前提下优化温度缩放后草稿分布与目标分布的重叠；3）两种目标可进一步结合以模拟接受长度为奖励的组相对强化学习阶段（GRPO）。

Result: 在不同目标模型与草稿模型、任务和解码设置下，所提损失相对KL类训练一致提升接受长度；WTV在采样解码下增益尤为显著，EAL则更契合贪心验证。

Conclusion: 直接优化接受目标、并按解码模式定制损失，比传统分布匹配目标更有效，为推测解码草稿模型训练提供了更贴合推理加速目标的方向。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a lightweight draft model to generate multiple candidate tokens that are verified by the target model in a single forward pass. Its speedup is largely determined by the acceptance length, yet existing draft-model training methods mainly optimize cross-entropy or Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence as proxies. These objectives encourage distribution matching but do not directly optimize acceptance length, and the acceptance mechanism also differs between greedy and sampling-based decoding.
  In this work, we propose acceptance-length-aware training losses that directly optimize the expected number of accepted tokens within a speculative window. For greedy verification, we derive an expected accepted length (EAL) loss that explicitly maximizes expected acceptance length. For sampling-based decoding, we introduce a window total variation (WTV) loss that optimizes the overlap between temperature-scaled draft and target distributions while accounting for sequential acceptance dependencies. Both objectives can be further combined with a group-relative reinforcement learning stage (GRPO) using simulated acceptance length as the reward.
  Experiments across different target and draft models, tasks, and decoding settings show that our losses consistently improve acceptance length over KL-based training. WTV provides particularly strong gains under sampling-based decoding, while EAL better matches greedy verification. These results show that directly optimizing the acceptance objective, with losses tailored to the decoding mode, is more effective than conventional distribution-matching objectives.

</details>


### [217] [Overlay\_dx - Automating forecasting evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24586)
*Long Ngo,Mohammed Amine Chamli,Jonathan Rivalan,Thomas Jaillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出overlay_dx——一种结合可视化与数值化的时间序列预测评估指标，通过置信区间覆盖率与overlay曲线下面积实现模型比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估指标仅给出数值，缺乏可解释性，难以有效区分不同模型的性能差异。

Method: 定义overlay_dx为预测值落入真实值置信区间内的百分比（可视化指标）；绘图后进一步计算overlay曲线下面积，实现不同阈值与预测下预测值与真实值对齐程度的量化。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法提供统一的可视化与数值化评估框架，改善模型比较并为进一步研究与优化提供洞见。

Conclusion: overlay_dx可同时提供可视与数值两种评估视角，弥补传统指标的不足。

Abstract: Traditional evaluation metrics provides numerical values but often lack comprehensibility, hindering effective differentiation of model performances. Our work addresses this challenge by introducing overlay\_dx, a novel evaluation metric measuring the performance of time series prediction models. Overlay\_dx is a visual metric that represents the percentage of predictions falling within a confidence interval around actual values. Additionally, once evaluation results are plotted, overlay\_dx computes the area under the overlay curve, providing a quantitative measure of alignment between predicted and actual values across different thresholds and predictions. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our approach offers a unified evaluation framework that combines both visual and numerical assessments, enabling improved model comparison and providing valuable insights for further research and optimization efforts in time series prediction.

</details>


### [218] [H-Spec: Parallel Speculative Decoding Without a Drafter-Side KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24197)
*Weifan Jiang,Krishna Teja Chitty-Venkata,Megan Flynn,Reed Meyerson,Zhenting Qi,Tianyu Wu,Eldar Kurtic,Minlan Yu,Alexandre Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 H-Spec：无需独立 drafter-side KV 缓存的混合上下文注入并行草稿模型，结合 Mamba 与 attention 模块，在保持块并行草稿的同时提升接受长度、延迟加速比与并发服务吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码可无损加速 LLM 推理，块扩散草稿器通过并行预测多 token 降低草稿延迟；但现有块草稿器将每个输入位置的目标隐状态投影到独立的 drafter-side KV 缓存，随并发增长带来单请求内存与 KV 写入开销；而直接在原位复用目标 KV 虽可消除该缓存，却无法在整个块内维持草稿质量。

Method: 提出混合目标上下文注入方法：在直接复用目标 KV 的基础上，仅对最后一个输入位置注入目标隐状态，从而无需独立 drafter-side KV 缓存。据此设计 H-Spec——混合 Mamba-attention 并行草稿器，通过互补模块消费两类目标上下文：Mamba 模块以投影后的最后 token 目标隐状态初始化，attention 模块原位复用目标 KV；借助 Mamba 的并行扫描，在循环式建模下仍保持块并行草稿。

Result: 在三个目标模型与多样化任务上，H-Spec 相较最佳基线平均接受长度提升 5.0–13.3%，batch-size-1 的 inter-token 延迟加速比提升 5.3–12.6%；在并发服务下，各评估并发级别均取得更高吞吐，同时保持低于基线的 KV 缓存利用率。

Conclusion: 通过混合目标上下文注入与 Mamba-attention 互补结构，H-Spec 在不引入独立草稿端 KV 缓存的前提下兼顾草稿质量与块并行效率，实现更优的加速与并发服务可扩展性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates large language model inference by having a lightweight draft model predict future tokens for verification by the target model. Recent block diffusion drafters further reduce drafting latency by predicting multiple tokens in parallel. However, existing block drafters project target hidden states at every input position into a separate drafter-side KV cache, incurring per-request memory and KV-write overhead that grow with concurrency; directly reusing target KVs in place removes this cache but fails to sustain draft quality throughout the block. We propose a hybrid target-context injection method that complements direct target KV reuse with target hidden states only at the last input position, requiring no separate drafter-side KV cache. Building on this design, we propose H-Spec, a hybrid Mamba-attention parallel drafter that consumes the two target-context sources through complementary modules. Mamba modules are initialized with projected last-token target hidden states, while attention modules reuse target KVs in place. Despite its recurrent formulation, Mamba's parallel scan allows H-Spec to preserve block-parallel drafting. Across three target models and diverse tasks, H-Spec improves over the best baseline by 5.0--13.3% in mean accepted length and 5.3--12.6% in batch-size-1 inter-token latency speedup. Under concurrent serving, H-Spec consistently achieves higher throughput while maintaining lower KV cache utilization than baselines across evaluated concurrency levels.

</details>


### [219] [GraphToolbox: A Configurable Python Framework for Graph Neural Network Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24609)
*Eloi Campagne,Yvenn Amara-Ouali,Yannig Goude,Argyris Kalogeratos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphToolbox是一个基于PyTorch Geometric的开源Python框架，将图构建、模型选择、训练、聚合与解释统一为配置驱动的电力预测流水线，并在法国区域负荷与净负荷两个案例中验证其系统性架构评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力预测涉及区域、变电站、馈线等空间相关信号，GNN可自然刻画这类关系；但完整的GNN预测实验成本高，因为图构建、模型选择、训练、聚合与解释分散在不兼容的工具中，缺乏统一流程。

Method: 提出GraphToolbox：单一配置驱动流水线，支持数据驱动图构建；通过适配器实例化并训练65种PyTorch Geometric卷积中的51种，以及PyTorch Geometric Temporal的循环单元；提供在线专家聚合、预测可解释性与基于缓存预测的显著性检验。在两个案例研究中评估。

Result: 法国区域负荷案例中，完整预测扫描覆盖的48种卷积误差位于1.14%–1.60%，在线聚合降至0.98%，图模型优于经典加法与提升基线；净负荷案例中，直接图模型精度低于经典加法模型，按物理分量分别预测可改善但仍未弥合差距。两案例使用同一实验接口。

Conclusion: GraphToolbox通过统一接口整合异构工具链，能够支持系统性的GNN架构评估；同时表明图模型并非在所有电力预测任务中都优于经典基线，需结合任务结构（如按物理分量建模）进行选择。

Abstract: Electricity forecasting often involves spatially related signals observed over regions, substations, and feeders, and Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) provide a natural way to represent these relations. Building a complete GNN forecasting experiment is nonetheless laborious, because graph construction, model selection, training, aggregation, and interpretation sit in incompatible tools. We present GraphToolbox, an open-source Python framework that unifies these stages in one configurationdriven pipeline built on PyTorch Geometric. It offers data-driven graph construction, an adapter that instantiates and trains 51 of the 65 PyTorch Geometric convolutions together with the recurrent cells of PyTorch Geometric Temporal, online expert aggregation, forecasting interpretability, and significance testing on cached forecasts. We evaluate the pipeline in two case studies. On French regional load, the 48 convolutions included in the complete forecasting sweep fall in a band from 1.14% to 1.60% error, online aggregation lowers this to 0.98%, and the graph models improve on classical additive and boosting baselines. On net-load, direct graph models are less accurate than a classical additive model, while forecasting each physical component separately improves them without closing that gap. Both comparisons use the same experimental interface, illustrating the role of GraphToolbox in systematic architectural evaluation.

</details>


### [220] [Augmented Hypothesis Testing with Persona-Based LLM Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24629)
*Ziyad Benomar,Aymen Al Marjani,Paul Missault,Saab Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个"学习增强假设检验"框架，利用未知质量的机器学习预测在保证统计有效性的前提下降低A/B实验样本量，覆盖总体级二值方向预测（非对称检验，具备一致性与鲁棒性界）与个体级预测（GPPI，将PPI++扩展到非线性误差）两种粒度，并用LLM人格化模拟作为预测源，在四个真实数据集上验证可大幅降低实验成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A/B测试需要大样本、长周期和高成本。虽然机器学习模型可给出实验结果的辅助预测，但预测质量不确定，无法完全替代真实实验；然而这些预测仍蕴含可用信号。因此需要一套既能利用预测降低样本量、又能保持统计有效性的原则性框架。

Method: 提出学习增强假设检验（learning-augmented hypothesis testing）框架，针对预测粒度的两端分别设计方法：(1) 总体级方向性预测（仅知处理效应符号的二值信号）采用非对称检验，并在学习增强算法范式下证明一致性与鲁棒性界；(2) 个体级预测提出Generalized PPI++（GPPI），通过高维变换扩展Prediction-Powered Inference以处理非线性预测误差。两种方法在预测准确时获益、在预测不准确或对抗性时保持鲁棒。使用基于人格的LLM模拟（具备用户人格的AI智能体预测个体行为）作为贯穿两种粒度的自然预测源进行验证。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，所提方法结合人格化预测能显著降低实验成本，同时保持严格的统计有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为利用未知质量的ML预测进行假设检验提供了统一且稳健的途径，覆盖从总体级到个体级的预测粒度，在保证统计有效性的同时有效降低A/B测试成本，并由真实数据实验验证。

Abstract: A/B testing requires large sample sizes, long timelines, and significant costs. When auxiliary predictions of experimental outcomes are available from machine learning models, uncertain prediction quality precludes replacing human experiments entirely, yet these predictions may still contain useful signal. We propose a principled framework for learning-augmented hypothesis testing that leverages predictions of unknown quality to reduce sample sizes while maintaining statistical validity. Predictions naturally vary in granularity, from coarse aggregate signals to fine-grained individual-level estimates, and our framework addresses both ends of this spectrum: (1) for population-level directional predictions, where only a binary signal on the treatment effect sign is available, we use an asymmetric test and prove consistency and robustness bounds within the learning-augmented algorithms paradigm; (2) for individual-level predictions, we introduce Generalized PPI++ (GPPI), extending Prediction-Powered Inference to handle nonlinear prediction errors through higher-dimensional transformations. Both methods benefit from accurate predictions while remaining robust to inaccurate or adversarial ones. We validate our framework using persona-based LLM simulations, where AI agents equipped with user personas predict individual behavior, as a natural prediction source spanning both granularity levels. Experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that our methods, combined with persona-based predictions, substantially reduce experimental costs while preserving rigorous statistical validity.

</details>


### [221] [Displacement Geometry Captures Platonic Shared Reality Across Models and Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24209)
*Chenming Shang,Yujin Tang,Jun Jie Ou Yang,Ruize Xu,Adam Breuer,Nikhil Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型间共享的不是样本的绝对位置，而是位移向量（方向）：在单一正交对齐下，位移向量跨模型显著保留，绝对位置则不然；这一几何源于共享语义成分与私有能力成分的分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柏拉图表征假说（PRH）认为独立训练的模型会收敛到共享的现实统计模型，但近期工作只在模型间发现弱的逐点相似性。作者希望调和这一理论与反证之间的矛盾，厘清模型之间究竟共享了什么。

Method: 提出单一正交对齐（仅旋转与反射）下的表征分解：将表征拆分为线性可对齐的共享语义成分与不可对齐的私有能力成分；在44个独立训练的视觉与语言编码器（跨模态、跨能力不对称配对）上验证位移向量保留性，并追溯至概念级结构（父概念与子变化向量正交、跨模型概念位移平行）。同时用微调与关系蒸馏进行可证伪预测与验证，并提出Shadow Casting与SHADOWCLIP实例。

Result: 位移向量在44个独立训练的视觉与语言编码器间显著保留，与PRH证据一致；样本绝对位置不保留，与近期反证一致。实验证实：微调保留逐点相似性但破坏位移，关系蒸馏则相反。SHADOWCLIP以数量级更少的算力超越强微调基线。

Conclusion: 模型共享的是位移方向而非绝对位置，二者都源自共享语义成分与私有能力成分的单一分解。由于语义可线性对齐而能力不可，能力可通过源模型的单次缓存前向传播导入其他模型（Shadow Casting）；缓存可与开源权重一同发布，使一个模型的能力无需微调即可被任意数量的其他模型下载并导入。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) claims that independently trained models converge on a shared statistical model of reality, yet recent work finds only weak pointwise similarity between models. In this paper, we show that what models share is not the location of samples in representation space, but the directions (displacement vectors) between them. Under a single orthogonal alignment--rotation and reflection only--these displacement vectors are substantially preserved across 44 independently trained vision and language encoders spanning modalities and asymmetric capability pairs, consistent with the PRH evidence. The samples' absolute positions are not, consistent with recent counter-evidence. Both arise from a single decomposition: representations split into a shared semantic component that is linearly aligned across models, and a private capability component that is not. We trace this geometry to concept-level structure: within a model, parent concepts are orthogonal to their child variation vectors; across models, concept displacements are parallel. Our theory falsifiably predicts (and experiments confirm) that fine-tuning preserves pointwise similarity but collapses displacement, and that relational distillation does the opposite.
  A major implication is that, because semantics align linearly but capabilities do not, capabilities can be imported from one model to another using a single cached forward pass through the source. We call this Shadow Casting. As a proof of concept, our SHADOWCLIP instantiation outperforms strong fine-tuned baselines at orders of magnitude less compute. A cache can be released alongside open model weights, letting one model's capabilities be downloaded and imported into any number of other models without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [222] [iSDFT: Information-Proximal Self-Distillation for Continual Learning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24646)
*Ahmed Khaled Khamis,Xiaotong Ji,Hassan Jaber,Rasul Tutunov,Matthieu Zimmer,Jun Wang,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iSDFT将教师视为有预算的信息源，在每个token处选择满足教师信息约束且最接近当前学生的分布，得到带局部倾斜的闭式指数目标，并将学生锚定于冻结基策略以控制累积漂移，从而在专门化与保持能力间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始SDFT虽能从示范中学习新技能并减少遗忘，但始终向完整的示范条件教师蒸馏，将教师影响固定在“全教师”端点，无法控制在每个预测状态下应迁移多少示范信息。

Method: 提出Information-Proximal SDFT（iSDFT）：把教师当作带预算的信息源；在每个token处，选取在满足规定教师信息约束前提下与当前学生最接近的分布，得到闭式指数目标，其倾斜程度由局部决定；并进一步将学生锚定到其冻结的基策略，以控制累积漂移。

Result: 在四个异构LLM主干与两个专门化任务上，iSDFT在8个模型-任务设置中的7个优于原始SDFT，剩余1个持平；在原始SDFT基准套件上保持更紧：73%的评估与基模型差距在0.5分以内，而最强基线为52%；同时在全部十个额外的数学、代码与竞赛数学基准上取得最大平均提升。

Conclusion: 控制教师信息引入的“量”和“时机”能够同时提升专门化效果并保持更广泛的通用能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation fine-tuning (SDFT) learns new skills from demonstrations while reducing forgetting, but it always distils toward the full demonstration-conditioned teacher. This fixes teacher influence at the full-teacher endpoint, providing no control over how much demonstration information should be transferred at each prediction state. We introduce Information-Proximal SDFT (iSDFT), which instead treats the teacher as a budgeted source of information. At each token, iSDFT selects the distribution closest to the current student that satisfies a prescribed teacher-information constraint, yielding a closed-form exponential target with a locally determined tilt.
