To control cumulative drift, we further anchor the student to its frozen base policy. Across four heterogeneous LLM backbones and two specialisation tasks, iSDFT improves vanilla SDFT in 7 of 8 model-task settings and matches it in the remaining one. It also provides tighter retention on the original SDFT benchmark suite, with 73% of evaluations remaining within 0.5 points of the base model versus 52% for the strongest baseline, while achieving the largest mean improvement on all ten additional mathematics, coding, and competition-mathematics benchmarks. These results show that controlling how much and when teacher information is introduced improves specialisation while preserving broader capability.

</details>


### [223] [Adaptive Forgetting for Nonstationary Optimization: Towards Robust EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24233)
*Hongyu Zhu,Lin Chen,Jing Chen,Yuting Zhou,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出张量化自适应优化器AFOR，将固定的二阶矩衰减系数改为由局部梯度状态在线估计的动态系数，在三个EEG跨被试基准上平均测试精度较Adam提升3.00%、2.07%、4.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码中跨被试泛化是实际应用的核心挑战。现有解码器通常以Adam/AdamW在固定二阶矩衰减系数下训练，而跨被试学习存在低信噪比、被试间差异与梯度非平稳性问题。固定系数隐含假设梯度统计量在层间与时间上同质，限制了模型对跨被试EEG信号的适应性，导致泛化性能下降。

Method: 提出AFOR（张量化自适应优化器），将固定的二阶矩衰减系数转化为由局部梯度状态在线估计的动态系数，包含两个模块：残差对齐信号评分器（RASS）将局部梯度残差与方向一致性汇总为信号质量分数；自适应遗忘控制器（AFC）通过自参照归一化将该分数映射为有界的逐步衰减系数，并采用累积乘积初始化校正以在时变衰减下保持一致性。

Result: 在覆盖三个代表性领域的三个EEG基准上，采用严格的跨被试协议，AFOR在对比优化器中取得最佳平均性能，平均测试精度较Adam分别提升3.00%、2.07%和4.38%。

Conclusion: 通过令二阶矩衰减系数随梯度信号质量动态变化，AFOR缓解了固定衰减系数在跨被试EEG学习中的同质性假设问题，有效提升跨被试泛化能力，验证了优化器层面自适应机制对EEG解码的价值。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) provides non-invasive monitoring of brain activity and is widely used in emotion recognition, motor imagery and sleep staging. Although within-subject decoding has achieved considerable progress, cross-subject generalization remains a central challenge in practical applications. EEG decoders are typically trained with Adam/AdamW under a fixed second-moment decay coefficient, even though cross-subject learning involves low signal-to-noise ratios, subject variability, and gradient nonstationarity. A fixed coefficient implicitly assumes that gradient statistics are homogeneous across layers and time, which can limit model's adaptability to cross-subject EEG signals and degrade generalization. To address these issues, we propose AFOR, a tensor-wise adaptive optimizer that converts the fixed second-moment decay coefficient into a dynamic coefficient estimated online from local gradient state. AFOR combines a Residual-Alignment Signal Scorer (RASS) and an Adaptive Forgetting Controller (AFC). RASS summarizes local gradient residuals and directional agreement into a signal-quality score, and AFC maps this score through self-referential normalization to a bounded per-step decay coefficient, with cumulative-product initialization correction maintaining consistency under time-varying decay.
  Under a strict cross-subject protocol on three EEG benchmarks that cover three representative fields, AFOR achieves the best average performance among the compared optimizers, improving the mean test accuracy over Adam by 3.00%, 2.07%, and 4.38%, respectively.

</details>


### [224] [Corrective Forcing: Unified Post-Training for Diffusions and Flows in Generative Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24651)
*Qing Yao,Lijian Gao,Qirong Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Corrective Forcing (CoF) 后训练范式，让扩散与流模型在自生成 rollout 状态上学习校正预测，缓解训练-推理不匹配，提升语音增强的感知质量与重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流模型用于语音增强时存在训练-推理不匹配：训练使用解析路径状态，而推理沿离散采样轨迹对模型自身生成的 rollout 状态递归评估，导致预测误差与离散化误差累积。

Method: CoF 是一种后训练范式：在动态采样调度下，将 rollout 状态上的干净语音预测校正向真值；并利用局部校正的反事实转移作为事实转移的参考，正则化局部演化；通过共享的干净语音预测参数化，使同一后训练目标可同时适用于扩散与流两类公式。

Result: 在 SB-VE 与 OT-CFM 上的实验表明，感知质量与重建保真度均有提升，且在不同采样步数下表现稳健。

Conclusion: CoF 通过让模型从自生成 rollout 中学习并校正预测，有效弥合训练-推理差距，且统一适用于扩散与流模型，具备良好的采样步数鲁棒性。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow models, as promising generative paradigms for speech enhancement, face a training--inference mismatch: training uses analytical path states, whereas inference recursively evaluates models on self-generated rollout states along discretized sampling trajectories. This mismatch causes prediction and discretization errors to accumulate. To address it, we introduce Corrective Forcing (CoF), a post-training paradigm that forces diffusion and flow models to learn from self-generated rollouts and correct their predictions. CoF corrects clean-speech predictions on rollout states toward the ground truth under dynamic sampling schedules, exposing the model to varying inference conditions. It further regularizes local evolution using locally corrected counterfactual transitions as references for factual transitions. By expressing model outputs through a shared clean-speech prediction parameterization, CoF applies the same post-training objective across diffusion and flow formulations. Experiments with SB-VE and OT-CFM demonstrate improvements in perceptual quality and reconstruction fidelity, together with robust performance across different numbers of sampling steps.

</details>


### [225] [Enhancing Transformer Representations of Symbolic ODE Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24746)
*Xiyue Fan,Adam Prugel-Bennett,Stuart E. Middleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将树位置编码引入Transformer以求解符号常微分方程（ODE），探究其对表达式树层级结构的建模效果，并结合对比学习捕获数学表达式的交换性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解微分方程的方法（符号回归、物理信息神经网络、神经算子等）多依赖数值近似或对数值数据的盲式符号搜索，较少关注能保留交换律、可支持符号形式数学推理的结构化表达式表示。Transformer虽在符号微分方程求解上表现良好，但其标准位置编码面向序列数据，难以有效刻画符号微分方程天然的表达式树层级结构。

Method: 系统考察多种现有树位置编码（tree positional embeddings）在符号ODE任务中的表现，在不同设置下对比其有效性；并基于所学结构表示引入对比学习，以支持数学中的交换性质；通过消融实验分析各方法在符号数学结构建模中的相互作用。

Result: 树位置编码有助于早期训练阶段的学习，并在整个训练过程中持续提升性能，最终在不同数据规模与任务上均取得一致优势；消融实验揭示了各方法在建模符号数学结构时的相互作用机制。

Conclusion: 树位置编码能有效提升Transformer对符号ODE表达式树结构的建模能力，结合对比学习可支撑交换性等数学性质的学习，为符号形式的数学推理提供了结构化表示基础。

Abstract: Existing approaches to solving differential equations, such as symbolic regression, physics informed neural networks, and neural operators, typically focus on numerical approximations or blind symbolic search via fitting to numerical data. Less attention has been paid to learning structured representations of mathematical expressions that preserve commutative properties and could support mathematical reasoning in symbolic forms. Transformer models have shown strong capabilities in solving symbolic differential equations. However, standard positional embeddings in transformers are designed for sequence data. Symbolic differential equations are naturally represented by expression trees, so these positional embeddings may not efficiently capture their hierarchical structures. We investigate existing tree positional embeddings in symbolic ordinary differential equation (ODE) tasks. We systematically study their effectiveness under different settings. Our results show that tree positional embeddings aid learning in early epochs and continue to improve performance throughout, ultimately yielding consistent advantages across various data sizes and tasks. Based on learned structural representations, we apply contrastive learning to support the commutative property in mathematics. Ablation studies provide insight into how these methods interact in modelling symbolic mathematical structures.

</details>


### [226] [Reinforcement Learning Inspired Black-box Adversarial Attacks for Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24249)
*Florian Krone,Elena Hoemann,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的黑盒对抗攻击RIBA，在极少查询下生成对抗扰动，性能优于现有攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络（卷积或Transformer）易受人类难以察觉的小扰动影响，导致预测改变，这对安全关键应用构成威胁。现有攻击多采用白盒威胁模型，需完全访问目标模型，实际中不现实，因此需要更现实的黑盒攻击方法。

Method: 提出一种黑盒威胁模型下的新方法，利用强化学习概念优化扰动，针对不可微目标模型进行攻击。强化学习算法已被优化为查询高效，适合设计黑盒对抗攻击，由此构建了强化学习启发的黑盒对抗攻击（RIBA）。

Result: 在Cifar10和ImageNet数据集上，与最先进攻击对比，RIBA仅需少量查询即可生成对抗扰动。对Cifar10上ResNet-18生成攻击图像所需中位查询数减少25.4%，对ImageNet上Vit-B/16模型减少22.5%。此外，RIBA在对抗训练模型上可匹配白盒攻击性能。

Conclusion: RIBA验证了强化学习在黑盒对抗攻击中的有效性，仅需少量查询即可取得优于现有攻击的表现，并能在对抗训练模型上达到白盒攻击水平。

Abstract: Neural networks, both convolution or transformer based, are essential for modern computer vision systems. However, they are vulnerable to small perturbations, almost imperceptible to humans, which significantly alter the model's prediction. These adversarial attacks are often considered to be a significant threat to the implementation of neural networks in safety-critical applications. Most attacks utilize the white-box threat model and therefore require full access to the target model, making them unrealistic to use in practice. We propose a novel approach under the more realistic black-box threat model that utilizes concepts from reinforcement learning to optimize perturbations with a non-differentiable target model. Reinforcement learning algorithms have already been optimized to be query efficient, making them an ideal starting point when designing black-box adversarial attacks. We show the success of our reinforcement learning inspired black-box adversarial attack (RIBA) in generating adversarial perturbations using only a small number of queries to the target model, by comparing it to state of the art attacks on different models on the Cifar10 and ImageNet data sets. RIBA takes $25.4\%$ fewer median queries to generate attacked images against a ResNet-18 on Cifar10 and $22.5\%$ fewer median queries to fool a Vit-B/16 model on ImageNet. Additionally, we demonstrate that RIBA can match the performance of white-box attacks on an adversarially trained model.

</details>


### [227] [When Tomorrow Becomes Today: Self-Evolving Policies for Agentic Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24862)
*Yifan Hu,Xilin Dai,Zhiyuan Qu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-ming Yang,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TimEvolve，一种冻结骨干的时间序列智能体，通过"预测-揭示-更新"时序协议将已实现结果转为专家信任、智能体路径选择与干预强度的持久联合策略更新，在八个Time-MMD域上取得最佳平均MSE/MAE排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体时间序列预测中，底层机制随时间演化，数值模型、推理策略与干预规则的相对有效性本质上是时变的，因此智能体必须同时调整其预测输出以及决定"信任哪些组件、如何协调它们"的编排策略。部署过程天然提供监督信号：随着预测视野到期、真实目标揭示，先前决策的有效性得以评估。若在观测目标前提交所有数值专家预测与候选智能体路径，每个已实现结果即可评估整个备选集合，从而在无额外标注的情况下获得延迟反馈。然而现有时间序列智能体主要通过预测精炼、反思或检索来利用先验经验，未系统地将已实现结果转化为对后续起点联合编排策略的持久更新。

Method: TimEvolve为冻结骨干（frozen-backbone）的时间序列智能体，将每个已实现结果转化为对三方面的持久联合更新：专家信任、智能体路径选择与干预强度。采用时间有序的"预测（predict）—揭示（reveal）—更新（update）"协议，把延迟反馈应用于后续预测，且所有数值专家预测与候选路径在目标观测前提交以保证无泄漏的集合评估。

Result: 在八个Time-MMD域、十五种方法的对比中，TimEvolve取得最佳平均MSE与MAE排名，并在七个域上两项指标均为最低误差。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从部署过程中遭遇的未来（已实现结果）学习预测策略具有显著价值，验证了延迟反馈驱动的联合编排策略持久更新范式的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic time series forecasting concerns systems whose underlying mechanisms evolve, making the relative effectiveness of numerical models, reasoning strategies, and intervention rules inherently time-varying. Consequently, a time series agent must adapt the forecasts it produces and the orchestration policy that determines which components to trust and how to coordinate them. The deployment process naturally provides supervision for this adaptation as forecast horizons elapse and realized targets reveal the effectiveness of earlier decisions. Committing all numerical expert forecasts and candidate agent paths before target observation allows each realized outcome to evaluate the entire alternative set, providing delayed feedback without additional annotation. However, existing time series agents primarily incorporate prior experience through forecast refinement, reflection, or retrieval, without systematically converting realized outcomes into persistent updates to the joint orchestration policy governing later origins. To exploit this delayed feedback systematically, we introduce TimEvolve, a frozen-backbone time series agent that converts each realized outcome into persistent joint updates of expert trust, agent path selection, and intervention strength. A temporally ordered predict, reveal, and update protocol applies this feedback to subsequent forecasts. Experiments across eight Time-MMD domains show that TimEvolve achieves the best average MSE and MAE ranks among fifteen methods and the lowest errors on both metrics in seven domains. These results demonstrate the value of learning forecasting policies from the futures encountered during deployment.

</details>


### [228] [Explainable Predictive Condition-based Maintenance of Naval-Propulsion Systems using Fuzzy Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24250)
*Dionisis Kalogeropoulos,Georgia Sovatzidi,Panagiotis G. Kalozoumis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合模糊决策树与深度残差神经网络的框架，用于舰船可解释预测性维护（PdM），在公开数据集上达到99.24%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航运业对全球经济影响重大，需通过有效维护保障运营可用性与航行安全。预测性维护（PdM）较传统维护方式优势明显（部件损伤预测、减少停机、延长机械寿命、提升安全性），但现有PdM方法不向用户解释其结果，用户难以理解可能发生的故障，缺乏信任。

Method: 提出一个基于模糊决策树与深度残差神经网络的新型框架，对海军舰船执行可解释的预测性维护。该框架可基于所用数据集生成模糊局部规则，并借助因果关系对输出结果给出用户可理解的解释。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验验证了框架的有效性，准确率达到99.24%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了兼具高精度与可解释性的舰船预测性维护，通过可理解的因果解释增强用户信任，为航运业维护决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The shipping industry has a significant impact on the global economy, emphasizing the need for operational availability and safety through the use of effective maintenance techniques. During the last decades, predictive maintenance (PdM) has emerged as a promising solution compared to the existing conventional maintenance systems. This is because it offers several advantageous functions, such as damage predictions for vessel components, reduced downtime, improved and extended life of machinery, as well as higher safety during voyages. However, existing methodologies developed for performing PdM do not provide explanations of their results to users, so that they can understand the failures that may occur. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a novel framework based on a fuzzy decision tree and a deep residual neural network, aiming to perform explainable PdM on naval vessels. The proposed framework is able to generate fuzzy local rules based on the dataset used, and can provide explanations of its outcomes, using cause-and-effect relationships, in a way that are understandable to users, thereby gaining their trust. Experiments using a publicly available dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, as it achieves an accuracy of 99.24%.

</details>


### [229] [Exactness at Inference: A Representational Criterion for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24942)
*Filipe Marinho Rocha,Inês Dutra,Vítor Santos Costa,Luís Paulo Reis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型仅当计算的结构等价于生成机制时才可外推；本文以张量逻辑为实例，提出"精确可表示性"判据，区分可通过与通不过的架构，并给出混合架构的传播规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在训练分布外泛化失败，通常被归因于拟合误差或数据不足。作者认为根本问题是表示与生成机制之间缺乏结构等价：拟合式代理即使训练残差达到算术下界，外推仍会发散并在复合下累积。因此需要一个刻画"精确性"而非"逼近程度"的判据。

Method: 以Tensor Logic为范例：零温收缩等价于离散逻辑，张量为布尔值、嵌入为正交基，仅算术连续，实现原位推理而无中间产物抽取。基于此提出精确可表示性判据，并将其施加于推理而非训练；进而推导混合架构的传播规则，即输出继承其路径上每个拟合估计器的界。

Result: 零温Tensor Logic与可微分ILP通过判据，Logic Tensor Networks不通过；阈值化到硬标签的神经网络不通过，而[0,1]区间内的精确边缘概率可通过。该判据解释了等变模型失效的轴、ARC-AGI的归纳/转导之分；在法则导出的划分上，仅精确假设类能找到56.3%可回答的远距离查询，而集成方法以虚假置信作答、距离度量排序相反。

Conclusion: 泛化的关键不是离散表示或抽取出的表达式，而是推理阶段的精确可表示性。常见归纳偏置（对称性、记忆等）之所以达到精确，仅因人类注入；因此应转向诱导精确表示，而非拟合在外推中发散、复合下累积误差的代理模型。

Abstract: A model generalizes outside its training distribution only when it computes a representation structurally equivalent to the generating mechanism, not an approximation fitted to it. Such equivalence is necessary for exactness in and out of distribution, and extrapolation is governed by this exactness at inference, whatever its realization. Tensor Logic shows this: a zero-temperature contraction is equivalent to discrete logic, deducing in place with no artefact extracted, its tensors Boolean, its embeddings orthonormal, only its arithmetic continuous. Lacking infinite recursion it reaches Datalog, not Prolog, and though exact over closed domains it needs external memory to bind a novel entity. The criterion needs neither a discrete representation nor an extracted expression, and constrains inference, not training: an exact marginal in $[0,1]$ passes, a Neural Network thresholded to a hard label does not. Logic Tensor Networks fail it, while differentiable ILP and Tensor Logic at $T=0$ pass. Piecewise-affine extrapolation divergence and an inability to bind novel entities are two faces of a shortfall in exact representability. For hybrid architectures, a propagation rule follows: the output inherits the bounds of every fitted estimator on its path, explaining which axes fail in equivariant models and the ARC-AGI induction/transduction split. Only an exact hypothesis class certifies what the training data leave underdetermined: on a law-derived partition it finds the $56.3\%$ of distant queries that are answerable, which ensembles meet with false confidence and distance metrics rank backwards. Common inductive biases, from symmetries to memory, reach exactness only because humans inject them, an argument for inducing exact representations rather than fitting surrogates whose residuals, even at the arithmetic floor in training, diverge outside the data and compound under composition.

</details>


### [230] [Rare Event Estimation via Iterative Unalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24969)
*Hanming Yang,Daksh Mittal,Jing Dong,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于权重扰动与可微语言模型的重要性采样方法，用于估计智能体随机输出轨迹中极罕见事件（低至10⁻⁹）的发生概率，实现超过800倍的计算加权效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体自主性增强，即使极罕见的事件也可能造成灾难性后果，因此安全部署的关键不在于事件是否可能发生，而在于其发生频率。估计此类风险需在组合爆炸的轨迹空间中搜索，朴素蒙特卡洛方法计算上不可行。

Method: 提出一种新的重要性采样方法，通过扰动原模型权重来构造提议分布。该提议分布本身是一个可微参数化的语言模型，从而支持基于梯度的权重空间搜索。设计的目标函数结合了事件放大的可微代理和自适应正则化机制，动态平衡放大效果与估计器稳定性。

Result: 在约1.2亿和26亿参数的模型上，覆盖三大事件族、300多个低至10⁻⁹的罕见事件进行评估，参考概率的相对标准误差小于10%。在最可验证的设置中，对于概率低于10⁻⁷的事件，该估计器相比朴素蒙特卡洛实现了超过800倍的计算加权效率增益。

Conclusion: 该方法为估计智能体极罕见事件风险提供了计算上可行的方案，显著优于朴素蒙特卡洛，代码已开源。

Abstract: As agents are deployed with increased autonomy, even extremely rare events along their stochastic output trajectories can occur and prove catastrophic. Safe deployment therefore does not depend on whether these events can occur, but on how often they might. We study the problem of estimating the probability of rare events that arise from stochastic variation in the agent's own actions. Estimating this type of risk requires searching over the combinatorially vast space of trajectories. Naive Monte Carlo is computationally prohibitive in this regime, and constructing effective importance sampling (IS) proposals requires coordinated changes to a context-dependent chain of conditional distributions. We develop a new IS method that perturbs the original model's weights to construct the proposal. The proposal is itself a differentiably parameterized language model, enabling gradient-based search over weight space. We formulate an objective that combines a differentiable surrogate for event amplification and an adaptive regularization scheme that dynamically balances amplification against estimator stability. We evaluate our approach on $\sim$120M and $\sim$2.6B models across three event families spanning 300+ rare events as rare as $10^{-9}$, with reference probabilities computed with $<10\%$ relative standard error. In our most verifiable settings, we observe that our IS estimator achieves over $800\times$ compute-weighted efficiency gains over naive Monte Carlo for events with probabilities lower than $10^{-7}$. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/namkoong-lab/iterative-unalignment.

</details>


### [231] [High-Dimensional Online Change Point Detection with Adaptive Thresholding and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24278)
*Sven Jacob,Bardh Prenkaj,Weijia Shao,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于切片Wasserstein距离的变点检测框架，提升高维数据的可扩展性与可解释性，并显著降低误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变点检测方法在高维场景下计算开销大，且对检测到的变点缺乏解释，限制了实际应用。

Method: 利用切片Wasserstein距离将多元序列数据转化为一维得分，使其兼容现有CPD方法；分析SW距离随机切片的分布，证明其可近似为Gamma分布以用于阈值校准；提出结合SW得分与自适应分位数阈值的在线CPD算法；并设计针对标注变点的对比解释生成框架。

Result: 相比主流在线和离线CPD基线，该方法平均将误报至少降低48%，同时保持相当或更优的检测性能，并能生成可解释的变点标注。

Conclusion: 该框架兼顾可扩展性与可解释性，适合高风险场景部署，但摘要未给出具体数据集与运行效率等细节。

Abstract: Change point detection (CPD) identifies abrupt and significant changes in sequential data, with applications in human activity recognition, financial markets, cybersecurity, manufacturing, and autonomous systems. Traditional CPD methods often face computational challenges in high-dimensional settings and typically provide limited explanations for detected changes, which can restrict their practical usability. This paper introduces a CPD framework that improves scalability and interpretability by leveraging the Sliced Wasserstein (SW) distance. Our contributions are fourfold: (1) we transform multivariate sequential data into one-dimensional scores using the SW distance, making the resulting representation compatible with existing CPD methods; (2) we analyze the distributional behavior of random slices of the SW distance and show that, under suitable assumptions, they can be approximated by a Gamma distribution, providing a principled basis for threshold calibration; (3) we propose a self-adapting online CPD algorithm that combines this SW-based score with an adaptive quantile-based threshold; (4) we introduce a model-specific framework for generating contrastive explanations for annotated change points. Empirically, our method reduces false positives by at least $48\%$ on average compared with popular online and offline CPD baselines, while maintaining competitive or superior detection performance. Code is available at https://github.com/jsve96/SWCPD_Code. At the same time, it produces interpretable change-point annotations, making it practical for deployment in high-stakes applications.

</details>


### [232] [RRSI: Regularized Recursive Self-Improvement of Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24972)
*Peng Xia,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Yanfei Chen,Yufan Zhang,Yoonho Lee,Chengsong Huang,Han Yu,Zhongying CuiZhu,Yifei Ming,Huaxiu Yao,Burak Gokturk,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出正则化递归自我改进（RRSI），通过对智能体"外架"演化过程中的候选提案与选择施加正则化约束，缓解对训练任务的过拟合，在分布内和分布外基准上均取得提升并降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的能力很大程度上取决于其外架（harness），即围绕冻结骨干模型的提示、控制流、工具、记忆与上下文管理。近期方法通过迭代地提出并选择外架的组件级修改，在智能体系统层面实现了递归自我改进（RSI）。但这类递归演化可能因记忆训练任务而过拟合：在分布内表现大幅提升，而在分布外基准上增益缩小甚至消失。因此需要将正则化思想引入外架自我改进，以提升泛化性。

Method: RRSI在外架的自我改进循环中对候选提案与选择环节施加约束：提案器采用时间退火预算，限制单个候选可捆绑的编辑数量，并基于演化历史鼓励探索未走过的轨迹；选择器配备评论器（critic）与剪枝器（pruner），评论器筛除针对特定基准的提案，剪枝器则移除过小、代价过高或已无用的改动。这些约束共同倾向于保留可复用的智能体机制，而非基准专用机制或噪声。

Result: 在涵盖编码、智能体工作空间与工程设计任务的八个基准上，RRSI在其所针对演化的划分上最高提升14.1分，在五个分布外基准上最高提升4.7分；同时所产生的外架相比未正则化的演化版本减少约30%的策略token消耗。

Conclusion: 将正则化原则（退火预算、探索激励、评论与剪枝）引入智能体外架的递归自我改进，可有效抑制对训练任务的过拟合，在分布内与分布外均取得更优表现，并提升token效率，表明正则化是实现可复用、可泛化智能体机制的关键。

Abstract: An LLM agent's capability is largely magnified by its harness, namely the prompts, control flow, tooling, memory, and context management surrounding the frozen backbone model. Recent methods increasingly automate this process by iteratively proposing and selecting component-wise edits of an agent harness, practically establishing a form of recursive self-improvement (RSI) at the agent-system level. However, such recursive evolution may overfit by memorizing the training tasks, showing large in-distribution gains that shrink or even vanish on out-of-distribution benchmarks. We introduce Regularized Recursive Self-Improvement of Agent Harnesses (RRSI), which incorporates the principles of regularizations into harness self-improvement by constraining the evolution candidate proposal and selection. The proposer operates with a temporally annealed budget, limiting how many edits a candidate can bundle, and it encourages unexplored trajectories based on evolution history. The selector is equipped with a critic and a pruner: the critic screens benchmark-specific proposals, while the pruner, removes changes that are too small, too expensive, or no longer useful. Together these constraints favor reusable agent mechanisms over benchmark-specific ones or even noises. Across eight benchmarks spanning coding, agentic workspace and engineering design tasks, RRSI gains up to 14.1 points on the split it evolves against and up to 4.7 points on the five out-of-distribution benchmarks, while producing a harness that runs on 30% fewer policy tokens than the unregularized evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/google-research/rrsi and project page is https://regularized-rsi.com/.

</details>


### [233] [SupportCal: Label-Free Calibration of Post-Trained LLMs via Reference Support and Corroboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24303)
*Linhan Luo,Lequan Lin,Dai Shi,Feng Chen,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无标签后验校准方法SupportCal：保留一致样本单位权重，对不一致样本按PLM相对支持度与多参考模型佐证赋连续权重，从而改善后训练语言模型的置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练提升任务性能但常损害置信度校准，使后训练模型比预训练模型更过度自信；而任务相关标注校准数据昂贵或不可得，因此对应的预训练模型可作为天然的无标签参考。已有的一致性门控校准仅用一致样本拟合标量温度，完全排除不一致样本，需重新审视这种二元处理方式。

Method: 通过受控重新引入诊断发现不一致样本的聚合效应呈非单调；据此提出SupportCal：一致样本保持单位权重，不一致样本赋予连续权重，依据own-base PLM的相对支持度以及从规模兼容候选池中选出的预训练参考模型提供的佐证；并刻画加权目标何时存在有限最优温度。

Result: 在MedMCQA与MathQA上，SupportCal在几乎所有评估的目标模型配置下ECE均低于仅用一致样本的基线；补充的TweetEval Sentiment结果在固定标签分类任务上呈现相同规律。

Conclusion: 不一致样本应以连续加权方式部分纳入而非二元排除或全量纳入，无标签的PLM参考可为后训练模型提供有效的后验校准信号。

Abstract: Post-training often improves task performance but can degrade confidence calibration, leaving post-trained language models (PoLMs) more overconfident than their corresponding pretrained language models (PLMs). Because task-specific labeled calibration data can be costly or unavailable, the corresponding pretrained PLM provides a natural label-free reference for post-hoc calibration. Prior agreement-gated PLM-referenced calibration fits a scalar temperature using only examples on which the PoLM and its PLM reference agree, excluding disagreement examples because direct alignment can drive the fitted temperature excessively high and induce under-confidence. We revisit this binary treatment. A controlled reintroduction diagnostic reveals a non-monotonic aggregate effect: admitting a moderate fraction of disagreement examples can improve calibration, whereas the benefit diminishes as unit-weight inclusion approaches the full disagreement set. We introduce SupportCal, a label-free post-hoc method that retains agreement examples at unit weight and assigns disagreement examples continuous weights based on the own-base PLM's relative support and corroboration from pretrained references selected from a size-compatible candidate pool. We further characterize when the resulting weighted objective admits a finite optimal temperature. Across MedMCQA and MathQA, SupportCal yields lower ECE than the agreement-only baseline for nearly all evaluated target-model configurations; supplementary TweetEval Sentiment results show the same pattern on a fixed-label classification task.

</details>


### [234] [A Distributional Optimisation Perspective on Combining Models in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24328)
*Congye Wang,Yan Lin,Zheyang Shen,Matthew A. Fisher,Chris. J. Oates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将模型集成与低秩适配器平均统一为熵正则化的分布式优化问题，揭示集成情形目标凸而适配器平均非凸，评估现有与新型算法（含函数型变分梯度下降），并在合成分类与大模型常识推理微调上开展实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，多模型预测的组合以及组合规则通常被分开设计且依赖经验性手段，缺乏统一的理论指导。分布优化（在概率分布集合上进行优化）的最新进展为模型的联合训练提供了原则性框架，可将模型集合视为待优化支撑点的离散分布，但这类方法在此任务上的潜力尚未被充分理解。

Method: 将两种标准组合策略——集成（ensembles）与低秩适配器平均（low-rank adapter averaging）——形式化为熵正则化的分布优化问题，分析其目标函数的凸性；评估已有及新提出的算法，包括一种函数型变分梯度下降（functional variational gradient descent）。

Result: 分析表明，集成情形下目标函数是凸的，而适配器平均情形下非凸，因此现有的平均场朗之万动力学收敛保证仅能迁移到前者；在此基础上对多种算法进行了比较，并通过覆盖合成分类任务以及大语言模型在常识推理基准上的微调开展了实证研究。

Conclusion: 该工作为模型组合提供了原则性的联合训练视角，明确了不同组合策略在分布优化框架下的理论性质差异，并通过实验验证了所提算法与框架的有效性。

Abstract: Combining predictions from different models can improve performance at machine learning tasks, but the training of the individual models and the rule used to combine them are typically chosen separately, and by ad hoc means. Recent advances in distributional optimisation (i.e. where the optimisation occurs over the set of probability distributions) offer an opportunity for principled joint training, viewing the collection of models as a discrete distribution whose support points are to be optimised, but the potential of these methods is not well-understood. In this paper we (1) cast two standard combination strategies - ensembles and low-rank adapter averaging - as entropy-regularised distributional optimisation, observing that the resulting objective is convex in the ensemble case but not in the adapter-averaging case, so that existing convergence guarantees for mean field Langevin dynamics transfer only to the former; (2) assess existing and novel algorithms for this task, including a functional variant of variational gradient descent; and (3) report an empirical study spanning synthetic classification tasks and fine-tuning of large language models on a commonsense reasoning benchmark.

</details>


### [235] [Pharmacokinetic State Space Models for Unbiased Prediction of Haemodynamic Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24338)
*Rithin Nagaraj,Sudiksha Chindula,Bhaskarjyoti Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将药物输注史（丙泊酚、瑞芬太尼效应室浓度）纳入输入的术中低血压（IOH）预测模型，采用基于Mamba的架构，AUROC 0.7360、AUPRC 0.1794（为随机基线的2.73倍），并证明领先间隔过滤与Mamba的常数内存开销对临床连续部署的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中低血压是全身麻醉常见且后果严重的并发症，但临床管理仍是被动反应式而非预测式。现有预测模型忽略了药物输注史这一具有直接药理学相关性的关键信号，且部分模型可能学成"检测正在发生的低血压"而非真正预测未来事件。

Method: 在预测模型中显式引入药物输注史（丙泊酚与瑞芬太尼的效应室浓度）；采用领先间隔（lead-gap）过滤的训练策略以避免信息泄漏；并提出基于Mamba的序列建模架构，以实现随序列长度保持恒定的内存占用。

Result: AUROC达0.7360，AUPRC达0.1794，相较于随机猜测基线（0.0657）提升2.73倍；移除丙泊酚与瑞芬太尼效应室浓度后AUPRC相对完整模型下降13.9%；不使用领先间隔过滤训练时AUROC下降16.7%；Mamba架构在保持高性能的同时，内存占用不随序列长度增长，无Transformer的二次方显存开销。

Conclusion: 药代动力学轨迹在平均动脉压（MAP）变化显现之前即已编码即将发生血流动力学改变的信息，药物输注史是有效预测信号；领先间隔过滤是确保模型真正"预测"而非"检测"的必要条件；Mamba因常数内存开销，比原版Transformer更适合术中连续部署。

Abstract: An Intraoperative Hypotension (IOH) event is a frequent complication during administration of general anaesthesia with serious downstream consequences, yet clinical management remains reactive and not predictive. Existing predictive models, however, ignore drug infusion history as a valuable signal for prediction despite its direct pharmacological relevance. Our model achieves an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.7360 and an Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC) of 0.1794, representing a 2.73-fold lift over the random guessing AUPRC baseline (0.0657), with the removal of propofol and remifentanil effect-site concentrations resulting in a 13.9% AUPRC drop compared to the full model. This is consistent with the hypothesis that pharmacokinetic trajectories encode impending haemodynamic changes before they manifest in the Mean Arterial Pressure (MAP). Additionally, this paper shows that training without lead-gap filtering degraded AUROC by 16.7%, empirically confirming that unfiltered models learn to detect ongoing hypotension rather than predict future events. Finally, a Mamba-based architecture achieves the aforementioned high prediction performance while maintaining a constant memory footprint across a range of sequence lengths, unlike the quadratic VRAM overhead typical of vanilla Transformers, making it the more practical choice for continuous intraoperative deployment.

</details>


### [236] [Explainable Neuro-Fuzzy Prediction for Trustworthy Decision-Making in Maritime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24358)
*Dionisis Kalogeropoulos,Georgia Sovatzidi,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释决策框架，将神经模糊预测模型与两阶段可解释组件结合：第一阶段用梯度显著性图生成特征归因解释，第二阶段用模糊决策树提取局部规则，可嵌入任意深度学习模型，在通用基准和海事推进系统早期故障检测任务上实现最高约99%的AUC-ROC并兼具可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事系统维护时机的预测对避免危险与高昂后果至关重要，但深度学习黑箱模型缺乏可解释性，难以建立决策信任，因此需要兼顾高预测性能与用户可理解解释的方法。

Method: 提出一个可解释决策框架，集成神经模糊预测模型与两阶段可解释组件。第一阶段基于梯度的显著性图生成特征归因解释；第二阶段利用模糊决策树提取局部规则。该框架具有通用性，可集成到任意基于深度学习的方法中，实现特征级与局部规则级的双重解释。采用深度残差神经网络作为骨干进行验证。

Result: 在多个通用公开基准数据集上评估，并在海军推进系统数据集的早期故障检测场景中验证其海事应用价值。结果显示其预测性能优于相关最先进方法，平均AUC-ROC最高达99%，同时具备可解释性优势。

Conclusion: 该框架是首个基于模糊逻辑、可同时提供黑箱模型特征级与局部规则级解释的方法，通过用户可理解的机器推断增强决策可信度，兼顾性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Predicting when maritime systems require maintenance can be critical, avoiding hazards and costly consequences. To address this problem, this paper proposes an explainable decision-making framework that integrates a neuro-fuzzy prediction model with a two-stage explainable component. The first stage of this component produces feature-attribution explanations, using gradient-based saliency maps, and the second stage extracts local rules using a fuzzy decision tree. The proposed framework is generic and can be integrated into any deep learning-based approach, rendering it explainable. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first fuzzy logic-based framework enabling both feature-level and local rule-based explanations of black box models. This approach aims to foster trustworthiness in decision making through user-understandable machine inferences. The performance of the proposed framework using a deep residual-based neural backbone is evaluated on various general-purpose public benchmark datasets, and its utility in maritime is demonstrated in the context of early fault detection in a naval propulsion system dataset. The results indicate that it can provide predictions outperforming relevant state-of-the-art approaches, with an average AUC-ROC (Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve) value, reaching up to 99%, while offering the advantage of explainability.

</details>


### [237] [Prescriptive SVD-Inspired Attention via Spectral Energy Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24370)
*Vasileios Arampatzakis,Vasileios Sevetlidis,George Pavlidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“诊断—干预—验证”框架，评估SVDA注意力中谱能量保留干预的效果，在四个数据集上以ρ=0.90移除24.5–53.7%的分数方向，参数量与MACs分别降2.6–4.3%与2.8–5.4%，精度变化仅−0.03至+0.05个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer的自注意力采用稠密点积形式，内部哪些方向结构重要、哪些可被修改而不破坏模型，难以识别。SVDA通过在学习到的对角谱中引入查询—键分数交互，使潜在注意力方向可被谱熵、有效秩、稀疏度等指标检验，但此前工作停留在诊断解释层面，缺乏从诊断到可操作干预的过渡研究。

Method: 提出诊断—干预—验证框架，并评估一种干预：在注意力分数通路上进行谱能量保留（以ρ=0.90的谱能量保留处方为依据，对分数方向做维度约简），在FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Food-101上进行验证，并与未约简模型配对比较（三个随机种子）。

Result: 在四个数据集上，ρ=0.90处方移除24.5–53.7%的分数方向；参数量减少2.6–4.3%，估计MACs减少2.8–5.4%；维度约简模型的配对平均精度变化为−0.03至+0.05个百分点（三个种子）。

Conclusion: 结果支持将SVDA视为一种内在可解释的注意力机制：其学习到的谱暴露了一个可操作的坐标系，使注意力分数形成过程能够被确定性且可验证地修改。该工作把谱诊断从解释工具推进为干预与验证手段。

Abstract: Self-attention is central to modern Transformer architectures, but its dense dot-product formulation makes it difficult to identify which internal directions are structurally important and which can be modified without disrupting the model. SVD-Inspired Attention (SVDA) addresses part of this problem by introducing a learned diagonal spectrum into the query-key score interaction, making latent attention directions explicitly inspectable through indicators such as spectral entropy, effective rank, sparsity, alignment, selectivity, and perturbation response. This paper examines the transition from diagnostic interpretation to operational intervention. A diagnosis--intervention--verification framework is proposed, and one intervention is evaluated: spectral energy retention in the attention-score pathway. Across FashionMNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Food-101, the $ρ=0.90$ prescription removes 24.5--53.7\% of score directions, reduces parameters by 2.6--4.3\%, and reduces estimated MACs by 2.8--5.4\%. The paired mean accuracy change of the dimension-reduced model ranges from $-0.03$ to $+0.05$ percentage points over three seeds. These results support SVDA as an intrinsically interpretable attention mechanism whose learned spectrum exposes an operational coordinate system for deterministic and verifiable modification of attention-score formation.

</details>


### [238] [Credit Access is Associated with Improved Food Security in the Horn of Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24382)
*Jordi Cerdà-Bautista,Vasileios Sitokonstantinou,José Manuel Veiga López-Peña,Duccio Piovani,José María Tárraga,Gustau Camps-Valls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用观察性机器学习框架，估计索马里及非洲之角信贷可及性与急性粮食不安全之间的因果关联，发现信贷可及性提高与急性粮食不安全下降约2%相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化加剧威胁粮食安全，脆弱社区尤为突出；在数据稀缺、危机频发地区难以开展随机评估，需借助观察性机器学习方法整合异质数据，评估金融可及性等社会经济因素对粮食安全的作用。

Method: 构建观察性机器学习框架，基于2015—2022年涵盖环境、社会经济与冲突相关因素的对齐数据集，估计信贷可及性与急性粮食不安全的因果关联；在明确的识别假设下解释结果，并进行稳健性与反驳检验。

Result: 信贷可及性提高与研究期内人群层面急性粮食不安全下降2%相关；鉴于平均约16%人口处于危机状态，该效应在风险群体中具有实质意义。

Conclusion: 研究提供了金融可及性与粮食安全结果在数据稀缺、危机受影响情境下的具体情境证据，并为无法开展随机评估时整合异质数据源提供了透明框架。

Abstract: The intensification of climate change poses a growing threat to food security, especially in vulnerable communities. This study employs an observational machine-learning framework to estimate the causal association between access to credit and acute food insecurity in Somalia and across the Horn of Africa, drawing on a harmonized dataset spanning key environmental, socioeconomic, and conflict-related factors from 2015 to 2022. Results indicate that greater credit access is associated with a 2% reduction in acute food insecurity at the population level over the study period. Given that, on average, 16% of the population is in crisis, this effect represents a meaningful shift within the at-risk group. We interpret these estimates under explicit identification assumptions and complement them with robustness and refutation tests. The results provide context-specific evidence on how financial access correlates with food security outcomes in data-scarce, crisis-affected settings, and offer a transparent framework for integrating heterogeneous data sources when randomized evaluations are infeasible.

</details>


### [239] [Machine Learning-Based Prediction of Childhood Stunting in Bangladesh: Fairness and Temporal Robustness Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24386)
*Md Ahshanul Haque,Muhammad Ashad Kabir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用2007–2022年孟加拉国人口与健康调查数据，评估11种机器学习模型（含TabPFN）对0–59月龄儿童发育迟缓的预测能力，并检验时间稳健性与亚组公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童发育迟缓在孟加拉国仍是重大公共卫生问题，反映儿童、母亲、家庭、社会经济及卫生服务等多层面因素的长期生长失败；需要在人群层面建模预测，并检验模型跨时间与跨亚组的稳健性和公平性。

Method: 使用2007–2022年全国代表性BDHS数据，纳入0–59月龄且人体测量与预测变量完整的儿童共18,844名。2007、2011、2014三轮用于模型开发，2018、2022轮作为时间外部测试集。评估12种特征选择方法，最终采用KNN置换重要性所选预测变量集；比较10种常规算法与预训练表格基础模型TabPFN共11个模型，指标包括平衡准确率、AUROC、F1、Brier分数和期望校准误差；并按儿童性别、居住地和社会经济status进行亚组公平性分析。

Result: 样本中35.05%的儿童存在发育迟缓。开发集留出测试中，TabPFN总体平衡准确率最高（67.58%），常规模型中AdaBoost最高（67.51%）。时间测试中，BDHS 2018以Gradient Boosting最高，BDHS 2022以XGBoost最高；模型性能在不同调查轮次和亚组间存在差异。

Conclusion: 公共卫生预测建模中应重视时间验证、亚组公平性评估与透明解释；TabPFN等模型在开发集表现良好，但跨时间与跨亚组的性能变化限制了其直接外推应用。

Abstract: Childhood stunting remains a major public health concern in Bangladesh and reflects long-term growth failure influenced by child, maternal, household, socioeconomic, and health-service factors. This study used nationally representative Bangladesh Demographic and Health Survey data from 2007 to 2022 to develop machine learning models for population-level prediction of childhood stunting and to assess temporal robustness and subgroup fairness. Children aged 0-59 months with complete anthropometric and predictor data were included. Data from the 2007, 2011, and 2014 survey rounds were used for model development, while the 2018 and 2022 rounds were retained as temporal test datasets. Twelve feature-selection approaches were assessed, and the KNN permutation importance-selected predictor set was used for final model evaluation. Eleven machine learning models were evaluated: ten conventional algorithms and one pretrained tabular foundation model, TabPFN. Performance was assessed using balanced accuracy, AUROC, F1-score, Brier score, and expected calibration error. Subgroup fairness was examined by child sex, place of residence, and socioeconomic status. The final analytic sample included 18,844 children, of whom 35.05% were stunted. In the development hold-out test dataset, TabPFN showed the highest observed balanced accuracy overall at 67.58%, while AdaBoost showed the highest observed balanced accuracy among conventional models at 67.51%. In temporal testing, the highest observed balanced accuracy was found for Gradient Boosting in BDHS 2018 and XGBoost in BDHS 2022. Model performance varied across survey rounds and subgroups, highlighting the importance of temporal validation, subgroup fairness assessment, and transparent interpretation in public health prediction modeling.

</details>


### [240] [NAVIR: Neuromorphic Audio-Visual Speech Recognition for Robust Human-Robot Interaction on Edge Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24391)
*Leonidas Delimpasis,Panagiota Moraiti,Antonis Porichis,Panos Chatzakos,Michail Karamousadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 NAVIR，一个面向 BrainChip Akida 神经形态处理器的端到端音视频语音识别（AVSR）系统，通过将时空编码分解为多个基于 AkidaNet 的二维卷积模块，在 GRID 基准和工业指令语料上以显著更低的能耗达到优于纯音频基线的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中的声学噪声严重破坏纯音频语音识别。音视频语音识别（AVSR）虽能通过融合唇动线索缓解该问题，但现有最优方案依赖三维卷积、循环单元和注意力模块，超出典型边缘设备的算力/功耗预算。作者希望将 AVSR 落地到仅原生支持顺序二维卷积推理的神经形态硬件（BrainChip Akida）上。

Method: NAVIR 将空间与时间编码分解为三个独立的 AkidaNet 模块：逐帧视觉编码器、时序视频编码器和频谱音频编码器；由轻量预测头进行融合，并用受限束搜索解码。模型先用带噪声增强音频上的 CTC 损失训练，再进行量化感知微调。

Result: 在 GRID 基准上，量化音视频模型在未见说话人划分的噪声条件下达到 14.0% WER（纯音频基线为 22.5%），在重叠说话人划分上为 3.3% WER（基线 11.8%）；在任务特定的工业指令语料上达到 98.6% 指令准确率、1.5% WER。运算量分析显示，在 27.6% 平均发放率下，脉冲形式的能效比其 ANN 对应版本高约 13 倍。板载实测表明，唇读模型每次推理能耗比 Raspberry Pi CPU 低约 5 倍、比笔记本 GPU 低 100 倍以上，同时维持 14.5 次/秒的推理速度。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个在该类神经形态硬件上运行的完整多模态 AVSR 流水线，验证了在严格边缘功耗预算下实现噪声鲁棒音视频语音识别的可行性。

Abstract: Voice-controlled interaction in industrial settings is hampered by acoustic noise, which severely degrades audio-only speech recognition. Audio-visual speech recognition (AVSR) addresses this by fusing lip-motion cues with the audio stream, but state-of-the-art pipelines rely on three-dimensional convolutions, recurrent units, and attention modules that exceed the budget of typical edge devices. We present NAVIR, an end-to-end AVSR system targeting the BrainChip Akida neuromorphic processor, which natively supports only sequential two-dimensional convolutional inference. The pipeline factorises spatial and temporal encoding into separate AkidaNet-based modules: a per-frame visual encoder, a temporal video encoder, and a spectrogram audio encoder, fused by a lightweight predictor head and decoded by constrained beam search. Models are trained with connectionist temporal classification on noise-augmented audio and then fine-tuned with quantization-aware training. On the GRID benchmark, the quantized audio-visual model reaches 14.0% word error rate (WER) under noise on the unseen-speaker split and 3.3% WER on the overlapped-speaker split, against 22.5% and 11.8% for audio-only baselines, and it attains 98.6% command accuracy at 1.5% WER on a task-specific industrial-command corpus. Operation-count analysis indicates a 13-fold energy advantage of the spiking formulation over its artificial neural network counterpart at 27.6% mean firing rate. On-board measurements show roughly 5-fold lower energy per inference than a Raspberry Pi central processing unit on the lip-reading model, and over 100-fold lower than a laptop graphics processing unit, while sustaining 14.5 inferences per second. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first complete multimodal AVSR pipeline running on neuromorphic hardware of this class.

</details>


### [241] [Climate Variability Modulates the Impact of Price Spikes on Food Insecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24394)
*Jordi Cerdà-Bautista,Vasileios Sitokonstantinou,Homer Durand,Gherardo Varando,Michele Ronco,Gustau Camps-Valls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出"敏感性机制区"（sensitivity regimes）概念，即依据植被对厄尔尼诺-南方涛动（ENSO）响应的方向与强度对区域分层，并结合遥感、社会经济数据与因果机器学习，量化撒哈拉以南非洲粮食价格飙升对急性粮食不安全的影响。结果显示，在ENSO系统性抑制植被的区域，价格飙升使下月处于急性风险的人口比例上升5.4个百分点；而在植被不受影响或正相关的区域，效应较小（约2个百分点）且统计不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变率决定了市场扰动是否会升级为粮食危机，但像ENSO这样可在数月前被监测的大尺度气候模式，尚未被纳入粮食安全响应的预警体系，导致脆弱区域识别滞后、应对被动。

Method: 引入"敏感性机制区"概念，按植被对ENSO响应的方向与强度对区域进行分层；融合遥感数据、社会经济数据与因果机器学习方法，估计粮食价格飙升对撒哈拉以南非洲急性粮食不安全的影响。

Result: 在ENSO系统性抑制植被的区域，价格飙升使下月处于急性粮食不安全风险的人口比例上升5.4个百分点；在植被不受ENSO影响或呈正相关的区域，该效应较小（约2个百分点）且统计上不显著，表明气候背景对理解粮食安全脆弱性至关重要。

Conclusion: 气候背景是理解粮食安全脆弱性的关键。敏感性机制区可与现有的价格飙升触发机制结合用于前瞻性行动：ENSO状态可提前数月标示脆弱区域，在这些区域预先部署应对措施，能够避免价格飙升引发的急性粮食不安全最大幅度上升。

Abstract: Climate variability influences whether a market disruption escalates into a food crisis, yet broad climate patterns like El Niño, tracked months before they alter hydro-climatic conditions, are still not incorporated as an early-warning component in food-security responses. We address this gap by introducing sensitivity regimes, a stratification of regions by the direction and strength of their vegetation response to the El Niño Southern Oscillation, and using them to estimate how food price spikes affect acute food insecurity across sub-Saharan Africa. Integrating remote sensing, socioeconomic data, and causal machine learning, we find that in regions where ENSO systematically suppresses vegetation, a price spike raises the share of the population at acute risk by 5.4 percentage points in the following month. In regions where vegetation is unaffected by or positively linked to ENSO, the estimated effect is smaller (around 2 percentage points) and statistically insignificant. These results demonstrate that climate context is critical for understanding food security vulnerabilities. Sensitivity regimes can be combined with operational price-spike triggers to stage anticipatory action: the ENSO state flags vulnerable regions months ahead, and a pre-positioned response in those regions to a price spike would avert the largest jump in acute food insecurity.

</details>


### [242] [Probabilistic Modelling of Operational Design Domains, A New Approach for Testing AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24397)
*Hans-Werner Wiesbrock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对ML系统（如车辆障碍物检测）测试无法用经典缺陷修复流程处理、需统计解释测试结果的问题，提出"概率扩展本体"（PEONs），通过本体描述ODD并附加其划分上的概率分布，以边缘分布和函数式依赖替代复杂的条件概率表，借助耦合与最优传输算法补全为贝叶斯网络，从而支持代表性测试用例采样、严格的测试终止判定及对已有测试结果与训练数据平衡性的评估，并以自动列车运行验证其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试流程在ML系统上失效：若测试中未检测到障碍物，无法沿用经典缺陷修复，AI系统会持续存在缺陷；测试结果只能作统计解释，因而要求测试集既覆盖又代表系统的运行设计域（ODD）。同时，完整定义高维ODD上的联合概率分布（如条件概率表）不可维护。

Method: 提出概率扩展本体（PEONs）：在本体描述ODD的基础上，附加其诱导划分上的概率分布。仅需指定边缘分布和函数式描述的依赖关系，再通过基于耦合（couplings）与最优传输（optimal transport）的算法补全为完整贝叶斯网络，避免不可维护的条件概率表。基于PEON推导代表性测试用例采样方法、给定质量目标与显著性水平下的严格测试终止准则，以及重评估已有测试结果与评估训练数据平衡性的方法。

Result: 以自动列车运行（automatic train operation）为例，展示了复杂ODD的实际建模，验证了PEON在ODD描述、概率规范及衍生测试方法上的可行性。

Conclusion: PEONs为ML系统提供了一种可维护、可扩展的ODD概率建模与方法框架，使测试集具备相对于ODD的完备性与代表性，并支撑统计意义上的测试终止判定、结果重评估与训练数据平衡性评估。

Abstract: The conventional testing process quickly fails when applied to ML-based systems such as obstacle detection in vehicles: if an obstacle is not detected in a test, classical bug fixing is impossible and an AI system will always retain shortcomings. Test results can therefore only be interpreted statistically, which in turn requires test sets that are not only complete with respect to the operational design domain (ODD) of the system, but also representative of it. To this end, we introduce probabilistically extended ontologies (PEONs): ontologies describing the ODD, augmented with a probability distribution over the partitioning they induce. Instead of unmaintainable conditional probability tables, only marginal distributions and functionally described dependencies need to be specified; algorithms based on couplings and optimal transport complete this specification to a Bayesian network. From a PEON we derive the sampling of representative test cases, rigorous end-of-test criteria for given quality targets and significance levels, and methods for re-evaluating existing test results and for assessing the balance of training data. We demonstrate the practical modelling of a complex ODD using the example of automatic train operation.

</details>


### [243] [Prior-Amortized In-Context Bayesian Inference for Generalized Linear Mixed-Effects Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24422)
*Alex Kipnis,Marcel Binz,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: metabeta是一个预训练神经网络，实现GLMM的、先验摊销（prior-amortized）的上下文贝叶斯推断：测试时可将先验族与超参数作为输入，实现零样本泛化；通过双层结构（集合Transformer + 条件归一化流）镜像后验的全局/局部参数结构，并用独立性Metropolis-Hastings或NUTS热启动精化，速度比NUTS快2–3个数量级，精度与NUTS相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层数据在实证科学中普遍存在，常用广义线性混合模型（GLMM）分析。GLMM的贝叶斯推断可提供校准的不确定性，但依赖MCMC；NUTS虽为金标准，却速度慢，且每遇新数据集、新模型或新先验都需从头重启。已有神经后验估计方法在训练时固定先验，无法灵活应对测试时的不同先验设定，因此需要一种可零样本泛化、快速且无需调参的贝叶斯推断方案。

Method: 提出metabeta：面向GLMM的预训练神经网络，进行先验摊销的上下文贝叶斯推断。测试时将先验族及超参数作为输入，实现零样本泛化。采用两个集合Transformer与条件归一化流，分别对应后验的两级结构：跨组共享的全局参数与每组各自的局部参数。训练数据为覆盖连续、二值与计数型结果的数百万个真实感模拟数据集。默认以独立性Metropolis-Hastings针对未归一化后验精化流式后验，使正确性由采样器而非网络保证；也可用该流后验热启动NUTS。

Result: 在具有真值参数的可控基准上，metabeta在参数恢复、校准与样本外预测方面与NUTS相当。默认MH精化实现无需调参的推断，速度比NUTS快两到三个数量级；以流热启动NUTS可获得几乎一致的推断，同时显著提升速度与稳定性。在分布外的真实数据集上，其各类参数后验与NUTS高度吻合，并在似然与先验误设、分布外预测变量、共线设计及数据稀缺情形下仍保持可靠。

Conclusion: metabeta为GLMM提供了先验摊销、上下文式的贝叶斯推断，兼顾NUTS级别的精度与远超NUTS的速度，具备对先验族与超参数的零样本泛化能力及强鲁棒性。模型开源且权重开放，可即时部署。

Abstract: Hierarchical data is ubiquitous in the empirical sciences and is most commonly analyzed with generalized linear mixed-effects models (GLMMs). Bayesian inference for GLMMs yields calibrated uncertainty but requires MCMC; the No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS) is the gold standard but is slow and must restart from scratch for every new dataset, model and prior. We introduce metabeta, a pretrained neural network for prior-amortized in-context Bayesian inference over GLMMs. Unlike previous neural posterior estimators that fix the prior at training time, metabeta accepts prior families and hyperparameters as inputs at test time, enabling zero-shot generalization. Two set transformers and conditional normalizing flows mirror the posterior's two-level structure (global parameters shared across groups, local parameters per group). The model is trained on millions of realistic simulated datasets spanning continuous, binary, and count outcomes. By default, the flow posterior is refined by Independence Metropolis-Hastings against the unnormalized posterior, so its correctness rests on the sampler rather than the network; this yields tuning-free inference two to three orders of magnitude faster than NUTS. Alternatively, the flow can warm-start NUTS, giving nearly identical inference with substantially increased speed and stability. On controlled benchmarks with ground-truth parameters, metabeta matches NUTS in parameter recovery, calibration and out-of-sample prediction. On out-of-distribution real datasets, its posteriors closely match those of NUTS across all parameter types, and they remain faithful under misspecified likelihoods and priors, out-of-distribution predictors, collinear designs, and data-poor regimes. The model is open-source and open-weights and thus immediately deployable.

</details>


### [244] [1% of Tokens Can Be Enough: On Gradient Estimation in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24432)
*Huanxin Sheng,Zhiling Ye,Haonan Wang,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Jian Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究稀疏在线策略蒸馏中的梯度估计问题，提出信息效率比（IER）指标，用于在固定前缀下衡量教师监督的相对梯度估计误差，并结合候选集近似实现 token 选择，在数学与医学推理任务上提升了现有选择器，0.1%–1% 稀疏预算即可匹配或超越全量 OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏在线策略蒸馏（OPD）只在学生生成轨迹的一小部分 token 上施加教师监督。然而，即使教师指导是有用的，当梯度由采样的下一个 token 估计时，仍可能产生噪声更新，即"有用性"与"估计可靠性"之间存在冲突。作者因此希望在信息几何框架下研究固定前缀处的估计问题，为稀疏监督分配同时考虑有用性和梯度估计可靠性。

Method: 在固定前缀处以信息几何视角分析梯度估计问题，基于信噪分解提出信息效率比（IER），刻画在最优标量基线下的相对梯度估计误差。引入候选集近似，使 token 选择可基于 IER 及其与现有有用性评分的组合进行，同时保留采样的反向 KL 训练目标。

Result: 在数学与医学推理任务上，加入 IER 在多种设置下改进了现有选择器；在 0.1%–1% 的小 token 预算下，稀疏配置可匹配或超过不进行 token 选择的全量 OPD。

Conclusion: 结果支持在分配稀疏监督时同时考虑有用性与梯度估计可靠性。代码已开源于 https://github.com/BruceSheng1202/IER-OPD。

Abstract: Sparse on-policy distillation (OPD) allocates teacher supervision to a small subset of tokens in student-generated trajectories. However, useful teacher guidance can yield a noisy update when its gradient is estimated from a sampled next token. We study this estimation problem at a fixed prefix in information geometry and propose an information-efficiency ratio (IER) based on a signal-to-noise decomposition. IER characterizes relative gradient estimation error under an optimal scalar baseline. A candidate-set approximation enables token selection based on IER and its combination with existing usefulness scores, while retaining the sampled reverse-KL training objective. On mathematical and medical reasoning tasks, adding IER improves existing selectors in multiple settings, with sparse configurations matching or exceeding full OPD without token selection at small token budgets of 0.1\%--1\%. These results support accounting for both usefulness and gradient-estimation reliability when allocating sparse supervision. Our code is available at https://github.com/BruceSheng1202/IER-OPD.

</details>


### [245] [Comparing Latent Concept Formation in State Space Models and Transformers via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24440)
*Rithin Nagaraj,Rupa Laalasa Oruganti,Prerna Subhashchandra Kunder,Ashwini M Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过稀疏自编码器对Mamba-130m与Pythia-70m进行特征级对应分析，发现99.98%的特征高度对齐，表征分歧仅占0.02%且集中于刚性句法边缘情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer自注意力的二次复杂度推动了Mamba等亚二次选择性状态空间模型的采用，但其将历史上下文压缩进固定大小循环隐状态，构成严格的信息瓶颈。这引出一个机制可解释性的基础问题：SSM与Transformer是否学习到本质不同的潜在表征。

Method: 在千万级token语料上，使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）对Mamba-130m与Pythia-70m开展大规模特征级对应分析，考察特征匹配的Jaccard分布，并对少数分歧特征进行定性刻画。

Result: 未发现架构间系统性表征分歧：在观测到的Jaccard分布中，99.98%的Mamba特征聚集于对齐上界，为"普适性假说"提供初步特征级支持。对0.02%分歧特征的刻画显示，循环瓶颈选择性地限制了对刚性句法的解析，而非广泛的语义本体；Pythia的无约束注意力可对特定格式边缘情形做单义分解，而Mamba被迫将无关句法异常压缩进多义"杂物抽屉"神经元以保留状态容量。

Conclusion: 架构路由机制对核心语义理解的影响可能可忽略，表征分歧局限于极端结构性边缘情形。

Abstract: The quadratic scaling of Transformer self-attention has driven the adoption of sub-quadratic Selective State Space Models (SSMs) like Mamba, which compress past context into a fixed-size recurrent hidden state. This strict informational bottleneck raises a foundational question for mechanistic interpretability: do SSMs and Transformers learn fundamentally distinct latent representations? In this work, we employ Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to conduct a large-scale, feature-level correspondence analysis between Mamba-130m and Pythia-70m over a 10-million token corpus. Contrary to hypotheses predicting widespread architectural divergence, we find no evidence of systematic representational divergence between architectures: across the observed Jaccard distribution, 99.98% of Mamba features cluster toward the upper alignment boundary, providing preliminary feature-level support for the Universality Hypothesis. We further identify and qualitatively characterize this microscopic fraction (0.02%) of diverging features, finding patterns consistent with the hypothesis that the recurrent bottleneck selectively limits the parsing of rigid syntax rather than broad semantic ontology. We demonstrate that while Pythia's unconstrained attention permits the monosemantic decomposition of distinct formatting edge-cases, Mamba is forced to compress unrelated syntactical anomalies into polysemantic "junk drawer" neurons to preserve state capacity. Collectively, these results suggest that architectural routing mechanisms may have negligible impact on core semantic understanding, with representational divergence confined to extreme structural margins.

</details>


### [246] [MUSE: Dependency-Aware Adaptation of a Frozen Vision Backbone for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24441)
*Xinying Cai,Junkai Lu,Yuhan Zhu,Xiaoyun Yu,Xiangfei Qiu,Jilin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MUSE框架，基于完全冻结的预训练MAE，通过变量上下文精炼（VCR）和时间-周期精炼（TPR）两个模块实现依赖感知的自适应，在10个真实数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测应用广泛，近期大视觉模型（LVMs）通过迁移跨域视觉先验提供新范式，但现有LVM方法存在两大挑战：难以平衡独立视觉表征空间与跨变量依赖建模；难以将自然图像预训练的视觉骨干适配到时间序列图像特有的时间语义。

Method: MUSE建立在完全冻结的预训练MAE之上。VCR模块在每个变量内聚合共享时间信息，并在保持独立视觉空间的同时建模跨变量上下文依赖；TPR模块在不同编码器深度进行轻量级精炼，显式建模跨周期时间依赖与周期内周期依赖。两模块独立产生预测，再通过可学习的预测级门控进行融合。

Result: 在10个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，MUSE达到最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: MUSE通过依赖感知的适配框架，有效解决了LVM迁移到时间序列预测中的表征空间平衡与语义适配问题，验证了冻结视觉骨干结合轻量精炼模块的有效性。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series forecasting is essential to many real-world applications. Recent large vision models (LVMs) offer a promising paradigm by transferring cross-domain visual priors to time-series forecasting. However, existing LVM-based methods face two key challenges: balancing independent visual representation spaces with cross-variable dependency modeling, and adapting vision backbones pretrained on natural images to the distinct temporal semantics of time-series images. To address these challenges, we propose MUSE, a dependency-aware adaptation framework built on a fully frozen pretrained MAE. First, the Variable Context Refinement Module (VCR) aggregates shared temporal information within each variable and models cross-variable contextual dependencies while preserving independent visual spaces. Second, the Temporal-Periodic Refinement Module (TPR) performs lightweight refinement at different encoder depths and explicitly models across-period temporal dependencies and within-period periodic dependencies. The two modules independently produce forecasts, which are fused through a learnable prediction-level gate. Experiments on 10 real-world datasets demonstrate that MUSE achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [247] [RAILS: Retrieval-Augmented Incremental LLM Clustering at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24464)
*Armin Oliya,Aleksandra Sawczuk,Radosław Białobrzeski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RAILS，一种检索增强的增量式 LLM 聚类器，通过增长的标签池与有界并发批处理解决生产规模下提示容量受限和吞吐不足问题，在六个基准上超越最强 LLM 聚类方法，并已在 SaaS 工单主题发现中替代 HDBSCAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产规模下用 LLM 做聚类很困难：提示无法容纳整个标签空间，且逐文档串行处理无法满足真实工作负载所需的吞吐量。因此需要一种能扩展、增量且受控的 LLM 聚类方案。

Method: RAILS 将聚类转化为对不断增长的标签池的简单循环，并采用检索增强机制选取相关标签；同时通过文档批处理与有界并发实现规模化，支持有状态增量运行。

Result: 在六个公开基准上平均优于最强先前的 LLM 聚类方法：准确率由 51.2% 提升至 59.3%，NMI 由 67.2% 提升至 74.8%，ARI 由 45.4% 提升至 54.7%。在生产 SaaS 工单主题发现流水线中替代传统 HDBSCAN 阶段，获得更高聚类质量、透明的提示驱动控制与有状态增量能力。

Conclusion: RAILS 通过检索增强、增量标签池与有界并发批处理，有效克服了 LLM 聚类在生产规模下的提示容量与吞吐瓶颈，在基准与真实生产部署中均验证了其优越性、可控性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Using a Large Language Model (LLM) as the clusterer at production scale is hard: prompts cannot hold the entire label space, and per-document serial processing does not deliver the throughput real workloads require. We present RAILS, a retrieval-augmented incremental LLM clusterer that turns clustering into a simple loop over a growing label pool and scales through document batching with bounded concurrency. On six public benchmarks RAILS exceeds the strongest prior LLM-clustering method on average, lifting accuracy from 51.2% to 59.3%, NMI from 67.2% to 74.8%, and ARI from 45.4% to 54.7%. We further report production-deployment evidence from a SaaS ticket-topic-discovery pipeline, where RAILS has replaced a traditional HDBSCAN stage with higher clustering quality, transparent prompt-driven control, and stateful incremental operation.

</details>


### [248] [A Temporal Knowledge Graph for Music Festival Lineup Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24467)
*Julia Gastinger,Thilo Dieing,Christian Meilicke,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个覆盖55年、380个音乐节、逾9万条演出四元组的时间知识图谱（TKG）资源，将音乐节阵容预测形式化为未来时间戳下艺术家与音乐节之间的时序链接预测任务，并评测了六种TKG预测模型及零样本大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐节阵容由艺术家、流派、发行作品、厂牌与历史演出之间的复杂关系共同决定，天然适合用时间知识图谱预测来建模。然而现有TKG基准缺乏具体真实应用领域的支撑，因此需要一个面向实际场景的评估资源。

Method: 构建了包含55年间380个音乐节、超过9万条音乐节演出四元组的时间知识图谱，并整合音乐节信息、艺术家巡演信息与艺术家元数据；将音乐节阵容预测定义为未来时间戳上艺术家—音乐节之间的时序链接预测任务，在该任务上评测六种TKG预测模型，并与零样本设置下的大语言模型进行对比。

Result: 在自建TKG资源上完成了六种TKG预测模型的评测，分析了各模型的能力与局限，并与零样本大语言模型的表现进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该资源通过将评估落地于具体真实应用领域，对现有TKG基准形成补充，为时间知识图谱预测评估提供了新的场景与基准。

Abstract: Music festival lineups emerge from complex relationships among artists, genres, releases, labels, and past performances, making the prediction of future lineups a natural fit for temporal knowledge graph (TKG) forecasting. In this work, we present a TKG covering 380 festivals over 55 years, comprising more than 90K festival performance quadruples along with information on festivals, artist tours, and artist metadata, and release it as a resource for TKG forecasting evaluation. We formalize festival lineup forecasting as temporal link prediction between artists and festivals at future timestamps. We evaluate six TKG forecasting models on this task, analyze their capabilities and limitations, and compare them against Large Language Models applied zero-shot. Our resource complements existing TKG benchmarks by grounding evaluation in a concrete, real-world application domain.

</details>


### [249] [Universal Multi-Modal Traceformer: Integrating Heterogeneous Context for Process Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24579)
*Fabian Spaeh,Jingxing Fang,Shandian Zhe,Bin Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一多模态Traceformer（UMT）框架，将异构过程上下文融入下一事件预测，在13个真实事件日志上同时提升活动与时间预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件序列模型（含多数时间点过程方法）主要建模事件活动与时间戳，忽略了异构上下文信息，如数值测量、类别属性、文本描述以及事件级与轨迹级的元数据。

Method: 基于Transformer骨干网络构建UMT：1）通用特征编码器，将多种特征类型映射到共享表示空间，并在事件级与轨迹级处理上下文；2）逐事件Perceiver模块，动态加权上下文特征并自适应地融入事件token表示；3）针对到达间隔时间的重尾与潜在多模态分布，在多时间尺度上表示间隔并联合预测各尺度对应的量。

Result: 在13个真实世界事件日志上的实验表明，UMT在下一事件活动预测与时间预测两项任务上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: UMT作为一种统一框架，能够有效整合异构多模态过程上下文，验证了上下文建模对下一事件预测的价值，并为事件序列建模提供了通用方案。

Abstract: Event logs arise in a wide range of real-world processes, capturing not only event activities and timestamps but also multi-modal contextual information. Existing event-sequence models, including many temporal point process approaches, primarily model event activities and timestamps while overlooking heterogeneous context, such as numerical measurements, categorical attributes, textual descriptions, and metadata associated with individual events and entire traces. In this paper, we propose Universal Multi-Modal Traceformer (UMT), a unified framework for incorporating heterogeneous process context into next-event prediction. Built on a Transformer backbone, UMT introduces a universal feature encoder that maps diverse feature types into a shared representation space and handles contextual information at both the event and trace levels. UMT further develops a per-event Perceiver module that dynamically weights contextual features and adaptively integrates them into event-token representations. To accommodate the heavy-tailed and potentially multi-modal distribution of inter-arrival times, UMT represents each interval at multiple temporal scales and jointly predicts the corresponding scale-specific quantities. Experiments on 13 real-world event logs show that UMT improves both next-event activity and time prediction over existing approaches.

</details>


### [250] [Taking a Second Look: Correcting Sea Ice Forecasts with Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24591)
*Tianshuo Zhang,Xianglei Xing,Aowen Yang,Jia Gao,Wenzhe Zhai,ShanShan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出固定传播的海冰浓度订正误差集中于高梯度冰缘，提出按状态自适应传播距离的ECHO方法（ECHO-Scale与ECHO-Delta），在96种标准评估设定下均优于固定传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海冰预报提前数天发布，期间误差累积，而新的海冰浓度观测往往稀疏。固定传播的订正方法在高梯度、结构化的冰缘处误差集中，而均质的冰内部仅需有限传播，说明传播距离应当依赖于状态。

Method: 提出ECHO（Evidence-guided Correction with Heterogeneous prOpagation），包含两个变体：ECHO-Scale在保持订正几何结构的前提下自适应调整传播距离；ECHO-Delta在固定传播基础上学习有界残差。

Result: 在覆盖多种先验、观测时间、稀疏度、几何形态与噪声条件的96种标准评估设定中，两种变体均优于固定传播。ECHO-Delta取得最佳平均精度，ECHO-Scale对几何变化更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 传播距离的状态依赖性对海冰浓度订正有效，ECHO框架通过自适应缩放或有界残差学习提升精度与鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Sea ice forecasts are issued several days ahead, allowing errors to accumulate while new, often sparse sea ice concentration (SIC) observations become available. We find that fixed-propagation errors concentrate near structured, high-gradient ice edges, whereas homogeneous interiors require limited propagation, suggesting that propagation distance should be state dependent. We therefore introduce ECHO (Evidence-guided Correction with Heterogeneous prOpagation), where ECHO-Scale adapts propagation distance while preserving correction geometry, and ECHO-Delta learns a bounded residual around fixed propagation. Across all 96 standard evaluation settings spanning diverse priors, observation times, sparsity levels, geometries, and noise conditions, both outperform fixed propagation. ECHO-Delta achieves the best average accuracy, while ECHO-Scale is more robust to geometry shifts. Code is available at https://github.com/yingtian22/TAKING-A-SECOND-LOOK.

</details>


### [251] [Muon Can Outperform Dedicated Continual Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24678)
*Sebastian George Sincari,Bogdan Alexandru Gheorghe,Antonio Barbalau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将普通增量 LoRA 配合 Muon 优化器（对更新做正交化）即可达到专用持续学习方法的精度水平，说明"约束更新"只需一个机制——来自损失或优化器均可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LoRA 的持续学习通过惩罚新更新与累积历史权重的重叠来缓解遗忘，但这只抑制了某些更新方向，并未控制更新在剩余方向上的能量分布。作者追问：这种限制是否必须任务感知（task-aware），还是优化器提供的通用限制就已足够。

Method: 训练普通增量 LoRA（IncLoRA）并配用 Muon 优化器（对每次更新做正交化），在 Standard CL Benchmark 上与 O-LoRA、ELLA 对比（5 个随机种子、3 种任务顺序），并在 TRACE 上用 3 个随机种子对比；同时分析两种优化器更新的有效奇异方向数与更新幅度。

Result: IncLoRA+Muon 在 Standard CL 上达到专用方法的精度区间，并在 TRACE 上优于所有 AdamW 配置。Standard CL 上第二个约束机制无增益，对最强约束方法反而损失 8.4 个精度点及拟合各任务的塑性。Muon 更新幅度为 AdamW 的 0.91–2.06 倍；AdamW 将更新限制在 1.4–1.8 个有效奇异方向，Muon 则分布于 7.0 个方向，二者在所有记录运行中均不重叠。

Conclusion: 只需一个更新约束机制，无论其来自损失函数还是优化器。区分两种优化器的不是更新幅度而是更新能量的分布几何。专用持续学习方法的部分优势可能源于优化器更新的几何性质。

Abstract: Continual learning with Low-Rank Adapters (LoRA) typically mitigates forgetting by penalizing the overlap between a new update and the accumulated past weights, which discourages certain update directions without controlling how an update distributes its energy over the ones that remain. We ask whether that restriction has to be task-aware, or whether a generic one supplied by the optimizer is enough. We train a plain incremental LoRA (IncLoRA) with Muon, which orthogonalizes each update, and compare it against O-LoRA and ELLA over five seeds and three task orders on the Standard CL Benchmark and three seeds on TRACE. IncLoRA+Muon reaches the accuracy band of the dedicated methods on Standard CL and improves on every AdamW configuration on TRACE. One update-constraining mechanism is enough, whether it comes from the loss or from the optimizer; on Standard CL a second one does not help, and for the most restrictive method it costs 8.4 points of accuracy and the plasticity to fit each task. What separates the two optimizers is not the size of the update, which under Muon is 0.91 to 2.06 times that under AdamW, but how it is distributed. AdamW confines it to between 1.4 and 1.8 effective singular directions, Muon spreads it over 7.0, and the two do not overlap in any tracked run. Part of the advantage usually attributed to dedicated CL methods may therefore be explained by the geometry of the optimizer's updates.

</details>


### [252] [Guaranteed Low-Rank Tensor Recovery from Modewise Measurements via Normalized Block-Weighted Riemannian Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24679)
*Yushi Zhou,Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应分块加权模态黎曼梯度下降方法，用于从线性测量中恢复低多重线性秩张量，在TRIP和合适初始化下建立局部线性收敛与采样保证，实验显示其减少迭代次数与计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低多重线性秩张量恢复中，传统黎曼梯度方法可能收敛较慢，且模态测量虽内存高效，但梯度核心与因子分量的尺度差异影响收敛效率；需要在不大幅增加秩界或核心尺寸的前提下加速收敛。

Method: 提出自适应分块加权模态黎曼梯度下降：结合内存高效的模态测量，并对黎曼梯度的核心分量与因子分量采用归一化自适应加权策略；该加权在不增加搜索方向多重线性秩界、也不增大用于回缩的缩减核心尺寸的情况下改善收敛。在张量受限等距性质（TRIP）与合适初始化下进行理论分析。

Result: 在TRIP和合适初始化下建立了局部线性收敛，并给出次高斯测量与SORS测量的采样保证。在合成低Tucker秩张量上的数值实验表明，所提方法在保持可靠恢复性能的同时减少迭代次数和计算时间，尤其在恢复阈值附近及结构化SORS测量下效果明显。

Conclusion: 该自适应分块加权模态黎曼梯度下降可在不增加计算或秩开销的条件下有效提升低多重线性秩张量恢复的收敛速度与效率，适用于近阈值和结构化模态测量场景。

Abstract: We consider the recovery of low-multilinear-rank tensors from linear measurements and propose an adaptive block-weighted modewise Riemannian gradient descent method. The method combines memory-efficient modewise measurements with a normalized adaptive weighting strategy for the core and factor components of the Riemannian gradient. The weighting improves convergence without increasing the multilinear-rank bound of the search direction or the size of the reduced core used for retraction. Under the tensor restricted isometry property and a suitable initialization, we establish local linear convergence and derive sampling guarantees for sub-Gaussian and subsampled orthogonal with random sign (SORS) measurements. Numerical experiments on synthetic low-Tucker-rank tensors show that the proposed method reduces iteration counts and computational time while maintaining reliable recovery performance, especially near the recovery threshold and for structured SORS measurements.

</details>


### [253] [A Federated Artificial Intelligence Framework for Optimizing Pancreatic Cancer Treatment - Strategy Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24718)
*Anne-Christin Hauschild,Amirreza Aleyasin,Nils H. Beyer,Lisa Fricke,Jonas Hügel,Maryam Moradpour,Anh-Tien Nguyen,Youngjun Park,Sophia Rheinländer,Tim Beissbarth,Elisabeth Hessmann,Martin Middeke,Matthias Lauth,Maximilian Reichert,Ulrich Sax*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文更新了胰腺癌联邦学习（FL）案例报告，介绍本地FL枢纽（分布式部署的Docker容器）中的AI流程、处理部分重叠特征的新FL算法及初步结果，并总结已克服与仍存在的推广难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式数据采集虽在数据质量与预测性能上最优，但在实践中（尤其涉及GDPR合规与多方协作）往往不可行；需探索在分布式医疗资源上可行的替代方案。

Method: 在各参与站点将可访问数据经提取与转换后接入本地FL枢纽，即以集中开发、分布式部署的Docker容器承载FL脚本以生成局部模型；采用能利用所有本地特征（含站点特有的部分重叠特征）的新FL算法，并探索基于德国肿瘤学核心数据集（oBDS）进行注释与队列构建。

Result: 报告了实际联邦学习AI流程的初步结果；新FL算法在公开数据集上表现出对部分重叠特征的稳健性；同时解决了基础设施运营概念、新型架构的伦理审批及各站点支持等主要障碍。

Conclusion: 该FL方法在胰腺癌亚型识别与治疗评估中具备可行性，但未来规模化面临挑战：纳入更广的多模态数据可行，而向更多站点大规模部署仍较困难。

Abstract: While a centralized approach involving patient consent to collect and analyze data centrally would theoretically offer the best data quality and predictive performance, it is not always feasible in practice. Federated Learning (FL) architectures have shown to be a very promising approach to use and access distributed disease related resources within the GDPR boundaries. In a previous case report, we described the preconditions at the participating sites and necessary administrative and process related steps to prepare data, people and infrastructure for improving subtype identification and assessing treatment options in pancreatic cancer. We update this report sharing our experience in tackling the challenges and show preliminary results of the actual federated learning AI pipelines. At the participating sites, we have to identify and annotate the data being accessible after extraction and transformation in a local FL hub - in our case a centrally developed and distributively deployed Docker container. This container comprises the FL scripts generating local models. We apply a newly developed FL algorithm considering all local features, including partial overlapping features specific to the local sites. Theoretically, an annotation in a cancer setting should succeed using the German oncology core data set (oBDS), which is already utilized for mandatory reporting to cancer registries, and can be sustained in the FL setting. The FL algorithms deal robustly with partially overlapping features as we showed with public data sets. Major roadblocks including straightening operational concepts for the infrastructures, ethics approval for such novel architectures and support for every site have been addressed. However, scaling up this approach in the future faces hurdles; while including broader multi-modal data sets should be feasible, large-scale deployment to more sites remains challenging.

</details>


### [254] [An Exact Junction-Tree Extended Formulation for Optimal Classification Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24741)
*Jiancheng TU,WenqiFan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于联结树的精确线性规划建模方法求解定深二值特征分类树，含精确约简、列生成与消息传递两种算法，实现显著加速与更多实例的最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定深分类树的最优求解在混合整数规划框架下常难以在给定预算内证明最优性，需要更紧凑、可分解的精确模型与高效求解手段。

Method: 用联结树表示建立定深二值特征分类树的精确LP公式，该公式具有整数性并支持递归子树优化；通过精确约简缩小模型并保持最优值与最优树可恢复性；在约简模型上采用列生成（求解整数限制LP并以全可行域界证明最优性）与消息传递（递归合并最优子树代价）两种方法，二者均可并行求解固定前置决策后的公共子树问题。

Result: 精确约简显著缩小联结树公式规模；该LP公式可在相同计算预算内证明部分测试MIP无法证明最优性的实例；列生成与消息传递证明更多实例最优，几何平均运行时间较现有最优分类树精确方法降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 联结树LP建模结合精确约简与可分解求解算法，为最优分类树提供了更紧凑、更可扩展的精确求解途径。

Abstract: We develop an exact linear programming (LP) formulation for bounded-depth classification trees with binary features, using a junction-tree representation. The formulation is integral and supports recursive subtree optimization. Exact reductions make the model smaller while preserving the optimal value and recovery of an optimal tree. The reduced model supports two solution methods: column generation and message passing. Column generation solves integral restricted LPs and uses bounds over the full feasible domain to certify optimality. Message passing recursively combines optimal subtree costs. Both methods solve common subtree problems that, once the preceding tree decisions are fixed, can be evaluated independently and in parallel. Computational experiments show that the exact reductions substantially reduce the size of the junction-tree formulation. The resulting linear programming formulation certifies instances for which the tested mixed-integer formulation does not establish optimality within the same computational budget, while the column-generation and message-passing methods certify more instances and achieve an order-of-magnitude reduction in geometric-mean runtime relative to an existing state-of-the-art exact method for optimal classification trees.

</details>


### [255] [Inference of Unknown Dynamical Components Using Next Generation Reservoir Computing: From Chaotic Systems to Climate Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24754)
*Jule Budnick,Andrew Keane,Serhiy Yanchuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将下一代储备池计算（NGRC）用于动力学系统缺失分量的数据驱动推断，在Lorenz、Rössler系统及ENSO气候观测数据上验证其高效性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储备池计算（RC）在推断动力学系统未观测分量时通常需要更多训练数据和计算时间，因此需要探索更高效的数据驱动方法。

Method: 将NGRC与RC对比，在Lorenz和Rössler系统中由单一已知分量推断两个未知分量；分析时延步数与时间分辨率的反比关系；并将NGRC应用于ENSO观测气候数据，由其余变量推断某一可观测量。

Result: 在两个受控系统中，NGRC均取得准确结果，且所需训练数据更少、计算时间更短；发现NGRC所需时延步数与时间分辨率呈反比，表明时延间隔覆盖的物理时间跨度是关键因素；在含噪声且复杂的真实ENSO数据上，NGRC也展现出有前景的结果。

Conclusion: NGRC在受控动力学系统与真实世界数据中均能高效推断未观测分量，具有较好的应用潜力。

Abstract: We investigate next generation reservoir computing (NGRC) as a data-driven approach for inferring unseen components of dynamical systems. We compare NGRC with traditional reservoir computing (RC) using the Lorenz and Rössler system, where two unknown components are inferred from one given component. For both systems, NGRC achieves accurate results while requiring fewer training data and less computational time than RC. We identified an inverse proportional behavior between the number of time-delayed steps needed for NGRC and the temporal resolution, indicating that the physical time span covered by the delay interval is an important factor in determining the required number of delayed steps. Finally, we apply NGRC to the observational climate data of ENSO (El Niño--Southern Oscillation) and infer one observable from the remaining variables. Despite the noise and complexity of the real-world data, the NGRC shows promising results. Our findings demonstrate the potential of NGRC for efficient inference of unseen components in both controlled dynamical systems and real-world data.

</details>


### [256] [Complex KDA: Understanding and Enhancing the Expressivity of Kimi Delta Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24797)
*Julien Siems,Riccardo Grazzi,Korbinian Pöppel,Jaisidh Singh,Arber Zela,Timur Carstensen,Jenia Jitsev,Frank Hutter,Volkan Cevher,Antonio Orvieto,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Complex KDA (CKDA)，通过将单一 delta-rule 变换与通道门控提供的反射相结合，使 KDA 能实现 2D 旋转，从而在保持对角加秩一结构与稳定性的同时达到 DeltaProduct₂ 的状态追踪表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 delta-rule 的线性 RNN 具有高效序列建模能力，但其带低秩校正的线性更新限制了表达力。已有工作表明，在单次循环更新中复合两个 delta-rule 过渡可建模 2D 旋转，但这相比单次过渡会增加更新秩与计算成本。因此需要一种在不增加秩和成本的前提下实现 2D 旋转的方法。

Method: 作者提出 CKDA，将单一 delta-rule 变换与其通道门控提供的第二次反射相结合来实现 2D 旋转。为此扩展 KDA 的参数范围：结合两种已有的范围扩展，允许门控取值于 [-1,1]，delta-rule 系数 β 取值于 [0,2]。所得模型保持对角加秩一且非扩张的过渡，并保持 KDA 的稳定性与效率。

Result: 作者刻画了 CKDA 的表达能力，并证明每个正交的对角加秩一矩阵都恰好是 CKDA 过渡矩阵。单层 CKDA 可追踪任何与 SO(3) 子群同构的有限群；相较于其他对角加秩一线性 RNN，许多状态追踪结果对 CKDA 可少用一层。实验上，在 S₃、S₄ 和周期音频续写上，组合两种扩展在测试的 KDA 范围设置中取得最强的长度外推。语言建模中 CKDA 优于 Transformer 和其他线性 RNN，与 KDA 基线结果相近，并展现出有前景的扩展行为。

Conclusion: CKDA 通过结合 gate 范围扩展与 β 范围扩展，使单次 delta-rule 更新即可实现 2D 旋转，在不增加秩与成本的情况下获得更强的状态追踪表达力，并在长度外推与语言建模上表现优异。代码与模型均已开源。

Abstract: Linear RNNs based on the delta-rule enable efficient sequence modeling, but their linear updates with a low-rank correction constrain their expressivity. Prior work has shown that composing two delta-rule transitions in a single recurrent update can model a 2D rotation, but this increases the rank and the cost of the updates compared to a single transition. We show that Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) can realize 2D rotations by combining a single delta-rule transformation with a second reflection supplied by its channel-wise gate. This requires extending the parameter ranges of KDA by combining two existing range extensions: allowing gates in $[-1,1]$ and the delta-rule coefficient $β$ in $[0,2]$. We call the resulting model Complex KDA (CKDA). It preserves KDA's stability and efficiency, with transitions that remain diagonal-plus-rank-one and non-expansive, while reaching the state-tracking expressivity of DeltaProduct$_2$. We characterize the expressivity of CKDA and prove that every orthogonal diagonal-plus-rank-one matrix is exactly a CKDA transition matrix. A single CKDA layer can track every finite group isomorphic to a subgroup of $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, and many state-tracking results use one fewer layer for CKDA compared to other diagonal-plus-rank-one Linear RNNs. Empirically, combining both extensions yields the strongest length extrapolation among tested KDA range settings on $S_3$, $S_4$, and periodic audio continuation. In language modeling, CKDA outperforms Transformers and other linear RNNs, obtains similar results to a KDA baseline, and shows promising scaling behavior. Our code is open source at https://github.com/OpenEuroLLM/ComplexKDA and our models are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/openeurollm/complexkda.

</details>


### [257] [G-NAC: Graph Neural Automata Clustering via Emergent Domain Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24823)
*Keith Miller,Tristan Crawford*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督聚类方法G-NAC，将观测作为固定邻域图上的元胞，用共享循环图神经元胞规则演化隐状态并转为秩基谱亲和度进行划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种以循环图元胞自动机规则驱动的聚类新范式，在保持可扩展性的同时达到与强基线可比的聚类性能。

Method: G-NAC为无监督聚类方法：观测在固定邻域图上以元胞形式局部交互；共享的循环图神经元胞规则通过局部交互演化潜在域状态；将演化后的状态转换为基于秩的谱亲和度（rank-based spectral affinity）用于图划分。

Result: 在来自57个基准数据集的73个聚类任务上，G-NAC平均ARI为0.7951，与Genie（0.7941）相当，优于其他评估基线；训练时间和GPU显存从5,000到100,000节点近似线性增长；学习到的转移规则可从较小源图迁移到相同条件下生成的独立100,000节点样本。

Conclusion: 结果验证了循环图聚类范式的可行性，同时指出该方法依赖图质量、读出设计以及源-目标图之间的相似性。

Abstract: We introduce Graph Neural Automata Clustering (G-NAC), an unsupervised clustering method in which observations interact as cells on a fixed neighborhood graph. A shared recurrent graph-neural cellular rule evolves latent domain states through local interactions, which are converted into a rank-based spectral affinity for partitioning.
